diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/already-encoded.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/already-encoded.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index aecc2a4..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/already-encoded.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,113 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * already-encoded.ts — the novelty tier's baseline builder. Proves each
- * source is read straight off disk (never hand-listed) and that an absent
- * source contributes nothing rather than throwing, matching the fail-soft
- * stance the rest of the corpus-reading layer takes.
- */
-
-import { mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { buildAlreadyEncoded } from '../src/already-encoded.js';
-import { emitProposalPages } from '../src/report.js';
-import type { Proposal } from '../src/propose.js';
-
-function tmpDir(prefix: string): string {
-  return mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), prefix));
-}
-
-function stubProposal(intent: string): Proposal {
-  return {
-    candidateId: 'tool:x',
-    clusterKey: 'tool:x',
-    score: 0.5,
-    intent,
-    form: 'standing-loop',
-    mechanizable: true,
-    mechanizabilityRationale: 'stable enough',
-    fitnessFunction: {
-      claim: `${intent} stays correct`,
-      method: 'synthetic-test',
-      assertion: 'PASS when it matches; FAIL when it diverges.',
-      draft: true,
-    },
-    payoff: `${intent} saves recurring work.`,
-    citations: ['Bash(x)'],
-    dossier: {
-      candidateId: 'tool:x',
-      clusterKey: 'tool:x',
-      kind: 'tool',
-      label: 'Bash(x)',
-      eventCount: 4,
-      sessionCount: 4,
-      projectCount: 1,
-      projects: ['/Users/me/repo'],
-      cadence: [],
-      exemplars: ['Bash(x)'],
-      toil: { frequency: 4, medianDurationMs: 1000, interventions: 0 },
-      firstSeen: '2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z',
-      lastSeen: '2026-06-04T09:00:00.000Z',
-      fingerprint: { kind: 'tool', memberHashes: ['Bash(x)'] },
-    },
-    tokensUsed: 64,
-  };
-}
-
-describe('buildAlreadyEncoded — instructions from CLAUDE.md files', () => {
-  it('reads bullet and heading lines from every existing path, skipping absent ones', () => {
-    const dir = tmpDir('loophole-already-encoded-');
-    const claudeMd = join(dir, 'CLAUDE.md');
-    writeFileSync(claudeMd, '# Rules\n- Never fix symptoms\n- Always test first\n\n```\nignored code fence\n```\n');
-
-    const encoded = buildAlreadyEncoded({ claudeMdPaths: [claudeMd, join(dir, 'missing.md')] });
-
-    expect(encoded.instructions).toEqual(['Rules', 'Never fix symptoms', 'Always test first']);
-    expect(encoded.skills).toEqual([]);
-    expect(encoded.loops).toEqual([]);
-  });
-
-  it('with no paths given, contributes zero instructions', () => {
-    expect(buildAlreadyEncoded({}).instructions).toEqual([]);
-    expect(buildAlreadyEncoded().instructions).toEqual([]);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('buildAlreadyEncoded — skills from skill directories', () => {
-  it('names each skill from its SKILL.md heading, falling back to the directory name', () => {
-    const dir = tmpDir('loophole-already-encoded-skills-');
-    mkdirSync(join(dir, 'tend-audit'));
-    writeFileSync(join(dir, 'tend-audit', 'SKILL.md'), '# Verify a feature against real evidence\nBody text.');
-    mkdirSync(join(dir, 'unlabeled-skill'));
-    writeFileSync(join(dir, 'unlabeled-skill', 'SKILL.md'), 'No heading here.');
-    mkdirSync(join(dir, 'not-a-skill')); // no SKILL.md — not counted
-    writeFileSync(join(dir, 'stray-file.md'), 'a file, not a skill dir');
-
-    const encoded = buildAlreadyEncoded({ skillsDirs: [dir, join(dir, 'nonexistent')] });
-
-    expect([...encoded.skills].sort()).toEqual(['Verify a feature against real evidence', 'unlabeled-skill'].sort());
-  });
-
-  it('an absent or unreadable skills dir contributes zero skills, never throws', () => {
-    expect(() => buildAlreadyEncoded({ skillsDirs: ['/definitely/not/a/real/path'] })).not.toThrow();
-    expect(buildAlreadyEncoded({ skillsDirs: ['/definitely/not/a/real/path'] }).skills).toEqual([]);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('buildAlreadyEncoded — standing loop titles from a prior out dir', () => {
-  it('reads every draft page title already emitted into outDir', () => {
-    const out = tmpDir('loophole-already-encoded-out-');

-

-  it('an absent out dir contributes zero standing loops', () => {

-  it('with no out dir given, contributes zero standing loops', () => {

-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/analyst-wiring.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/analyst-wiring.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index c83e903..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/analyst-wiring.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,275 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Pipeline wiring for the agent-lane judgment tier (src/analyst.ts) — the
- * missing integration a real run surfaced (design:
- * docs/loop-hole-episodes-design.md, "THE BUILD CONTRACT"; propose.loop.html's
- * "Tried" log, 2026-07-03): novelty/reach/typology were module-true in
- * isolation, but `runPipeline` never invoked the analyst model pass, and a
- * refused triage/analyst judgment swallowed its own reason. This file proves
- * four things:
- *  - the one-command pipeline actually APPLIES the analyst pass (merges
- *    same-rituals, drops noise) rather than silently bypassing the module;
- *  - emitted proposals carry a typology form, a computed novelty verdict, and
- *    a reach tier derivable from their own evidence;
- *  - a refused (or absent) analyst run degrades visibly — an UN-ANALYZED
- *    banner, never a silent pass-through that looks identical to a clean run;
- *  - both triage and analyst refusals surface their REASON, not just the
- *    fact of failure.
- */
-
-import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { extractPayload } from '../../../loop/src/core/extract.js';
-import { runPipeline } from '../src/run.js';
-import type { AnalystJudge } from '../src/analyst.js';
-import type { TriageJudge } from '../src/triage.js';
-import type { AlreadyEncoded } from '../src/facets.js';
-import { judgeReach } from '../src/reach.js';
-import { PROPOSAL_FORMS } from '../src/typology.js';
-import { normalizeCommand } from '../src/normalize.js';
-import { makeCorpus, toolCall, userTurn } from './helpers.js';
-
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-const START = Date.parse('2026-06-01T09:00:00Z');
-
-/** Two halves of one ritual (find a stuck port, then kill it) recurring
- * across sessions, plus an unrelated routine tool call — three tool
- * templates, each occurring often enough to clear the detector's floor. */
-function toolRitualCorpus(): string {
-  const sessions = [];
-  for (let day = 0; day < 4; day += 1) {
-    const at = (offset: number) => new Date(START + day * DAY + offset * 60_000).toISOString();
-    sessions.push({
-      dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-      sessionId: `session-${day}`,
-      records: [
-        userTurn('is the dev server still stuck?', at(0)),
-        toolCall('Bash', { command: `lsof -i :${8471 + day}` }, at(1)),
-        toolCall('Bash', { command: `kill -9 ${9000 + day}` }, at(2)),
-        toolCall('Bash', { command: 'git status' }, at(3)),
-      ],
-    });
-  }
-  return makeCorpus(sessions);
-}
-
-// Derived from the real normalizer (not hand-typed) so the fixture never
-// drifts from what normalizeCommand actually produces — `kill -9 …` collapses
-// the flag's own digit too, giving `kill -{n} {n}`, not the naively-guessed
-// `kill -9 {n}`.
-const PORT_TEMPLATE = `Bash(${normalizeCommand('lsof -i :8471')})`;
-const KILL_TEMPLATE = `Bash(${normalizeCommand('kill -9 9000')})`;
-const STATUS_TEMPLATE = `Bash(${normalizeCommand('git status')})`;
-const PORT_ID = `tool:${PORT_TEMPLATE}`;
-const KILL_ID = `tool:${KILL_TEMPLATE}`;
-const STATUS_ID = `tool:${STATUS_TEMPLATE}`;
-
-function outDir(): string {
-  return mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-analyst-wiring-'));
-}
-
-/** Merges the kill half into the port half (retitled), drops the routine
- * status check as noise. */
-function mergingAnalyst(calls: string[]): AnalystJudge {
-  return (prompt) => {
-    calls.push(prompt);
-    return JSON.stringify([
-      { id: PORT_ID, classification: 'automation-candidate', title: 'Free a stuck dev-server port', mergeInto: null, reason: 'find-then-kill pair' },
-      { id: KILL_ID, classification: 'automation-candidate', mergeInto: PORT_ID, reason: 'same ritual, second half' },
-      { id: STATUS_ID, classification: 'noise', reason: 'routine status check, not a ritual' },
-    ]);
-  };
-}
-
-function briefingPayload(outPath: string): string {
-  const html = readFileSync(join(outPath, 'loop-hole-briefing.loop.html'), 'utf8');
-  return extractPayload(html)!.payload;
-}
-
-describe('analyst pipeline wiring — the pass actually runs', () => {
-  it('applies the analyst pass: merges the same ritual, drops noise, and the retitled survivor reaches propose', async () => {
-    const corpus = toolRitualCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-    const calls: string[] = [];
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out, analystJudge: mergingAnalyst(calls) });
-
-    expect(calls).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(result.analyst.applied).toBe(true);
-    expect(result.analyst.dropped).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.analyst.merged).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.analyst.reason).toBeUndefined();
-
-    const toolProposals = result.propose.proposals.filter((p) => p.dossier.kind === 'tool');
-    expect(toolProposals).toHaveLength(1);
-    const survivor = toolProposals[0]!;
-    expect(survivor.intent).toContain('Free a stuck dev-server port');
-    // Counts recomputed from the UNION of both templates' real events, not prose.
-    expect(survivor.dossier.eventCount).toBe(8);
-
-    // The noise candidate never reaches propose — dropped, not merely unranked.
-    expect(result.propose.proposals.some((p) => p.dossier.label === STATUS_TEMPLATE)).toBe(false);
-
-    // No un-analyzed banner on a clean, applied pass.
-    expect(briefingPayload(out)).not.toContain('UN-ANALYZED');
-  });
-
-  it('a repairable-malformed analyst reply (#46) still applies, and the pipeline surfaces repairedChunks instead of a refusal', async () => {
-    const corpus = toolRitualCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-    const repairableAnalyst: AnalystJudge = () =>
-      `[{id: '${PORT_ID}', classification: 'automation-candidate', title: 'Free a stuck dev-server port', mergeInto: null, reason: 'r',}, {id: '${KILL_ID}', classification: 'automation-candidate', mergeInto: '${PORT_ID}', reason: 'r'}, {id: '${STATUS_ID}', classification: 'noise', reason: 'r'}]`;
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out, analystJudge: repairableAnalyst });
-
-    expect(result.analyst.applied).toBe(true);
-    expect(result.analyst.reason).toBeUndefined();
-    expect(result.analyst.repairedChunks).toBe(1);
-    expect(briefingPayload(out)).not.toContain('UN-ANALYZED');
-  });
-
-  it('emitted proposals carry a typology form and a reach tier derivable from their own evidence', async () => {
-    const corpus = toolRitualCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out, analystJudge: mergingAnalyst([]) });
-    expect(result.propose.proposals.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-
-    for (const proposal of result.propose.proposals) {
-      expect(PROPOSAL_FORMS).toContain(proposal.form);
-      // Reach is a pure function of the dossier's own projectCount — carried
-      // by construction, never a separate hand-set field to drift from it.
-      expect(() => judgeReach(proposal.dossier.projectCount)).not.toThrow();
-    }
-  });
-
-  it('attaches a computed novelty verdict to emitted proposals when an already-encoded baseline is given', async () => {
-    const corpus = toolRitualCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-    const alreadyEncoded: AlreadyEncoded = {
-      instructions: ['Free a stuck dev-server port'],
-      skills: [],
-      loops: [],
-    };
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({
-      corpusDir: corpus,
-      outDir: out,
-      analystJudge: mergingAnalyst([]),
-      alreadyEncoded,
-    });
-
-    const survivor = result.propose.proposals.find((p) => p.dossier.kind === 'tool')!;
-    expect(survivor).toBeDefined();
-    expect(survivor.novelty).toBe('covered');
-    expect(survivor.noveltyMatch).toBe('Free a stuck dev-server port');
-  });
-
-  it('marks a proposal novel when nothing in the already-encoded set restates it', async () => {
-    const corpus = toolRitualCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-    const alreadyEncoded: AlreadyEncoded = {
-      instructions: ['always write the release notes before tagging a version'],
-      skills: [],
-      loops: [],
-    };
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({
-      corpusDir: corpus,
-      outDir: out,
-      analystJudge: mergingAnalyst([]),
-      alreadyEncoded,
-    });
-
-    const survivor = result.propose.proposals.find((p) => p.dossier.kind === 'tool')!;
-    expect(survivor.novelty).toBe('novel');
-    expect(survivor.noveltyMatch).toBeUndefined();
-  });
-
-  it('with no already-encoded baseline given, proposals carry no novelty verdict at all', async () => {
-    const corpus = toolRitualCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out, analystJudge: mergingAnalyst([]) });
-
-    for (const proposal of result.propose.proposals) {
-      expect(proposal.novelty).toBeUndefined();
-    }
-  });
-
-  it('a refused analyst pass never silently bypasses the module: the agent lane runs un-analyzed, the briefing carries a visible banner, and the reason surfaces in the result', async () => {
-    const corpus = toolRitualCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-    const throwingJudge: AnalystJudge = () => {
-      throw new Error('model unavailable');
-    };
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out, analystJudge: throwingJudge });
-
-    expect(result.analyst.applied).toBe(false);
-    expect(result.analyst.dropped).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.analyst.merged).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.analyst.reason).toContain('model unavailable');
-
-    // Un-analyzed, not blocked: the agent lane's raw (unmerged, un-dropped)
-    // tool candidates still reach propose — degraded, never withheld.
-    const toolProposals = result.propose.proposals.filter((p) => p.dossier.kind === 'tool');
-    expect(toolProposals.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-    expect(toolProposals.some((p) => p.dossier.label === STATUS_TEMPLATE)).toBe(true);
-
-    expect(briefingPayload(out)).toContain('UN-ANALYZED');
-    expect(briefingPayload(out)).toContain('model unavailable');
-  });
-
-  it('malformed analyst output refuses the whole pass with a structural reason, not a thrown-error reason', async () => {
-    const corpus = toolRitualCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-    const gibberishJudge: AnalystJudge = () => 'the model wrote an essay instead';
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out, analystJudge: gibberishJudge });
-
-    expect(result.analyst.applied).toBe(false);
-    expect(result.analyst.reason).toContain('structural validation');
-  });
-
-  it('with no analyst judge configured at all, the run performs zero judge calls, reports zero counts with no reason, and the agent lane still proposes un-analyzed', async () => {
-    const corpus = toolRitualCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out });
-
-    expect(result.analyst).toEqual({ applied: false, dropped: 0, merged: 0 });
-    expect(result.propose.proposals.some((p) => p.dossier.kind === 'tool')).toBe(true);
-    expect(briefingPayload(out)).toContain('UN-ANALYZED');
-  });
-
-  it('the env off-switch shape is deterministic: an absent analystJudge and a throwing one both leave the run reproducible (no nondeterministic model call)', async () => {
-    const corpus = toolRitualCorpus();
-
-    const first = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: outDir() });
-    const second = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: outDir() });
-
-    expect(second.propose.proposals.map((p) => p.dossier.label).sort()).toEqual(
-      first.propose.proposals.map((p) => p.dossier.label).sort(),
-    );
-  });
-});
-
-describe('triage refusal reason surfaces the same way', () => {
-  it('a throwing triage judge leaves its reason on the result and in the UNTRIAGED banner', async () => {
-    const corpus = toolRitualCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-    const throwingJudge: TriageJudge = () => {
-      throw new Error('triage model unavailable');
-    };
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out, triageJudge: throwingJudge });
-
-    expect(result.triage.applied).toBe(false);
-    expect(result.triage.reason).toContain('triage model unavailable');
-    expect(briefingPayload(out)).toContain('UNTRIAGED');
-    expect(briefingPayload(out)).toContain('triage model unavailable');
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/analyst.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/analyst.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 8eed61e..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/analyst.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,287 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * The analyst tier's application layer — the code that decides whether a
- * model's agent-lane verdicts are USABLE. The model itself is a seam
- * (AnalystJudge); what this file pins is the fail-closed contract around it:
- * invented ids, illegal merges (chains, merges into a noise candidate,
- * self-merges), untitled survivors, or a merge that loses its trail back to
- * real evidence refuse the WHOLE analyst pass — the pipeline is left to run
- * the agent lane un-analyzed rather than act on a partially-trusted verdict
- * batch. When verdicts are valid, merges are recomputed from the store's own
- * events, never from prose.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { applyAnalyst, parseAnalystVerdicts, runAnalyst } from '../src/analyst.js';
-import type { RecurrenceCandidate } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import type { ActionEvent, StoredState } from '../src/types.js';
-
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-const START = Date.parse('2026-06-01T09:00:00Z');
-
-/** Two rituals sharing a template each (tool:0, tool:1) + one orphan template (tool:2). */
-function state(): StoredState {
-  const templates = ['Bash(lsof -i :{port})', 'Bash(kill -9 {pid})', 'Grep({pattern})'];
-  const events: ActionEvent[] = [];
-  for (let day = 0; day < 4; day += 1) {
-    for (let index = 0; index < templates.length; index += 1) {
-      const epoch = START + day * DAY + index * 1000;
-      events.push({
-        kind: 'tool',
-        ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(),
-        epoch,
-        project: index === 2 ? '/repo/b' : '/repo/a',
-        session: `session-${day}-${index}`,
-        tool: 'Bash',
-        template: templates[index]!,
-      });
-    }
-  }
-  return { version: 2, watermark: {}, turns: [], events };
-}
-
-function candidate(id: string, template: string, score = 0.4): RecurrenceCandidate {
-  return {
-    id,
-    kind: 'tool',
-    label: template,
-    eventCount: 4,
-    sessionCount: 4,
-    projectCount: 1,
-    firstSeen: new Date(START).toISOString(),
-    lastSeen: new Date(START + 3 * DAY).toISOString(),
-    provenance: [`tool template ${template}`],
-    score: { score, components: { frequency: 1, cadenceRegularity: 1, mechanizability: 1, manualCost: 1, stalenessRisk: 1 } },
-  };
-}
-
-const A = candidate('tool:0', 'Bash(lsof -i :{port})', 0.5);
-const B = candidate('tool:1', 'Bash(kill -9 {pid})', 0.4);
-const C = candidate('tool:2', 'Grep({pattern})', 0.3);
-const IDS = new Set([A.id, B.id, C.id]);
-
-const VALID = JSON.stringify([
-  { id: A.id, classification: 'automation-candidate', title: 'Free a stuck port', mergeInto: null, reason: 'find-then-kill pair' },
-  { id: B.id, classification: 'automation-candidate', mergeInto: A.id, reason: 'same ritual, second half' },
-  { id: C.id, classification: 'noise', reason: 'one-off search' },
-]);
-
-describe('analyst application layer', () => {
-  it('merges same-rituals (recomputed from events), drops noise, retitles', () => {
-    const verdicts = parseAnalystVerdicts(VALID, IDS)!;
-    expect(verdicts).not.toBeNull();
-    const result = applyAnalyst([A, B, C], verdicts, state())!;
-
-    expect(result).not.toBeNull();
-    expect(result.candidates).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(result.dropped).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.merged).toBe(1);
-    const survivor = result.candidates[0]!;
-    expect(survivor.label).toBe('Free a stuck port');
-    // Counts recomputed from the UNION of both templates' events, not prose.
-    expect(survivor.eventCount).toBe(8);
-    expect(survivor.projectCount).toBe(1);
-    // Verbatim provenance from both members survives the merge.
-    expect(survivor.provenance.join('\n')).toContain('Bash(lsof -i :{port})');
-    expect(survivor.provenance.join('\n')).toContain('Bash(kill -9 {pid})');
-  });
-
-  it('candidates beyond the analyzed head pass through untouched', () => {
-    const judge = (): string => JSON.stringify([
-      { id: A.id, classification: 'automation-candidate', title: 'Free a stuck port', mergeInto: null, reason: 'ok' },
-    ]);
-    const result = runAnalyst([A, B, C], state(), judge, 1)!;
-    expect(result.candidates.map((c) => c.id).sort()).toEqual([A.id, B.id, C.id].sort());
-  });
-
-  it.each([
-    ['invented id', JSON.stringify([{ id: 'tool:999', classification: 'automation-candidate', title: 'x', reason: 'r' }])],
-    ['duplicate verdicts', JSON.stringify([
-      { id: 'tool:0', classification: 'noise', reason: 'r' },
-      { id: 'tool:0', classification: 'automation-candidate', title: 'x', reason: 'r' },
-    ])],
-    ['merge chain', JSON.stringify([
-      { id: 'tool:0', classification: 'automation-candidate', mergeInto: 'tool:1', reason: 'r' },
-      { id: 'tool:1', classification: 'automation-candidate', mergeInto: 'tool:2', reason: 'r' },
-      { id: 'tool:2', classification: 'automation-candidate', title: 'x', reason: 'r' },
-    ])],
-    ['merge into noise', JSON.stringify([
-      { id: 'tool:0', classification: 'automation-candidate', mergeInto: 'tool:2', reason: 'r' },
-      { id: 'tool:2', classification: 'noise', reason: 'r' },
-    ])],
-    ['untitled survivor', JSON.stringify([{ id: 'tool:0', classification: 'automation-candidate', reason: 'r' }])],
-    ['self-merge', JSON.stringify([{ id: 'tool:0', classification: 'automation-candidate', mergeInto: 'tool:0', reason: 'r' }])],
-    ['unrecognized classification', JSON.stringify([{ id: 'tool:0', classification: 'looks-useful', title: 'x', reason: 'r' }])],
-    ['not json', 'the model wrote an essay instead'],
-  ])('refuses the whole analyst pass on %s', (_name, raw) => {
-    expect(parseAnalystVerdicts(raw, IDS)).toBeNull();
-  });
-
-  it('refuses the whole pass when a merge loses its provenance trail', () => {
-    // Adversarial input: two candidates whose provenance names templates that
-    // resolve to zero real events (data the analyst tier did not itself
-    // produce, but must not trust blindly) — merging them must not fabricate
-    // a survivor out of evidence that traces to nothing in the store.
-    const ghostA: RecurrenceCandidate = { ...candidate('tool:3', 'Bash(does-not-exist-a)'), provenance: ['tool template Bash(does-not-exist-a)'] };
-    const ghostB: RecurrenceCandidate = { ...candidate('tool:4', 'Bash(does-not-exist-b)'), provenance: ['tool template Bash(does-not-exist-b)'] };
-    const verdicts = parseAnalystVerdicts(
-      JSON.stringify([
-        { id: ghostA.id, classification: 'automation-candidate', mergeInto: ghostB.id, reason: 'r' },
-        { id: ghostB.id, classification: 'automation-candidate', title: 'Looks plausible', reason: 'r' },
-      ]),
-      new Set([ghostA.id, ghostB.id]),
-    );
-    expect(verdicts).not.toBeNull();
-    expect(applyAnalyst([ghostA, ghostB], verdicts!, state())).toBeNull();
-  });
-
-  it('refuses the whole pass when the model output is empty of any real candidate provenance', () => {
-    const empty: RecurrenceCandidate = { ...candidate('tool:4', 'Bash(nothing)'), provenance: [] };
-    const verdicts = parseAnalystVerdicts(
-      JSON.stringify([{ id: empty.id, classification: 'automation-candidate', title: 'x', reason: 'r' }]),
-      new Set([empty.id]),
-    )!;
-    expect(applyAnalyst([empty], verdicts, state())).toBeNull();
-  });
-
-  it('a throwing judge refuses only its own chunk, not the whole pass — a single-chunk head degrades to fully un-analyzed', () => {
-    const judge = (): string => {
-      throw new Error('model unavailable');
-    };
-    const result = runAnalyst([A, B, C], state(), judge)!;
-    expect(result).not.toBeNull();
-    expect(result.refusedChunks).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.totalChunks).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.candidates.map((c) => c.id).sort()).toEqual([A.id, B.id, C.id].sort());
-  });
-
-  it('an empty candidate list never calls the judge and yields an empty, unrefused result', () => {
-    const judge = (): string => {
-      throw new Error('should never be called');
-    };
-    expect(runAnalyst([], state(), judge)).toEqual({
-      candidates: [],
-      dropped: 0,
-      merged: 0,
-      refusedChunks: 0,
-      totalChunks: 0,
-      repairedChunks: 0,
-      malformedUnrepairableChunks: 0,
-      wrongChunks: 0,
-    });
-  });
-
-  it('onRefusal reports why: a thrown judge names the thrown error', () => {
-    const judge = (): string => {
-      throw new Error('model unavailable');
-    };
-    let reason: string | undefined;
-    const result = runAnalyst([A, B, C], state(), judge, 30, (r) => { reason = r; })!;
-    expect(result).not.toBeNull();
-    expect(result.refusedChunks).toBe(1);
-    expect(reason).toContain('model unavailable');
-  });
-
-  it('onRefusal reports why: a lost-provenance merge names that failure specifically, not a generic one', () => {
-    const ghostA: RecurrenceCandidate = { ...candidate('tool:3', 'Bash(does-not-exist-a)'), provenance: ['tool template Bash(does-not-exist-a)'] };
-    const ghostB: RecurrenceCandidate = { ...candidate('tool:4', 'Bash(does-not-exist-b)'), provenance: ['tool template Bash(does-not-exist-b)'] };
-    const judge = (): string => JSON.stringify([
-      { id: ghostA.id, classification: 'automation-candidate', mergeInto: ghostB.id, reason: 'r' },
-      { id: ghostB.id, classification: 'automation-candidate', title: 'Looks plausible', reason: 'r' },
-    ]);
-    let reason: string | undefined;
-    expect(runAnalyst([ghostA, ghostB], state(), judge, 30, (r) => { reason = r; })).toBeNull();
-    expect(reason).toContain('provenance');
-  });
-
-  it('onRefusal never fires on a successful analyst pass', () => {
-    const judge = (): string => VALID;
-    let fired = false;
-    runAnalyst([A, B, C], state(), judge, 30, () => { fired = true; });
-    expect(fired).toBe(false);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('analyst batch resilience — repair-before-reject (#46: malformed ≠ wrong)', () => {
-  it('a repairable-malformed chunk succeeds and is counted as repaired, not refused', () => {
-    const judge = (): string =>
-      `[{id: '${A.id}', classification: 'automation-candidate', title: 'Free a stuck port', mergeInto: null, reason: 'r',}, {id: '${B.id}', classification: 'noise', reason: 'r'}, {id: '${C.id}', classification: 'noise', reason: 'r'}]`;
-
-    const result = runAnalyst([A, B, C], state(), judge, 30)!;
-
-    expect(result).not.toBeNull();
-    expect(result.refusedChunks).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.repairedChunks).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.malformedUnrepairableChunks).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.wrongChunks).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.candidates.find((c) => c.id === A.id)?.label).toBe('Free a stuck port');
-  });
-
-  it('an unrepairable chunk still refuses fail-closed, classified malformed-unrepairable', () => {
-    const judge = (): string => 'the model wrote an essay instead';
-
-    const result = runAnalyst([A, B, C], state(), judge)!;
-
-    expect(result.refusedChunks).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.repairedChunks).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.malformedUnrepairableChunks).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.wrongChunks).toBe(0);
-  });
-
-  it('syntactically valid JSON that fails the verdict schema is classified "wrong" — the genuine judgment failure', () => {
-    const judge = (): string => JSON.stringify([{ id: A.id, classification: 'automation-candidate', reason: 'r' }]); // untitled survivor
-
-    const result = runAnalyst([A, B, C], state(), judge)!;
-
-    expect(result.refusedChunks).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.repairedChunks).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.malformedUnrepairableChunks).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.wrongChunks).toBe(1);
-  });
-
-  it('a thrown judge is classified malformed-unrepairable, never wrong', () => {
-    const judge = (): string => {
-      throw new Error('model unavailable');
-    };
-
-    const result = runAnalyst([A, B, C], state(), judge)!;
-
-    expect(result.malformedUnrepairableChunks).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.wrongChunks).toBe(0);
-  });
-
-  it('a repair-hint line carries into the NEXT chunk only, after the previous chunk needed repair', () => {
-    const templates = Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => `Bash(cmd-${i} {arg})`);
-    const events: ActionEvent[] = [];
-    for (let day = 0; day < 4; day += 1) {
-      templates.forEach((template, index) => {
-        const epoch = START + day * DAY + index * 1000;
-        events.push({ kind: 'tool', ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(), epoch, project: '/repo/a', session: `s-${day}-${index}`, tool: 'Bash', template });
-      });
-    }
-    const bigState: StoredState = { version: 2, watermark: {}, turns: [], events };
-    const candidates = templates.map((template, i) => candidate(`tool:${i}`, template));
-
-    const prompts: string[] = [];
-    let call = 0;
-    const judge = (prompt: string): string => {
-      prompts.push(prompt);
-      call += 1;
-      const ids = [...prompt.matchAll(/"id":\s*"(tool:\d+)"/g)].map((m) => m[1]!);
-      if (call === 1) {
-        const [first, ...rest] = ids;
-        return `[{id: '${first}', classification: 'automation-candidate', title: 'Do the thing', mergeInto: null, reason: 'ok'}, ${rest
-          .map((id) => JSON.stringify({ id, classification: 'noise', reason: 'r' }))
-          .join(', ')}]`;
-      }
-      return JSON.stringify(ids.map((id) => ({ id, classification: 'noise', reason: 'r' })));
-    };
-
-    const result = runAnalyst(candidates, bigState, judge, 6, undefined, 3)!;
-
-    expect(result.totalChunks).toBe(2);
-    expect(result.repairedChunks).toBe(1);
-    expect(prompts[0]).not.toContain('required repair');
-    expect(prompts[1]).toContain('your previous output required repair');
-    expect(prompts[1]).toContain('single-quoted-strings');
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/briefing-banners.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/briefing-banners.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 9b7a59e..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/briefing-banners.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,138 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Honesty banners (report.loop.html c6) — a refused or absent tier must be
- * DECLARED in the briefing, never silently degraded into an empty section or
- * a quietly-lower count. The pipeline names three real tiers this way
- * (triage/"untriaged", the facet reader/"unread", the analyst/"un-analyzed"),
- * but the property under test here is generic: `emitProposalPages` must
- * carry an arbitrary number of independent tier banners through to the
- * rendered page, in order, none dropped or overwritten by another, and
- * regardless of whether the run produced any proposals at all.
- */
-
-import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { emitProposalPages } from '../src/report.js';
-import type { Proposal } from '../src/propose.js';
-import type { ProposalForm } from '../src/typology.js';
-import { extractPayload } from '../../../loop/src/core/extract.js';
-
-const outDirs: string[] = [];
-afterEach(() => {
-  for (const dir of outDirs.splice(0)) rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
-});
-
-function outDir(): string {
-  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loop-hole-briefing-banners-'));
-  outDirs.push(dir);
-  return dir;
-}
-
-function makeProposal(intent: string, form: ProposalForm): Proposal {
-  return {
-    candidateId: intent,
-    clusterKey: `tool:${intent}`,
-    score: 0.5,
-    intent,
-    form,
-    mechanizable: true,
-    mechanizabilityRationale: 'stable shape',
-    fitnessFunction: {
-      claim: `${intent} stays correct unattended`,
-      method: 'synthetic-test',
-      assertion: 'PASS when the scheduled run matches the manual result; FAIL otherwise.',
-      draft: true,
-    },
-    payoff: 'reclaims recurring hand-work',
-    citations: [intent],
-    dossier: {
-      candidateId: intent,
-      clusterKey: `tool:${intent}`,
-      kind: 'tool',
-      label: intent,
-      eventCount: 4,
-      sessionCount: 4,
-      projectCount: 1,
-      projects: ['/repo/only'],
-      cadence: [],
-      exemplars: [intent],
-      toil: { frequency: 4, medianDurationMs: 1000, interventions: 0 },
-      firstSeen: '2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z',
-      lastSeen: '2026-06-04T09:00:00.000Z',
-      fingerprint: { kind: 'tool', memberHashes: [intent] },
-    },
-    tokensUsed: 40,
-  };
-}
-
-function emitBriefing(proposals: Proposal[], tierBanners?: readonly string[]): string {
-  const emitted = emitProposalPages(proposals, {
-    outDir: outDir(),
-    briefingId: 'briefing-banners',
-    ...(tierBanners !== undefined ? { tierBanners } : {}),
-  });
-  const html = readFileSync(emitted.briefing.path, 'utf8');
-  return extractPayload(html)!.payload;
-}
-
-describe('briefing honesty banners', () => {
-  it('declares nothing when every tier ran — no stray banner markup', () => {
-    const payload = emitBriefing([makeProposal('normal run', 'standing-loop')]);
-    // The only bold lines in a clean run are inside cards/goal text, never a
-    // standalone "**...**" banner line of its own.
-    const standaloneBoldLines = payload.split('\n').filter((line) => /^\*\*.*\*\*$/.test(line.trim()));
-    expect(standaloneBoldLines).toEqual([]);
-  });
-
-  it('declares a single refused tier verbatim', () => {
-    const banner = 'UNTRIAGED run — the judgment tier was unavailable, so conversational fragments may appear below.';
-    const payload = emitBriefing([makeProposal('one row', 'standing-loop')], [banner]);
-    expect(payload).toContain(banner);
-  });
-
-  it('declares every refused tier when more than one degrades in the same run — none overwrites another', () => {
-    const untriaged = 'UNTRIAGED run — the judgment tier was unavailable, so conversational fragments may appear below.';
-    const unread = 'UNREAD sessions — the comprehension tier was unavailable this pass, so no session was freshly read.';
-    const unanalyzed = 'UN-ANALYZED agent lane — the analyst tier was unavailable, so tool-ritual rows carry no classification this run.';
-
-    const payload = emitBriefing([makeProposal('one row', 'standing-loop')], [untriaged, unread, unanalyzed]);
-
-    expect(payload).toContain(untriaged);
-    expect(payload).toContain(unread);
-    expect(payload).toContain(unanalyzed);
-
-    // Order given is order rendered — a reader scans top to bottom and each
-    // tier keeps its own line rather than one clobbering the others.
-    const positions = [untriaged, unread, unanalyzed].map((banner) => payload.indexOf(banner));
-    expect(positions[0]!).toBeLessThan(positions[1]!);
-    expect(positions[1]!).toBeLessThan(positions[2]!);
-  });
-
-  it('positions every banner ahead of the form sections — declared where the tier\'s own output would have landed, not buried after it', () => {
-    const banners = [
-      'UNTRIAGED run — the judgment tier was unavailable, so conversational fragments may appear below.',
-      'UNREAD sessions — the comprehension tier was unavailable this pass, so no session was freshly read.',
-    ];
-    // Presence-driven section elision (report.loop.html's "briefing signal
-    // density" check) means only a form with an actual proposal renders its
-    // heading — this proposal is 'standing-instruction' specifically so
-    // '## Standing instructions' (the section this test anchors on) is the
-    // one guaranteed to render.
-    const payload = emitBriefing([makeProposal('one row', 'standing-instruction')], banners);
-
-    const firstSectionHeading = payload.indexOf('## Standing instructions');
-    expect(firstSectionHeading).toBeGreaterThan(-1);
-    for (const banner of banners) {
-      const index = payload.indexOf(banner);
-      expect(index).toBeGreaterThan(-1);
-      expect(index).toBeLessThan(firstSectionHeading);
-    }
-  });
-
-  it('still declares a degraded tier even when the run produced zero proposals — absence of output is not mistaken for absence of a problem', () => {
-    const banner = 'UNREAD sessions — the comprehension tier was unavailable this pass, so no session was freshly read.';
-    const payload = emitBriefing([], [banner]);
-    expect(payload).toContain(banner);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/briefing-cards.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/briefing-cards.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index a2d7ab6..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/briefing-cards.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,324 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Card anatomy (report.loop.html c5) — every proposal card the briefing
- * emits must carry a title, evidence, a price line, a covered/novel marker,
- * and the dismiss-teaches-the-ranker affordance, and every corpus-derived
- * string on it must pass through the single-line guard (report.ts's
- * `briefLine`) before it reaches the page. Each assertion here recomputes
- * its expectation from the input `Proposal`/`ToilPricing` directly — never
- * by calling report.ts's own private formatters — the same discipline
- * briefing-v3.spec.ts already holds the briefing's other axes to.
- */
-
-import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { emitProposalPages } from '../src/report.js';
-import type { Proposal } from '../src/propose.js';
-import type { ProposalForm } from '../src/typology.js';
-import type { ToilPricing } from '../src/toil.js';
-import type { NoveltyVerdict } from '../src/novelty.js';
-import { extractPayload } from '../../../loop/src/core/extract.js';
-
-const outDirs: string[] = [];
-afterEach(() => {
-  for (const dir of outDirs.splice(0)) rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
-});
-
-function outDir(): string {
-  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loop-hole-briefing-cards-'));
-  outDirs.push(dir);
-  return dir;
-}
-
-/** All five section headings, exactly as report.ts renders them (mirrors
- * briefing-v3.spec.ts's own local copy — report.ts does not export them). */
-const SECTION_HEADING: Readonly<Record<ProposalForm, string>> = {
-  'standing-instruction': '## Standing instructions',
-  'push-loop': '## Push loops',
-  'distilled-skill': '## Skills',
-  'standing-loop': '## Standing loops',
-  'missing-tool': '## Missing tools',
-};
-
-interface ProposalSpec {
-  intent: string;
-  form: ProposalForm;
-  clusterKey: string;
-  citations: string[];
-  toil: ToilPricing;
-  novelty?: NoveltyVerdict;
-  noveltyMatch?: string;
-}
-
-function makeProposal(spec: ProposalSpec): Proposal {
-  const { intent, form, clusterKey, citations, toil, novelty, noveltyMatch } = spec;
-  return {
-    candidateId: clusterKey,
-    clusterKey,
-    score: 0.5,
-    intent,
-    form,
-    mechanizable: true,
-    mechanizabilityRationale: 'stable shape',
-    fitnessFunction: {
-      claim: `${intent} stays correct unattended`,
-      method: 'synthetic-test',
-      assertion: 'PASS when the scheduled run matches the manual result; FAIL otherwise.',
-      draft: true,
-    },
-    payoff: 'reclaims recurring hand-work',
-    citations,
-    dossier: {
-      candidateId: clusterKey,
-      clusterKey,
-      kind: 'tool',
-      label: intent,
-      eventCount: toil.frequency,
-      sessionCount: Math.max(toil.frequency, 1),
-      projectCount: 1,
-      projects: ['/repo/only'],
-      cadence: [],
-      exemplars: citations,
-      toil,
-      firstSeen: '2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z',
-      lastSeen: '2026-06-07T09:00:00.000Z',
-      fingerprint: { kind: 'tool', memberHashes: citations },
-    },
-    tokensUsed: 40,
-    ...(novelty !== undefined ? { novelty } : {}),
-    ...(noveltyMatch !== undefined ? { noveltyMatch } : {}),
-  };
-}
-
-function emitBriefing(proposals: Proposal[]): string {
-  const emitted = emitProposalPages(proposals, { outDir: outDir(), briefingId: 'briefing-cards' });
-  const html = readFileSync(emitted.briefing.path, 'utf8');
-  return extractPayload(html)!.payload;
-}
-
-/** The slice of the payload belonging to one form-section — from its heading
- * up to (not including) the next `## ` heading, or the end of the payload. */
-function sectionBody(payload: string, form: ProposalForm): string {
-  const heading = SECTION_HEADING[form];
-  const start = payload.indexOf(heading);
-  expect(start, `missing heading: ${heading}`).toBeGreaterThan(-1);
-  const next = payload.indexOf('\n## ', start + heading.length);
-  return payload.slice(start, next === -1 ? undefined : next);
-}
-
-/** The exact single-line-guard algorithm report.ts's `briefLine` applies —
- * reimplemented here, independently, so a regression in the production
- * function (or a call site that forgets to apply it) is actually caught. */
-function guarded(raw: string, max = 120): string {
-  const flat = raw.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
-  return flat.length > max ? `${flat.slice(0, max - 1)}…` : flat;
-}
-
-describe('card anatomy — every card carries all five elements', () => {
-  it('renders a title, evidence, a price line, a novelty marker, and a dismiss affordance', () => {
-    const proposal = makeProposal({
-      intent: 'Check whether CI is passing',
-      form: 'push-loop',
-      clusterKey: 'tool:ci-status-poll',
-      citations: ['Bash(gh pr checks)'],
-      toil: { frequency: 7, medianDurationMs: 300_000, interventions: 1 },
-    });
-
-    const payload = emitBriefing([proposal]);
-    const body = sectionBody(payload, 'push-loop');
-
-    // Title.
-    expect(body).toContain('Check whether CI is passing');
-    // Evidence.
-    expect(body).toContain('"Bash(gh pr checks)"');
-    expect(body).toContain('[opportunity](');
-    // Price line, recomputed from the dossier's own toil.
-    expect(body).toContain('7×');
-    expect(body).toContain(`${(300_000 / 60_000).toFixed(1)}min`);
-    expect(body).toContain('1 intervention(s)');
-    // Novelty marker (unjudged → defaults novel).
-    expect(body.toLowerCase()).toContain('novelty: novel');
-    // Dismiss affordance names the exact suppression key.
-    expect(body.toLowerCase()).toContain('dismiss');
-    expect(body).toContain('`tool:ci-status-poll`');
-  });
-});
-
-describe('card anatomy — evidence quotes the proposal\'s own citations', () => {
-  it('quotes every citation verbatim (through the guard) and links to its own opportunity page', () => {
-    const proposal = makeProposal({
-      intent: 'Restart the dev server after a build',
-      form: 'standing-loop',
-      clusterKey: 'tool:dev-restart',
-      citations: ['Bash(pkill -f vite)', 'Bash(npm run dev)'],
-      toil: { frequency: 3, medianDurationMs: 60_000, interventions: 0 },
-    });
-
-    const payload = emitBriefing([proposal]);
-    const body = sectionBody(payload, 'standing-loop');
-
-    expect(body).toContain('"Bash(pkill -f vite)"');
-    expect(body).toContain('"Bash(npm run dev)"');
-    const opportunityHref = payload.match(/\[opportunity\]\(([^)]+)\)/)?.[1];
-    expect(opportunityHref).toBeDefined();
-    expect(body).toContain(`[opportunity](${opportunityHref})`);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('card anatomy — price line matches the dossier\'s toil numbers exactly', () => {
-  it('reproduces frequency, median minutes, and interventions per-card, never a neighbor\'s numbers', () => {
-    const a = makeProposal({
-      intent: 'candidate alpha',
-      form: 'standing-instruction',
-      clusterKey: 'tool:alpha',
-      citations: ['Bash(alpha)'],
-      toil: { frequency: 17, medianDurationMs: 137_450, interventions: 4 },
-    });
-    const b = makeProposal({
-      intent: 'candidate beta',
-      form: 'missing-tool',
-      clusterKey: 'tool:beta',
-      citations: ['Bash(beta)'],
-      toil: { frequency: 2, medianDurationMs: 9_000, interventions: 0 },
-    });
-
-    const payload = emitBriefing([a, b]);
-    const bodyA = sectionBody(payload, 'standing-instruction');
-    const bodyB = sectionBody(payload, 'missing-tool');
-
-    expect(bodyA).toContain('17×');
-    expect(bodyA).toContain(`${(137_450 / 60_000).toFixed(1)}min`);
-    expect(bodyA).toContain('4 intervention(s)');
-    // Alpha's numbers must not leak into beta's card.
-    expect(bodyB).not.toContain('17×');
-    expect(bodyB).not.toContain('4 intervention(s)');
-
-    expect(bodyB).toContain('2×');
-    expect(bodyB).toContain(`${(9_000 / 60_000).toFixed(1)}min`);
-    expect(bodyB).toContain('0 intervention(s)');
-  });
-});
-
-describe('card anatomy — a source with no duration evidence renders an honest unpriced marker, never zeros', () => {
-  it('a proposal whose dossier carries priced: false never prints "0×" or a fabricated "0.0min"', () => {
-    const proposal = makeProposal({
-      intent: 'Run the nightly sweep',
-      form: 'standing-loop',
-      clusterKey: 'tool:nightly-sweep',
-      citations: ['Bash(cron-triggered-sweep)'],
-      toil: { frequency: 0, medianDurationMs: 0, interventions: 0, priced: false },
-    });
-
-    const payload = emitBriefing([proposal]);
-    const body = sectionBody(payload, 'standing-loop');
-
-    expect(body.toLowerCase()).toContain('unpriced');
-    // Never the old fabricated-measurement shape — a zero pretending to be a count.
-    expect(body).not.toContain('0× · median 0.0min · 0 intervention(s)');
-    expect(body).not.toMatch(/price:.*\d/);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('card anatomy — covered/novel marker reflects the proposal\'s own verdict', () => {
-  it('an unjudged proposal defaults to novel; an explicit novel/covered verdict renders as given', () => {
-    const unjudged = makeProposal({
-      intent: 'unjudged candidate',
-      form: 'standing-instruction',
-      clusterKey: 'tool:unjudged',
-      citations: ['Bash(unjudged)'],
-      toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1_000, interventions: 0 },
-    });
-    const explicitNovel = makeProposal({
-      intent: 'explicit novel candidate',
-      form: 'push-loop',
-      clusterKey: 'tool:explicit-novel',
-      citations: ['Bash(novel)'],
-      toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1_000, interventions: 0 },
-      novelty: 'novel',
-    });
-    const covered = makeProposal({
-      intent: 'covered candidate',
-      form: 'distilled-skill',
-      clusterKey: 'tool:covered',
-      citations: ['Bash(covered)'],
-      toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1_000, interventions: 0 },
-      novelty: 'covered',
-      noveltyMatch: 'already a CLAUDE.md rule about this',
-    });
-
-    const payload = emitBriefing([unjudged, explicitNovel, covered]);
-
-    expect(sectionBody(payload, 'standing-instruction').toLowerCase()).toContain('novelty: novel');
-    expect(sectionBody(payload, 'push-loop').toLowerCase()).toContain('novelty: novel');
-    const coveredBody = sectionBody(payload, 'distilled-skill').toLowerCase();
-    expect(coveredBody).toContain('novelty: covered');
-    expect(coveredBody).toContain('already a claude.md rule about this');
-  });
-});
-
-describe('card anatomy — dismiss affordance is per-proposal, not shared', () => {
-  it('names the exact clusterKey a dismissal would suppress, and only that one', () => {
-    const first = makeProposal({
-      intent: 'first candidate',
-      form: 'standing-loop',
-      clusterKey: 'tool:first-cluster',
-      citations: ['Bash(first)'],
-      toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1_000, interventions: 0 },
-    });
-    const second = makeProposal({
-      intent: 'second candidate',
-      form: 'missing-tool',
-      clusterKey: 'tool:second-cluster',
-      citations: ['Bash(second)'],
-      toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1_000, interventions: 0 },
-    });
-
-    const payload = emitBriefing([first, second]);
-    const bodyFirst = sectionBody(payload, 'standing-loop');
-    const bodySecond = sectionBody(payload, 'missing-tool');
-
-    expect(bodyFirst).toContain('`tool:first-cluster`');
-    expect(bodyFirst).not.toContain('`tool:second-cluster`');
-    expect(bodySecond).toContain('`tool:second-cluster`');
-    expect(bodySecond).not.toContain('`tool:first-cluster`');
-  });
-});
-
-describe('card anatomy — every corpus string renders through the single-line guard', () => {
-  it('flattens embedded newlines/whitespace and truncates an overlong citation, never leaking the raw text', () => {
-    const raw = `Bash(curl\n\n\t  --retry   3   ${'x'.repeat(150)}   --output final.json)`;
-    const proposal = makeProposal({
-      intent: 'guarded candidate',
-      form: 'standing-loop',
-      clusterKey: 'tool:guarded',
-      citations: [raw],
-      toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1_000, interventions: 0 },
-    });
-
-    const payload = emitBriefing([proposal]);
-    const body = sectionBody(payload, 'standing-loop');
-
-    // The guard actually ran: the raw, newline-riddled string never appears verbatim.
-    expect(payload.includes(raw)).toBe(false);
-    // What it produced matches the guard's own known algorithm, exactly.
-    expect(body).toContain(`"${guarded(raw)}"`);
-    expect(guarded(raw).endsWith('…')).toBe(true);
-  });
-
-  it('a short, already-flat citation passes through unchanged and unquoted-raw-newline-free', () => {
-    const raw = 'Bash(git status)';
-    const proposal = makeProposal({
-      intent: 'flat candidate',
-      form: 'push-loop',
-      clusterKey: 'tool:flat',
-      citations: [raw],
-      toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1_000, interventions: 0 },
-    });
-
-    const payload = emitBriefing([proposal]);
-    const body = sectionBody(payload, 'push-loop');
-
-    expect(body).toContain(`"${raw}"`);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/briefing-noise.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/briefing-noise.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index abdee98..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/briefing-noise.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,206 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Briefing signal density (report.loop.html c12) — three defects observed on
- * a real slice run (owner verdict, 2026-07-18): the dismiss mechanism was
- * re-explained on all 25 cards instead of once; empty form sections rendered
- * "No X cleared the floor this run." scaffolding instead of eliding; and the
- * synthesis lead's own prose headings could collide, character-for-character,
- * with a mechanical receipts section heading ("## Standing instructions"
- * appeared twice). Each describe block below proves one of the three, using
- * only report.ts's public `emitProposalPages` entry point — the same
- * discipline briefing-v3.spec.ts and briefing-cards.spec.ts already hold.
- */
-
-import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { emitProposalPages } from '../src/report.js';
-import type { Proposal } from '../src/propose.js';
-import type { ProposalForm } from '../src/typology.js';
-import type { ToilPricing } from '../src/toil.js';
-import { extractPayload } from '../../../loop/src/core/extract.js';
-
-const outDirs: string[] = [];
-afterEach(() => {
-  for (const dir of outDirs.splice(0)) rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
-});
-
-function outDir(): string {
-  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loop-hole-briefing-noise-'));
-  outDirs.push(dir);
-  return dir;
-}
-
-const DEFAULT_TOIL: ToilPricing = { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1000, interventions: 0 };
-
-function makeProposal(intent: string, form: ProposalForm, clusterKey: string, toil: ToilPricing = DEFAULT_TOIL): Proposal {
-  return {
-    candidateId: clusterKey,
-    clusterKey,
-    score: 0.5,
-    intent,
-    form,
-    mechanizable: true,
-    mechanizabilityRationale: 'stable shape',
-    fitnessFunction: {
-      claim: `${intent} stays correct unattended`,
-      method: 'synthetic-test',
-      assertion: 'PASS when the scheduled run matches the manual result; FAIL otherwise.',
-      draft: true,
-    },
-    payoff: 'reclaims recurring hand-work',
-    citations: [intent],
-    dossier: {
-      candidateId: clusterKey,
-      clusterKey,
-      kind: 'tool',
-      label: intent,
-      eventCount: toil.frequency,
-      sessionCount: Math.max(toil.frequency, 1),
-      projectCount: 1,
-      projects: ['/repo/only'],
-      cadence: [],
-      exemplars: [intent],
-      toil,
-      firstSeen: '2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z',
-      lastSeen: '2026-06-04T09:00:00.000Z',
-      fingerprint: { kind: 'tool', memberHashes: [intent] },
-    },
-    tokensUsed: 40,
-  };
-}
-
-function emitBriefing(proposals: Proposal[], synthesisLead?: string): string {
-  const emitted = emitProposalPages(proposals, {
-    outDir: outDir(),
-    briefingId: 'briefing-noise',
-    ...(synthesisLead !== undefined ? { synthesisLead } : {}),
-  });
-  const html = readFileSync(emitted.briefing.path, 'utf8');
-  return extractPayload(html)!.payload;
-}
-
-/** Every markdown heading line (any level), in document order. */
-function headingLines(payload: string): string[] {
-  return payload.split('\n').filter((line) => /^#{1,6}\s+\S/.test(line.trim())).map((line) => line.trim());
-}
-
-describe('briefing signal density — the dismiss mechanism explains itself once', () => {
-  it('explains the mechanism exactly once, and every card carries only its short suppress-key line', () => {
-    const proposals = [
-      makeProposal('first ritual', 'standing-loop', 'tool:first'),
-      makeProposal('second ritual', 'standing-loop', 'tool:second'),
-      makeProposal('third ritual', 'standing-loop', 'tool:third'),
-    ];
-    const payload = emitBriefing(proposals);
-
-    // The explainer names the mechanism ("trains the ranker") — stated once
-    // for the whole briefing, not once per card.
-    const explainerMentions = payload.match(/trains the ranker/gi) ?? [];
-    expect(explainerMentions.length).toBe(1);
-
-    for (const proposal of proposals) {
-      const cardLine = payload.split('\n').find((line) => line.includes(`dismiss: \`${proposal.clusterKey}\``));
-      expect(cardLine, `missing dismiss line for ${proposal.clusterKey}`).toBeDefined();
-      // Single short line, carrying only the suppress key — no re-explanation.
-      expect(cardLine!).not.toMatch(/trains the ranker/i);
-      expect(cardLine!).not.toMatch(/resurfacing/i);
-      expect(cardLine!.trim().length).toBeLessThan(60);
-    }
-
-    // The explainer reads before any card's own row, at the top of the
-    // receipts area — not interleaved between cards.
-    const explainerIndex = payload.search(/trains the ranker/i);
-    const firstCardIndex = payload.indexOf('1. ');
-    expect(explainerIndex).toBeGreaterThan(-1);
-    expect(firstCardIndex).toBeGreaterThan(-1);
-    expect(explainerIndex).toBeLessThan(firstCardIndex);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('briefing signal density — empty form sections elide instead of rendering scaffolding', () => {
-  it('a form with zero proposals produces no section at all — no header, no "cleared the floor" text', () => {
-    const proposals = [
-      makeProposal('only ritual', 'standing-loop', 'tool:only-a'),
-      makeProposal('another ritual', 'standing-loop', 'tool:only-b'),
-    ];
-    const payload = emitBriefing(proposals);
-
-    expect(payload).not.toContain('## Standing instructions');
-    expect(payload).not.toContain('## Push loops');
-    expect(payload).not.toContain('## Skills');
-    expect(payload).not.toContain('## Missing tools');
-    expect(payload.toLowerCase()).not.toContain('cleared the floor');
-
-    // The one form that actually has proposals still renders normally.
-    expect(payload).toContain('## Standing loops');
-    expect(payload).toContain('only ritual');
-    expect(payload).toContain('another ritual');
-
-    // The at-a-glance header is not part of the elision — it still states
-    // the honest zero for every empty form, independently recomputable.
-    expect(payload).toContain('0 standing instructions');
-    expect(payload).toContain('0 push loops');
-    expect(payload).toContain('0 skills');
-    expect(payload).toContain('0 missing tools');
-    expect(payload).toContain('2 standing loops');
-  });
-
-  it('a run with zero proposals at all renders no form section whatsoever', () => {
-    const payload = emitBriefing([]);
-
-    expect(payload).not.toContain('## Standing instructions');
-    expect(payload).not.toContain('## Push loops');
-    expect(payload).not.toContain('## Skills');
-    expect(payload).not.toContain('## Standing loops');
-    expect(payload).not.toContain('## Missing tools');
-    expect(payload.toLowerCase()).not.toContain('cleared the floor');
-  });
-});
-
-describe('briefing signal density — no two rendered sections share identical header text', () => {
-  it('every heading line in the whole briefing is unique, with no synthesis lead present', () => {
-    const proposals = [
-      makeProposal('a standing instruction', 'standing-instruction', 'tool:si'),
-      makeProposal('a push loop', 'push-loop', 'tool:pl'),
-      makeProposal('a skill', 'distilled-skill', 'tool:ds'),
-      makeProposal('a standing loop', 'standing-loop', 'tool:sl'),
-      makeProposal('a missing tool', 'missing-tool', 'tool:mt'),
-    ];
-    const payload = emitBriefing(proposals);
-
-    const headings = headingLines(payload);
-    expect(new Set(headings).size).toBe(headings.length);
-  });
-
-  it('a synthesis lead heading that collides with a mechanical section name is disambiguated, not duplicated', () => {
-    const proposals = [makeProposal('a standing instruction', 'standing-instruction', 'tool:si')];
-    const lead = '## Standing instructions\nYou keep re-asking for the same discipline check [p:1].';
-    const payload = emitBriefing(proposals, lead);
-
-    // The collision never renders twice, character-for-character, as a
-    // standalone heading LINE — a naive substring count is not enough here,
-    // since a disambiguated deeper heading ("### Standing instructions …")
-    // still contains "## Standing instructions" as a raw substring.
-    const headings = headingLines(payload);
-    const exactMatches = headings.filter((line) => line === '## Standing instructions');
-    expect(exactMatches.length).toBe(1);
-
-    // Every heading in the finished page is still unique.
-    expect(new Set(headings).size).toBe(headings.length);
-
-    // The mechanical section's own content still reached the page somewhere,
-    // just under a disambiguated heading — it was not silently dropped.
-    expect(payload).toContain('a standing instruction');
-  });
-
-  it('a synthesis lead with no colliding heading leaves the mechanical section heading exactly as-is', () => {
-    const proposals = [makeProposal('a standing loop', 'standing-loop', 'tool:sl')];
-    const lead = '## What recurs\nYou did this once [p:1].';
-    const payload = emitBriefing(proposals, lead);
-
-    expect(payload).toContain('## Standing loops');
-    const headings = headingLines(payload);
-    expect(new Set(headings).size).toBe(headings.length);
-  });
-});

-/**
- * Briefing v3 — the analyst tier's own artifact-kind axis (typology.ts)
- * replaces raw detector score as the briefing's primary organizing axis
- * (report.ts): five form-sections in fixed adoption-cost order, a
- * deterministic at-a-glance header, and reach-tier-then-toil ordering within
- * each section. Every assertion here recomputes its expectation from the
- * input proposals directly — never from report.ts's own internal counting
- * or sorting — so a regression in the production code cannot hide behind a
- * test that merely re-asserts what the code already decided.
- */
-
-import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { emitProposalPages, FORM_SECTION_ORDER, formSectionLabel } from '../src/report.js';
-import type { Proposal } from '../src/propose.js';
-import type { ProposalForm } from '../src/typology.js';
-import type { ToilPricing } from '../src/toil.js';
-import { extractPayload } from '../../../loop/src/core/extract.js';
-
-const outDirs: string[] = [];
-afterEach(() => {
-  for (const dir of outDirs.splice(0)) rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
-});
-
-function outDir(): string {
-  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loop-hole-briefing-v3-'));
-  outDirs.push(dir);
-  return dir;
-}
-
-interface ProposalSpec {
-  intent: string;
-  form: ProposalForm;
-  projectCount: number;
-  toil: ToilPricing;
-  score?: number;
-}
-
-function makeProposal(spec: ProposalSpec): Proposal {
-  const { intent, form, projectCount, toil } = spec;
-  const projects = Array.from({ length: Math.max(projectCount, 1) }, (_, i) => `/repo/${intent}-${i}`);
-  return {
-    candidateId: intent,
-    clusterKey: `tool:${intent}`,
-    score: spec.score ?? 0.5,
-    intent,
-    form,
-    mechanizable: true,
-    mechanizabilityRationale: 'stable shape',
-    fitnessFunction: {
-      claim: `${intent} stays correct unattended`,
-      method: 'synthetic-test',
-      assertion: 'PASS when the scheduled run matches the manual result; FAIL otherwise.',
-      draft: true,
-    },
-    payoff: 'reclaims recurring hand-work',
-    citations: [intent],
-    dossier: {
-      candidateId: intent,
-      clusterKey: `tool:${intent}`,
-      kind: 'tool',
-      label: intent,
-      eventCount: toil.frequency,
-      sessionCount: Math.max(toil.frequency, 1),
-      projectCount,
-      projects,
-      cadence: [],
-      exemplars: [intent],
-      toil,
-      firstSeen: '2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z',
-      lastSeen: '2026-06-07T09:00:00.000Z',
-      fingerprint: { kind: 'tool', memberHashes: [intent] },
-    },
-    tokensUsed: 40,
-  };
-}
-
-/** All five section headings, in the exact adoption-cost order report.ts renders them. */
-const SECTION_HEADINGS: readonly [ProposalForm, string][] = [
-  ['standing-instruction', '## Standing instructions'],
-  ['push-loop', '## Push loops'],
-  ['distilled-skill', '## Skills'],
-  ['standing-loop', '## Standing loops'],
-  ['missing-tool', '## Missing tools'],
-];
-
-function emitBriefing(proposals: Proposal[]): string {
-  const emitted = emitProposalPages(proposals, { outDir: outDir(), briefingId: 'briefing-v3' });
-  const html = readFileSync(emitted.briefing.path, 'utf8');
-  return extractPayload(html)!.payload;
-}
-
-describe('briefing v3 — five form-sections in adoption-cost order', () => {
-  it('renders all five section headings in fixed order, cheapest-to-adopt first, regardless of score', () => {
-    // Scores are assigned in the OPPOSITE order to adoption cost — if the
-    // briefing were still sorting by raw score, the headings would come out
-    // reversed. They must not.
-    const proposals = [
-      makeProposal({ intent: 'missing tool candidate', form: 'missing-tool', projectCount: 1, toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1, interventions: 0 }, score: 0.95 }),
-      makeProposal({ intent: 'standing loop candidate', form: 'standing-loop', projectCount: 1, toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1, interventions: 0 }, score: 0.8 }),
-      makeProposal({ intent: 'skill candidate', form: 'distilled-skill', projectCount: 1, toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1, interventions: 0 }, score: 0.6 }),
-      makeProposal({ intent: 'push loop candidate', form: 'push-loop', projectCount: 1, toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1, interventions: 0 }, score: 0.4 }),
-      makeProposal({ intent: 'standing instruction candidate', form: 'standing-instruction', projectCount: 1, toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1, interventions: 0 }, score: 0.1 }),
-    ];
-
-    const payload = emitBriefing(proposals);
-
-    const positions = SECTION_HEADINGS.map(([, heading]) => {
-      const index = payload.indexOf(heading);
-      expect(index, `missing heading: ${heading}`).toBeGreaterThan(-1);
-      return index;
-    });
-
-    for (let i = 1; i < positions.length; i += 1) {
-      expect(positions[i]!, `${SECTION_HEADINGS[i]![1]} must follow ${SECTION_HEADINGS[i - 1]![1]}`).toBeGreaterThan(positions[i - 1]!);
-    }
-  });
-
-  it('an empty form elides entirely — no header, no scaffolding text — while the one form with a proposal renders normally', () => {
-    const proposals = [
-      makeProposal({ intent: 'lone skill', form: 'distilled-skill', projectCount: 1, toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1, interventions: 0 } }),
-    ];
-    const payload = emitBriefing(proposals);
-
-    for (const [form, heading] of SECTION_HEADINGS) {
-      if (form === 'distilled-skill') {
-        expect(payload).toContain(heading);
-      } else {
-        // Presence-driven elision (report.loop.html's "briefing signal
-        // density" check): a form with nothing this run renders no section
-        // at all, not a heading padded with "cleared the floor" scaffolding.
-        expect(payload).not.toContain(heading);
-      }
-    }
-    expect(payload.toLowerCase()).not.toContain('cleared the floor');
-  });
-});
-
-describe('briefing v3 — deterministic at-a-glance header', () => {
-  it('the header count for every form matches an independent recount of the input proposals', () => {
-    const specs: ProposalSpec[] = [
-      { intent: 'si-1', form: 'standing-instruction', projectCount: 1, toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1, interventions: 0 } },
-      { intent: 'si-2', form: 'standing-instruction', projectCount: 1, toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1, interventions: 0 } },
-      { intent: 'pl-1', form: 'push-loop', projectCount: 1, toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1, interventions: 0 } },
-      { intent: 'ds-1', form: 'distilled-skill', projectCount: 1, toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1, interventions: 0 } },
-      { intent: 'ds-2', form: 'distilled-skill', projectCount: 1, toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1, interventions: 0 } },
-      { intent: 'ds-3', form: 'distilled-skill', projectCount: 1, toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1, interventions: 0 } },
-      { intent: 'sl-1', form: 'standing-loop', projectCount: 1, toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1, interventions: 0 } },
-      // No missing-tool proposals this run at all — the count must read 0.
-    ];
-    const proposals = specs.map(makeProposal);
-    const payload = emitBriefing(proposals);
-
-    // Recompute counts straight from the input, independent of report.ts's
-    // own internal atAGlanceLine tally.
-    const recount = new Map<ProposalForm, number>();
-    for (const spec of specs) recount.set(spec.form, (recount.get(spec.form) ?? 0) + 1);
-
-    const glanceHeadingIndex = payload.indexOf('## At a glance');
-    expect(glanceHeadingIndex).toBeGreaterThan(-1);
-    const nextHeadingIndex = payload.indexOf('##', glanceHeadingIndex + '## At a glance'.length);
-    const glanceBlock = payload.slice(glanceHeadingIndex, nextHeadingIndex === -1 ? undefined : nextHeadingIndex);
-
-    for (const form of FORM_SECTION_ORDER) {
-      const count = recount.get(form) ?? 0;
-      const expectedFragment = `${count} ${formSectionLabel(form, count)}`;
-      expect(glanceBlock, `at-a-glance must report "${expectedFragment}" for ${form}`).toContain(expectedFragment);
-    }
-  });
-
-  it('a changed input count changes the header — it is not a cached or hardcoded string', () => {
-    const fewer = [makeProposal({ intent: 'one skill', form: 'distilled-skill', projectCount: 1, toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1, interventions: 0 } })];
-    const more = [
-      makeProposal({ intent: 'skill a', form: 'distilled-skill', projectCount: 1, toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1, interventions: 0 } }),
-      makeProposal({ intent: 'skill b', form: 'distilled-skill', projectCount: 1, toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1, interventions: 0 } }),
-    ];
-
-    const fewerPayload = emitBriefing(fewer);
-    const morePayload = emitBriefing(more);
-
-    expect(fewerPayload).toContain(`1 ${formSectionLabel('distilled-skill', 1)}`);
-    expect(morePayload).toContain(`2 ${formSectionLabel('distilled-skill', 2)}`);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('briefing v3 — reach-tier-then-toil ordering within a section', () => {
-  it('orders cross-project ahead of single-project, then by toil cost, then interventions, then alphabetically', () => {
-    const form: ProposalForm = 'push-loop';
-    // Cross-project tier (projectCount >= 3): toil cost = frequency * medianDurationMs.
-    const crossHighToil = makeProposal({ intent: 'bbb-cross-hi-toil', form, projectCount: 5, toil: { frequency: 2, medianDurationMs: 1000, interventions: 0 } }); // cost 2000
-    const crossLowToil = makeProposal({ intent: 'mmm-cross-lo-toil', form, projectCount: 3, toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 50, interventions: 0 } }); // cost 50
-
-    // Single-project tier (projectCount < 3), ordered purely by toil cost first.
-    const singleHighest = makeProposal({ intent: 'zzz-single-hi-toil', form, projectCount: 1, toil: { frequency: 10, medianDurationMs: 1000, interventions: 0 } }); // cost 10000
-    const singleTieMoreInterventions = makeProposal({ intent: 'ccc-tie-toil-high-interventions', form, projectCount: 1, toil: { frequency: 2, medianDurationMs: 500, interventions: 3 } }); // cost 1000
-    const singleTieFewerInterventions = makeProposal({ intent: 'ddd-tie-toil-low-interventions', form, projectCount: 2, toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1000, interventions: 1 } }); // cost 1000
-    const singleMid = makeProposal({ intent: 'aaa-single-lo-toil', form, projectCount: 1, toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 100, interventions: 0 } }); // cost 100
-    const singleAlphaY = makeProposal({ intent: 'yyy-alpha-tie', form, projectCount: 1, toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1, interventions: 0 } }); // cost 1
-    const singleAlphaX = makeProposal({ intent: 'xxx-alpha-tie', form, projectCount: 1, toil: { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1, interventions: 0 } }); // cost 1
-
-    // Shuffle the input so section order can only come from the ranking rule.
-    const proposals = [
-      singleMid,
-      crossLowToil,
-      singleAlphaY,
-      singleHighest,
-      crossHighToil,
-      singleTieFewerInterventions,
-      singleAlphaX,
-      singleTieMoreInterventions,
-    ];
-
-    const payload = emitBriefing(proposals);
-
-    // Expected order derived by hand from the reach/toil/intervention/alpha
-    // rule above — never by calling report.ts's own ranking function.
-    const expectedOrder = [
-      crossHighToil.intent, // cross-project, cost 2000
-      crossLowToil.intent, // cross-project, cost 50
-      singleHighest.intent, // single, cost 10000
-      singleTieMoreInterventions.intent, // single, cost 1000, 3 interventions
-      singleTieFewerInterventions.intent, // single, cost 1000, 1 intervention
-      singleMid.intent, // single, cost 100
-      singleAlphaX.intent, // single, cost 1, alphabetically first
-      singleAlphaY.intent, // single, cost 1, alphabetically second
-    ];
-
-    const positions = expectedOrder.map((intent) => {
-      const index = payload.indexOf(intent);
-      expect(index, `missing row for ${intent}`).toBeGreaterThan(-1);
-      return index;
-    });
-
-    for (let i = 1; i < positions.length; i += 1) {
-      expect(positions[i]!, `${expectedOrder[i]} must follow ${expectedOrder[i - 1]}`).toBeGreaterThan(positions[i - 1]!);
-    }
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/budget.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/budget.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index a1c7738..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/budget.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,87 +0,0 @@
-import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { ClaudeCodeAdapter, defaultClaudeProjectsDir } from '../src/claude-adapter.js';
-import { detectRecurrenceCandidates, estimateEmbeddingSpend } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import { runExtract } from '../src/extract.js';
-import { StateStore } from '../src/store.js';
-import { buildDossier, runPropose, LocalProposer } from '../src/propose.js';
-import { seededState } from './cluster-fixture.js';
-
-const NOW = Date.parse('2026-06-05T09:00:00Z');
-
-describe('embedding budget', () => {
-  it('charges the seeded corpus by unique turn, not repeated user event', () => {
-    const spend = estimateEmbeddingSpend(seededState());
-
-    expect(spend.userEvents).toBe(6);
-    expect(spend.uniqueTurns).toBe(3);
-    expect(spend.savedEmbeddings).toBe(3);
-  });
-
-  it.runIf(existsSync(defaultClaudeProjectsDir()))('keeps full-corpus embedding spend under the measured ceiling', async () => {
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(), store);
-
-    const spend = estimateEmbeddingSpend({ events: store.events, turns: store.turns });
-
-    expect(spend.uniqueTurns).toBeLessThanOrEqual(store.turns.length);
-    expect(spend.uniqueTurns).toBeLessThanOrEqual(spend.userEvents);
-    expect(spend.savedEmbeddings).toBe(spend.userEvents - spend.uniqueTurns);
-  }, 300_000);
-});
-
-describe('LLM propose budget', () => {
-  it('degrades by proposing fewer, not worse — top-K truncation under a tight budget', async () => {
-    const state = seededState();
-    const candidates = await detectRecurrenceCandidates(state, { now: NOW, intentSimilarity: 0.45 });
-    const proposer = new LocalProposer();
-
-    // Per-candidate cost in score order — the schedule the budget truncates.
-    const costs = candidates.map((candidate) => proposer.estimateTokens(buildDossier(candidate, state)));
-    expect(costs.length).toBeGreaterThan(2);
-
-    // Full budget proposes every survivor.
-    const full = await runPropose(candidates, state, { proposer, budgetTokens: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY });
-    expect(full.proposals.length).toBe(candidates.length);
-    expect(full.truncated).toBe(0);
-
-    // A tight budget that admits only the first two proposals by cost.
-    const tightBudget = costs[0]! + costs[1]! + Math.floor(costs[2]! / 2);
-    const tight = await runPropose(candidates, state, { proposer, budgetTokens: tightBudget });
-
-    // Fewer proposals, and the spend never breaches the ceiling.
-    expect(tight.proposals.length).toBeLessThan(full.proposals.length);
-    expect(tight.proposals.length).toBe(2);
-    expect(tight.spentTokens).toBeLessThanOrEqual(tightBudget);
-    expect(tight.truncated).toBe(full.proposals.length - tight.proposals.length);
-
-    // Not worse: the survivors are the exact top-K prefix, each byte-identical
-    // to the full-budget proposal — quality is untouched, only the tail is cut.
-    for (let i = 0; i < tight.proposals.length; i += 1) {
-      expect(tight.proposals[i]).toEqual(full.proposals[i]);
-    }
-  });
-
-  it('a zero budget proposes nothing rather than a degraded something', async () => {
-    const state = seededState();
-    const candidates = await detectRecurrenceCandidates(state, { now: NOW, intentSimilarity: 0.45 });
-    const result = await runPropose(candidates, state, { proposer: new LocalProposer(), budgetTokens: 0 });
-
-    expect(result.proposals).toHaveLength(0);
-    expect(result.spentTokens).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.truncated).toBe(candidates.length);
-  });
-
-  it('caps proposal count independently of remaining budget', async () => {
-    const state = seededState();
-    const candidates = await detectRecurrenceCandidates(state, { now: NOW, intentSimilarity: 0.45 });
-    const result = await runPropose(candidates, state, {
-      proposer: new LocalProposer(),
-      budgetTokens: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY,
-      maxProposals: 1,
-    });
-
-    expect(result.proposals).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(result.truncated).toBe(candidates.length - 1);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/chain-triviality.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/chain-triviality.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
