--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/chain-triviality.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,124 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Chain triviality floor (episodes.loop.html c10) — a shape recurring often
- * enough is necessary but not sufficient. Round-4's receipts verdict caught
- * "Read({path}) -> Write({path})" and "Read({path}) -> Edit({path})" proposed
- * as Skills cards: bare workbench file-io verb pairs, the exact
- * complexity-free noise the owner rejected at this spike's birth. The floor
- * must kill those while leaving a real 2-step workaround (a failing command
- * routed around by hand) and a real 2-step procedure (build then test) alone
- * — step count is identical across all three, so the fix can only be
- * content/diversity, never step count.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { detectChainCandidates } from '../src/chains.js';
-import type { Episode, EpisodeAction } from '../src/episodes.js';
-
-const BASE = Date.parse('2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z');
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-
-function action(template: string, outcome: EpisodeAction['outcome'], day: number, offsetMs: number): EpisodeAction {
-  const epoch = BASE + day * DAY + offsetMs;
-  return { tool: template.split('(')[0] ?? template, template, outcome, ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(), epoch };
-}
-
-function episode(session: string, project: string, day: number, actions: EpisodeAction[]): Episode {
-  const askEpoch = BASE + day * DAY;
-  const last = actions[actions.length - 1];
-  return {
-    session,
-    project,
-    turn: 0,
-    ts: new Date(askEpoch).toISOString(),
-    epoch: askEpoch,
-    actions,
-    durationMs: last === undefined ? 0 : last.epoch - askEpoch,
-    tokens: { input: 0, output: 0 },
-    resolution: 'resolved',
-  };
-}
-
-describe('chain triviality floor', () => {
-  it('repeated bare Read->Write and Read->Edit traces yield zero chain candidates', () => {
-    const readWrite = Array.from({ length: 4 }, (_, i) =>
-      episode(`sess-rw-${i}`, `/Users/me/repo${i}`, i + 1, [
-        action('Read({path})', 'ok', i + 1, 0),
-        action('Write({path})', 'ok', i + 1, 500),
-      ]),
-    );
-    const readEdit = Array.from({ length: 4 }, (_, i) =>
-      episode(`sess-re-${i}`, `/Users/me/repo${i}`, i + 10, [
-        action('Read({path})', 'ok', i + 10, 0),
-        action('Edit({path})', 'ok', i + 10, 500),
-      ]),
-    );
-
-    expect(detectChainCandidates([...readWrite, ...readEdit])).toEqual([]);
-  });
-
-  it('a workaround chain (a failing command routed around by hand) still detects', () => {
-    const episodes = [
-      episode('sess-wa-1', '/Users/me/repoA', 1, [
-        action('Bash(curl {url})', 'error', 1, 0),
-        action('Bash(cat {path})', 'ok', 1, 1_000),
-      ]),
-      episode('sess-wa-2', '/Users/me/repoA', 2, [
-        action('Bash(curl {url})', 'error', 2, 0),
-        action('Bash(cat {path})', 'ok', 2, 1_000),
-      ]),
-      episode('sess-wa-3', '/Users/me/repoA', 3, [
-        action('Bash(curl {url})', 'error', 3, 0),
-        action('Bash(cat {path})', 'ok', 3, 1_000),
-      ]),
-    ];
-
-    const candidates = detectChainCandidates(episodes);
-    expect(candidates).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(candidates[0]?.occurrences).toBe(3);
-    expect(candidates[0]?.shape).toEqual(['Bash(curl {url})::error', 'Bash(cat {path})::ok']);
-  });
-
-  it('a clean build->test procedure still detects', () => {
-    const episodes = [
-      episode('sess-bt-1', '/Users/me/repoA', 1, [
-        action('Bash(npm run build)', 'ok', 1, 0),
-        action('Bash(npm test)', 'ok', 1, 1_000),
-      ]),
-      episode('sess-bt-2', '/Users/me/repoA', 2, [
-        action('Bash(npm run build)', 'ok', 2, 0),
-        action('Bash(npm test)', 'ok', 2, 1_000),
-      ]),
-      episode('sess-bt-3', '/Users/me/repoA', 3, [
-        action('Bash(npm run build)', 'ok', 3, 0),
-        action('Bash(npm test)', 'ok', 3, 1_000),
-      ]),
-    ];
-
-    const candidates = detectChainCandidates(episodes);
-    expect(candidates).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(candidates[0]?.occurrences).toBe(3);
-    expect(candidates[0]?.shape).toEqual(['Bash(npm run build)::ok', 'Bash(npm test)::ok']);
-  });
-
-  it('a mixed file-io + non-file-io chain still detects — one non-file-io step is enough to clear the floor', () => {
-    const episodes = [
-      episode('sess-mx-1', '/Users/me/repoA', 1, [
-        action('Read({path})', 'ok', 1, 0),
-        action('Bash(npm run lint --fix)', 'ok', 1, 500),
-        action('Write({path})', 'ok', 1, 1_000),
-      ]),
-      episode('sess-mx-2', '/Users/me/repoA', 2, [
-        action('Read({path})', 'ok', 2, 0),
-        action('Bash(npm run lint --fix)', 'ok', 2, 500),
-        action('Write({path})', 'ok', 2, 1_000),
-      ]),
-      episode('sess-mx-3', '/Users/me/repoA', 3, [
-        action('Read({path})', 'ok', 3, 0),
-        action('Bash(npm run lint --fix)', 'ok', 3, 500),

-
-    expect(detectChainCandidates(episodes)).toHaveLength(1);

diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/chains.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/chains.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 6e12bf8..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/chains.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,194 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Chain (trace-shape) mining — a repeated ORDERED multi-step shape must
- * collapse into one procedure candidate citing every episode that ran it,
- * never fragment into one candidate per step template (that atom-level
- * reading is exactly the bug docs/loop-hole-episodes-design.md was born to
- * kill). Recall, precision, and a negative control together: recurrence
- * alone is not enough — the trace has to match exactly, in order, outcome
- * included, or two unrelated procedures would collapse into one lie.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { detectChainCandidates } from '../src/chains.js';
-import type { Episode, EpisodeAction } from '../src/episodes.js';
-
-const BASE = Date.parse('2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z');
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-
-function action(template: string, outcome: EpisodeAction['outcome'], day: number, offsetMs: number): EpisodeAction {
-  const epoch = BASE + day * DAY + offsetMs;
-  return { tool: template.split('(')[0] ?? template, template, outcome, ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(), epoch };
-}
-
-function episode(session: string, project: string, day: number, actions: EpisodeAction[]): Episode {
-  const askEpoch = BASE + day * DAY;
-  const last = actions[actions.length - 1];
-  return {
-    session,
-    project,
-    turn: 0,
-    ts: new Date(askEpoch).toISOString(),
-    epoch: askEpoch,
-    actions,
-    durationMs: last === undefined ? 0 : last.epoch - askEpoch,
-    tokens: { input: 0, output: 0 },
-    resolution: 'resolved',
-  };
-}
-
-/** The five-step shape under test: run the check → read the failure → find
- * the cause → fix → push. Every "true" occurrence repeats it symbol-for-
- * symbol; helpers below let precision cases perturb one symbol at a time. */
-function shipFixTrace(day: number, overrides: Partial<{ order: 'forward' | 'swapped'; lastOutcome: EpisodeAction['outcome'] }> = {}): EpisodeAction[] {
-  const grep = action('Bash(grep {pattern} {log})', 'ok', day, 2_000);
-  const read = action('Read({log})', 'ok', day, 1_000);
-  const steps = [
-    action('Bash(npm test)', 'error', day, 0),
-    overrides.order === 'swapped' ? grep : read,
-    overrides.order === 'swapped' ? read : grep,
-    action('Edit({file})', 'ok', day, 3_000),
-    action('Bash(git push)', overrides.lastOutcome ?? 'ok', day, 4_000),
-  ];
-  return steps;
-}
-
-describe('chain recall', () => {
-  it('a five-step episode shape repeated four times yields one procedure candidate citing the shared trace', () => {
-    const recurring: Episode[] = [
-      episode('sess-1', '/Users/me/repoA', 1, shipFixTrace(1)),
-      episode('sess-2', '/Users/me/repoA', 2, shipFixTrace(2)),
-      episode('sess-3', '/Users/me/repoB', 3, shipFixTrace(3)),
-      episode('sess-4', '/Users/me/repoB', 4, shipFixTrace(4)),
-    ];
-
-    // Distractors: every one of the five templates above ALSO shows up, on
-    // its own, in unrelated one-off episodes — enough that a naive
-    // per-template ("atom-level") count would call each of the five
-    // templates its own recurring candidate.
-    const distractors: Episode[] = [
-      episode('sess-5', '/Users/me/repoC', 5, [action('Bash(npm test)', 'ok', 5, 0)]),
-      episode('sess-6', '/Users/me/repoC', 6, [action('Bash(npm test)', 'ok', 6, 0)]),
-      episode('sess-7', '/Users/me/repoC', 7, [action('Bash(git push)', 'ok', 7, 0)]),
-      episode('sess-8', '/Users/me/repoC', 8, [action('Bash(git push)', 'ok', 8, 0)]),
-    ];
-
-    const allEpisodes = [...recurring, ...distractors];
-
-    // Prove the fixture actually poses the risk the check names: under a
-    // naive "count occurrences of one template" reading (the same >=3
-    // threshold this module's own default uses), all five step templates
-    // individually clear it — atom-level counting on this exact corpus
-    // would yield (at least) five candidates, one per template.
-    const templateCounts = new Map<string, number>();
-    for (const ep of allEpisodes) {
-      for (const step of ep.actions) templateCounts.set(step.template, (templateCounts.get(step.template) ?? 0) + 1);
-    }
-    const atomLevelHits = [...templateCounts.values()].filter((count) => count >= 3).length;
-    expect(atomLevelHits).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(5);
-
-    const candidates = detectChainCandidates(allEpisodes);
-
-    expect(candidates).toHaveLength(1);
-    const candidate = candidates[0];
-    if (candidate === undefined) throw new Error('expected one chain candidate');
-    expect(candidate.steps).toBe(5);
-    expect(candidate.occurrences).toBe(4);
-    expect(candidate.sessionCount).toBe(4);
-    expect(candidate.projectCount).toBe(2);
-    expect(candidate.provenance).toHaveLength(4);
-    expect(new Set(candidate.provenance).size).toBe(4);
-    expect(candidate.shape).toEqual([
-      'Bash(npm test)::error',
-      'Read({log})::ok',
-      'Bash(grep {pattern} {log})::ok',
-      'Edit({file})::ok',
-      'Bash(git push)::ok',
-    ]);
-    expect(candidate.firstSeen).toBe(new Date(BASE + 1 * DAY).toISOString());
-    expect(candidate.lastSeen).toBe(new Date(BASE + 4 * DAY).toISOString());
-  });
-});
-
-describe('chain precision: adversarial near-misses must not join the true shape', () => {
-  it('the same steps in a different order form a distinct shape, not the same candidate', () => {
-    const forward = [
-      episode('sess-a1', '/Users/me/repoA', 1, shipFixTrace(1)),
-      episode('sess-a2', '/Users/me/repoA', 2, shipFixTrace(2)),
-      episode('sess-a3', '/Users/me/repoA', 3, shipFixTrace(3)),
-    ];
-    const swapped = [
-      episode('sess-b1', '/Users/me/repoB', 4, shipFixTrace(4, { order: 'swapped' })),
-      episode('sess-b2', '/Users/me/repoB', 5, shipFixTrace(5, { order: 'swapped' })),
-      episode('sess-b3', '/Users/me/repoB', 6, shipFixTrace(6, { order: 'swapped' })),
-    ];
-
-    const candidates = detectChainCandidates([...forward, ...swapped]);
-
-    expect(candidates).toHaveLength(2);
-    expect(candidates.every((c) => c.occurrences === 3)).toBe(true);
-    const ids = new Set(candidates.map((c) => c.id));
-    expect(ids.size).toBe(2);
-  });
-
-  it('the same steps ending in a different outcome form a distinct shape (push failed vs push succeeded)', () => {
-    const succeeded = [
-      episode('sess-c1', '/Users/me/repoC', 1, shipFixTrace(1)),
-      episode('sess-c2', '/Users/me/repoC', 2, shipFixTrace(2)),
-      episode('sess-c3', '/Users/me/repoC', 3, shipFixTrace(3)),
-    ];
-    const failed = [
-      episode('sess-d1', '/Users/me/repoD', 4, shipFixTrace(4, { lastOutcome: 'error' })),
-      episode('sess-d2', '/Users/me/repoD', 5, shipFixTrace(5, { lastOutcome: 'error' })),
-    ];
-
-    // The failing variant only occurs twice — under the default threshold
-    // it must not surface at all, and it must never inflate the succeeded
-    // shape's count either.
-    const candidates = detectChainCandidates([...succeeded, ...failed]);
-
-    expect(candidates).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(candidates[0]?.occurrences).toBe(3);
-  });
-
-  it('a shape below the occurrence threshold yields no candidate', () => {
-    const episodes = [
-      episode('sess-e1', '/Users/me/repoE', 1, shipFixTrace(1)),
-      episode('sess-e2', '/Users/me/repoE', 2, shipFixTrace(2)),
-    ];
-
-    expect(detectChainCandidates(episodes)).toEqual([]);
-  });
-
-  it('single-step traces never form a chain, however often they repeat', () => {
-    const episodes = Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) =>
-      episode(`sess-f${i}`, '/Users/me/repoF', i + 1, [action('Bash(npm test)', 'ok', i + 1, 0)]),
-    );
-
-    expect(detectChainCandidates(episodes)).toEqual([]);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('chain negative control', () => {
-  it('varied one-off episode traces yield zero chain candidates', () => {
-    const episodes: Episode[] = [
-      episode('sess-g1', '/Users/me/repoG', 1, [
-        action('Bash(npm test)', 'ok', 1, 0),
-        action('Bash(npm publish)', 'ok', 1, 1_000),
-      ]),
-      episode('sess-g2', '/Users/me/repoG', 2, [
-        action('WebFetch({url})', 'ok', 2, 0),
-        action('Read({file})', 'ok', 2, 1_000),
-        action('Edit({file})', 'ok', 2, 2_000),
-      ]),
-      episode('sess-g3', '/Users/me/repoG', 3, [
-        action('Grep({pattern})', 'ok', 3, 0),
-      ]),
-      episode('sess-g4', '/Users/me/repoG', 4, [
-        action('Bash(git status)', 'ok', 4, 0),
-        action('Bash(git log -5)', 'ok', 4, 1_000),
-      ]),
-    ];
-
-    expect(detectChainCandidates(episodes)).toEqual([]);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/citations.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/citations.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 740d9d7..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/citations.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,86 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Citation discipline is enforced in code, not promised in prose: every quote a
- * proposal carries must exist verbatim in the corpus (a stored template or an
- * interned intent turn). A model that invents its evidence must produce nothing,
- * because a proposal that fabricates a receipt poisons the whole product.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { detectRecurrenceCandidates } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import {
-  corpusStrings,
-  runPropose,
-  LocalProposer,
-  type Dossier,
-  type Proposer,
-} from '../src/propose.js';
-import { seededState } from './cluster-fixture.js';
-
-const NOW = Date.parse('2026-06-05T09:00:00Z');
-
-async function seededCandidates() {
-  return detectRecurrenceCandidates(seededState(), { now: NOW, intentSimilarity: 0.45 });
-}
-
-describe('citation honesty', () => {
-  it('emits proposals and every citation resolves verbatim in the corpus', async () => {
-    const state = seededState();
-    const candidates = await seededCandidates();
-    const corpus = corpusStrings(state);
-
-    const result = await runPropose(candidates, state, {
-      proposer: new LocalProposer(),
-      budgetTokens: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY,
-    });
-
-    expect(result.proposals.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-    for (const proposal of result.proposals) {
-      expect(proposal.citations.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-      for (const citation of proposal.citations) {
-        expect(corpus.has(citation)).toBe(true);
-      }
-    }
-  });
-
-  it('strips a fabricated citation and refuses a proposal left with no evidence', async () => {
-    const state = seededState();
-    const candidates = await seededCandidates();
-    const corpus = corpusStrings(state);
-
-    // A rogue model that appends a plausible-but-fabricated quote to every
-    // proposal, and one candidate whose *only* citation is fabricated.
-    const local = new LocalProposer();
-    const fabricated = 'Bash(rm -rf /var/lib/hallucinated)';
-    expect(corpus.has(fabricated)).toBe(false);
-
-    const rogue: Proposer = {
-      estimateTokens: (dossier: Dossier) => local.estimateTokens(dossier),
-      propose: (dossier: Dossier) => {
-        const draft = local.propose(dossier);
-        if (dossier.kind === 'intent') {
-          // Replace all real evidence with a fabrication — must be refused.
-          return { ...draft, citations: [fabricated] };
-        }
-        // Slip a fabrication in beside the real ones — must be stripped.
-        return { ...draft, citations: [...draft.citations, fabricated] };
-      },
-    };
-
-    const result = await runPropose(candidates, state, {
-      proposer: rogue,
-      budgetTokens: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY,
-    });
-
-    // No proposal anywhere carries the fabricated quote.
-    for (const proposal of result.proposals) {
-      expect(proposal.citations).not.toContain(fabricated);
-      for (const citation of proposal.citations) {
-        expect(corpus.has(citation)).toBe(true);
-      }
-    }
-    // The all-fabricated (intent) candidate produced no proposal at all.
-    expect(result.proposals.some((proposal) => proposal.dossier.kind === 'intent')).toBe(false);
-    // Tool proposals survived — their real citations remained after the strip.
-    expect(result.proposals.some((proposal) => proposal.dossier.kind === 'tool')).toBe(true);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/cluster-fixture.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/cluster-fixture.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 1933df5..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/cluster-fixture.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,60 +0,0 @@
-import { normalizeToolCall } from '../src/normalize.js';
-import type { ActionEvent, StoredState } from '../src/types.js';
-
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-const START = Date.parse('2026-06-01T09:00:00Z');
-
-export const SEEDED_RITUALS = {
-  portCheck: normalizeToolCall('Bash', { command: 'lsof -i :8471' }),
-  mcpRestart: normalizeToolCall('Bash', { command: 'pkill -f mcp-server && npm run mcp:start' }),
-  competitorFetch: normalizeToolCall('WebFetch', { url: 'https://rival.example.com/pricing' }),
-  symbolSearch: normalizeToolCall('mcp__tilth__tilth_search', { kind: 'symbol', query: 'handleRequest' }),
-  competitorIntent: 'check competitor pricing',
-} as const;
-
-export function seededState(): StoredState {
-  const turns = [
-    'What changed in competitor pricing this week?',
-    'Check the rival price page for updates',
-    'Any competitor pricing changes today?',
-  ];
-  const events: ActionEvent[] = [];
-
-  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) {
-    events.push(toolEvent(SEEDED_RITUALS.portCheck, i, i));
-    events.push(toolEvent(SEEDED_RITUALS.mcpRestart, i, i));
-    events.push(toolEvent(SEEDED_RITUALS.competitorFetch, i, i));
-    events.push(toolEvent(SEEDED_RITUALS.symbolSearch, i, i));
-  }
-
-  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i += 1) {
-    events.push(userEvent(i % turns.length, i, i + 10));
-  }
-
-  return { version: 1, watermark: {}, turns, events };
-}
-
-function toolEvent(template: string, day: number, sequence: number): ActionEvent {
-  const epoch = START + day * DAY + sequence * 1000;
-  return {
-    kind: 'tool',
-    ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(),
-    epoch,
-    project: '/Users/me/repo',
-    session: `session-${day}`,
-    tool: template.slice(0, template.indexOf('(')),
-    template,
-  };
-}
-
-function userEvent(turn: number, day: number, sequence: number): ActionEvent {
-  const epoch = START + day * DAY + sequence * 1000;
-  return {
-    kind: 'user',
-    ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(),
-    epoch,
-    project: '/Users/me/repo',
-    session: `session-${day}`,
-    turn,
-  };
-}
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/concept-precision.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/concept-precision.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 2a4816d..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/concept-precision.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,84 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Precision counter-check to paraphrase recall — the other blade of the
- * scissors. Same-topic-but-different-intent turns are seeded ALONGSIDE the
- * ask groups; a clusterer that greedily keys on topical words ("restart",
- * "build cache", "staging api") would swallow them. Each adversarial turn
- * must stay OUT of the ask cluster it shadows.
- *
- * These adversarials sit at measured similarity 0.166–0.635 against their
- * shadowed groups — real threshold work, not free wins. The known harder
- * case (a command vs a QUESTION about the same object, measured 0.83) is
- * beyond every embedding tier and is documented as the LLM merge tier's
- * responsibility, not silently claimed here.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { detectRecurrenceCandidates } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import { StaticEmbeddingProvider, defaultModelDir } from '../src/embed/static.js';
-import { ADVERSARIAL_TURNS, PARAPHRASE_GROUPS } from './fixtures.js';
-import type { ActionEvent, StoredState } from '../src/types.js';
-
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-const START = Date.parse('2026-06-01T09:00:00Z');
-const NOW = Date.parse('2026-06-08T09:00:00Z');
-
-const ALL_TURNS = [...PARAPHRASE_GROUPS.flat(), ...ADVERSARIAL_TURNS];
-
-/**
- * The ask groups recur as in paraphrase.spec; every adversarial turn ALSO
- * recurs (4×) so it clears the occurrence floor — it must be excluded by
- * distance, not by being too rare to matter.
- */
-function mixedState(): StoredState {
-  const events: ActionEvent[] = [];
-  let seq = 0;
-  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i += 1) {
-    let offset = 0;
-    for (const group of PARAPHRASE_GROUPS) {
-      events.push(userEvent(offset + (i % group.length), i, seq++));
-      offset += group.length;
-    }
-  }
-  const adversarialBase = PARAPHRASE_GROUPS.flat().length;
-  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) {
-    for (let a = 0; a < ADVERSARIAL_TURNS.length; a += 1) {
-      events.push(userEvent(adversarialBase + a, i, seq++));
-    }
-  }
-  return { version: 1, watermark: {}, turns: [...ALL_TURNS], events };
-}
-
-function userEvent(turn: number, day: number, sequence: number): ActionEvent {
-  const epoch = START + day * DAY + sequence * 1000;
-  return {
-    kind: 'user',
-    ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(),
-    epoch,
-    project: '/Users/me/repo',
-    session: `session-${day}`,
-    turn,
-  };
-}
-
-describe('cluster precision against same-topic different-intent turns', () => {
-  it('no adversarial turn joins the ask cluster it shadows', async () => {
-    const embedder = StaticEmbeddingProvider.load(defaultModelDir());
-    const candidates = await detectRecurrenceCandidates(mixedState(), { now: NOW, embedder });
-    const intents = candidates.filter((candidate) => candidate.kind === 'intent');
-
-    for (const group of PARAPHRASE_GROUPS) {
-      const cluster = intents.find((candidate) =>
-        group.some((turn) => candidate.provenance.some((line) => line.includes(turn))),
-      );
-      expect(cluster, `no cluster found for group "${group[0]}"`).toBeDefined();
-      for (const adversarial of ADVERSARIAL_TURNS) {
-        expect(
-          cluster!.provenance.some((line) => line.includes(adversarial)),
-          `adversarial "${adversarial}" leaked into the "${group[0]}" cluster`,
-        ).toBe(false);
-      }
-      // The ask group still coheres with the adversarial noise present.
-      expect(cluster!.eventCount).toBe(6);
-    }
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/convergence.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/convergence.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 9194ab3..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/convergence.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,213 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Convergence-measured mechanizability (c4, docs/loop-hole-episodes-design.md
- * line 24) — mechanizability must be MEASURED from how much a cluster's own
- * traces agree with each other, never assigned from a lookup keyed on what
- * kind of thing the cluster is. Recall: a convergent cluster (same trace,
- * every time) scores mechanizable. Precision/negative control: a divergent
- * cluster (a different trace every time) does not. Genericity: the score is
- * provably blind to template names, tool identity, and "kind" — only the
- * SHAPE of agreement between traces moves it, proven by relabeling symbols
- * and by swapping which literal templates appear while holding the
- * agreement pattern constant.
- */
-
-import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { scoreConvergence } from '../src/convergence.js';
-import type { Episode, EpisodeAction } from '../src/episodes.js';
-
-const SRC_DIR = join(fileURLToPath(new URL('.', import.meta.url)), '..', 'src');
-
-const BASE = Date.parse('2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z');
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-
-function action(template: string, outcome: EpisodeAction['outcome'], day: number, offsetMs: number): EpisodeAction {
-  const epoch = BASE + day * DAY + offsetMs;
-  return { tool: template.split('(')[0] ?? template, template, outcome, ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(), epoch };
-}
-
-function episode(session: string, day: number, actions: EpisodeAction[]): Episode {
-  const askEpoch = BASE + day * DAY;
-  const last = actions[actions.length - 1];
-  return {
-    session,
-    project: '/Users/me/repo',
-    turn: 0,
-    ts: new Date(askEpoch).toISOString(),
-    epoch: askEpoch,
-    actions,
-    durationMs: last === undefined ? 0 : last.epoch - askEpoch,
-    tokens: { input: 0, output: 0 },
-    resolution: 'resolved',
-  };
-}
-
-/** The same four-step trace every time — the convergent case. */
-function shipFixTrace(day: number): EpisodeAction[] {
-  return [
-    action('Bash(npm test)', 'error', day, 0),
-    action('Read({log})', 'ok', day, 1_000),
-    action('Edit({file})', 'ok', day, 2_000),
-    action('Bash(git push)', 'ok', day, 3_000),
-  ];
-}
-
-describe('convergence recall: a convergent-trace cluster scores mechanizable', () => {
-  it('five episodes running the identical trace score zero variance and full mechanizability', () => {
-    const cluster: Episode[] = Array.from({ length: 5 }, (_, i) => episode(`sess-${i}`, i + 1, shipFixTrace(i + 1)));
-
-    const score = scoreConvergence(cluster);
-
-    expect(score.episodeCount).toBe(5);
-    expect(score.variance).toBe(0);
-    expect(score.mechanizability).toBe(1);
-    expect(score.mechanizable).toBe(true);
-  });
-
-  it('a one-symbol perturbation in a minority of episodes still converges (small variance, still mechanizable)', () => {
-    const cluster: Episode[] = [
-      episode('sess-a', 1, shipFixTrace(1)),
-      episode('sess-b', 2, shipFixTrace(2)),
-      episode('sess-c', 3, shipFixTrace(3)),
-      episode('sess-d', 4, shipFixTrace(4)),
-      // One outlier: the push failed instead of succeeding — a single
-      // substituted symbol out of four, not a different procedure.
-      episode('sess-e', 5, [
-        action('Bash(npm test)', 'error', 5, 0),
-        action('Read({log})', 'ok', 5, 1_000),
-        action('Edit({file})', 'ok', 5, 2_000),
-        action('Bash(git push)', 'error', 5, 3_000),
-      ]),
-    ];
-
-    const score = scoreConvergence(cluster);
-
-    expect(score.variance).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-    expect(score.variance).toBeLessThan(0.2);
-    expect(score.mechanizable).toBe(true);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('convergence negative control: a divergent-trace cluster does not score mechanizable', () => {
-  it('five episodes each running an unrelated one-off trace score high variance and low mechanizability', () => {
-    const cluster: Episode[] = [
-      episode('sess-p', 1, [action('WebFetch({url})', 'ok', 1, 0)]),
-      episode('sess-q', 2, [
-        action('Grep({pattern})', 'ok', 2, 0),
-        action('Read({file})', 'ok', 2, 1_000),
-        action('Edit({file})', 'ok', 2, 2_000),
-        action('Bash(npm run build)', 'error', 2, 3_000),
-        action('Read({log})', 'ok', 2, 4_000),
-      ]),
-      episode('sess-r', 3, [action('Bash(ls -la)', 'ok', 3, 0), action('Bash(rm {tmp})', 'ok', 3, 1_000)]),
-      episode('sess-s', 4, [
-        action('mcp__tilth__tilth_search({query})', 'ok', 4, 0),
-        action('Write({file})', 'ok', 4, 1_000),
-      ]),
-      episode('sess-t', 5, [action('Bash(git log -5)', 'ok', 5, 0)]),
-    ];
-
-    const score = scoreConvergence(cluster);
-
-    expect(score.episodeCount).toBe(5);
-    expect(score.variance).toBeGreaterThan(0.5);
-    expect(score.mechanizability).toBeLessThan(0.5);
-    expect(score.mechanizable).toBe(false);
-  });
-
-  it('a cluster with no shared trace at all bottoms out near full variance, not exactly the recurring cluster score', () => {
-    const convergent = scoreConvergence(Array.from({ length: 4 }, (_, i) => episode(`c-${i}`, i + 1, shipFixTrace(i + 1))));
-    const divergent = scoreConvergence([
-      episode('d-1', 1, [action('Bash(npm test)', 'ok', 1, 0)]),
-      episode('d-2', 2, [action('WebFetch({url})', 'ok', 2, 0), action('Read({file})', 'ok', 2, 1_000)]),
-      episode('d-3', 3, [
-        action('Grep({pattern})', 'ok', 3, 0),
-        action('Edit({file})', 'ok', 3, 1_000),
-        action('Bash(git push)', 'error', 3, 2_000),
-      ]),
-      episode('d-4', 4, [action('mcp__tilth__tilth_read({path})', 'ok', 4, 0)]),
-    ]);
-
-    expect(divergent.mechanizability).toBeLessThan(convergent.mechanizability);
-    expect(divergent.mechanizable).toBe(false);
-    expect(convergent.mechanizable).toBe(true);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('convergence genericity: the score is blind to template identity and "kind" — only shape agreement moves it', () => {
-  it('relabeling every symbol with a bijection leaves the score unchanged', () => {
-    const relabel = new Map([
-      ['Bash(npm test)::error', 'mcp__lint__run({target})::error'],
-      ['Read({log})::ok', 'mcp__lint__report({target})::ok'],
-      ['Edit({file})::ok', 'mcp__lint__patch({target})::ok'],
-      ['Bash(git push)::ok', 'mcp__lint__publish({target})::ok'],
-    ]);
-
-    function relabeled(actions: EpisodeAction[]): EpisodeAction[] {
-      return actions.map((a) => {
-        const symbol = `${a.template}::${a.outcome}`;
-        const mapped = relabel.get(symbol);
-        if (mapped === undefined) throw new Error(`no relabel for ${symbol}`);
-        const [template] = mapped.split('::');
-        return { ...a, template: template ?? a.template, tool: (template ?? a.template).split('(')[0] ?? 'tool' };
-      });
-    }
-
-    const original = Array.from({ length: 5 }, (_, i) => episode(`o-${i}`, i + 1, shipFixTrace(i + 1)));
-    const swapped = original.map((ep) => ({ ...ep, actions: relabeled(ep.actions) }));
-
-    expect(scoreConvergence(swapped)).toEqual(scoreConvergence(original));
-  });
-
-  it('two clusters built from entirely different literal templates score identically when their agreement pattern is identical', () => {
-    // "Tool-flavored" templates (as cluster.ts's toolMechanizability would
-    // treat as structurally high-prior Bash/mcp__ calls) vs. "intent-flavored"
-    // one-off prose-adjacent templates — same shape: four matching traces,
-    // one with the last symbol swapped. If mechanizability came from a
-    // per-kind prior these would differ; from pure trace variance they must not.
-    function fourMatchOneSwap(templates: readonly [string, string, string, string]): Episode[] {
-      const [t1, t2, t3, t4] = templates;
-      const trace = (day: number, lastOutcome: EpisodeAction['outcome'] = 'ok'): EpisodeAction[] => [
-        action(t1, 'error', day, 0),
-        action(t2, 'ok', day, 1_000),
-        action(t3, 'ok', day, 2_000),
-        action(t4, lastOutcome, day, 3_000),
-      ];
-      return [
-        episode('m-1', 1, trace(1)),
-        episode('m-2', 2, trace(2)),
-        episode('m-3', 3, trace(3)),
-        episode('m-4', 4, trace(4)),
-        episode('m-5', 5, trace(5, 'error')),
-      ];
-    }
-
-    const toolFlavored = fourMatchOneSwap(['Bash(npm test)', 'Read({log})', 'Edit({file})', 'Bash(git push)']);
-    const intentFlavored = fourMatchOneSwap([
-      'mcp__crm__lookup({account})',
-      'mcp__crm__draft({account})',
-      'mcp__crm__review({account})',
-      'mcp__crm__send({account})',
-    ]);
-
-    expect(scoreConvergence(intentFlavored)).toEqual(scoreConvergence(toolFlavored));
-  });
-});
-
-describe('the claim\'s strongest clause: no per-kind priors ANYWHERE in src', () => {
-  // The convergence module being prior-free is necessary but not sufficient —
-  // the claim says the PRODUCT has no hardcoded per-kind mechanizability
-  // anywhere. This scan enforces it the way the fixture-leak lint enforces
-  // its ban: against the source itself, so a prior-free module bolted beside
-  // a prior-driven scorer can never stamp this check again (lead-audit catch:
-  // the first close left toolMechanizability and the 0.72 intent prior alive
-  // in cluster.ts while this spec looked only at convergence.ts).
-  it('cluster.ts derives mechanizability from measured convergence, not kind priors', () => {
-    const clusterSource = readFileSync(join(SRC_DIR, 'cluster.ts'), 'utf8');
-    expect(clusterSource).not.toContain('toolMechanizability');
-    expect(clusterSource).not.toContain('0.72');
-    expect(clusterSource).not.toContain("startsWith('Bash(')");
-  });
-});

-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { detectRecurrenceCandidates, type EmbeddingProvider } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import type { ActionEvent, StoredState } from '../src/types.js';
-
-describe('intent embedding spend', () => {
-  it('embeds a repeated turn once before clustering', async () => {
-    const repeated = 'Check whether competitor pricing changed';
-    const turns = [repeated, 'Check if rival prices changed'];
-    const events: ActionEvent[] = [];
-    for (let i = 0; i < 12; i += 1) {
-      events.push(userEvent(i < 10 ? 0 : 1, i));
-    }
-    const state: StoredState = { version: 1, watermark: {}, turns, events };
-
-    const batches: string[][] = [];
-    const embedder: EmbeddingProvider = {
-      embed(texts) {
-        batches.push([...texts]);
-        return texts.map((text) => text === repeated ? [1, 0] : [0.96, 0.04]);
-      },
-    };
-
-    await detectRecurrenceCandidates(state, {
-      embedder,
-      intentSimilarity: 0.9,
-      now: Date.parse('2026-06-20T00:00:00Z'),
-    });
-
-    expect(batches).toEqual([[repeated, 'Check if rival prices changed']]);
-  });
-});
-
-function userEvent(turn: number, day: number): ActionEvent {
-  const epoch = Date.parse('2026-06-01T00:00:00Z') + day * 86_400_000;
-  return {
-    kind: 'user',
-    ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(),
-    epoch,
-    project: '/Users/me/repo',
-    session: `session-${day}`,
-    turn,
-  };
-}
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/digest.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/digest.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index ebdfb14..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/digest.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,93 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Digest determinism — the property the facet reader tier depends on:
- * building a digest twice from the same session bytes must produce
- * byte-identical output, and a session that gets appended to long after it
- * started (a "resume") must still be dated by its first message, never by
- * the append/reprocess time or any clock read.
- */
-
-import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
-import { ClaudeCodeAdapter } from '../src/claude-adapter.js';
-import { runExtract } from '../src/extract.js';
-import { StateStore } from '../src/store.js';
-import { buildSessionDigests } from '../src/digest.js';
-import { makeCorpus, appendRecord, userTurn, toolCall } from './helpers.js';
-
-describe('digest determinism', () => {
-  it('yields byte-identical digests across independent extraction passes over the same session file', async () => {
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repoA',
-        sessionId: 'sess-a',
-        records: [
-          userTurn('run the release checklist', '2026-06-01T10:00:00.000Z'),
-          toolCall('Bash', { command: 'npm test' }, '2026-06-01T10:00:01.000Z'),
-          toolCall('Bash', { command: 'npm publish' }, '2026-06-01T10:00:02.000Z'),
-          userTurn('check the deploy status', '2026-06-01T10:05:00.000Z'),
-          toolCall('Bash', { command: 'curl https://status.example/health' }, '2026-06-01T10:05:01.000Z'),
-        ],
-      },
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repoB',
-        sessionId: 'sess-b',
-        records: [toolCall('Bash', { command: 'git status' }, '2026-06-02T09:00:00.000Z')],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const storeA = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), storeA);
-    const digestsA = buildSessionDigests(storeA);
-
-    // A wholly independent pass over the same bytes — new adapter, new store.
-    const storeB = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), storeB);
-    const digestsB = buildSessionDigests(storeB);
-
-    expect(JSON.stringify(digestsA)).toBe(JSON.stringify(digestsB));
-
-    // And re-deriving from the SAME store twice is equally stable.
-    expect(JSON.stringify(buildSessionDigests(storeA))).toBe(JSON.stringify(digestsA));
-
-    const sessA = digestsA.find((d) => d.session === 'sess-a');
-    expect(sessA).toBeDefined();
-    expect(sessA?.episodes).toHaveLength(2);
-    expect(sessA?.episodes[0]?.intent).toBe('run the release checklist');
-    expect(sessA?.episodes[0]?.actions).toEqual([
-      'Bash(npm test):unknown',
-      'Bash(npm publish):unknown',
-    ]);
-    expect(sessA?.episodes[1]?.intent).toBe('check the deploy status');
-  });
-
-  it('dates a resumed session by its first message, never by the resume/append time', async () => {
-    const FIRST_TS = '2026-01-05T08:00:00.000Z';
-    const RESUME_TS = '2026-06-30T23:59:59.000Z';
-
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repoC',
-        sessionId: 'sess-resumed',
-        records: [
-          userTurn('start the migration', FIRST_TS),
-          toolCall('Bash', { command: 'echo start' }, FIRST_TS),
-        ],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-    const firstPassDate = buildSessionDigests(store).find((d) => d.session === 'sess-resumed')?.date;
-    expect(firstPassDate).toBe(FIRST_TS);
-
-    // Simulate a resume: the file is appended to months later, which changes
-    // its mtime (the watermark signature) — the classic "resume time" lie.
-    appendRecord(root, '-Users-me-repoC', 'sess-resumed', userTurn('finish the migration', RESUME_TS));
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-
-    const resumed = buildSessionDigests(store).find((d) => d.session === 'sess-resumed');
-    expect(resumed?.date).toBe(FIRST_TS);
-    expect(resumed?.date).not.toBe(RESUME_TS);
-    expect(resumed?.episodes).toHaveLength(2);
-    expect(resumed?.episodes[1]?.intent).toBe('finish the migration');
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/diversity.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/diversity.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 4676689..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/diversity.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,88 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Head diversity — "mostly one project" was half the owner's refutation of
- * the first real briefing. When the corpus spans several projects, the head
- * of a lane must too: single-project sources are capped inside the first ten
- * rows, capped rows defer past the head in score order, and multi-project
- * proposals are never capped (spanning projects IS the diversity).
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { diversifyHead } from '../src/report.js';
-import type { Proposal } from '../src/propose.js';
-
-function makeProposal(intent: string, score: number, projects: string[]): Proposal {
-  return {
-    candidateId: `tool:${intent}`,
-    clusterKey: `tool:${intent}`,
-    score,
-    intent,
-    form: 'standing-loop',
-    mechanizable: true,
-    mechanizabilityRationale: 'stable shape',
-    fitnessFunction: {
-      claim: `${intent} stays correct unattended`,
-      method: 'synthetic-test',
-      assertion: 'PASS when the scheduled run matches the manual result; FAIL otherwise.',
-      draft: true,
-    },
-    payoff: 'reclaims recurring hand-work',
-    citations: [intent],
-    dossier: {
-      candidateId: `tool:${intent}`,
-      clusterKey: `tool:${intent}`,
-      kind: 'tool',
-      label: intent,
-      eventCount: 5,
-      sessionCount: 3,
-      projectCount: projects.length,
-      projects,
-      cadence: [],
-      exemplars: [intent],
-      toil: { frequency: 0, medianDurationMs: 0, interventions: 0 },
-      firstSeen: '2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z',
-      lastSeen: '2026-06-07T09:00:00.000Z',
-      fingerprint: { kind: 'tool', memberHashes: [intent] },
-    },
-    tokensUsed: 50,
-  };
-}
-
-/** Ranked input: project A holds the six highest scores, then B and C. */
-function threeProjectLane(): Proposal[] {
-  const lane: Proposal[] = [];
-  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i += 1) lane.push(makeProposal(`a${i}`, 0.9 - i * 0.01, ['/repo/a']));
-  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i += 1) lane.push(makeProposal(`b${i}`, 0.5 - i * 0.01, ['/repo/b']));
-  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i += 1) lane.push(makeProposal(`c${i}`, 0.4 - i * 0.01, ['/repo/c']));
-  return lane;
-}
-
-describe('diversifyHead', () => {
-  it('a three-project corpus yields a three-project top ten', () => {
-    const result = diversifyHead(threeProjectLane());
-    const headProjects = new Set(result.slice(0, 10).map((proposal) => proposal.dossier.projects[0]));
-    expect(headProjects.size).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);
-  });
-
-  it('caps a single project inside the head and defers the overflow in score order', () => {
-    const result = diversifyHead(threeProjectLane());
-    const headFromA = result.slice(0, 10).filter((proposal) => proposal.dossier.projects[0] === '/repo/a');
-    expect(headFromA).toHaveLength(4);
-    // The two deferred A-proposals still appear, still in their own score order.
-    const deferredA = result.slice(10).map((proposal) => proposal.intent);
-    expect(deferredA).toEqual(['a4', 'a5']);
-  });
-
-  it('a single-project corpus keeps pure score order — the cap changes nothing', () => {
-    const lane = Array.from({ length: 8 }, (_, i) => makeProposal(`p${i}`, 0.9 - i * 0.01, ['/repo/only']));
-    const result = diversifyHead(lane);
-    expect(result.map((proposal) => proposal.intent)).toEqual(lane.map((proposal) => proposal.intent));
-  });
-
-  it('multi-project proposals are never capped', () => {
-    const lane = Array.from({ length: 8 }, (_, i) =>
-      makeProposal(`m${i}`, 0.9 - i * 0.01, ['/repo/a', '/repo/b', '/repo/c']),
-    );
-    const result = diversifyHead(lane);
-    expect(result.map((proposal) => proposal.intent)).toEqual(lane.map((proposal) => proposal.intent));
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/domain-generality.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/domain-generality.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 8ce8d41..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/domain-generality.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,147 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Domain generality: the detector pipeline must work on a support-team corpus
- * whose alphabet is email/document/search activity, not development tools.
- *
- * The support-specific knowledge below lives in a corpus adapter. The pipeline
- * still consumes the same neutral event table and the same cluster/propose/
- * report stages; no detector gets a table of support phrases or tool names.
- */
-
-import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { extractPayload } from '../../../loop/src/core/extract.js';
-import type { CorpusAdapter, SessionSource } from '../src/types.js';
-import { runPipeline } from '../src/run.js';
-
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-const START = Date.parse('2026-06-08T08:30:00Z');
-
-interface SupportAction {
-  atMinutes: number;
-  name: string;
-  input: Record<string, unknown>;
-}
-
-interface SupportSession {
-  sessionId: string;
-  workspace: string;
-  receivedAt: string;
-  requesterNote: string;
-  actions: SupportAction[];
-}
-
-/**
- * A support-log adapter: it translates a non-Claude, non-development record
- * into the extractor's existing raw-message shape. The mapping is deliberately
- * local to the adapter; downstream stages only see `user` and `tool` events.
- */
-class SupportTeamAdapter implements CorpusAdapter {
-  readonly name = 'support-team';
-
-  constructor(private readonly records: readonly SupportSession[]) {}
-
-  *sessions(): Iterable<SessionSource> {
-    for (const record of this.records) {
-      yield {
-        sessionId: record.sessionId,
-        project: record.workspace,
-        signature: `${record.sessionId}:${record.receivedAt}:${record.actions.length}`,
-        lines: () => supportLines(record),
-      };
-    }
-  }
-}
-
-function* supportLines(record: SupportSession): Iterable<string> {
-  yield JSON.stringify({
-    type: 'user',
-    timestamp: record.receivedAt,
-    cwd: record.workspace,
-    message: { role: 'user', content: record.requesterNote },
-  });
-
-  const start = Date.parse(record.receivedAt);
-  for (const action of record.actions) {
-    yield JSON.stringify({
-      type: 'assistant',
-      timestamp: new Date(start + action.atMinutes * 60_000).toISOString(),
-      cwd: record.workspace,
-      message: {
-        role: 'assistant',
-        content: [{ type: 'tool_use', name: action.name, input: action.input }],
-      },
-    });
-  }
-}
-
-function supportCorpus(): SupportSession[] {
-  const sessions: SupportSession[] = [];
-  for (let day = 0; day < 5; day += 1) {
-    const receivedAt = new Date(START + day * DAY).toISOString();
-    sessions.push({
-      sessionId: `support-shift-${day}`,
-      workspace: day % 2 === 0 ? 'support/refunds' : 'support/accounts',
-      receivedAt,
-      requesterNote: 'Please review the refund escalation queue before the morning handoff.',
-      actions: [
-        {
-          atMinutes: 1,
-          name: 'EmailSearch',
-          input: { mailbox: 'refunds-escalations', query: `handoff older_than:${day + 1}d` },
-        },
-        {
-          atMinutes: 2,
-          name: 'DocumentOpen',
-          input: { documentId: `refund-policy-${2026 + day}`, section: 'exceptions' },
-        },
-        {
-          atMinutes: 3,
-          name: 'KnowledgeSearch',
-          input: { collection: 'support-kb', query: `refund exception language ${day}` },
-        },
-      ],
-    });
-  }
-  return sessions;
-}
-
-function emittedPayloads(result: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof runPipeline>>): string[] {
-  return result.pages.items.flatMap((item) => {
-    const opportunity = extractPayload(readFileSync(item.opportunity.path, 'utf8'))!.payload;
-    const draft = extractPayload(readFileSync(item.draft.path, 'utf8'))!.payload;
-    return [opportunity, draft];
-  });
-}
-
-describe('domain-general pipeline detection', () => {
-  it('finds recurring rituals in a non-development support corpus through an adapter seam', async () => {
-    const outDir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-support-out-'));
-    const result = await runPipeline({
-      corpusDir: 'unused-by-adapter',
-      outDir,
-      adapter: new SupportTeamAdapter(supportCorpus()),
-      detection: {
-        minToolOccurrences: 4,
-        minIntentOccurrences: 4,
-        embedder: {
-          embed: (texts: readonly string[]) => texts.map((text) => [text.length]),
-          similarityThreshold: 1,
-        },
-      },
-    });
-
-    expect(result.extract.newSessions).toBe(5);
-    expect(result.propose.proposals.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);
-
-    const proposals = result.propose.proposals;
-    expect(proposals.some((proposal) => proposal.dossier.kind === 'intent')).toBe(true);
-    expect(proposals.filter((proposal) => proposal.dossier.kind === 'tool').length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
-
-    const payload = emittedPayloads(result).join('\n');
-    expect(payload).toContain('Please review the refund escalation queue before the morning handoff.');
-    expect(payload).toContain('EmailSearch({mailbox,query})');
-    expect(payload).toContain('DocumentOpen({documentId,section})');
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/emit-assets.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/emit-assets.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 42e1330..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/emit-assets.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,93 +0,0 @@
-import { existsSync, mkdtempSync, readFileSync, readdirSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { dirname, isAbsolute, join, relative } from 'node:path';
-import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { detectRecurrenceCandidates } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import { LocalProposer, runPropose } from '../src/propose.js';
-import { emitProposalPages } from '../src/report.js';
-import { seededState } from './cluster-fixture.js';
-
-const NOW = Date.parse('2026-06-05T09:00:00Z');
-
-/** Repo-root slice dir: the canonical renderer the whole map shares. */
-const SLICE_DIR = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '../../../slice');
-
-/** Every value inside src="…" / href="…" on a page. */
-function assetRefs(html: string): string[] {
-  const refs: string[] = [];
-  const re = /(?:src|href)="([^"]*)"/g;
-  let match: RegExpExecArray | null;
-  while ((match = re.exec(html)) !== null) {
-    refs.push(match[1]!);
-  }
-  return refs;
-}
-
-async function emitInto(outDir: string) {
-  const state = seededState();
-  const candidates = await detectRecurrenceCandidates(state, { now: NOW, intentSimilarity: 0.45 });
-  const { proposals } = await runPropose(candidates, state, {
-    proposer: new LocalProposer(),
-    budgetTokens: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY,
-  });
-  return emitProposalPages(proposals, { outDir });
-}
-
-describe('self-contained output dir (c3)', () => {
-  it('emits at least a briefing plus opportunity + draft pages', async () => {
-    const outDir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-assets-'));
-    const result = await emitInto(outDir);
-    expect(result.opportunities.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-    expect(result.drafts.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-    expect(existsSync(result.briefing.path)).toBe(true);
-  });
-
-  it('no emitted page references an asset with a ../ escape', async () => {
-    const outDir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-assets-'));
-    await emitInto(outDir);
-
-    const pages = readdirSync(outDir).filter((name) => name.endsWith('.loop.html'));
-    expect(pages.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-
-    for (const page of pages) {
-      const html = readFileSync(join(outDir, page), 'utf8');
-      for (const ref of assetRefs(html)) {
-        expect(isAbsolute(ref), `${page} references absolute asset ${ref}`).toBe(false);
-        expect(ref.includes('..'), `${page} escapes the out dir via ${ref}`).toBe(false);
-      }
-    }
-  });
-
-  it('every referenced asset resolves to a real file that lands inside the out dir', async () => {
-    const outDir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-assets-'));
-    await emitInto(outDir);
-
-    const pages = readdirSync(outDir).filter((name) => name.endsWith('.loop.html'));
-    for (const page of pages) {
-      const pageDir = dirname(join(outDir, page));
-      const html = readFileSync(join(outDir, page), 'utf8');
-      const refs = assetRefs(html).filter((ref) => !ref.startsWith('data:') && !/^[a-z]+:/i.test(ref));
-      expect(refs.length, `${page} references no renderer assets`).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-      for (const ref of refs) {
-        // Resolve the asset exactly as a browser would from the page's own dir.
-        const resolved = join(pageDir, ref);
-        expect(existsSync(resolved), `${page} → ${ref} does not exist on disk`).toBe(true);
-        // The resolved path must stay inside the out dir (double-click renders where it lands).
-        const rel = relative(outDir, resolved);
-        expect(rel.startsWith('..') || isAbsolute(rel), `${page} → ${ref} escapes ${outDir}`).toBe(false);
-      }
-    }
-  });
-
-  it('the copied renderer assets are the real slice renderer, byte-for-byte', async () => {
-    const outDir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-assets-'));
-    await emitInto(outDir);
-
-    for (const asset of ['loop.css', 'loop.js']) {
-      const copied = join(outDir, asset);
-      expect(existsSync(copied), `${asset} missing from out dir`).toBe(true);
-      expect(readFileSync(copied)).toEqual(readFileSync(join(SLICE_DIR, asset)));
-    }
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/emit-prune.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/emit-prune.spec.ts

-import { existsSync, mkdtempSync, readFileSync, readdirSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { basename, join } from 'node:path';
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { detectRecurrenceCandidates } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import { LocalProposer, runPropose } from '../src/propose.js';
-import { emitProposalPages } from '../src/report.js';
-import type { Proposal } from '../src/propose.js';
-import { seededState } from './cluster-fixture.js';
-
-const NOW = Date.parse('2026-06-05T09:00:00Z');
-
-async function proposalsFor(): Promise<Proposal[]> {
-  const state = seededState();
-  const candidates = await detectRecurrenceCandidates(state, { now: NOW, intentSimilarity: 0.45 });
-  const { proposals } = await runPropose(candidates, state, {
-    proposer: new LocalProposer(),
-    budgetTokens: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY,
-  });
-  return proposals;
-}
-
-describe('emit prunes orphan pages (c7)', () => {
-  it('deletes a stale page left over from an earlier run, keeping state stores and assets untouched', async () => {
-    const outDir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-prune-'));
-
-    // Leftovers a prior proposer pass could plausibly have left behind: an
-    // orphan proposal page, plus the two persistent stores that must survive
-    // because they are not derived pages — they are this run's own inputs.
-    const orphanPath = join(outDir, 'a-stale-proposal-from-last-week-draft.loop.html');
-    writeFileSync(orphanPath, '<!doctype html>\n<title>stale</title>\n');
-    const storePath = join(outDir, 'loop-hole.store.json');
-    writeFileSync(storePath, '{"version":1,"watermark":{},"turns":[],"events":[]}');
-    const facetStorePath = join(outDir, 'loop-hole.facets.json');
-    writeFileSync(facetStorePath, '{}');
-
-    const proposals = await proposalsFor();
-    const emitted = emitProposalPages(proposals, { outDir });
-
-    expect(existsSync(orphanPath)).toBe(false);
-    expect(readFileSync(storePath, 'utf8')).toBe('{"version":1,"watermark":{},"turns":[],"events":[]}');
-    expect(readFileSync(facetStorePath, 'utf8')).toBe('{}');
-
-    const expectedPages = new Set([
-      basename(emitted.briefing.path),
-      ...emitted.opportunities.map((page) => basename(page.path)),
-      ...emitted.drafts.map((page) => basename(page.path)),
-    ]);
-    const pagesOnDisk = readdirSync(outDir).filter((name) => name.endsWith('.loop.html'));
-    expect(new Set(pagesOnDisk)).toEqual(expectedPages);
-  });
-
-  it('a second emit into the same dir prunes pages the first emit produced but the second no longer does', async () => {
-    const outDir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-prune-'));
-    const allProposals = await proposalsFor();
-    expect(allProposals.length).toBeGreaterThan(1);
-
-    const first = emitProposalPages(allProposals, { outDir });
-    expect(first.drafts.length).toBeGreaterThan(1);
-
-    const kept = allProposals[0]!;
-    const second = emitProposalPages([kept], { outDir });
-
-    const survivingPaths = new Set([
-      second.briefing.path,
-      ...second.opportunities.map((page) => page.path),
-      ...second.drafts.map((page) => page.path),
-    ]);
-    const droppedFromFirstRun = [
-      first.briefing.path,
-      ...first.opportunities.map((page) => page.path),
-      ...first.drafts.map((page) => page.path),
-    ].filter((path) => !survivingPaths.has(path));
-
-    expect(droppedFromFirstRun.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-    for (const path of droppedFromFirstRun) {
-      expect(existsSync(path), `${path} is an orphan from the first emit and should have been pruned`).toBe(false);
-    }
-    for (const path of survivingPaths) {
-      expect(existsSync(path), `${path} should still exist after the second emit`).toBe(true);
-    }
-
-    const pagesOnDisk = readdirSync(outDir).filter((name) => name.endsWith('.loop.html'));
-    expect(new Set(pagesOnDisk)).toEqual(new Set([...survivingPaths].map((path) => basename(path))));
-  });
-});

-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { detectRecurrenceCandidates } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import { LocalProposer, runPropose } from '../src/propose.js';
-import { emitProposalPages } from '../src/report.js';

-import { lint } from '../../../loop/src/core/lint.js';
-import { parse } from '../../../loop/src/core/parse.js';
-
-const NOW = Date.parse('2026-06-05T09:00:00Z');
-
-describe('proposal emission', () => {
-  it('writes draft loops that parse and lint clean with every check open', async () => {
