diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/episodes-negctrl.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/episodes-negctrl.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 0ad20e2..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/episodes-negctrl.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,166 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * The episode-layer negative control (c6, docs/loop-hole-episodes-design.md
- * line 41). chains.ts and polling.ts are the two detectors that read Episode
- * traces directly and turn a recurring MOLECULE into a candidate — the whole
- * point of this design over atom counting. A detector that "finds" a shape
- * or a burst in a corpus of genuinely varied, one-off activity manufactures
- * busywork instead of surfacing real recurrence, which is worse than finding
- * nothing.
- *
- * The corpus is seeded as a raw event stream (the same shape `extract.ts`
- * would hand off), not hand-built `Episode[]` fixtures — `segmentEpisodes`
- * runs for real here, so this proves the whole path from log to zero
- * candidates, not just the detectors in isolation. It is deliberately not
- * merely unique: one shape repeats exactly twice (one under chains' default
- * occurrence floor of three) and one burst is exactly two asks (one under
- * polling's default ask floor of three), so the test proves the thresholds
- * are doing the silencing, not that nothing recurring was ever seeded.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { detectChainCandidates } from '../src/chains.js';
-import { detectPollingFamilies } from '../src/polling.js';
-import { segmentEpisodes } from '../src/episodes.js';
-import { normalizeToolCall } from '../src/normalize.js';
-import type { ActionEvent, EventTable } from '../src/types.js';
-
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-const MIN = 60_000;
-const START = Date.parse('2026-06-01T09:00:00Z');
-
-let turnSeq = 0;
-const turns: string[] = [];
-
-function internTurn(text: string): number {
-  const index = turnSeq;
-  turns[index] = text;
-  turnSeq += 1;
-  return index;
-}
-
-function userEvent(session: string, project: string, text: string, epoch: number): ActionEvent {
-  return { kind: 'user', ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(), epoch, project, session, turn: internTurn(text) };
-}
-
-function toolEvent(
-  session: string,
-  project: string,
-  tool: string,
-  input: unknown,
-  epoch: number,
-  outcome: 'ok' | 'error' | 'unknown' = 'ok',
-): ActionEvent {
-  return { kind: 'tool', ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(), epoch, project, session, tool, template: normalizeToolCall(tool, input), outcome };
-}
-
-/**
- * Varied, genuinely one-off activity: five sessions, each a single episode
- * with its own distinct action trace (or none at all), no two of which share
- * a `template::outcome` sequence and none of which recurs anywhere else.
- */
-function distinctOneOffEvents(): ActionEvent[] {
-  const events: ActionEvent[] = [];
-
-  const a = START + 1 * DAY;
-  events.push(userEvent('sess-a', '/Users/me/repoA', 'Ship the build to staging', a));
-  events.push(toolEvent('sess-a', '/Users/me/repoA', 'Bash', { command: 'npm run build' }, a + 30_000));
-  events.push(toolEvent('sess-a', '/Users/me/repoA', 'Bash', { command: 'npm run deploy' }, a + 60_000));
-
-  const b = START + 2 * DAY;
-  events.push(userEvent('sess-b', '/Users/me/repoB', 'Update the API client from the docs', b));
-  events.push(toolEvent('sess-b', '/Users/me/repoB', 'WebFetch', { url: 'https://docs.example.com/api' }, b + 10_000));
-  events.push(toolEvent('sess-b', '/Users/me/repoB', 'Read', { file_path: '/Users/me/repoB/src/api.ts' }, b + 20_000));
-  events.push(toolEvent('sess-b', '/Users/me/repoB', 'Edit', { file_path: '/Users/me/repoB/src/api.ts' }, b + 30_000));
-
-  const c = START + 3 * DAY;
-  events.push(userEvent('sess-c', '/Users/me/repoC', 'Where does the retry loop live', c));
-  events.push(toolEvent('sess-c', '/Users/me/repoC', 'Grep', { pattern: 'retry' }, c + 5_000));
-  events.push(toolEvent('sess-c', '/Users/me/repoC', 'Read', { file_path: '/Users/me/repoC/src/retry.ts' }, c + 15_000));
-
-  const d = START + 4 * DAY;
-  events.push(userEvent('sess-d', '/Users/me/repoD', 'Show me what changed before I commit', d));
-  events.push(toolEvent('sess-d', '/Users/me/repoD', 'Bash', { command: 'git status' }, d + 5_000));
-  events.push(toolEvent('sess-d', '/Users/me/repoD', 'Bash', { command: 'git diff' }, d + 10_000));
-  events.push(toolEvent('sess-d', '/Users/me/repoD', 'Bash', { command: 'git add -A' }, d + 15_000));
-
-  const e = START + 5 * DAY;
-  events.push(userEvent('sess-e', '/Users/me/repoE', 'List the files in this directory', e));
-  events.push(toolEvent('sess-e', '/Users/me/repoE', 'Bash', { command: 'ls -la' }, e + 5_000));
-
-  return events;
-}
-
-/**
- * A three-step shape (failing test → read the log → fix) that recurs exactly
- * TWICE — one occurrence short of chains.ts's default floor of three.
- */
-function twiceRepeatedChainShapeEvents(): ActionEvent[] {
-  const events: ActionEvent[] = [];
-  for (const [session, day] of [['sess-f1', 6], ['sess-f2', 7]] as const) {
-    const project = `/Users/me/${session}`;
-    const ask = START + day * DAY;
-    events.push(userEvent(session, project, 'The test suite is red, figure out why', ask));
-    events.push(toolEvent(session, project, 'Bash', { command: 'npm test' }, ask + 5_000, 'error'));
-    events.push(toolEvent(session, project, 'Read', { file_path: `${project}/test.log` }, ask + 10_000));
-    events.push(toolEvent(session, project, 'Edit', { file_path: `${project}/src/index.ts` }, ask + 15_000));
-  }
-  return events;
-}
-
-/**
- * Two quick, trace-less asks three minutes apart in one session — one ask
- * short of polling.ts's default floor of three asks per burst.
- */
-function twiceAskedPollingNearMissEvents(): ActionEvent[] {
-  const session = 'sess-g';
-  const project = '/Users/me/repoG';
-  const first = START + 8 * DAY;
-  return [
-    userEvent(session, project, 'Any update on that build', first),
-    userEvent(session, project, 'Still going', first + 3 * MIN),
-  ];
-}
-
-function seededCorpus(): EventTable {
-  turnSeq = 0;
-  turns.length = 0;
-  const events = [
-    ...distinctOneOffEvents(),
-    ...twiceRepeatedChainShapeEvents(),
-    ...twiceAskedPollingNearMissEvents(),
-  ];
-  return { turns: [...turns], events };
-}
-
-describe('episode-layer negative control', () => {
-  it('a seeded corpus of varied one-off episodes yields zero chain candidates and zero polling families', async () => {
-    const table = seededCorpus();
-    const episodes = segmentEpisodes(table);
-
-    // Sanity: the corpus really did segment into the nine episodes seeded
-    // above — this is not an empty or malformed table producing a vacuous
-    // zero.
-    expect(episodes).toHaveLength(9);
-
-    expect(detectChainCandidates(episodes)).toEqual([]);
-    expect(await detectPollingFamilies(episodes)).toEqual([]);
-  });
-
-  it('the occurrence floor is what silences the chain shape — dropping it to two surfaces the repeated fix', () => {
-    const episodes = segmentEpisodes(seededCorpus());
-    const loosened = detectChainCandidates(episodes, { minOccurrences: 2 });
-
-    expect(loosened).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(loosened[0]?.occurrences).toBe(2);
-    expect(loosened[0]?.shape).toEqual(['Bash(npm test)::error', 'Read({path})::ok', 'Edit({path})::ok']);
-  });
-
-  it('the ask floor is what silences the polling burst — dropping it to two (with a matching span) surfaces it', async () => {
-    const episodes = segmentEpisodes(seededCorpus());
-    const loosened = await detectPollingFamilies(episodes, { minAsks: 2, minSpanMs: MIN });
-
-    expect(loosened).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(loosened[0]?.askCount).toBe(2);
-    expect(loosened[0]?.session).toBe('sess-g');
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/episodes.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/episodes.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index c38cbc3..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/episodes.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,140 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Episode segmentation (L0.5, docs/loop-hole-episodes-design.md) — the unit
- * the trace-mining layer clusters next. An episode is anchored at a user
- * turn and carries everything that happened before the next ask (or the
- * session's end): the ordered tool trace with outcomes, how long it took,
- * what it cost, and a resolution signal read off structure alone (no text
- * matching — that stays the reader tier's job).
- */
-
-import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
-import { ClaudeCodeAdapter } from '../src/claude-adapter.js';
-import { runExtract } from '../src/extract.js';
-import { StateStore } from '../src/store.js';
-import { segmentEpisodes, type Episode } from '../src/episodes.js';
-import { makeCorpus, toolCallRecord, toolResult, userTurn } from './helpers.js';
-
-const CWD = '/Users/me/repo';
-
-function actionFor(episode: Episode, needle: string) {
-  const action = episode.actions.find((a) => a.template.includes(needle));
-  if (action === undefined) throw new Error(`no action matching "${needle}" in episode at ${episode.ts}`);
-  return action;
-}
-
-describe('episode segmentation', () => {
-  it('a seeded session with three asks yields three episodes, each carrying its ordered action trace with outcomes, duration, token cost, and resolution signal', async () => {
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-episodes',
-        records: [
-          userTurn('run the release checklist', '2026-06-25T09:00:00.000Z', CWD),
-          toolCallRecord('msg-1', 'tool-1', 'Bash', { command: 'npm test' }, '2026-06-25T09:00:01.000Z', { input_tokens: 10, output_tokens: 5 }, CWD),
-          toolResult('tool-1', false, '2026-06-25T09:00:02.000Z', 'tests passed', CWD),
-          toolCallRecord('msg-2', 'tool-2', 'Bash', { command: 'npm publish' }, '2026-06-25T09:00:05.000Z', { input_tokens: 8, output_tokens: 4 }, CWD),
-          toolResult('tool-2', true, '2026-06-25T09:00:06.000Z', 'permission denied', CWD),
-
-          userTurn('check the deploy status', '2026-06-25T09:05:00.000Z', CWD),
-          toolCallRecord('msg-3', 'tool-3', 'Bash', { command: 'curl https://status.example/health' }, '2026-06-25T09:05:02.000Z', { input_tokens: 6, output_tokens: 2 }, CWD),
-          toolResult('tool-3', false, '2026-06-25T09:05:03.000Z', 'ok', CWD),
-
-          userTurn('write the release notes', '2026-06-25T09:10:00.000Z', CWD),
-          toolCallRecord('msg-4', 'tool-4', 'Bash', { command: 'git log -5' }, '2026-06-25T09:10:07.000Z', { input_tokens: 4, output_tokens: 1 }, CWD),
-          toolResult('tool-4', false, '2026-06-25T09:10:08.000Z', 'log output', CWD),
-          // session ends here — the third ask never gets a follow-up.
-        ],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-
-    const episodes = segmentEpisodes(store);
-    expect(episodes).toHaveLength(3);
-    const [first, second, third] = episodes;
-    if (first === undefined || second === undefined || third === undefined) throw new Error('expected three episodes');
-
-    // Episode 1: two-step trace, ordered, outcomes resolved, an error inside it.
-    expect(first.session).toBe('sess-episodes');
-    expect(first.ts).toBe('2026-06-25T09:00:00.000Z');
-    expect(first.actions.map((a) => a.outcome)).toEqual([
-      actionFor(first, 'npm test').outcome,
-      actionFor(first, 'npm publish').outcome,
-    ]);
-    expect(actionFor(first, 'npm test').outcome).toBe('ok');
-    expect(actionFor(first, 'npm publish').outcome).toBe('error');
-    // Order preserved: npm test ran before npm publish.
-    expect(first.actions[0]?.template).toContain('npm test');
-    expect(first.actions[1]?.template).toContain('npm publish');
-    expect(first.durationMs).toBe(Date.parse('2026-06-25T09:00:05.000Z') - Date.parse('2026-06-25T09:00:00.000Z'));
-    expect(first.tokens).toEqual({ input: 10 + 8, output: 5 + 4 });
-    // A follow-up ask arrived, and this episode's trace contains an error —
-    // presumed a fix-forward rather than an unrelated new request.
-    expect(first.resolution).toBe('unresolved');
-
-    // Episode 2: single-step, clean trace, followed by another ask.
-    expect(second.actions).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(actionFor(second, 'curl').outcome).toBe('ok');
-    expect(second.durationMs).toBe(Date.parse('2026-06-25T09:05:02.000Z') - Date.parse('2026-06-25T09:05:00.000Z'));
-    expect(second.tokens).toEqual({ input: 6, output: 2 });
-    expect(second.resolution).toBe('resolved');
-
-    // Episode 3: last ask in the session — nothing followed it.
-    expect(third.actions).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(actionFor(third, 'git log').outcome).toBe('ok');
-    expect(third.durationMs).toBe(Date.parse('2026-06-25T09:10:07.000Z') - Date.parse('2026-06-25T09:10:00.000Z'));
-    expect(third.tokens).toEqual({ input: 4, output: 1 });
-    expect(third.resolution).toBe('abandoned');
-  });
-});
-
-describe('episode segmentation: negative controls', () => {
-  it('an ask with no follow-up tool calls is still one episode, not zero — an empty trace is a real episode', async () => {
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-quiet',
-        records: [userTurn('is the pipeline still running', '2026-06-26T09:00:00.000Z', CWD)],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-    const episodes = segmentEpisodes(store);
-
-    expect(episodes).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(episodes[0]?.actions).toEqual([]);
-    expect(episodes[0]?.durationMs).toBe(0);
-    expect(episodes[0]?.tokens).toEqual({ input: 0, output: 0 });
-    expect(episodes[0]?.resolution).toBe('abandoned');
-  });
-
-  it('two sessions sharing the exact same ask text never merge into one episode', async () => {
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repoA',
-        sessionId: 'sess-x',
-        records: [
-          userTurn('restart the worker', '2026-06-27T09:00:00.000Z', '/Users/me/repoA'),
-          toolCallRecord('msg-x', 'tool-x', 'Bash', { command: 'systemctl restart worker' }, '2026-06-27T09:00:01.000Z', { input_tokens: 3, output_tokens: 1 }, '/Users/me/repoA'),
-          toolResult('tool-x', false, '2026-06-27T09:00:02.000Z', 'restarted', '/Users/me/repoA'),
-        ],
-      },
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repoB',
-        sessionId: 'sess-y',
-        records: [userTurn('restart the worker', '2026-06-27T10:00:00.000Z', '/Users/me/repoB')],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-    const episodes = segmentEpisodes(store);
-
-    expect(episodes).toHaveLength(2);
-    const bySession = new Map(episodes.map((e) => [e.session, e]));
-    expect(bySession.get('sess-x')?.actions).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(bySession.get('sess-y')?.actions).toHaveLength(0);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/extract-v2.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/extract-v2.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 8d134c6..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/extract-v2.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,133 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * v2 outcomes: a tool event's outcome (ok/error/unknown) resolves against the
- * transcript's tool_result records, and a user turn's token cost is aggregated
- * from the assistant usage records between it and the next turn — deduped by
- * assistant message id, since one completion repeats its usage across every
- * JSONL line it spans. Both derive from a single forward pass over the
- * session; nothing is buffered by re-reading it.
- */
-
-import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
-import { ClaudeCodeAdapter } from '../src/claude-adapter.js';
-import { runExtract } from '../src/extract.js';
-import { StateStore } from '../src/store.js';
-import type { ActionEvent, CorpusAdapter, SessionSource } from '../src/types.js';
-import { makeCorpus, toolCallRecord, assistantText, toolResult, userTurn } from './helpers.js';
-
-const CWD = '/Users/me/repo';
-
-function toolEventFor(store: StateStore, needle: string) {
-  const event = store.events.find((e): e is Extract<ActionEvent, { kind: 'tool' }> => e.kind === 'tool' && e.template.includes(needle));
-  if (event === undefined) throw new Error(`no tool event matching "${needle}"`);
-  return event;
-}
-
-function userEvents(store: StateStore) {
-  return store.events.filter((e): e is Extract<ActionEvent, { kind: 'user' }> => e.kind === 'user');
-}
-
-describe('extract v2: tool outcome derivation', () => {
-  it('resolves ok/error from the matching tool_result, and unknown when none ever arrives', async () => {
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-outcome',
-        records: [
-          userTurn('run the release checklist', '2026-06-20T10:00:00.000Z', CWD),
-          toolCallRecord('msg-1', 'tool-ok', 'Bash', { command: 'npm test' }, '2026-06-20T10:00:01.000Z', { input_tokens: 10, output_tokens: 5 }, CWD),
-          toolResult('tool-ok', false, '2026-06-20T10:00:02.000Z', 'tests passed', CWD),
-          toolCallRecord('msg-2', 'tool-err', 'Bash', { command: 'npm publish' }, '2026-06-20T10:00:03.000Z', { input_tokens: 8, output_tokens: 4 }, CWD),
-          toolResult('tool-err', true, '2026-06-20T10:00:04.000Z', 'permission denied', CWD),
-          toolCallRecord('msg-3', 'tool-unresolved', 'Bash', { command: 'sleep 5' }, '2026-06-20T10:00:05.000Z', { input_tokens: 2, output_tokens: 1 }, CWD),
-          // session ends here — no result ever arrives for tool-unresolved
-        ],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-
-    expect(toolEventFor(store, 'npm test').outcome).toBe('ok');
-    expect(toolEventFor(store, 'npm publish').outcome).toBe('error');
-    expect(toolEventFor(store, 'sleep').outcome).toBe('unknown');
-  });
-});
-
-describe('extract v2: per-turn token aggregation', () => {
-  it('sums usage once per distinct assistant message id, and resets at the next turn', async () => {
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-tokens',
-        records: [
-          userTurn('summarize open PRs', '2026-06-21T09:00:00.000Z', CWD),
-          // msg-a spans two JSONL lines (text then tool_use); its usage repeats
-          // verbatim on both — must be counted once, not twice.
-          assistantText('msg-a', 'Checking...', '2026-06-21T09:00:01.000Z', { input_tokens: 100, output_tokens: 20, cache_read_input_tokens: 30 }, CWD),
-          toolCallRecord('msg-a', 'tool-a', 'Bash', { command: 'gh pr list' }, '2026-06-21T09:00:01.000Z', { input_tokens: 100, output_tokens: 20, cache_read_input_tokens: 30 }, CWD),
-          toolResult('tool-a', false, '2026-06-21T09:00:02.000Z', 'PR #1, PR #2', CWD),
-          // msg-b is a second, distinct completion closing out the same turn.
-          assistantText('msg-b', 'Here is the summary.', '2026-06-21T09:00:03.000Z', { input_tokens: 40, output_tokens: 60 }, CWD),
-
-          // A second, independent turn — its usage must not leak into the first.
-          userTurn('now check CI status', '2026-06-21T09:05:00.000Z', CWD),
-          assistantText('msg-c', 'CI is green.', '2026-06-21T09:05:01.000Z', { input_tokens: 5, output_tokens: 5 }, CWD),
-        ],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-
-    const [first, second] = userEvents(store);
-    expect(userEvents(store)).toHaveLength(2);
-    if (first === undefined || second === undefined) throw new Error('expected two user events');
-
-    // msg-a's usage counted once (input_tokens 100 + cache_read 30) + msg-b's (40).
-    expect(first.tokens).toEqual({ input: 100 + 30 + 40, output: 20 + 60 });
-    expect(second.tokens).toEqual({ input: 5, output: 5 });
-  });
-});
-
-describe('extract v2: streaming preserved, never slurped', () => {
-  it('reads each session with exactly one pass over its lines', async () => {
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-stream',
-        records: [
-          userTurn('ship it', '2026-06-22T09:00:00.000Z', CWD),
-          toolCallRecord('msg-1', 'tool-1', 'Bash', { command: 'git push' }, '2026-06-22T09:00:01.000Z', { input_tokens: 1, output_tokens: 1 }, CWD),
-          toolResult('tool-1', false, '2026-06-22T09:00:02.000Z', 'done', CWD),
-        ],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    let linesCallCount = 0;
-    const real = new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root);
-    const countingAdapter: CorpusAdapter = {
-      name: real.name,
-      async *sessions(): AsyncIterable<SessionSource> {
-        for await (const session of real.sessions() as AsyncIterable<SessionSource> | Iterable<SessionSource>) {
-          const originalLines = session.lines.bind(session);
-          yield {
-            ...session,
-            lines: () => {
-              linesCallCount += 1;
-              return originalLines();
-            },
-          };
-        }
-      },
-    };
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    const result = await runExtract(countingAdapter, store);
-
-    expect(result.newSessions).toBe(1);
-    // Resolving outcomes/tokens must not cost a second pass over the same file
-    // — a real filesystem stream can't be rewound, so a second call would mean
-    // the whole session got buffered somewhere to look ahead.
-    expect(linesCallCount).toBe(1);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/extract.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/extract.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index ff098b8..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/extract.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,48 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * The end-to-end check: point the real Claude Code adapter at this machine's
- * session logs and extract them. It must run without throwing (the corpus has
- * malformed lines and every record shape the CLI has ever emitted), produce a
- * non-empty event table, and every timestamp must be sane.
- */
-
-import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
-import { ClaudeCodeAdapter, defaultClaudeProjectsDir } from '../src/claude-adapter.js';
-import { runExtract } from '../src/extract.js';
-import { StateStore } from '../src/store.js';
-import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
-
-const MIN_EPOCH = Date.parse('2020-01-01T00:00:00Z');
-
-describe('extract over the real corpus', () => {
-  const corpusPresent = existsSync(defaultClaudeProjectsDir());
-
-  it.runIf(corpusPresent)('yields a non-empty event table with sane timestamps', async () => {
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    const result = await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(), store);
-
-    expect(result.newSessions).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-    expect(store.events.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-    expect(result.totalEvents).toBe(store.events.length);
-
-    // Both channels the layer exists to capture are present.
-    const kinds = new Set(store.events.map((e) => e.kind));
-    expect(kinds.has('tool')).toBe(true);
-    expect(kinds.has('user')).toBe(true);
-
-    // Every event carries a sane timestamp and a resolvable payload.
-    const now = Date.now();
-    for (const event of store.events) {
-      expect(Number.isFinite(event.epoch)).toBe(true);
-      expect(event.epoch).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(MIN_EPOCH);
-      expect(event.epoch).toBeLessThanOrEqual(now + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
-      expect(Date.parse(event.ts)).toBe(event.epoch);
-      expect(event.project.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-      if (event.kind === 'tool') {
-        expect(event.template.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-        expect(event.tool.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-      } else {
-        expect(store.turns[event.turn]).toBeTypeOf('string');
-      }
-    }
-  }, 300_000);
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-cache.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-cache.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 9038af0..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-cache.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,172 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Facet cache incrementality — the property that makes comprehension
- * O(new sessions per night) instead of O(corpus): a second pass over an
- * unchanged corpus must perform ZERO reader calls, the cache must be
- * complete (one entry per session id), and a session whose digest actually
- * changes must be the ONLY one re-read.
- */
-
-import { mkdirSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
-import { ClaudeCodeAdapter } from '../src/claude-adapter.js';
-import { runExtract } from '../src/extract.js';
-import { StateStore } from '../src/store.js';
-import { buildSessionDigests } from '../src/digest.js';
-import { buildFacetCache, type FacetReader } from '../src/facets.js';
-import { makeCorpus, appendRecord, userTurn, toolCall } from './helpers.js';
-
-function stubReader(calls: string[]): FacetReader {
-  return (prompt) => {
-    calls.push(prompt);
-    return JSON.stringify([
-      { category: 'instruction', summary: 'a recurring ask', quotes: ['run the release checklist'] },
-    ]);
-  };
-}
-
-describe('facet cache incrementality', () => {
-  it('performs zero reader calls on a second pass over an unchanged corpus, cache keyed by session id', async () => {
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repoA',
-        sessionId: 'sess-a',
-        records: [
-          userTurn('run the release checklist', '2026-06-01T10:00:00.000Z'),
-          toolCall('Bash', { command: 'npm test' }, '2026-06-01T10:00:01.000Z'),
-        ],
-      },
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repoB',
-        sessionId: 'sess-b',
-        records: [userTurn('check the deploy status', '2026-06-02T09:00:00.000Z')],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-    const digests = buildSessionDigests(store);
-    expect(digests).toHaveLength(2);
-
-    const calls: string[] = [];
-    const reader = stubReader(calls);
-
-    const first = buildFacetCache(digests, {}, reader);
-    expect(first.readerCalls).toBe(2);
-    expect(calls).toHaveLength(2);
-    expect(Object.keys(first.cache).sort()).toEqual(['sess-a', 'sess-b']);
-    for (const session of Object.values(first.cache)) {
-      expect(session.findings).toHaveLength(1);
-    }
-
-    // A second pipeline run: same corpus, same digests, cache seeded from the first pass.
-    const second = buildFacetCache(digests, first.cache, reader);
-    expect(second.readerCalls).toBe(0);
-    expect(calls).toHaveLength(2); // no new prompts sent
-    expect(second.cache).toEqual(first.cache);
-  });
-
-  it('re-reads only the session whose digest actually changed', async () => {
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repoC',
-        sessionId: 'sess-stable',
-        records: [userTurn('summarize the changelog', '2026-06-03T10:00:00.000Z')],
-      },
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repoD',
-        sessionId: 'sess-resumed',
-        records: [userTurn('start the migration', '2026-06-04T10:00:00.000Z')],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-    const digestsBefore = buildSessionDigests(store);
-
-    const calls: string[] = [];
-    const reader = stubReader(calls);
-    const first = buildFacetCache(digestsBefore, {}, reader);
-    expect(first.readerCalls).toBe(2);
-
-    // Only sess-resumed changes — a genuine resume, new episode appended.
-    appendRecord(root, '-Users-me-repoD', 'sess-resumed', userTurn('finish the migration', '2026-06-04T11:00:00.000Z'));
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-    const digestsAfter = buildSessionDigests(store);
-
-    const second = buildFacetCache(digestsAfter, first.cache, reader);
-    expect(second.readerCalls).toBe(1);
-    expect(calls).toHaveLength(3); // 2 from the first pass + exactly 1 new one
-
-    // The untouched session's cache entry is carried over byte-identical.
-    expect(second.cache['sess-stable']).toEqual(first.cache['sess-stable']);
-    // The changed session's entry was actually replaced (new hash).
-    expect(second.cache['sess-resumed']?.digestHash).not.toBe(first.cache['sess-resumed']?.digestHash);
-  });
-
-  it('a brand-new session added to the corpus costs exactly one reader call; prior sessions stay cached', async () => {
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repoE',
-        sessionId: 'sess-one',
-        records: [userTurn('list open pull requests', '2026-06-05T10:00:00.000Z')],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-    const digestsBefore = buildSessionDigests(store);
-
-    const calls: string[] = [];
-    const reader = stubReader(calls);
-    const first = buildFacetCache(digestsBefore, {}, reader);
-    expect(first.readerCalls).toBe(1);
-
-    const root2dir = '-Users-me-repoF';
-    mkdirSync(join(root, root2dir), { recursive: true });
-    appendRecord(root, root2dir, 'sess-two', userTurn('draft the release notes', '2026-06-06T10:00:00.000Z'));
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-    const digestsAfter = buildSessionDigests(store);
-    expect(digestsAfter).toHaveLength(2);
-
-    const second = buildFacetCache(digestsAfter, first.cache, reader);
-    expect(second.readerCalls).toBe(1);
-    expect(calls).toHaveLength(2);
-    expect(second.cache['sess-one']).toEqual(first.cache['sess-one']);
-    expect(second.cache['sess-two']).toBeDefined();
-  });
-
-  it('a refused reading (malformed output) is not cached, so the next pass retries it instead of poisoning the cache', async () => {
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repoG',
-        sessionId: 'sess-flaky',
-        records: [userTurn('rebase onto main', '2026-06-07T10:00:00.000Z')],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-    const digests = buildSessionDigests(store);
-
-    // Fails twice — the initial call AND its one SYN-N2 repair re-ask — so
-    // the first pass genuinely exhausts its repair budget and still refuses;
-    // only a THIRD call (the next pass) succeeds.
-    let attempt = 0;
-    const flakyReader: FacetReader = () => {
-      attempt += 1;
-      return attempt <= 2 ? 'not json at all' : JSON.stringify([
-        { category: 'workflow-pattern', summary: 'rebase before pushing', quotes: ['rebase onto main'] },
-      ]);
-    };
-
-    const first = buildFacetCache(digests, {}, flakyReader);
-    expect(first.readerCalls).toBe(2); // initial call + one repair re-ask, both refused
-    expect(first.cache['sess-flaky']).toBeUndefined(); // refused, not stamped
-
-    const second = buildFacetCache(digests, first.cache, flakyReader);
-    expect(second.readerCalls).toBe(1); // retried, since nothing was cached; succeeds on the first call
-    expect(second.cache['sess-flaky']).toBeDefined();
-    expect(attempt).toBe(3);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-checkpoint.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-checkpoint.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 41204be..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-checkpoint.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,124 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Backfill checkpointing (SNAG-18, root cause 2): the run-end-only cache save
- * meant a backfill killed mid-pass lost every reader call it had already paid
- * for. The facet cache must hit disk after EVERY completed session read, not
- * only once the whole pass finishes — so an interruption anywhere in a long
- * backfill loses zero paid reads, and the next pass resumes from exactly
- * where it stopped instead of re-reading sessions already answered.
- */
-
-import { existsSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { runPipeline } from '../src/run.js';
-import { FacetStore } from '../src/facet-store.js';
-import type { FacetReader } from '../src/facets.js';
-import { makeCorpus, userTurn } from './helpers.js';
-
-function outDir(): string {
-  return mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-facet-checkpoint-'));
-}
-
-function threeSessionCorpus(): string {
-  return makeCorpus([
-    {
-      dir: '-Users-me-repoA',
-      sessionId: 'sess-1',
-      records: [
-        userTurn('first real ask', '2026-06-01T10:00:00.000Z'),
-        userTurn('first follow-up', '2026-06-01T10:05:00.000Z'),
-      ],
-    },
-    {
-      dir: '-Users-me-repoB',
-      sessionId: 'sess-2',
-      records: [
-        userTurn('second real ask', '2026-06-02T10:00:00.000Z'),
-        userTurn('second follow-up', '2026-06-02T10:05:00.000Z'),
-      ],
-    },
-    {
-      dir: '-Users-me-repoC',
-      sessionId: 'sess-3',
-      records: [
-        userTurn('third real ask', '2026-06-03T10:00:00.000Z'),
-        userTurn('third follow-up', '2026-06-03T10:05:00.000Z'),
-      ],
-    },
-  ]);
-}
-
-describe('backfill checkpointing', () => {
-  it('persists the facet cache to disk after EVERY completed session read, not only at the end of the pass', async () => {
-    const corpus = threeSessionCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-    const facetStorePath = join(out, 'loop-hole.facets.json');
-
-    // Records how many sessions were already ON DISK the instant each reader
-    // call fires — the old (buggy) behavior only ever writes once, at the
-    // very end of the pass, so every call would see 0 no matter how many
-    // prior sessions had already finished reading.
-    const onDiskBeforeEachCall: number[] = [];
-    let calls = 0;
-    const reader: FacetReader = () => {
-      onDiskBeforeEachCall.push(existsSync(facetStorePath) ? FacetStore.load(facetStorePath).size : 0);
-      calls += 1;
-      const quote = calls === 1 ? 'first real ask' : calls === 2 ? 'second real ask' : 'third real ask';
-      return JSON.stringify([{ category: 'instruction', summary: `finding ${calls}`, quotes: [quote] }]);
-    };
-
-    await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out, facetReader: reader, minUserTurns: 2 });
-
-    expect(calls).toBe(3);
-    expect(onDiskBeforeEachCall).toEqual([0, 1, 2]);
-
-    const finalStore = FacetStore.load(facetStorePath);
-    expect(finalStore.size).toBe(3);
-  });
-
-  it('an interrupted backfill loses zero paid reads — the next pass resumes exactly where it stopped', async () => {
-    const corpus = threeSessionCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-    const facetStorePath = join(out, 'loop-hole.facets.json');
-
-    // Sessions sort sess-1 < sess-2 < sess-3; the third call stands in for
-    // the process dying mid-backfill (a network drop, an OOM kill, ctrl-c) —
-    // the pipeline call itself still returns (a refused session is handled),
-    // but the two sessions that DID finish reading before it must already be
-    // safely checkpointed, not lost because the pass never reached the end.
-    let callCount = 0;
-    const crashingReader: FacetReader = () => {
-      callCount += 1;
-      if (callCount === 3) throw new Error('simulated process interruption');
-      const quote = callCount === 1 ? 'first real ask' : 'second real ask';
-      return JSON.stringify([{ category: 'instruction', summary: `finding ${callCount}`, quotes: [quote] }]);
-    };
-
-    const first = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out, facetReader: crashingReader, minUserTurns: 2 });
-    expect(first.facets.refusedSessions).toBe(1);
-
-    const afterCrash = FacetStore.load(facetStorePath);
-    expect(afterCrash.get('sess-1')).toBeDefined();
-    expect(afterCrash.get('sess-2')).toBeDefined();
-    expect(afterCrash.get('sess-3')).toBeUndefined();
-
-    // Resume with a healthy reader — it must be invoked exactly once, for
-    // sess-3 only; sess-1 and sess-2 are not re-read (zero reads lost).
-    const resumeCalls: string[] = [];
-    const resumeReader: FacetReader = (prompt) => {
-      resumeCalls.push(prompt);
-      return JSON.stringify([{ category: 'instruction', summary: 'finding 3', quotes: ['third real ask'] }]);
-    };
-    const second = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out, facetReader: resumeReader, minUserTurns: 2 });
-
-    expect(resumeCalls).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(second.facets.readerCalls).toBe(1);
-
-    const finalStore = FacetStore.load(facetStorePath);
-    expect(finalStore.size).toBe(3);
-    expect(finalStore.get('sess-1')).toEqual(afterCrash.get('sess-1'));
-    expect(finalStore.get('sess-2')).toEqual(afterCrash.get('sess-2'));
-    expect(finalStore.get('sess-3')).toBeDefined();
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-chunking.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-chunking.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 4673bb1..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-chunking.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,230 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Oversized sessions: long-context recall degrades 15-30% from 4K->128K with
- * a lost-in-the-middle dip even on frontier models, so a digest whose full
- * prompt would overflow the reader's window is never handed over flat. It is
- * split into reader-sized chunks, each carrying the COMPLETE data-not-a-task
- * framing and already-encoded block (not just the first chunk), and every
- * chunk's findings are merged into that session's one cache entry — never a
- * single oversized prompt, never a partial reading silently treated as done
- * (design: docs/loop-hole-episodes-design.md, v3, "digest chunking rule").
- */
-
-import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
-import type { EpisodeDigest, SessionDigest } from '../src/digest.js';
-import {
-  buildFacetCache,
-  buildFacetPrompt,
-  chunkDigestForReading,
-  estimatePromptTokens,
-  hashDigest,
-  READER_WINDOW_TOKENS,
-  type AlreadyEncoded,
-  type FacetReader,
-} from '../src/facets.js';
-
-const FRAMING_OPENER = 'THIS IS DATA, NOT A TASK.';
-
-function digestOf(session: string, episodes: EpisodeDigest[]): SessionDigest {
-  return { session, project: '/Users/me/repo', date: '2026-07-01T00:00:00.000Z', episodes };
-}
-
-function bigEpisode(marker: number): EpisodeDigest {
-  return { intent: `${'a'.repeat(4000)} marker-${marker}`, actions: [] };
-}
-
-describe('chunkDigestForReading — small digests pass through untouched', () => {
-  it('returns exactly one chunk, the digest itself, when the whole prompt already fits the window', () => {
-    const d = digestOf('sess-small', [{ intent: 'a short ask', actions: [] }]);
-    const chunks = chunkDigestForReading(d, undefined, READER_WINDOW_TOKENS);
-    expect(chunks).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(chunks[0]).toEqual(d);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('chunkDigestForReading — oversized digests are split, never one flat oversized prompt', () => {
-  it('splits into more than one chunk once the whole prompt exceeds the window', () => {
-    const first = bigEpisode(1);
-    const episodes = [first, bigEpisode(2), bigEpisode(3)];
-    const d = digestOf('sess-big', episodes);
-    const oneEpisodeTokens = estimatePromptTokens(buildFacetPrompt(digestOf('sess-big', [first])));
-    const maxTokens = oneEpisodeTokens + 20; // room for one episode's worth, not two
-
-    const chunks = chunkDigestForReading(d, undefined, maxTokens);
-
-    expect(chunks.length).toBeGreaterThan(1);
-  });
-
-  it('no individual chunk prompt exceeds the budget it was chunked against', () => {
-    const first = bigEpisode(1);
-    const episodes = [first, bigEpisode(2), bigEpisode(3)];
-    const d = digestOf('sess-big', episodes);
-    const oneEpisodeTokens = estimatePromptTokens(buildFacetPrompt(digestOf('sess-big', [first])));
-    const maxTokens = oneEpisodeTokens + 20;
-
-    const chunks = chunkDigestForReading(d, undefined, maxTokens);
-
-    for (const chunk of chunks) {
-      expect(estimatePromptTokens(buildFacetPrompt(chunk, undefined))).toBeLessThanOrEqual(maxTokens);
-    }
-  });
-
-  it('front-loads the full data-not-a-task framing and already-encoded block into EVERY chunk, not only the first', () => {
-    const first = bigEpisode(1);
-    const episodes = [first, bigEpisode(2), bigEpisode(3)];
-    const d = digestOf('sess-big', episodes);
-    const encoded: AlreadyEncoded = { instructions: ['never fix symptoms, fix root causes'], skills: [], loops: [] };
-    const oneEpisodeTokens = estimatePromptTokens(buildFacetPrompt(digestOf('sess-big', [first]), encoded));
-    const maxTokens = oneEpisodeTokens + 20;
-
-    const chunks = chunkDigestForReading(d, encoded, maxTokens);
-
-    expect(chunks.length).toBeGreaterThan(1);
-    for (const chunk of chunks) {
-      const prompt = buildFacetPrompt(chunk, encoded);
-      expect(prompt.startsWith(FRAMING_OPENER)).toBe(true);
-      expect(prompt).toContain('never fix symptoms, fix root causes');
-    }
-  });
-
-  it('never drops or duplicates episodes — concatenating every chunk reconstructs the original order exactly', () => {
-    const first = bigEpisode(1);
-    const episodes = [first, bigEpisode(2), bigEpisode(3), bigEpisode(4)];
-    const d = digestOf('sess-big', episodes);
-    const oneEpisodeTokens = estimatePromptTokens(buildFacetPrompt(digestOf('sess-big', [first])));
-
-    const chunks = chunkDigestForReading(d, undefined, oneEpisodeTokens + 20);
-
-    expect(chunks.flatMap((chunk) => chunk.episodes)).toEqual(episodes);
-  });
-
-  it('keeps session, project, and date identical across every chunk — only episodes are sliced', () => {
-    const first = bigEpisode(1);
-    const episodes = [first, bigEpisode(2), bigEpisode(3)];
-    const d = digestOf('sess-big', episodes);
-    const oneEpisodeTokens = estimatePromptTokens(buildFacetPrompt(digestOf('sess-big', [first])));
-
-    const chunks = chunkDigestForReading(d, undefined, oneEpisodeTokens + 20);
-
-    expect(chunks.length).toBeGreaterThan(1);
-    for (const chunk of chunks) {
-      expect(chunk.session).toBe(d.session);
-      expect(chunk.project).toBe(d.project);
-      expect(chunk.date).toBe(d.date);
-    }
-  });
-
-  it('a single episode that alone exceeds the budget still gets its own chunk rather than being dropped or split mid-episode', () => {
-    const huge: EpisodeDigest = { intent: 'z'.repeat(20000), actions: [] };
-    const d = digestOf('sess-huge', [huge]);
-
-    const chunks = chunkDigestForReading(d, undefined, 10); // an absurdly small budget
-
-    expect(chunks).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(chunks[0]?.episodes).toEqual([huge]);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('buildFacetCache — chunks an oversized session and merges findings across all its chunks', () => {
-  it('calls the reader once per chunk, merges every chunk\'s findings under one entry keyed by the whole-digest hash', () => {
-    const episodes = Array.from({ length: 40 }, (_, i) => ({ intent: `${'p'.repeat(4000)} ep-${i}`, actions: [] }));
-    const digest: SessionDigest = {
-      session: 'sess-oversized',
-      project: '/Users/me/repo',
-      date: '2026-07-01T00:00:00.000Z',
-      episodes,
-    };
-    expect(estimatePromptTokens(buildFacetPrompt(digest))).toBeGreaterThan(READER_WINDOW_TOKENS);
-
-    const prompts: string[] = [];
-    let call = 0;
-    const reader: FacetReader = (prompt) => {
-      prompts.push(prompt);
-      call += 1;
-      return JSON.stringify([{ category: 'friction', summary: `finding from chunk ${call}`, quotes: [] }]);
-    };
-
-    const result = buildFacetCache([digest], {}, reader);
-
-    // Never one flat oversized prompt: more than one call, and every call individually fits the window.
-    expect(result.readerCalls).toBeGreaterThan(1);
-    expect(prompts).toHaveLength(result.readerCalls);
-    for (const prompt of prompts) {
-      expect(prompt.startsWith(FRAMING_OPENER)).toBe(true);
-      expect(estimatePromptTokens(prompt)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(READER_WINDOW_TOKENS);
-    }
-
-    const entry = result.cache['sess-oversized'];
-    expect(entry).toBeDefined();
-    expect(entry?.digestHash).toBe(hashDigest(digest)); // hashed whole, unchunked — independent of the slicing
-    expect(entry?.findings).toHaveLength(result.readerCalls); // one finding per chunk, all merged in
-  });
-
-  // SYN-N4 (synN3-finding.md): the old all-or-nothing chunk merge made
-  // refusal probability COMPOUND with chunk count (one bad chunk among nine
-  // refused the whole mega-session, even though every OTHER chunk read
-  // cleanly). Per-chunk salvage replaces "one bad chunk drops everything"
-  // with "one bad chunk is dropped and counted; its siblings still cache".
-  it('a chunk that stays unreadable after its one SYN-N2 repair re-ask is dropped and counted — its siblings still cache', () => {
-    const episodes = Array.from({ length: 40 }, (_, i) => ({ intent: `${'q'.repeat(4000)} ep-${i}`, actions: [] }));
-    const digest: SessionDigest = {
-      session: 'sess-partial-fail',
-      project: '/Users/me/repo',
-      date: '2026-07-01T00:00:00.000Z',
-      episodes,
-    };
-
-    // The 2nd chunk's failure AND its one SYN-N2 repair re-ask (call 3) both
-    // fail, so the session's repair budget is genuinely exhausted rather
-    // than the transient blip recovering — every OTHER chunk reads cleanly.
-    let call = 0;
-    const reader: FacetReader = () => {
-      call += 1;
-      if (call === 2 || call === 3) return 'not json at all';
-      return JSON.stringify([{ category: 'friction', summary: 'ok finding', quotes: [] }]);
-    };
-
-    const result = buildFacetCache([digest], {}, reader);
-
-    expect(call).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3); // more than one chunk was attempted, plus the repair re-ask
-    const entry = result.cache['sess-partial-fail'];
-    expect(entry).toBeDefined(); // salvaged, not dropped whole
-    expect(entry?.droppedChunks).toBe(1);
-    expect(entry?.findings.length).toBeGreaterThan(0); // the surviving chunks' findings still cache
-    expect(result.partialSessions).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.droppedChunks).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.refusedSessions).toBe(0);
-  });
-
-  it('when EVERY chunk fails, the session still refuses outright — nothing survives it to cache', () => {
-    const episodes = Array.from({ length: 40 }, (_, i) => ({ intent: `${'w'.repeat(4000)} ep-${i}`, actions: [] }));
-    const digest: SessionDigest = {
-      session: 'sess-total-fail',
-      project: '/Users/me/repo',
-      date: '2026-07-01T00:00:00.000Z',
-      episodes,
-    };
-
-    const reader: FacetReader = () => 'not json at all, ever';
-
-    const result = buildFacetCache([digest], {}, reader);
-
-    expect(result.cache['sess-total-fail']).toBeUndefined();
-    expect(result.refusedSessions).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.partialSessions).toBe(0);
-  });
-
-  it('a digest that fits the window in one piece is unaffected — still exactly one reader call', () => {
-    const digest = digestOf('sess-small', [{ intent: 'a short ask', actions: [] }]);
-    let calls = 0;
-    const reader: FacetReader = () => {
-      calls += 1;
-      return '[]';
-    };
-
-    const result = buildFacetCache([digest], {}, reader);
-
-    expect(calls).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.readerCalls).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.cache['sess-small']).toBeDefined();
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-negctrl.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-negctrl.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index a51c97c..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-negctrl.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,127 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * The negative control for the comprehension tier: a corpus of one-off,
- * preference-free sessions — every ask distinct, nothing stated twice, no
- * "always"/"never"/quality-bar language anywhere — must yield zero
- * recurring-instruction findings. This is the facets analogue of
- * tests/negctrl.spec.ts's guard against the whole product's failure mode: a
- * reader that "finds" standing instructions in ordinary one-off chatter
- * manufactures busywork instead of surfacing real signal.
- *
- * Unlike the cluster negative control, the facets tier has no deterministic
- * algorithm to interrogate — the reader is a model seam (`FacetReader`,
- * per facets.ts). So the zero has to be proven at the layer that IS
- * deterministic: the quote-honesty gate (`enforceQuoteHonesty`). A well-
- * behaved reader answering `[]` is not proof of anything — it is the second
- * test here that does the real work, feeding an over-eager reader that tries
- * to invent a recurring-instruction finding on this exact one-off corpus,
- * and showing the gate silences it because no such text ever appears
- * verbatim in any session. The third test proves that silence is the gate
- * doing its job, not blanket suppression: the identical reader, grounding
- * its finding in a real quote from the corpus, survives.
- */
-
-import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
-import { ClaudeCodeAdapter } from '../src/claude-adapter.js';
-import { runExtract } from '../src/extract.js';
-import { StateStore } from '../src/store.js';
-import { buildSessionDigests } from '../src/digest.js';
-import { buildFacetCache, enforceQuoteHonesty, type FacetReader } from '../src/facets.js';
-import { makeCorpus, userTurn } from './helpers.js';
-
-/** Five sessions, five unrelated one-off asks — nothing recurs, nothing
- * reads as a standing preference or quality bar. */
-const ONE_OFF_SESSIONS = [
-  { dir: '-Users-me-repoA', sessionId: 'sess-status', intent: 'check the deploy status', ts: '2026-06-10T09:00:00.000Z' },
-  { dir: '-Users-me-repoB', sessionId: 'sess-prs', intent: 'list open pull requests', ts: '2026-06-11T09:00:00.000Z' },
-  { dir: '-Users-me-repoC', sessionId: 'sess-parser', intent: 'explain how the parser handles block comments', ts: '2026-06-12T09:00:00.000Z' },
-  { dir: '-Users-me-repoD', sessionId: 'sess-rebase', intent: 'rebase this branch onto main', ts: '2026-06-13T09:00:00.000Z' },
-  { dir: '-Users-me-repoE', sessionId: 'sess-async', intent: 'convert this callback into an async function', ts: '2026-06-14T09:00:00.000Z' },
-] as const;
-
-async function buildOneOffDigests() {
-  const root = makeCorpus(
-    ONE_OFF_SESSIONS.map((s) => ({
-      dir: s.dir,
-      sessionId: s.sessionId,
-      records: [userTurn(s.intent, s.ts, `/Users/me/${s.dir.slice(1)}`)],
-    })),
-  );
-  const store = StateStore.empty();
-  await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-  return buildSessionDigests(store);
-}
-
-describe('facet negative control', () => {
-  it('a corpus of one-off, preference-free sessions yields zero findings from a compliant reader', async () => {
-    const digests = await buildOneOffDigests();
-    expect(digests).toHaveLength(ONE_OFF_SESSIONS.length);
-
-    const reader: FacetReader = () => '[]';
-    const { cache, readerCalls } = buildFacetCache(digests, {}, reader);
-
-    expect(readerCalls).toBe(ONE_OFF_SESSIONS.length);
-    expect(Object.keys(cache)).toHaveLength(ONE_OFF_SESSIONS.length);
-    const totalFindings = Object.values(cache).reduce((n, s) => n + s.findings.length, 0);
-    expect(totalFindings).toBe(0);
-  });
-
-  it('quote honesty silences an over-eager reader that invents a recurring-instruction finding on the same corpus', async () => {
-    const digests = await buildOneOffDigests();
-
-    // A reader that claims the same "standing instruction" on every session,
-    // regardless of what that session actually contains — the failure mode
-    // this check guards against, since the words never appear verbatim in
-    // any of these one-off asks.
-    const reader: FacetReader = () =>
-      JSON.stringify([
-        {
-          category: 'instruction',
-          summary: 'always run the release checklist before shipping',
-          quotes: ['always run the release checklist before shipping'],
-        },
-      ]);
-
-    const { cache } = buildFacetCache(digests, {}, reader);
-
-    let totalFindings = 0;
-    let totalDropped = 0;
-    for (const digest of digests) {
-      const session = cache[digest.session];
-      expect(session).toBeDefined();
-      const honest = enforceQuoteHonesty(session!.findings, digest);
-      totalFindings += honest.findings.length;
-      totalDropped += honest.droppedFindings;
-    }
-
-    expect(totalFindings).toBe(0);
-    expect(totalDropped).toBe(ONE_OFF_SESSIONS.length);
-  });
-
-  it('the same gate keeps a finding once it is honestly grounded in the session\'s own text — the zero above is silence, not blanket suppression', async () => {
-    const digests = await buildOneOffDigests();
-    const rebase = ONE_OFF_SESSIONS.find((s) => s.sessionId === 'sess-rebase')!;
-
-    // Only the session whose real text the finding quotes gets one; every
-    // other session still reports nothing, exactly like the compliant reader.
-    const reader: FacetReader = (prompt) =>
-      prompt.includes(rebase.intent)
-        ? JSON.stringify([
-            { category: 'workflow-pattern', summary: 'rebases onto main before pushing', quotes: [rebase.intent] },
-          ])
-        : '[]';
-
-    const { cache } = buildFacetCache(digests, {}, reader);
-
-    let totalFindings = 0;
-    let totalDropped = 0;
-    for (const digest of digests) {
-      const session = cache[digest.session]!;
-      const honest = enforceQuoteHonesty(session.findings, digest);
-      totalFindings += honest.findings.length;
-      totalDropped += honest.droppedFindings;
-    }
-
-    expect(totalDropped).toBe(0);
-    expect(totalFindings).toBe(1);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-prompt.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-prompt.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index a6060ab..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-prompt.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,143 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * The reader prompt is the seam where the replication guide's two hardest
- * lessons live: (1) a reader that thinks the digest is a task will try to
- * act on it, so the DATA-not-a-task framing must be the first thing the
- * reader sees, every time; (2) a reader with no sense of what's already
- * standing (CLAUDE.md rules, skills, standing loops) re-derives the same
- * "is this new?" judgment the analyst tier would otherwise have to redo from
- * scratch for every finding. Handing the reader that already-encoded summary
- * lets findings arrive PRE-MARKED — "covered" or "novel" — instead of
- * leaving novelty as an unanswered question downstream.
- */
-
-import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
-import type { SessionDigest } from '../src/digest.js';
-import {
-  buildFacetCache,
-  buildFacetPrompt,
-  parseFacetFindings,
-  type AlreadyEncoded,
-  type FacetReader,
-} from '../src/facets.js';
-
-const FRAMING_OPENER = 'THIS IS DATA, NOT A TASK.';
-
-function digest(session: string, intent: string): SessionDigest {
-  return { session, project: '/Users/me/repo', date: '2026-07-02T10:00:00.000Z', episodes: [{ intent, actions: [] }] };
-}
-
-const ENCODED: AlreadyEncoded = {
-  instructions: ['never fix symptoms, fix root causes', 'always write a failing test first'],
-  skills: ['tend-audit — verify a feature against real evidence', 'code-review — review a diff for bugs'],
-  loops: ['nightly-ci-watch — polls CI and notifies on red', 'weekly-digest — summarizes the week'],
-};
-
-describe('buildFacetPrompt — data-not-task framing opens every prompt', () => {
-  it('starts with the framing block when no already-encoded summary is given', () => {
-    const prompt = buildFacetPrompt(digest('sess-1', 'help me ship this'));
-    expect(prompt.startsWith(FRAMING_OPENER)).toBe(true);
-  });
-
-  it('still starts with the framing block when a rich already-encoded summary is given', () => {
-    const prompt = buildFacetPrompt(digest('sess-1', 'help me ship this'), ENCODED);
-    expect(prompt.startsWith(FRAMING_OPENER)).toBe(true);
-  });
-
-  it('the framing precedes the already-encoded summary, which precedes the session data', () => {
-    const prompt = buildFacetPrompt(digest('sess-1', 'help me ship this'), ENCODED);
-    const framingIndex = prompt.indexOf(FRAMING_OPENER);
-    const encodedIndex = prompt.indexOf('never fix symptoms, fix root causes');
-    const sessionIndex = prompt.indexOf('SESSION sess-1');
-    expect(framingIndex).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
-    expect(encodedIndex).toBeGreaterThan(framingIndex);
-    expect(sessionIndex).toBeGreaterThan(encodedIndex);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('buildFacetPrompt — carries the already-encoded summary', () => {
-  it('carries every standing instruction, skill, and loop verbatim', () => {
-    const prompt = buildFacetPrompt(digest('sess-1', 'an intent'), ENCODED);
-    for (const instruction of ENCODED.instructions) expect(prompt).toContain(instruction);
-    for (const skill of ENCODED.skills) expect(prompt).toContain(skill);
-    for (const loop of ENCODED.loops) expect(prompt).toContain(loop);
-  });
-
-  it('carries a partial summary (only skills known, no instructions or loops) without crashing', () => {
-    const partial: AlreadyEncoded = { instructions: [], skills: ['tend-audit — verify with evidence'], loops: [] };
-    const prompt = buildFacetPrompt(digest('sess-1', 'an intent'), partial);
-    expect(prompt).toContain('tend-audit — verify with evidence');
-  });
-
-  it('states plainly that everything is novel when the encoded summary is entirely empty', () => {
-    const empty: AlreadyEncoded = { instructions: [], skills: [], loops: [] };
-    const prompt = buildFacetPrompt(digest('sess-1', 'an intent'), empty);
-    expect(prompt.toLowerCase()).toContain('novel');
-  });
-
-  it('instructs the reader to mark each finding covered or novel against the summary', () => {
-    const prompt = buildFacetPrompt(digest('sess-1', 'an intent'), ENCODED);
-    expect(prompt).toContain('novelty');
-    expect(prompt).toContain('"covered"');
-    expect(prompt).toContain('"novel"');
-  });
-});
-
-describe('parseFacetFindings — novelty is an optional, validated field', () => {
-  it('accepts and preserves a "covered" finding', () => {
-    const raw = JSON.stringify([{ category: 'instruction', summary: 'reiterates a rule', quotes: ['x'], novelty: 'covered' }]);
-    const findings = parseFacetFindings(raw);
-    expect(findings?.[0]?.novelty).toBe('covered');
-  });
-
-  it('accepts and preserves a "novel" finding', () => {
-    const raw = JSON.stringify([{ category: 'friction', summary: 'a new pain point', quotes: ['x'], novelty: 'novel' }]);
-    const findings = parseFacetFindings(raw);
-    expect(findings?.[0]?.novelty).toBe('novel');
-  });
-
-  it('still accepts a finding that omits novelty entirely (older prompt, no encoded set) with the field absent', () => {
-    const raw = JSON.stringify([{ category: 'friction', summary: 'a pain point', quotes: ['x'] }]);
-    const findings = parseFacetFindings(raw);
-    expect(findings).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(findings?.[0]?.novelty).toBeUndefined();
-  });
-
-  it('refuses the whole reading when novelty is present but not one of the two declared values', () => {
-    const raw = JSON.stringify([{ category: 'friction', summary: 'x', quotes: ['x'], novelty: 'maybe' }]);
-    expect(parseFacetFindings(raw)).toBeNull();
-  });
-});
-
-describe('buildFacetCache — wires the already-encoded summary into every reader call', () => {
-  it('every prompt the reader receives carries the encoded summary handed to buildFacetCache', () => {
-    const digests = [digest('sess-a', 'first session'), digest('sess-b', 'second session')];
-    const seenPrompts: string[] = [];
-    const reader: FacetReader = (prompt) => {
-      seenPrompts.push(prompt);
-      return '[]';
-    };
-
-    buildFacetCache(digests, {}, reader, ENCODED);
-
-    expect(seenPrompts).toHaveLength(2);
-    for (const prompt of seenPrompts) {
-      expect(prompt.startsWith(FRAMING_OPENER)).toBe(true);
-      expect(prompt).toContain('never fix symptoms, fix root causes');
-    }
-  });
-
-  it('a cache hit (unchanged digest) makes no reader call at all, so the encoded summary is irrelevant to it', () => {
-    const digests = [digest('sess-a', 'first session')];
-    const reader: FacetReader = () => '[]';
-    const first = buildFacetCache(digests, {}, reader, ENCODED);
-    let calls = 0;
-    const countingReader: FacetReader = (prompt) => {
-      calls += 1;
-      return reader(prompt);
-    };
-
-    buildFacetCache(digests, first.cache, countingReader, ENCODED);
-
-    expect(calls).toBe(0);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-proposals.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-proposals.spec.ts

-/**
- * Facet findings become first-class proposals (task #44, docs/tend2-build-
- * contract.md): cross-session facet theming groups same-category findings
- * from >=2 distinct sessions by semantic similarity (themes.ts, reusing
- * StaticEmbeddingProvider on finding summaries), and each surviving theme
- * becomes a proposal candidate parallel to a cluster candidate — same
- * budget/suppression/emit paths (propose.ts's shared `runProposeSources`
- * engine), fingerprinted from its own verbatim quote hashes so a rejection
- * survives a retitle exactly the way stable-keys.spec.ts already proves for
- * cluster candidates.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import type { SessionDigest } from '../src/digest.js';
-import { StaticEmbeddingProvider, defaultModelDir } from '../src/embed/static.js';
-import { FACET_CATEGORIES, type FacetCache, type FacetCategory, type FacetFinding, type SessionFacets } from '../src/facets.js';
-import { ProposalMemory, fingerprintTheme } from '../src/memory.js';
-import { LocalProposer, runProposeMixed, runProposeThemes } from '../src/propose.js';
-import type { RecurrenceCandidate } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import { buildFacetThemes, formForTheme, themeKey, type ThemeSessionMeta } from '../src/themes.js';
-import type { EventTable } from '../src/types.js';
-import {
-  ADVERSARIAL_UNRELATED_SUMMARIES,
-  CORRECTION_THEME_SUMMARIES,
-  FRICTION_REPEATED_SUMMARIES,
-  INSTRUCTION_THEME_SUMMARIES,
-  SINGLE_SESSION_FINDING_SUMMARY,
-} from './facet-theme-fixtures.js';
-
-const embedder = StaticEmbeddingProvider.load(defaultModelDir());
-const DAY = '2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z';
-
-function cacheFrom(entries: Record<string, FacetFinding[]>): FacetCache {
-  const cache: Record<string, SessionFacets> = {};
-  for (const [session, findings] of Object.entries(entries)) {
-    cache[session] = { session, digestHash: `hash-${session}`, findings };
-  }
-  return cache;
-}
-
-function metaFrom(entries: Record<string, ThemeSessionMeta>): Map<string, ThemeSessionMeta> {
-  return new Map(Object.entries(entries));
-}
-
-function digestFor(session: string, project: string, date: string, intents: readonly string[]): SessionDigest {
-  return { session, project, date, episodes: intents.map((intent) => ({ intent, actions: [] })) };
-}
-
-/** The 3-session instruction fixture, wired into a cache + session metadata
- * + digests that all agree with each other — the shared setup underneath
- * several tests below. */
-function threeSessionInstructionFixture(): {
-  cache: FacetCache;
-  meta: Map<string, ThemeSessionMeta>;
-  digests: SessionDigest[];
-} {
-  const [s1, s2, s3] = INSTRUCTION_THEME_SUMMARIES;
-  const meta = metaFrom({
-    'sess-1': { project: '/repo/a', date: '2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z' },
-    'sess-2': { project: '/repo/b', date: '2026-06-02T09:00:00.000Z' },
-    'sess-3': { project: '/repo/c', date: '2026-06-03T09:00:00.000Z' },
-  });
-  const cache = cacheFrom({
-    'sess-1': [{ category: 'instruction', summary: s1, quotes: [s1] }],
-    'sess-2': [{ category: 'instruction', summary: s2, quotes: [s2] }],
-    'sess-3': [{ category: 'instruction', summary: s3, quotes: [s3] }],
-  });
-  const digests = [
-    digestFor('sess-1', '/repo/a', meta.get('sess-1')!.date, [s1]),
-    digestFor('sess-2', '/repo/b', meta.get('sess-2')!.date, [s2]),
-    digestFor('sess-3', '/repo/c', meta.get('sess-3')!.date, [s3]),
-  ];
-  return { cache, meta, digests };
-}
-
-describe('formForTheme — category->form table-driven', () => {
-  it.each([
-    ['instruction', 'standing-instruction'],
-    ['correction', 'standing-instruction'],
-    ['quality-bar', 'standing-instruction'],

-    ['tool-candidate', 'missing-tool'],
-    ['friction', undefined],
-  ] satisfies [FacetCategory, string | undefined][])('%s -> %s', (category, expected) => {

-
-  it('answers every real FacetCategory — the table above is not missing a category', () => {

-});
-
-describe('buildFacetThemes — cross-session theming', () => {

-
-  it('never themes a single-session finding, however clearly it names a recurring instruction', async () => {
-    const cache = cacheFrom({
-      'sess-1': [
-        { category: 'instruction', summary: SINGLE_SESSION_FINDING_SUMMARY, quotes: [SINGLE_SESSION_FINDING_SUMMARY] },
-      ],
-    });
-    const meta = metaFrom({ 'sess-1': { project: '/repo/a', date: DAY } });
-
-    const themes = await buildFacetThemes(cache, meta, embedder);
-    expect(themes).toEqual([]);
-  });
-
-  it('never themes friction, regardless of repetition or how tightly the phrasings cluster', async () => {
-    const entries = Object.fromEntries(
-      FRICTION_REPEATED_SUMMARIES.map((summary, i) => [
-        `sess-${i}`,

-      ]),
-    );
-    const cache = cacheFrom(entries);

-      Object.fromEntries(FRICTION_REPEATED_SUMMARIES.map((_, i) => [`sess-${i}`, { project: `/repo/${i}`, date: DAY }])),
-    );
-
-    const themes = await buildFacetThemes(cache, meta, embedder);

-
-  it('negative control: same-category but dissimilar one-off findings never connect into a theme', async () => {

-        [{ category: 'instruction', summary, quotes: [summary] } satisfies FacetFinding],
-      ]),
-    );
-    const cache = cacheFrom(entries);
-    const meta = metaFrom(
-      Object.fromEntries(ADVERSARIAL_UNRELATED_SUMMARIES.map((_, i) => [`sess-${i}`, { project: `/repo/${i}`, date: DAY }])),
-    );
-
-    const themes = await buildFacetThemes(cache, meta, embedder);
-    expect(themes).toEqual([]);
-  });
-
-  it('a lone correction across enough sessions still themes into a standing instruction (unlike a single finding)', async () => {
-    const [c1, c2, c3] = CORRECTION_THEME_SUMMARIES;
-    const cache = cacheFrom({
-      'sess-1': [{ category: 'correction', summary: c1, quotes: [c1] }],
-      'sess-2': [{ category: 'correction', summary: c2, quotes: [c2] }],
-      'sess-3': [{ category: 'correction', summary: c3, quotes: [c3] }],
-    });
-    const meta = metaFrom({
-      'sess-1': { project: '/repo/a', date: DAY },
-      'sess-2': { project: '/repo/b', date: DAY },
-      'sess-3': { project: '/repo/c', date: DAY },
-    });
-
-    const themes = await buildFacetThemes(cache, meta, embedder);
-    expect(themes).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(themes[0]!.form).toBe('standing-instruction');
-  });
-});
-
-describe('theme proposals — parallel to cluster proposals', () => {
-  it('a 3-session repeated instruction becomes ONE standing-instruction proposal carrying every quote', async () => {
-    const { cache, meta, digests } = threeSessionInstructionFixture();
-    const themes = await buildFacetThemes(cache, meta, embedder);
-    expect(themes).toHaveLength(1);

-    const result = await runProposeThemes(themes, digests, {
-      proposer: new LocalProposer(),
-      budgetTokens: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY,
-    });
-
-    expect(result.proposals).toHaveLength(1);
-    const [proposal] = result.proposals;
-    expect(proposal!.form).toBe('standing-instruction');
-    expect(proposal!.mechanizable).toBe(true);
-    expect(proposal!.dossier.kind).toBe('facet-theme');
-    expect(proposal!.dossier.sessionCount).toBe(3);
-    expect(proposal!.dossier.projectCount).toBe(3);
-    expect([...proposal!.citations].sort()).toEqual([...INSTRUCTION_THEME_SUMMARIES].sort());
-    expect(proposal!.fitnessFunction.draft).toBe(true);
-  });
-
-  it('a theme whose quotes stop resolving drops, counted', async () => {
-    const { cache, meta } = threeSessionInstructionFixture();
-    const themes = await buildFacetThemes(cache, meta, embedder);
-    expect(themes).toHaveLength(1);
-
-    // The corpus has moved on: none of the theme's member sessions still
-    // contain any of its quotes verbatim (a resume, an edit, or the session
-    // leaving the corpus between the read pass and this propose pass).
-    const staleDigests = [
-      digestFor('sess-1', '/repo/a', meta.get('sess-1')!.date, ['an unrelated turn about something else entirely']),
-      digestFor('sess-2', '/repo/b', meta.get('sess-2')!.date, ['an unrelated turn about something else entirely']),
-      digestFor('sess-3', '/repo/c', meta.get('sess-3')!.date, ['an unrelated turn about something else entirely']),
-    ];
-
-    const result = await runProposeThemes(themes, staleDigests, {
-      proposer: new LocalProposer(),
-      budgetTokens: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY,
-    });
-
-    expect(result.proposals).toHaveLength(0);
-    // Counted, not silently swallowed: the theme was considered and never
-    // truncated for budget — it was dropped purely for citations resolving
-    // to nothing, and that drop is derivable straight from the totals.
-    expect(result.consideredCandidates).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.truncated).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.consideredCandidates - result.truncated - result.proposals.length).toBe(1);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('fingerprint suppression survives a theme retitle', () => {
-  it('a rejection recorded against a 2-session theme still suppresses the retitled 3-session theme', async () => {
-    const [a, b, c] = INSTRUCTION_THEME_SUMMARIES; // lengths 49 / 42 / 39 — c is shortest

-    const v1Cache = cacheFrom({
-      'sess-a': [{ category: 'instruction', summary: a, quotes: [a] }],
-      'sess-b': [{ category: 'instruction', summary: b, quotes: [b] }],
-    });
-    const v1Meta = metaFrom({
-      'sess-a': { project: '/repo/a', date: '2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z' },
-      'sess-b': { project: '/repo/b', date: '2026-06-02T09:00:00.000Z' },
-    });
-    const [v1] = await buildFacetThemes(v1Cache, v1Meta, embedder);
-    expect(v1!.label).toBe(b); // shortest of {a, b}
-
-    const v2Cache = cacheFrom({
-      'sess-a': [{ category: 'instruction', summary: a, quotes: [a] }],
-      'sess-b': [{ category: 'instruction', summary: b, quotes: [b] }],
-      'sess-c': [{ category: 'instruction', summary: c, quotes: [c] }],
-    });
-    const v2Meta = metaFrom({
-      'sess-a': { project: '/repo/a', date: '2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z' },
-      'sess-b': { project: '/repo/b', date: '2026-06-02T09:00:00.000Z' },
-      'sess-c': { project: '/repo/c', date: '2026-06-03T09:00:00.000Z' },
-    });
-    const [v2] = await buildFacetThemes(v2Cache, v2Meta, embedder);
-    expect(v2!.label).toBe(c); // retitled: shortest of {a, b, c}
-
-    // A real retitle: the display/suppression fast-path key actually changed.
-    expect(themeKey(v1!)).not.toBe(themeKey(v2!));
-
-    const memory = ProposalMemory.empty();
-    memory.reject(
-      themeKey(v1!),
-      v1!.id,

-      'not now',
-      '2026-06-05T10:00:00Z',
-      fingerprintTheme(v1!),
-    );
-
-    // The naive exact-key check would miss it — proves the test exercises drift.
-    expect(memory.isSuppressed(themeKey(v2!))).toBe(false);
-    expect(memory.isSuppressedNear(themeKey(v2!), fingerprintTheme(v2!))).toBe(true);
-
-    const digests = [
-      digestFor('sess-a', '/repo/a', v2Meta.get('sess-a')!.date, [a]),
-      digestFor('sess-b', '/repo/b', v2Meta.get('sess-b')!.date, [b]),
-      digestFor('sess-c', '/repo/c', v2Meta.get('sess-c')!.date, [c]),

-    const result = await runProposeThemes([v2!], digests, {
-      proposer: new LocalProposer(),
-      budgetTokens: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY,
-      memory,
-    });
-
-    expect(result.proposals).toHaveLength(0);
-    expect(result.suppressed).toBe(1);
-  });

-
-describe('runProposeMixed — cluster candidates and facet themes ranked together', () => {
-  function fakeIntentCandidate(id: string, label: string, score: number): RecurrenceCandidate {
-    return {
-      id,
-      kind: 'intent',
-      label,

-      projectCount: 1,
-      firstSeen: DAY,

-      score: {

-    };
-  }
-
-  it('lets a higher-scoring facet theme outrank a low-scoring intent candidate for the shared reserved-slot budget', async () => {
-    const lowLabel = 'a one-off ask nobody else ever repeated';
-    const state: EventTable = {

-        { kind: 'user', ts: DAY, epoch: Date.parse(DAY), project: '/repo/x', session: 'sess-x', turn: 0 },

-    };
-    const lowCandidate = fakeIntentCandidate('intent:low', lowLabel, 0.01);
-
-    const { cache, meta, digests } = threeSessionInstructionFixture();

-
-    const result = await runProposeMixed([lowCandidate], themes, state, digests, {

-    });
-
-    // Only one reserved slot, shared across both sources: the theme's higher

-    expect(result.proposals).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(result.proposals[0]!.dossier.kind).toBe('facet-theme');
-    expect(result.truncated).toBe(1);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-quotes.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-quotes.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 0f6cee8..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-quotes.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,123 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Quote honesty — the application-layer gate the facets.ts doc comment defers
- * to explicitly ("quote honesty is a later tier's job"): a reader is never
- * trusted to police its own evidence, so every finding's quotes are checked
- * against its own session's digest before the finding is allowed to survive.
- * A finding that fails is dropped, fail-closed, and the drop is counted so a
- * caller can surface it rather than silently losing findings.
- */
-
-import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
-import type { SessionDigest, EpisodeDigest } from '../src/digest.js';
-import { enforceQuoteHonesty, type FacetFinding } from '../src/facets.js';
-
-function digest(episodes: EpisodeDigest[]): SessionDigest {
-  return { session: 'sess-x', project: '/Users/me/repo', date: '2026-06-01T10:00:00.000Z', episodes };
-}
-
-describe('quote honesty', () => {
-  it('keeps a finding whose quote resolves as a verbatim fragment of an episode intent', () => {

-/**
- * Per-session refusal reasons (SYN-N): `buildFacetCache`'s refusal path
- * already classifies WHY a session was refused (thrown reader vs. a schema
- * violation vs. unrepairable JSON) but only ever rolled that up into a bare
- * count. A run whose facets line reads "4 session(s) cached" out of 8 gives
- * no way to tell which 4 refused or why without re-running the reader by
- * hand. `onRefusal` surfaces one sanitized reason per refused session, and
- * run.ts's CLI summary renders one "<session-8chars>: <reason>" line per
- * refusal after the counts line — see facets.loop.html's Tried log,
- * 2026-07-24, and refusal-hygiene.spec.ts for the sibling tiers' convention
- * this follows (judge-error.ts's `sanitizeReason`, one line, capped).
- */
-

-import { runPipeline, facetRefusalLines, facetSummaryLine } from '../src/run.js';
-import { makeCorpus, userTurn } from './helpers.js';

-  it('never fires onRefusal for a session that succeeds or is merely repaired', () => {

-    const reasons: { session: string; reason: string }[] = [];

-    expect(result.facets.refusedSessions).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.facets.refusalReasons).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(result.facets.refusalReasons[0]?.session).toBe('sess-refuses');
-
-    const lines = facetRefusalLines(result.facets);
-    expect(lines).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(lines[0]).toContain('sess-ref');
-  });
-});
-
-// SYN-N4 (synN3-finding.md): the run summary must surface partial reads —
-// sessions salvaged rather than fully cached or fully refused — never

-// line already holds itself to (build-contract).
-describe('facetSummaryLine — surfaces partial-session salvage counts, never silently', () => {

-describe('buildFacetCache — one repair re-ask before refusing (SYN-N2)', () => {
-  it('an invalid-category refusal gets one hinted re-ask; a corrected reply on that re-ask caches and counts as repaired', () => {

diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-schema.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-schema.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index f585139..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-schema.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,271 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Schema discipline — the reader is a raw-text seam, so its output is
- * adversarial input from the application's point of view. `parseFacetFindings`
- * must accept only entries that fully satisfy the finding schema (a known
- * category, a non-empty summary, a quotes array of strings); an individual
- * entry that fails is dropped and counted (SYN-N4 per-entry salvage,
- * synN3-finding.md) while its valid siblings survive — a top-level
- * structural failure (no array found, unparseable JSON, not an array at
- * all) still refuses the WHOLE reading, and so does an array where EVERY
- * entry fails (nothing left to salvage). `buildFacetCache` must carry a
- * genuine whole-reading refusal through without poisoning the cache: the
- * refused session gets no entry (so the next pass retries it), and —
- * critically — a malformed reading for one session in a batch must never
- * affect any sibling session's entry.
- */
-
-import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
-import type { SessionDigest } from '../src/digest.js';
-import {
-  buildFacetCache,
-  parseFacetFindings,
-  parseFacetFindingsDetailed,
-  type FacetCategory,
-  type FacetReader,
-} from '../src/facets.js';
-
-const ALL_CATEGORIES: FacetCategory[] = [
-  'instruction',
-  'correction',
-  'quality-bar',
-  'friction',
-  'workflow-pattern',
-  'tool-candidate',
-];
-
-function digest(session: string, intent: string): SessionDigest {
-  return { session, project: '/Users/me/repo', date: '2026-07-02T10:00:00.000Z', episodes: [{ intent, actions: [] }] };
-}
-
-describe('parseFacetFindings — structural schema validation', () => {
-  it('accepts every declared category value', () => {
-    for (const category of ALL_CATEGORIES) {
-      const raw = JSON.stringify([{ category, summary: 'a finding', quotes: ['x'] }]);
-      const findings = parseFacetFindings(raw);
-      expect(findings).not.toBeNull();
-      expect(findings?.[0]?.category).toBe(category);
-    }
-  });
-
-  it('accepts an explicitly empty array as a valid "nothing recurs" answer', () => {
-    expect(parseFacetFindings('[]')).toEqual([]);
-  });
-
-  it('tolerates reader chatter surrounding the JSON array', () => {
-    const raw = [
-      'Here is my analysis of the session:',
-      JSON.stringify([{ category: 'friction', summary: 'lint keeps failing', quotes: ['x'] }]),
-      'Let me know if you need anything else.',
-    ].join('\n');
-    const findings = parseFacetFindings(raw);
-    expect(findings).toHaveLength(1);
-  });
-
-  // SYN-N4 (synN3-finding.md): per-entry salvage only ever SAVES a valid
-  // sibling — it never lowers the bar for a lone bad entry. Every case below
-  // is a single-entry array where that one entry is the whole reading, so
-  // there is nothing to salvage and the reading still refuses, unchanged.
-  it.each([
-    ['unknown category value', [{ category: 'vibe-check', summary: 's', quotes: [] as string[] }]],
-    ['category missing entirely', [{ summary: 's', quotes: [] as string[] }]],
-    ['category is not a string', [{ category: 7, summary: 's', quotes: [] as string[] }]],
-    ['summary missing entirely', [{ category: 'friction', quotes: [] as string[] }]],
-    ['summary is not a string', [{ category: 'friction', summary: 42, quotes: [] as string[] }]],
