-    ['summary is blank', [{ category: 'friction', summary: '   ', quotes: [] as string[] }]],
-    ['quotes missing entirely', [{ category: 'friction', summary: 's' }]],
-    ['quotes is not an array', [{ category: 'friction', summary: 's', quotes: 'x' }]],
-    ['quotes contains a non-string element', [{ category: 'friction', summary: 's', quotes: ['ok', 5] }]],
-  ])('refuses the whole reading when %s (its only entry, nothing to salvage)', (_label, entries) => {
-    expect(parseFacetFindings(JSON.stringify(entries))).toBeNull();
-  });
-
-  // Same reasoning: a top-level structural failure (no array, unparseable,
-  // not an array) always refuses whole — per-entry salvage never applies
-  // because there is no valid array to iterate. A single malformed ENTRY
-  // inside an otherwise-valid array (the '["not an object"]'-shaped cases
-  // below) also still refuses here because, again, it is the only entry —
-  // see the mixed-entries test below for the case that now survives.
-  it.each([
-    ['an entry that is a string, not an object', '["not an object"]'],
-    ['an entry that is a number', '[5]'],
-    ['an entry that is null', '[null]'],
-    ['an entry that is itself an array', '[[]]'],
-    ['a top-level object with no array inside at all', '{"error": "could not analyze this session"}'],
-    ['an array wrapped in an object, entry missing required fields', '{"findings": [{"category": "friction"}]}'],
-    ['unparseable JSON', 'not json at all, just prose'],
-    ['no brackets anywhere', 'The session shows no recurring pattern.'],
-  ])('refuses the whole reading given %s', (_label, raw) => {
-    expect(parseFacetFindings(raw)).toBeNull();
-  });
-
-  // SYN-N4 (synN3-finding.md): this used to assert "no partial admission" —
-  // the exact all-or-nothing stance the mega-session forensics identified as
-  // the root cause of compounding refusal. Per-entry salvage flips it: valid
-  // siblings survive, the one bad entry is dropped and counted.
-  it('salvages the valid entries and drops only the malformed one, counted in droppedEntries', () => {
-    const raw = JSON.stringify([
-      { category: 'instruction', summary: 'a real finding', quotes: ['x'] },
-      { category: 'instruction', summary: 'a real finding two', quotes: ['y'] },
-      { category: 'not-a-real-category', summary: 'the bad one', quotes: ['z'] },
-    ]);
-
-    expect(parseFacetFindings(raw)).toHaveLength(2);
-
-    const detailed = parseFacetFindingsDetailed(raw);
-    expect(detailed).not.toBeNull();
-    expect(detailed?.findings).toHaveLength(2);
-    expect(detailed?.findings.map((f) => f.summary)).toEqual(['a real finding', 'a real finding two']);
-    expect(detailed?.droppedEntries).toBe(1);
-  });
-
-  it('accepts a finding carrying additional unexpected fields (structurally tolerant, not brittle)', () => {
-    const raw = JSON.stringify([
-      { category: 'tool-candidate', summary: 's', quotes: ['x'], confidence: 0.9, extra: { nested: true } },
-    ]);
-    const findings = parseFacetFindings(raw);
-    expect(findings).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(findings?.[0]).toEqual({ category: 'tool-candidate', summary: 's', quotes: ['x'] });
-  });
-});
-
-describe('buildFacetCache — malformed output refuses the session without poisoning the cache', () => {
-  it('a malformed reading for one session in a batch never affects a sibling session in the same pass', () => {
-    const digests = [digest('sess-bad', 'first session'), digest('sess-good', 'second session')];
-    const reader: FacetReader = (prompt) =>
-      prompt.includes('sess-bad')
-        ? 'this is not valid json at all'
-        : JSON.stringify([{ category: 'instruction', summary: 'a real finding', quotes: ['second session'] }]);
-
-    const result = buildFacetCache(digests, {}, reader);
-
-    // sess-bad's malformed reply never validates even after its one SYN-N2
-    // repair re-ask (the stub ignores the hint), so it costs 2 calls;
-    // sess-good validates on the first try and costs 1.
-    expect(result.readerCalls).toBe(3);
-    expect(result.cache['sess-bad']).toBeUndefined();
-    expect(result.cache['sess-good']).toBeDefined();
-    expect(result.cache['sess-good']?.findings).toHaveLength(1);
-  });
-
-  // SYN-N4 (synN3-finding.md): this used to assert a whole-session refusal —
-  // the pre-N4 all-or-nothing stance. Per-entry salvage means the valid
-  // sibling survives on the FIRST call (no reask needed: something already
-  // validated), and the bad one is dropped and counted instead.
-  it('a structurally invalid finding (bad category) among valid ones is salvaged — only the bad entry is dropped', () => {
-    const digests = [digest('sess-mixed', 'the only episode')];
-    const reader: FacetReader = () =>
-      JSON.stringify([
-        { category: 'instruction', summary: 'a valid one', quotes: ['the only episode'] },
-        { category: 'made-up-category', summary: 'an invalid one', quotes: ['the only episode'] },
-      ]);
-
-    const result = buildFacetCache(digests, {}, reader);
-
-    expect(result.readerCalls).toBe(1); // no reask needed — a valid sibling already survived
-    expect(result.refusedSessions).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.wrongSessions).toBe(0);
-    const entry = result.cache['sess-mixed'];
-    expect(entry).toBeDefined();
-    expect(entry?.findings).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(entry?.findings[0]?.summary).toBe('a valid one');
-    expect(entry?.droppedEntries).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.partialSessions).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.droppedEntries).toBe(1);
-  });
-
-  it('a refused session is retried on the next pass while its previously-cached siblings stay untouched', () => {
-    const digests = [digest('sess-flaky', 'flaky episode'), digest('sess-stable', 'stable episode')];
-    // Fails twice — the initial call AND its one SYN-N2 repair re-ask — so
-    // the first pass genuinely exhausts its repair budget and still refuses;
-    // only the THIRD call (the next pass, a fresh session) succeeds.
-    let attempt = 0;
-    const reader: FacetReader = (prompt) => {
-      if (prompt.includes('flaky episode')) {
-        attempt += 1;
-        return attempt <= 2 ? '{"not": "an array"}' : JSON.stringify([
-          { category: 'workflow-pattern', summary: 'recovered', quotes: ['flaky episode'] },
-        ]);
-      }
-      return JSON.stringify([{ category: 'friction', summary: 'stable finding', quotes: ['stable episode'] }]);
-    };
-
-    const first = buildFacetCache(digests, {}, reader);
-    expect(first.cache['sess-flaky']).toBeUndefined();
-    expect(first.cache['sess-stable']).toBeDefined();
-
-    const second = buildFacetCache(digests, first.cache, reader);
-    expect(second.cache['sess-flaky']).toBeDefined();
-    expect(second.cache['sess-stable']).toEqual(first.cache['sess-stable']);
-    expect(attempt).toBe(3);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('buildFacetCache — repair-before-reject (#46: malformed ≠ wrong)', () => {
-  it('a repairable-malformed reading (single-quoted, trailing comma) succeeds and is counted as repaired, not refused', () => {
-    const digests = [digest('sess-repairable', 'run the release checklist')];
-    const reader: FacetReader = () =>
-      "[{'category': 'instruction', 'summary': 'always run the release checklist', 'quotes': ['run the release checklist'],}]";
-
-    const result = buildFacetCache(digests, {}, reader);
-
-    expect(result.cache['sess-repairable']).toBeDefined();
-    expect(result.cache['sess-repairable']?.findings).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(result.refusedSessions).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.repairedSessions).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.malformedUnrepairableSessions).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.wrongSessions).toBe(0);
-  });
-
-  it('an unrepairable reading still refuses fail-closed, classified malformed-unrepairable (not wrong)', () => {
-    const digests = [digest('sess-unrepairable', 'first episode')];
-    const reader: FacetReader = () => 'the model wrote an essay instead, no JSON anywhere';
-
-    const result = buildFacetCache(digests, {}, reader);
-
-    expect(result.cache['sess-unrepairable']).toBeUndefined();
-    expect(result.refusedSessions).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.repairedSessions).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.malformedUnrepairableSessions).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.wrongSessions).toBe(0);
-  });
-
-  it('syntactically valid JSON that fails the finding schema is classified "wrong", the genuine judgment failure', () => {
-    const digests = [digest('sess-wrong', 'first episode')];
-    const reader: FacetReader = () => JSON.stringify([{ category: 'not-a-real-category', summary: 's', quotes: ['x'] }]);
-
-    const result = buildFacetCache(digests, {}, reader);
-
-    expect(result.cache['sess-wrong']).toBeUndefined();
-    expect(result.refusedSessions).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.repairedSessions).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.malformedUnrepairableSessions).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.wrongSessions).toBe(1);
-  });
-
-  it('a repair-hint line carries into the NEXT chunk of the same oversized session only, after the previous chunk needed repair', () => {
-    const episodes = Array.from({ length: 100 }, (_, i) => ({ intent: `${'r'.repeat(4000)} ep-${i}`, actions: [] }));
-    const oversized: SessionDigest = { session: 'sess-hinted', project: '/Users/me/repo', date: '2026-07-01T00:00:00.000Z', episodes };
-
-    const prompts: string[] = [];
-    let call = 0;
-    const reader: FacetReader = (prompt) => {
-      prompts.push(prompt);
-      call += 1;
-      if (call === 1) {
-        // Repairable form-noise on the first chunk only.
-        return "[{'category': 'friction', 'summary': 'chunk one finding', 'quotes': ['x']}]";
-      }
-      return JSON.stringify([{ category: 'friction', summary: `chunk ${call} finding`, quotes: [] }]);
-    };
-
-    const result = buildFacetCache([oversized], {}, reader);
-
-    expect(call).toBeGreaterThan(2); // more than 2 chunks so we can see the hint reset after one step
-    expect(result.repairedSessions).toBe(1);
-    expect(prompts[0]).not.toContain('required repair'); // nothing to hint before the first call
-    expect(prompts[1]).toContain('your previous output required repair');
-    expect(prompts[1]).toContain('single-quoted-strings');
-    // The hint does not persist past the very next call once that call succeeded cleanly.
-    expect(prompts[2]).not.toContain('required repair');
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-theme-fixtures.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-theme-fixtures.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 943a7e4..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-theme-fixtures.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,68 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Shared fixtures for the facet-theme tier (themes.ts) — the finding-summary
- * analogue of fixtures.ts's turn-paraphrase groups: facet-proposals.spec.ts
- * (recall + negative control) and fixture-leak.spec.ts (the anti-Goodhart
- * lint, extended to scan this file too) both draw from this one home.
- *
- * Groups are synonym/paraphrase-anchored on purpose, same discipline as
- * fixtures.ts's own groups: no synonym table anywhere in src, semantics come
- * from the static distilled model (embed/static.ts). Measured against
- * potion-base-8M (2026-07-05, `.loop-scratch/probe-theme-fixtures.mjs`):
- * every within-group pair sits at 0.794-0.955, well clear of the 0.70
- * calibrated threshold (themes.ts's `THEME_SIMILARITY_THRESHOLD`); the
- * adversarial cross-topic pair sits at 0.181.
- */
-
-/**
- * Same standing instruction, three independent phrasings, of three DIFFERENT
- * lengths (49 / 42 / 39 chars) — the 3-session repeated-instruction case a
- * theme is built to catch, AND the retitle fixture: a 2-session theme over
- * just the first two picks the 42-char phrase as its shortest-summary
- * `label` (themes.ts's `buildTheme`); adding the third session's shorter
- * (39-char) phrasing genuinely retitles the theme (a new, different
- * `themeKey`) while its quotes stay mostly the same — exactly the drift a
- * fingerprint-based suppression (memory.ts's `fingerprintTheme`) must survive.
- */
-export const INSTRUCTION_THEME_SUMMARIES = [
-  'always write a failing test before fixing any bug',
-  'write a failing test before fixing the bug',
-  'a failing test must precede any bug fix',
-] as const;
-
-/** Same standing correction, three independent phrasings — proves
- * `formForTheme('correction')` (themes.ts) licenses `standing-instruction`
- * once the SAME correction recurs across sessions, unlike typology.ts's
- * single-finding matrix (which prices, never justifies, a lone correction). */
-export const CORRECTION_THEME_SUMMARIES = [
-  'never use rm to delete files, use trash instead',
-  'never use rm for deletion, use trash instead',
-  'use trash instead of rm when deleting files',
-] as const;
-
-/** Same recurring pain, three independent phrasings, but `friction` — proves
- * a category with no legal form never themes no matter how many sessions or
- * how tightly the phrasings cluster (themes.ts's `formForTheme`). */
-export const FRICTION_REPEATED_SUMMARIES = [
-  'the ci pipeline keeps timing out on the same integration test',
-  'the ci pipeline times out on that integration test again',
-  'that integration test times out the ci pipeline once more',
-] as const;
-
-/** One real finding, present in exactly one session — never a theme
- * regardless of how clearly it names a recurring-sounding instruction. */
-export const SINGLE_SESSION_FINDING_SUMMARY = 'always squash commits before merging a pull request' as const;
-
-/** Two dissimilar, one-off asks — the negative-control corpus: nothing here
- * should ever connect into a theme (measured cross-pair similarity: 0.181). */
-export const ADVERSARIAL_UNRELATED_SUMMARIES = [
-  'explain how the parser tokenizes block comments',
-  'rebase this feature branch onto main',
-] as const;
-
-export const ALL_FACET_THEME_FIXTURE_SUMMARIES = [
-  ...INSTRUCTION_THEME_SUMMARIES,
-  ...CORRECTION_THEME_SUMMARIES,
-  ...FRICTION_REPEATED_SUMMARIES,
-  SINGLE_SESSION_FINDING_SUMMARY,
-  ...ADVERSARIAL_UNRELATED_SUMMARIES,
-] as const;
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-wiring.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-wiring.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 8996358..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/facet-wiring.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,140 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Pipeline wiring for the comprehension tier: the one-command run must build
- * digests, hand them to the facet reader through the SAME incremental cache
- * contract facet-cache.spec.ts proves (zero reader calls for unchanged
- * sessions), persist that cache beside the event store, and surface both
- * `readerCalls` and dropped-quote counts in the run summary it returns. This
- * is the missing integration the lead audit found — facets.ts was contract-
- * true in isolation, but run.ts never called it.
- */
-
-import { existsSync, mkdirSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { runPipeline } from '../src/run.js';
-import { FacetStore } from '../src/facet-store.js';
-import type { FacetReader } from '../src/facets.js';
-import { appendRecord, makeCorpus, toolCall, userTurn } from './helpers.js';
-
-function outDir(): string {
-  return mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-facet-wiring-'));
-}
-
-/** A reader whose findings include one honest quote and one fabricated one —
- * proves dropped-quote counting flows all the way through the wiring. */
-function stubReader(calls: string[]): FacetReader {
-  return (prompt) => {
-    calls.push(prompt);
-    return JSON.stringify([
-      {
-        category: 'instruction',
-        summary: 'always run the release checklist before shipping',
-        quotes: ['run the release checklist before shipping'],
-      },
-      {
-        category: 'correction',
-        summary: 'fabricated — no such quote in this session',
-        quotes: ['delete the production database'],
-      },
-    ]);
-  };
-}
-
-describe('facet pipeline wiring', () => {
-  it('reads only new sessions through the facet reader, persists the cache beside the store, and surfaces readerCalls + dropped-quote counts', async () => {
-    const corpus = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-a',
-        records: [
-          userTurn('run the release checklist before shipping', '2026-06-01T10:00:00.000Z'),
-          toolCall('Bash', { command: 'npm test' }, '2026-06-01T10:00:01.000Z'),
-        ],
-      },
-    ]);
-    const out = outDir();
-    const calls: string[] = [];
-
-    const first = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out, facetReader: stubReader(calls) });
-
-    expect(first.facets.readerCalls).toBe(1);
-    expect(first.facets.droppedQuotes).toBe(1);
-    expect(calls).toHaveLength(1);
-
-    // The cache lives beside the event store, not inside it.
-    const storePath = join(out, 'loop-hole.store.json');
-    const facetStorePath = join(out, 'loop-hole.facets.json');
-    expect(existsSync(storePath)).toBe(true);
-    expect(existsSync(facetStorePath)).toBe(true);
-
-    const persisted = FacetStore.load(facetStorePath);
-    const entry = persisted.get('sess-a');
-    expect(entry).toBeDefined();
-    // The fabricated finding never reaches disk — only the honest one does.
-    expect(entry?.findings).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(entry?.findings[0]?.summary).toBe('always run the release checklist before shipping');
-
-    // Second pass, unchanged corpus, a fresh reader — must load the persisted
-    // cache from disk (not carry anything over in memory) and read nothing.
-    const second = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out, facetReader: stubReader(calls) });
-    expect(second.facets.readerCalls).toBe(0);
-    expect(second.facets.droppedQuotes).toBe(0);
-    expect(calls).toHaveLength(1);
-
-    // A brand-new session costs exactly one new reader call; the old one
-    // stays cached and is not re-read.
-    const repo2 = '-Users-me-repo2';
-    mkdirSync(join(corpus, repo2), { recursive: true });
-    appendRecord(corpus, repo2, 'sess-b', userTurn('check the deploy status', '2026-06-02T09:00:00.000Z'));
-
-    const third = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out, facetReader: stubReader(calls) });
-    expect(third.facets.readerCalls).toBe(1);
-    expect(calls).toHaveLength(2);
-
-    const persistedThird = FacetStore.load(facetStorePath);
-    expect(persistedThird.get('sess-a')).toEqual(entry);
-    expect(persistedThird.get('sess-b')).toBeDefined();
-    expect(persistedThird.size).toBe(2);
-  });
-
-  it('with no facet reader configured, the run performs zero reader calls and reports zero counts honestly', async () => {
-    const corpus = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-a',
-        records: [userTurn('check the deploy status', '2026-06-01T10:00:00.000Z')],
-      },
-    ]);
-    const out = outDir();
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out });
-
-    expect(result.facets.readerCalls).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.facets.droppedQuotes).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.facets.repairedSessions).toBe(0);
-    // No reader configured: nothing to persist yet.
-    expect(existsSync(join(out, 'loop-hole.facets.json'))).toBe(false);
-  });
-
-  it('a repairable-malformed reader reply (#46) still reaches the cache, and the pipeline surfaces repairedSessions instead of a refusal', async () => {
-    const corpus = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-repairable',
-        records: [userTurn('run the release checklist before shipping', '2026-06-01T10:00:00.000Z')],
-      },
-    ]);
-    const out = outDir();
-    const repairableReader: FacetReader = () =>
-      "[{'category': 'instruction', 'summary': 'always run the release checklist', 'quotes': ['run the release checklist before shipping'],}]";
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out, facetReader: repairableReader });
-
-    expect(result.facets.refusedSessions).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.facets.repairedSessions).toBe(1);
-
-    const persisted = FacetStore.load(join(out, 'loop-hole.facets.json'));
-    expect(persisted.get('sess-repairable')?.findings).toHaveLength(1);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/fitness-falsifiability.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/fitness-falsifiability.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index dc76f6c..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/fitness-falsifiability.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,154 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * The drafted fitness function is the one artifact a human never rewrites
- * from scratch before grommet's gate reads it — it should already be a rule
- * a judge can evaluate against real output. That means every number and
- * named artifact in the assertion has to come off THIS dossier: the cadence
- * window off its own histogram, the evidence off its own exemplars, the
- * counts off its own eventCount/projectCount. A template that pastes the
- * same sentence onto every proposal (only the label substituted) is not
- * falsifiable — nothing in it lets a judge tell this proposal's pass/fail
- * apart from any other proposal's.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { LocalProposer, type CadenceBucket, type Dossier } from '../src/propose.js';
-
-function bucket(band: string, fromMs: number, toMs: number, count: number): CadenceBucket {
-  return { band, fromMs, toMs, count };
-}
-
-function makeDossier(overrides: Partial<Dossier> = {}): Dossier {
-  return {
-    candidateId: 'tool:Bash(gh pr checks)',
-    clusterKey: 'tool:Bash(gh pr checks)',
-    kind: 'tool',
-    label: 'Bash(gh pr checks)',
-    eventCount: 6,
-    sessionCount: 6,
-    projectCount: 1,
-    projects: ['/Users/me/repo'],
-    cadence: [
-      bucket('<1h', 0, 3_600_000, 0),
-      bucket('1-6h', 3_600_000, 21_600_000, 4),
-      bucket('6-24h', 21_600_000, 86_400_000, 1),
-      bucket('1-3d', 86_400_000, 259_200_000, 0),
-      bucket('3-7d', 259_200_000, 604_800_000, 0),
-      bucket('>7d', 604_800_000, Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY, 0),
-    ],
-    exemplars: ['Bash(gh pr checks)'],
-    toil: { frequency: 6, medianDurationMs: 4000, interventions: 0 },
-    firstSeen: '2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z',
-    lastSeen: '2026-06-04T09:00:00.000Z',
-    fingerprint: { kind: 'tool', memberHashes: ['Bash(gh pr checks)'] },
-    ...overrides,
-  };
-}
-
-const OLD_TOOL_BOILERPLATE = 'matches what the operator acted on by hand';
-const OLD_INTENT_BOILERPLATE = 'within one cadence interval';
-
-describe('drafted fitness functions are falsifiable per dossier', () => {
-  it('names the dominant cadence band and its exact count/denominator, not an adjective', () => {
-    const dossier = makeDossier();
-    const draft = new LocalProposer().propose(dossier);
-
-    expect(draft.fitnessFunction.assertion).toContain('1-6h');
-    expect(draft.fitnessFunction.assertion).toContain('4 of 5 observed gaps');
-    expect(draft.fitnessFunction.assertion).not.toContain(OLD_TOOL_BOILERPLATE);
-  });
-
-  it('cites the dossier label, its own exemplar, and its own occurrence/project counts', () => {
-    const dossier = makeDossier({ eventCount: 6, projectCount: 3 });
-    const draft = new LocalProposer().propose(dossier);
-
-    expect(draft.fitnessFunction.assertion).toContain(dossier.label);
-    expect(draft.fitnessFunction.assertion).toContain(dossier.exemplars[0]!);
-    expect(draft.fitnessFunction.assertion).toContain('6 recorded hand-run occurrence(s)');
-    expect(draft.fitnessFunction.assertion).toContain('3 project(s)');
-  });
-
-  it('degrades honestly to an occurrence count when no gap is measurable, never fabricating a window', () => {
-    const dossier = makeDossier({
-      eventCount: 1,
-      cadence: [
-        bucket('<1h', 0, 3_600_000, 0),
-        bucket('1-6h', 3_600_000, 21_600_000, 0),
-        bucket('6-24h', 21_600_000, 86_400_000, 0),
-        bucket('1-3d', 86_400_000, 259_200_000, 0),
-        bucket('3-7d', 259_200_000, 604_800_000, 0),
-        bucket('>7d', 604_800_000, Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY, 0),
-      ],
-    });
-    const draft = new LocalProposer().propose(dossier);
-
-    expect(draft.fitnessFunction.assertion).toContain('no repeat gap yet');
-    expect(draft.fitnessFunction.assertion).toContain('1 occurrence(s)');
-    expect(draft.fitnessFunction.assertion).not.toMatch(/\d+ of \d+ observed gaps/);
-  });
-
-  it('two tool dossiers with different cadence/counts draft two different assertions', () => {
-    const local = new LocalProposer();
-    const frequent = makeDossier({
-      candidateId: 'tool:a', label: 'Bash(a)', exemplars: ['Bash(a)'],
-      fingerprint: { kind: 'tool', memberHashes: ['Bash(a)'] },
-    });
-    const rare = makeDossier({
-      candidateId: 'tool:b', label: 'Bash(b)', exemplars: ['Bash(b)'],
-      fingerprint: { kind: 'tool', memberHashes: ['Bash(b)'] },
-      eventCount: 4,
-      projectCount: 2,
-      cadence: [
-        bucket('<1h', 0, 3_600_000, 0),
-        bucket('1-6h', 3_600_000, 21_600_000, 0),
-        bucket('6-24h', 21_600_000, 86_400_000, 0),
-        bucket('1-3d', 86_400_000, 259_200_000, 3),
-        bucket('3-7d', 259_200_000, 604_800_000, 0),
-        bucket('>7d', 604_800_000, Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY, 0),
-      ],
-    });
-
-    const draftA = local.propose(frequent);
-    const draftB = local.propose(rare);
-
-    expect(draftA.fitnessFunction.assertion).not.toBe(draftB.fitnessFunction.assertion);
-    expect(draftA.fitnessFunction.assertion).toContain('1-6h');
-    expect(draftB.fitnessFunction.assertion).toContain('1-3d');
-    expect(draftB.fitnessFunction.assertion).toContain('4 recorded hand-run occurrence(s)');
-    expect(draftB.fitnessFunction.assertion).toContain('2 project(s)');
-  });
-
-  it('is deterministic — the same dossier drafts the same assertion every time', () => {
-    const dossier = makeDossier();
-    const local = new LocalProposer();
-    expect(local.propose(dossier).fitnessFunction.assertion).toBe(local.propose(dossier).fitnessFunction.assertion);
-  });
-
-  it('drafts an intent-kind assertion around its own cadence window, evidence, and counts', () => {
-    const dossier = makeDossier({
-      candidateId: 'intent:check ci status',
-      clusterKey: 'intent:check ci status',
-      kind: 'intent',
-      label: 'check ci status',
-      eventCount: 5,
-      sessionCount: 5,
-      projectCount: 2,
-      exemplars: ['Is CI green yet?'],
-      fingerprint: { kind: 'intent', memberHashes: ['Is CI green yet?'] },
-      cadence: [
-        bucket('<1h', 0, 3_600_000, 0),
-        bucket('1-6h', 3_600_000, 21_600_000, 0),
-        bucket('6-24h', 21_600_000, 86_400_000, 3),
-        bucket('1-3d', 86_400_000, 259_200_000, 1),
-        bucket('3-7d', 259_200_000, 604_800_000, 0),
-        bucket('>7d', 604_800_000, Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY, 0),
-      ],
-    });
-    const draft = new LocalProposer().propose(dossier);
-
-    expect(draft.fitnessFunction.assertion).toContain('6-24h');
-    expect(draft.fitnessFunction.assertion).toContain('3 of 4 observed gaps');
-    expect(draft.fitnessFunction.assertion).toContain('Is CI green yet?');
-    expect(draft.fitnessFunction.assertion).toContain('5 recorded occurrence(s)');
-    expect(draft.fitnessFunction.assertion).not.toContain(OLD_INTENT_BOILERPLATE);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/fixture-leak.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/fixture-leak.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 4b31e78..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/fixture-leak.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,66 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * The anti-Goodhart lint — mechanized, not cultural. A membership/similarity
- * check can always be "passed" by hardcoding the test's example inputs into
- * source (a synonym table, a phrase regex, a concept list); that is exactly
- * how this spike's first paraphrase check was gamed (docs/overfit-postmortem.md).
- * This spec makes that cheat structurally impossible for the current fixtures:
- * no fixture phrase — and no fragment of one longer than a single common word —
- * may appear anywhere in src/.
- *
- * The scan algorithm (function-word set, 2+-content-word windows) is
- * generalized in loop/src/core/fixture-leak.ts and shared with the first-party
- * `loop lint --fixtures` CLI, so the two can never drift apart
- * (docs/tend2-build-contract.md, "one source of truth per rule") — this spec
- * is a consumer of that module, not its own implementation.
- *
- * Rotating the fixtures (new phrasings, same behavior) keeps the defense
- * honest over time; each kind of fixture has its own single home (turns in
- * fixtures.ts, facet-theme summaries in facet-theme-fixtures.ts, synthesis
- * dossier prose in synthesis-fixtures.ts) — this spec unions every one of
- * them so a rotation, or a brand-new fixture file, still exercises the whole
- * defense at once. A new fixture file must be added to `FIXTURE_SOURCES`
- * below or this lint silently stops covering it.
- */
-
-import { readFileSync, readdirSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { join, relative } from 'node:path';
-import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { scanFixtureLeaks } from '../../../loop/src/core/fixture-leak.js';
-import { ALL_FIXTURE_TURNS } from './fixtures.js';
-import { ALL_FACET_THEME_FIXTURE_SUMMARIES } from './facet-theme-fixtures.js';
-import { ALL_SYNTHESIS_FIXTURE_STRINGS } from './synthesis-fixtures.js';
-
-/** Every fixture source this lint scans — one entry per fixture file. */
-const FIXTURE_SOURCES: readonly string[] = [
-  ...ALL_FIXTURE_TURNS,
-  ...ALL_FACET_THEME_FIXTURE_SUMMARIES,
-  ...ALL_SYNTHESIS_FIXTURE_STRINGS,
-];
-
-const SRC_DIR = join(fileURLToPath(new URL('.', import.meta.url)), '..', 'src');
-
-function sourceFiles(dir: string): string[] {
-  const out: string[] = [];
-  for (const entry of readdirSync(dir)) {
-    const path = join(dir, entry);
-    if (statSync(path).isDirectory()) out.push(...sourceFiles(path));
-    else if (path.endsWith('.ts')) out.push(path);
-  }
-  return out;
-}
-
-describe('fixture phrases must not leak into src/ (anti-Goodhart lint)', () => {
-  it('no fixture phrase or multi-word fragment appears in any source file', () => {
-    const files = sourceFiles(SRC_DIR);
-    expect(files.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-    const leaks = scanFixtureLeaks(
-      FIXTURE_SOURCES,
-      files.map((path) => ({ path: relative(SRC_DIR, path), content: readFileSync(path, 'utf8') })),
-    );
-    expect(
-      leaks,
-      leaks.map((l) => `src leak: "${l.fragment}" (from fixture "${l.fixture}") found in ${l.file}`).join('\n'),
-    ).toEqual([]);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/fixtures.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/fixtures.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 43a5d58..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/fixtures.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,134 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Shared paraphrase fixtures — one home, three consumers: paraphrase.spec
- * (recall), concept-precision.spec (adversarial precision), and
- * fixture-leak.spec (the anti-Goodhart lint proving none of these strings
- * leaked into src/ as a hand-listed answer key).
- *
- * Groups are synonym-anchored on purpose: restart/reboot, clear/wipe,
- * deploy/ship/roll-out. That is what the deterministic semantic tier can
- * honestly join (measured 2026-07-02: within-group best edges ≥0.762 on
- * potion-base-8M). Zero-lexical-overlap asks and speech-act distinctions are
- * deliberately NOT here — they are the LLM merge tier's job.
- */
-
-/** Same ask, synonym verbs, no synonym table anywhere in src. */
-export const RESTART_TURNS = [
-  'restart the ingest worker',
-  'reboot the ingest worker',
-  'kill and restart the ingest worker',
-] as const;
-
-export const CLEANUP_TURNS = [
-  'clear the build cache',
-  'wipe the build cache',
-  'the build cache needs clearing',
-] as const;
-
-export const DEPLOY_TURNS = [
-  'deploy the api to staging',
-  'ship the api to staging',
-  'roll the api out to staging',
-] as const;
-
-export const PARAPHRASE_GROUPS = [RESTART_TURNS, CLEANUP_TURNS, DEPLOY_TURNS] as const;
-
-/**
- * Same topic, different intent — lexically close to a group but NOT the ask.
- * Each pairs with the group it shadows; none may join that group's cluster.
- * (Measured similarities 0.166–0.635, all under the 0.70 threshold.)
- */
-export const ADVERSARIAL_TURNS = [
-  'document the restart flag in the readme',
-  'update the status field in the schema',
-  'the build cache design doc needs a diagram',
-  'write docs for the staging api',
-] as const;
-
-/**
- * A same-intent "checking on it" burst, phrased with zero lexical overlap
- * between asks — the polling.spec.ts contrast fixture proving cluster.ts's
- * embedding-threshold intent grouping fragments a real polling burst into
- * near-singletons (its documented blind spot), which is exactly the gap
- * polling.ts's timing-only detector closes.
- *
- * Note (episodes.loop.html c9, polling-sameness.spec.ts): these six phrases
- * also measure BELOW the real calibrated threshold pairwise on potion-base-8M
- * (0.12-0.44, max "done yet"<>"making progress" 0.440 vs the 0.70 floor) —
- * static.ts's own documented "Honest limits" for zero-lexical-overlap
- * pragmatic asks. They stay scoped to demonstrating cluster.ts's lexical
- * blind spot only; polling.ts's same-ask gate never asserts these six as a
- * "should cohere" case (its own fixtures are separate, synonym-anchored
- * paraphrases that do clear the threshold).
- */
-export const POLLING_BURST_TURNS = [
-  'any update',
-  'still running',
-  'status check',
-  'done yet',
-  'hows it going',
-  'making progress',
-] as const;
-
-/**
- * novelty.spec.ts's already-encoded set (a standing instruction, an
- * invocable skill, a standing loop) paired with a finding that only restates
- * each one — and one finding that restates none of them. Measured against
- * potion-base-8M (2026-07-02): each paired finding sits at 0.790-0.816
- * against its own entry (above cluster.ts's 0.70 calibrated threshold), and
- * the unrelated finding tops out at 0.407 against the closest entry —
- * comfortable margin on both sides of the threshold, same tier and same
- * calibration the paraphrase groups above already prove.
- */
-export const NOVELTY_ALREADY_ENCODED = {
-  instructions: ['never merge a pull request without a passing test suite'],
-  skills: ['runs a security review skill that checks pull requests for common vulnerabilities before merge'],
-  loops: ['a nightly watcher that checks deploy status and pings when a deploy fails'],
-} as const;
-
-export const NOVELTY_COVERED_FINDINGS = [
-  'always run the test suite before merging any pull request',
-  'the agent keeps manually checking pull requests for vulnerabilities instead of using the security review skill',
-  'the agent keeps checking whether the deploy status failed because nothing watches deploy status automatically',
-] as const;
-
-export const NOVELTY_NOVEL_FINDING =
-  'the agent keeps redoing the same six-step deployment runbook whenever a new task starts' as const;
-
-/**
- * polling.ts's same-ask gate fixtures (episodes.loop.html c9,
- * polling-sameness.spec.ts) — real ask text, unlike `POLLING_BURST_TURNS`
- * above (timing-only, no text ever reaches the detector). Measured on
- * potion-base-8M: `POLLING_OFFTOPIC_TURNS` never exceeds 0.292 pairwise (six
- * genuinely different technical questions, one connected component each);
- * `POLLING_CHECKIN_TURNS` forms exactly one connected component of all six
- * at the 0.70 threshold (pairwise range 0.50-0.88) — a real "is it done"
- * check-in, six distinct phrasings.
- */
-export const POLLING_OFFTOPIC_TURNS = [
-  'should the retry backoff be exponential or fixed',
-  'does the cache need to be invalidated on deploy',
-  'what is the timeout for the webhook handler',
-  'should we log the request id on every line',
-  'is the migration reversible if it fails halfway',
-  'why does the linter ignore the generated folder',
-] as const;
-
-export const POLLING_CHECKIN_TURNS = [
-  'did the migration finish',
-  'is the migration complete now',
-  'migration done running yet',
-  'still waiting on the migration to complete',
-  'any word on the migration finishing',
-  'has the migration completed',
-] as const;
-
-export const ALL_FIXTURE_TURNS = [
-  ...PARAPHRASE_GROUPS.flat(),
-  ...ADVERSARIAL_TURNS,
-  ...POLLING_BURST_TURNS,
-  ...POLLING_OFFTOPIC_TURNS,
-  ...POLLING_CHECKIN_TURNS,
-  ...Object.values(NOVELTY_ALREADY_ENCODED).flat(),
-  ...NOVELTY_COVERED_FINDINGS,
-  NOVELTY_NOVEL_FINDING,
-] as const;
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/form-decision.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/form-decision.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 745f222..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/form-decision.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,361 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * form-decision.ts (design: propose.loop.html check c10, "classification
- * honesty") — two things must be true:
- *
- *  1. Per-form criteria are structural and total: `decideChainForm`,
- *     `decidePollingForm`, `decideCandidateForm`, and `decideThemeForm` each
- *     read only the SHAPE of their own evidence (trace outcome, timing,
- *     recurrence kind, facet category) and always return a decision with a
- *     reason — `standing-loop` is reached only via `decideCandidateForm`'s
- *     own criterion, never as a shared fallthrough default.
- *  2. On evidence spanning all five forms, the emitted proposal set is
- *     multi-form — proven END TO END through `runPipeline` (the one-command
- *     pipeline entry, never module calls in isolation), because that is
- *     exactly where the real defect lived: chains.ts and polling.ts were
- *     stamped-green at the module level but never wired into propose.ts or
- *     run.ts, so a real corpus containing a workaround chain or a polling
- *     burst could never emit anything but `standing-loop`. The at-a-glance
- *     counts a reader sees are asserted to match the SAME classified set the
- *     briefing's sections render, so prose and counts cannot disagree.
- */
-
-import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { extractPayload } from '../../../loop/src/core/extract.js';
-import { detectChainCandidates } from '../src/chains.js';
-import { detectPollingFamilies } from '../src/polling.js';
-import type { Episode, EpisodeAction } from '../src/episodes.js';
-import type { CandidateKind, RecurrenceCandidate } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import type { FacetTheme } from '../src/themes.js';
-import type { FacetReader } from '../src/facets.js';
-import {
-  decideCandidateForm,
-  decideChainForm,
-  decidePollingForm,
-  decideThemeForm,
-} from '../src/form-decision.js';
-import { runPipeline } from '../src/run.js';
-import { formSectionLabel } from '../src/report.js';
-import type { ProposalForm } from '../src/typology.js';
-import { makeCorpus, toolCallRecord, toolResult, userTurn } from './helpers.js';
-import { POLLING_CHECKIN_TURNS } from './fixtures.js';
-
-const BASE = Date.parse('2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z');
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-
-function action(template: string, outcome: EpisodeAction['outcome'], day: number, offsetMs: number): EpisodeAction {
-  const epoch = BASE + day * DAY + offsetMs;
-  return { tool: template.split('(')[0] ?? template, template, outcome, ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(), epoch };
-}
-
-function episode(session: string, project: string, day: number, actions: EpisodeAction[]): Episode {
-  const askEpoch = BASE + day * DAY;
-  const last = actions[actions.length - 1];
-  return {
-    session,
-    project,
-    turn: 0,
-    ts: new Date(askEpoch).toISOString(),
-    epoch: askEpoch,
-    actions,
-    durationMs: last === undefined ? 0 : last.epoch - askEpoch,
-    tokens: { input: 0, output: 0 },
-    resolution: 'resolved',
-  };
-}
-
-/** A trivial-trace episode — polling.ts's own "quick ask" shape (empty
- * trace, zero duration), mirroring polling.spec.ts's own fixture helper. */
-function quickAsk(session: string, project: string, turn: number, epoch: number): Episode {
-  return {
-    session,
-    project,
-    turn,
-    ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(),
-    epoch,
-    actions: [],
-    durationMs: 0,
-    tokens: { input: 0, output: 0 },
-    resolution: 'resolved',
-  };
-}
-
-function fakeCandidate(kind: CandidateKind, label: string, eventCount: number, sessionCount: number): RecurrenceCandidate {
-  return {
-    id: `${kind}:${label}`,
-    kind,
-    label,
-    eventCount,
-    sessionCount,
-    projectCount: 1,
-    firstSeen: '2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z',
-    lastSeen: '2026-06-07T09:00:00.000Z',
-    provenance: [],
-    score: {
-      score: 0.5,
-      components: { frequency: 0.5, cadenceRegularity: 0.5, mechanizability: 0.5, manualCost: 0.5, stalenessRisk: 0.5 },
-    },
-  };
-}
-
-function fakeTheme(): FacetTheme {
-  return {
-    id: 'theme:instruction:s1,s2',
-    category: 'instruction',
-    form: 'standing-instruction',
-    label: 'always run the checklist',
-    sessions: ['s1', 's2'],
-    projects: ['/repo/a', '/repo/b'],
-    quotes: ['always run the checklist'],
-    occurrences: 2,
-    firstSeen: '2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z',
-    lastSeen: '2026-06-02T09:00:00.000Z',
-  };
-}
-
-describe('form-decision — structural per-form criteria (unit)', () => {
-  it('a chain whose trace ends in a failing step decides missing-tool, with a reason naming the failure', () => {
-    const episodes = [
-      episode('s1', '/repo/a', 1, [action('Bash(curl {url})', 'error', 1, 0), action('Bash(cat {path})', 'ok', 1, 1_000)]),
-      episode('s2', '/repo/a', 2, [action('Bash(curl {url})', 'error', 2, 0), action('Bash(cat {path})', 'ok', 2, 1_000)]),
-      episode('s3', '/repo/a', 3, [action('Bash(curl {url})', 'error', 3, 0), action('Bash(cat {path})', 'ok', 3, 1_000)]),
-    ];
-    const [chain] = detectChainCandidates(episodes);
-    if (chain === undefined) throw new Error('expected one chain candidate');
-
-    const decision = decideChainForm(chain);
-    expect(decision.shape).toBe('workaround-chain');
-    expect(decision.form).toBe('missing-tool');
-    expect(decision.reason).toMatch(/error/i);
-  });
-
-  it('a chain whose trace resolves cleanly every time decides distilled-skill, with a different shape and reason', () => {
-    const episodes = [
-      episode('s1', '/repo/a', 1, [action('Bash(npm run build)', 'ok', 1, 0), action('Bash(npm test)', 'ok', 1, 1_000)]),
-      episode('s2', '/repo/a', 2, [action('Bash(npm run build)', 'ok', 2, 0), action('Bash(npm test)', 'ok', 2, 1_000)]),
-      episode('s3', '/repo/a', 3, [action('Bash(npm run build)', 'ok', 3, 0), action('Bash(npm test)', 'ok', 3, 1_000)]),
-    ];
-    const [chain] = detectChainCandidates(episodes);
-    if (chain === undefined) throw new Error('expected one chain candidate');
-
-    const decision = decideChainForm(chain);
-    expect(decision.shape).toBe('procedure-chain');
-    expect(decision.form).toBe('distilled-skill');
-    expect(decision.reason).not.toMatch(/error/i);
-  });
-
-  it('a polling family always decides push-loop, naming the ask count and span in its reason', async () => {
-    const burst = Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => quickAsk('s1', '/repo/a', i, BASE + DAY + i * 5 * 60_000));
-    const [family] = await detectPollingFamilies(burst);
-    if (family === undefined) throw new Error('expected one polling family');
-
-    const decision = decidePollingForm(family);
-    expect(decision.shape).toBe('polling');
-    expect(decision.form).toBe('push-loop');
-    expect(decision.reason).toContain(`${family.askCount}`);
-  });
-
-  it('a tool-recurrence candidate and an intent-recurrence candidate both decide standing-loop — via their OWN shape, not a shared fallthrough', () => {
-    const toolDecision = decideCandidateForm(fakeCandidate('tool', 'git status', 4, 4));
-    const intentDecision = decideCandidateForm(fakeCandidate('intent', 'what changed today', 4, 4));
-
-    expect(toolDecision.form).toBe('standing-loop');
-    expect(intentDecision.form).toBe('standing-loop');
-    // Same form, but reached via two DISTINCT structural shapes — proof
-    // this isn't one shared "else" branch defaulting both kinds to the
-    // same value.
-    expect(toolDecision.shape).toBe('tool-recurrence');
-    expect(intentDecision.shape).toBe('intent-recurrence');
-    expect(toolDecision.shape).not.toBe(intentDecision.shape);
-    expect(toolDecision.reason).not.toBe(intentDecision.reason);
-  });
-
-  it('a theme decides its form from the reader-assigned category, never from dossier.kind alone', () => {
-    const decision = decideThemeForm(fakeTheme());
-    expect(decision.shape).toBe('instruction');
-    expect(decision.form).toBe('standing-instruction');
-    expect(decision.reason).toContain('instruction');
-  });
-});
-
-describe('form-decision — mixed-shape corpus through the one-command pipeline', () => {
-  const outDirs: string[] = [];
-  afterEach(() => {
-    for (const dir of outDirs.splice(0)) rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
-  });
-
-  function outDir(): string {
-    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loop-hole-form-decision-'));
-    outDirs.push(dir);
-    return dir;
-  }
-
-  const MIN = 60_000;
-  function ts(day: number, offsetMin = 0): string {
-    return new Date(BASE + day * DAY + offsetMin * MIN).toISOString();
-  }
-
-  const INSTRUCTION_SUMMARY = 'always double check the release notes before shipping';
-  const FACET_SESSIONS = new Set(['facet-0', 'facet-1']);
-
-  /** Returns the same instruction finding for the two facet-theme sessions,
-   * and an honest empty read for every other session in the corpus — a
-   * FacetReader stub, never a mocked unit under test (facets.ts's own
-   * parse/quote-honesty gates still run for real on this reply). */
-  function instructionReader(): FacetReader {
-    return (prompt) => {
-      const match = /^SESSION (\S+) /m.exec(prompt);
-      const session = match?.[1];
-      if (session === undefined || !FACET_SESSIONS.has(session)) return '[]';
-      return JSON.stringify([{ category: 'instruction', summary: INSTRUCTION_SUMMARY, quotes: [INSTRUCTION_SUMMARY] }]);
-    };
-  }
-
-  /** One corpus carrying, deliberately, all five evidence shapes named in
-   * propose.loop.html's c10: a plain recurring request (intent-recurrence),
-   * a convergent multi-step procedure (procedure-chain), a workaround chain
-   * substituting for an absent command (workaround-chain), a polling burst
-   * (polling), and a repeated-instruction facet finding (theme). */
-  function mixedShapeCorpus(): string {
-    const dir = '-Users-me-repo';
-    const sessions = [];
-
-    // Shape: plain recurring request -> intent-recurrence -> standing-loop.
-    for (let d = 0; d < 4; d += 1) {
-      sessions.push({
-        dir,
-        sessionId: `intent-${d}`,
-        records: [userTurn('is the nightly ingest job still green?', ts(d, 0))],
-      });
-    }
-
-    // Shape: convergent multi-step procedure -> procedure-chain -> distilled-skill.
-    for (let d = 10; d < 13; d += 1) {
-      sessions.push({
-        dir,
-        sessionId: `skill-${d}`,
-        records: [
-          userTurn(`please run the release pipeline for build ${d}`, ts(d, 0)),
-          toolCallRecord(`m-skill-${d}-1`, `t-skill-${d}-1`, 'Bash', { command: 'npm run build' }, ts(d, 1), {}),
-          toolResult(`t-skill-${d}-1`, false, ts(d, 1), 'build ok'),
-          toolCallRecord(`m-skill-${d}-2`, `t-skill-${d}-2`, 'Bash', { command: 'npm test' }, ts(d, 2), {}),
-          toolResult(`t-skill-${d}-2`, false, ts(d, 2), 'tests ok'),
-        ],
-      });
-    }
-
-    // Shape: workaround chain substituting for an absent status API ->
-    // workaround-chain -> missing-tool. The status endpoint fails; the
-    // agent falls back to reading the raw deploy log by hand every time.
-    for (let d = 20; d < 23; d += 1) {
-      sessions.push({
-        dir,
-        sessionId: `workaround-${d}`,
-        records: [
-          userTurn(`did deploy ${d} finish`, ts(d, 0)),
-          toolCallRecord(`m-wa-${d}-1`, `t-wa-${d}-1`, 'Bash', { command: 'curl https://deploy-status.example.com/health' }, ts(d, 1), {}),
-          toolResult(`t-wa-${d}-1`, true, ts(d, 1), 'connection refused'),
-          toolCallRecord(`m-wa-${d}-2`, `t-wa-${d}-2`, 'Bash', { command: 'cat /var/log/deploy.log' }, ts(d, 2), {}),
-          toolResult(`t-wa-${d}-2`, false, ts(d, 2), 'deploy finished'),
-        ],
-      });
-    }
-
-    // Shape: polling burst -> polling -> push-loop. Six "is the migration
-    // done" phrasings (POLLING_CHECKIN_TURNS — same-ask under the real
-    // calibrated embedder, episodes.loop.html c9), 5 minutes apart, trivial
-    // (tool-free) traces.
-    sessions.push({
-      dir,
-      sessionId: 'polling-burst',
-      records: POLLING_CHECKIN_TURNS.map((text, i) => userTurn(text, ts(30, 10 + i * 5))),
-    });
-
-    // Shape: repeated-instruction facet finding -> instruction (themed
-    // across 2 sessions) -> standing-instruction. Only 2 occurrences —
-    // under cluster.ts's own intent-recurrence floor (3), so this can only
-    // ever surface through the facet-theme path, never double-counted as
-    // its own intent-recurrence candidate.
-    for (let i = 0; i < 2; i += 1) {
-      sessions.push({
-        dir,
-        sessionId: `facet-${i}`,
-        records: [userTurn(INSTRUCTION_SUMMARY, ts(40 + i, 0))],
-      });
-    }
-
-    return makeCorpus(sessions);
-  }
-
-  it('emits proposals spanning all five forms — an all-one-form result on this input is the failing test this check exists to catch', async () => {
-    const corpus = mixedShapeCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out, facetReader: instructionReader() });
-
-    const forms = new Set(result.propose.proposals.map((p) => p.form));
-    expect(result.chains, 'chain detection must have run and surfaced both chains').toBe(2);
-    expect(result.pollingFamilies, 'polling detection must have run and surfaced the burst').toBe(1);
-    expect(
-      forms.size,
-      `expected >=4 distinct forms, got: ${[...forms].sort().join(', ')} (proposals: ${result.propose.proposals.map((p) => `${p.form}:${p.intent}`).join(' | ')})`,
-    ).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4);
-
-    // Every one of the five evidence shapes this corpus carries is
-    // represented by its own decided form — not merely "at least four
-    // distinct", but the five this fixture was built to prove.
-    expect(forms.has('standing-loop')).toBe(true);
-    expect(forms.has('distilled-skill')).toBe(true);
-    expect(forms.has('missing-tool')).toBe(true);
-    expect(forms.has('push-loop')).toBe(true);
-    expect(forms.has('standing-instruction')).toBe(true);
-  });
-
-  it('the briefing\'s at-a-glance counts match the SAME classified set the form-sections render — on real, not hand-built, proposals', async () => {
-    const corpus = mixedShapeCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out, facetReader: instructionReader() });
-
-    const html = readFileSync(join(out, 'loop-hole-briefing.loop.html'), 'utf8');
-    const payload = extractPayload(html)!.payload;
-
-    // Independent recount straight from the pipeline's own emitted
-    // proposals — never from report.ts's internal tally.
-    const recount = new Map<string, number>();
-    for (const proposal of result.propose.proposals) recount.set(proposal.form, (recount.get(proposal.form) ?? 0) + 1);
-
-    const glanceHeadingIndex = payload.indexOf('## At a glance');
-    expect(glanceHeadingIndex).toBeGreaterThan(-1);
-    const nextHeadingIndex = payload.indexOf('##', glanceHeadingIndex + '## At a glance'.length);
-    const glanceBlock = payload.slice(glanceHeadingIndex, nextHeadingIndex === -1 ? undefined : nextHeadingIndex);
-
-    const SECTION_HEADINGS: readonly [ProposalForm, string][] = [
-      ['standing-instruction', '## Standing instructions'],
-      ['push-loop', '## Push loops'],
-      ['distilled-skill', '## Skills'],
-      ['standing-loop', '## Standing loops'],
-      ['missing-tool', '## Missing tools'],
-    ];
-
-    for (const [form, heading] of SECTION_HEADINGS) {
-      const count = recount.get(form) ?? 0;
-      // The glance line states this form's count as the exact
-      // "<count> <label>" fragment report.ts's own atAGlanceLine renders.
-      const expectedFragment = `${count} ${formSectionLabel(form, count)}`;
-      expect(glanceBlock, `at-a-glance must report "${expectedFragment}" for ${form}`).toContain(expectedFragment);
-
-      // And the section itself renders exactly `count` cards — counted by
-      // the row markers report.ts emits (`N. <intent> — seen …`), scoped to
-      // this section's own body.
-      const sectionIndex = payload.indexOf(heading);
-      expect(sectionIndex, `missing section: ${heading}`).toBeGreaterThan(-1);
-      const sectionEnd = payload.indexOf('\n## ', sectionIndex + heading.length);
-      const sectionBody = payload.slice(sectionIndex, sectionEnd === -1 ? undefined : sectionEnd);
-      const cardRows = sectionBody.match(/^\d+\. /gm) ?? [];
-      expect(cardRows.length, `${heading} must render exactly ${count} card(s)`).toBe(count);
-    }
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/helpers.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/helpers.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 9e29a39..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/helpers.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,149 +0,0 @@
-/** Test helpers: build throwaway JSONL corpora on a temp dir, shaped exactly
- * like a real `~/.claude/projects/<encoded-cwd>/<sessionId>.jsonl` tree. */
-
-import { mkdtempSync, mkdirSync, writeFileSync, appendFileSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-
-export interface SessionSpec {
-  /** Encoded project directory name, e.g. `-Users-me-repo`. */
-  dir: string;
-  sessionId: string;
-  /** One object per JSONL line, written verbatim. */
-  records: unknown[];
-}
-
-/** Create a fresh temp corpus root and write the given sessions into it. */
-export function makeCorpus(sessions: SessionSpec[]): string {
-  const root = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-corpus-'));
-  for (const session of sessions) {
-    const dir = join(root, session.dir);
-    mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
-    const body = session.records.map((r) => JSON.stringify(r)).join('\n') + '\n';
-    writeFileSync(join(dir, `${session.sessionId}.jsonl`), body);
-  }
-  return root;
-}
-
-/** Append one record to an existing session file, changing its byte length. */
-export function appendRecord(root: string, dir: string, sessionId: string, record: unknown): void {
-  appendFileSync(join(root, dir, `${sessionId}.jsonl`), JSON.stringify(record) + '\n');
-}
-
-/** A well-formed user prompt record. */
-export function userTurn(text: string, timestamp: string, cwd = '/Users/me/repo'): unknown {
-  return { type: 'user', timestamp, cwd, message: { role: 'user', content: text } };
-}
-
-/** An assistant record carrying one tool call (plus optional extra blocks). */
-export function toolCall(
-  name: string,
-  input: unknown,
-  timestamp: string,
-  extraBlocks: unknown[] = [],
-  cwd = '/Users/me/repo',
-): unknown {
-  return {
-    type: 'assistant',
-    timestamp,
-    cwd,
-    message: { role: 'assistant', content: [...extraBlocks, { type: 'tool_use', name, input }] },
-  };
-}
-
-/** The token-cost fields a real `usage` record carries (v2 fixtures). */
-export interface UsageInput {
-  input_tokens?: number;
-  output_tokens?: number;
-  cache_creation_input_tokens?: number;
-  cache_read_input_tokens?: number;
-}
-
-/** An assistant record carrying one tool call with a stable id (for a later
- * `toolResult` to resolve) and the completion's usage (v2 fixtures). */
-export function toolCallRecord(
-  messageId: string,
-  toolUseId: string,
-  name: string,
-  input: unknown,
-  timestamp: string,
-  usage: UsageInput,
-  cwd = '/Users/me/repo',
-): unknown {
-  return {
-    type: 'assistant',
-    timestamp,
-    cwd,
-    message: { role: 'assistant', id: messageId, content: [{ type: 'tool_use', id: toolUseId, name, input }], usage },
-  };
-}
-
-/** An assistant text-only record (no tool call) carrying usage (v2 fixtures) —
- * the shape a completion's text block takes when it shares a message id with
- * a sibling `toolCallRecord` line. */
-export function assistantText(
-  messageId: string,
-  text: string,
-  timestamp: string,
-  usage: UsageInput,
-  cwd = '/Users/me/repo',
-): unknown {
-  return {
-    type: 'assistant',
-    timestamp,
-    cwd,
-    message: { role: 'assistant', id: messageId, content: [{ type: 'text', text }], usage },
-  };
-}
-
-/** A user record carrying one tool_result block resolving the given tool_use
- * id (v2 fixtures). */
-export function toolResult(toolUseId: string, isError: boolean, timestamp: string, content: string, cwd = '/Users/me/repo'): unknown {
-  return {
-    type: 'user',
-    timestamp,
-    cwd,
-    message: { role: 'user', content: [{ type: 'tool_result', tool_use_id: toolUseId, is_error: isError, content }] },
-  };
-}
-
-/** A harness-injected task-notification record, shaped exactly like the
- * literal `<task-notification>...</task-notification>` block Claude Code
- * folds into a session as a "user" turn when a background task reports in —
- * no human typed this; it carries no genuine intent. */
-export function taskNotificationTurn(
-  taskId: string,
-  summary: string,
-  event: string,
-  timestamp: string,
-  cwd = '/Users/me/repo',
-): unknown {
-  const content = `<task-notification> <task-id>${taskId}</task-id> <summary>${summary}</summary> <event>${event}</event> </task-notification>`;
-  return { type: 'user', timestamp, cwd, message: { role: 'user', content } };
-}
-
-/** A Conductor queue-operation bookkeeping record — no `message` field at
- * all; the real shape carries the prompt text directly on `content`. */
-export function queueOperationTurn(content: string, timestamp: string, sessionId = 'sess'): unknown {
-  return { type: 'queue-operation', operation: 'enqueue', timestamp, sessionId, content };
-}
-
-/** A Conductor last-prompt snapshot record — no `message` field; carries the
- * prompt on `lastPrompt` and has no `timestamp` in the real shape either. */
-export function lastPromptTurn(lastPrompt: string, leafUuid: string, sessionId = 'sess'): unknown {
-  return { type: 'last-prompt', lastPrompt, leafUuid, sessionId };
-}
-
-/** A harness attachment record (e.g. a budget check-in) — no `message`
- * field; carries structured data on `attachment`. */
-export function attachmentTurn(timestamp: string, cwd = '/Users/me/repo', sessionId = 'sess'): unknown {
-  return {
-    type: 'attachment',
-    attachment: { type: 'budget_usd', used: 0, total: 1.5, remaining: 1.5 },
-    uuid: `uuid-att-${timestamp}`,
-    timestamp,
-    userType: 'external',
-    cwd,
-    sessionId,
-  };
-}
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/lanes.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/lanes.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 34aaf84..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/lanes.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,61 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * splitLanes — the v2 kind-split (user asks vs. agent tool-rituals), kept as
- * a standalone utility over raw `kind` (report.ts's own docstring: "kept as
- * a standalone, still-useful split ... for anything that wants it"). No
- * longer wired into `emitProposalPages`, which now sections by `form`
- * (typology.ts) in adoption-cost order — see briefing-v3.spec.ts for that
- * axis's own tests.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { splitLanes } from '../src/report.js';
-import type { Proposal } from '../src/propose.js';
-
-function makeProposal(kind: 'intent' | 'tool', intent: string, score: number, projects: string[] = ['/repo/a']): Proposal {
-  return {
-    candidateId: `${kind}:${intent}`,
-    clusterKey: `${kind}:${intent}`,
-    score,
-    intent,
-    form: 'standing-loop',
-    mechanizable: true,
-    mechanizabilityRationale: 'stable shape',
-    fitnessFunction: {
-      claim: `${intent} stays correct unattended`,
-      method: 'synthetic-test',
-      assertion: 'PASS when the scheduled run matches the manual result; FAIL otherwise.',
-      draft: true,
-    },
-    payoff: 'reclaims recurring hand-work',
-    citations: [intent],
-    dossier: {
-      candidateId: `${kind}:${intent}`,
-      clusterKey: `${kind}:${intent}`,
-      kind,
-      label: intent,
-      eventCount: 5,
-      sessionCount: 3,
-      projectCount: projects.length,
-      projects,
-      cadence: [],
-      exemplars: [intent],
-      toil: { frequency: 0, medianDurationMs: 0, interventions: 0 },
-      firstSeen: '2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z',
-      lastSeen: '2026-06-07T09:00:00.000Z',
-      fingerprint: { kind, memberHashes: [intent] },
-    },
-    tokensUsed: 50,
-  };
-}
-
-describe('splitLanes', () => {
-  it('ranks within a lane and never across lanes', () => {
-    const lanes = splitLanes([
-      makeProposal('intent', 'low ask', 0.2),
-      makeProposal('tool', 'high tool', 0.95),
-      makeProposal('intent', 'top ask', 0.5),
-    ]);
-    expect(lanes.user.map((proposal) => proposal.intent)).toEqual(['top ask', 'low ask']);
-    expect(lanes.agent.map((proposal) => proposal.intent)).toEqual(['high tool']);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/negctrl.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/negctrl.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 6c8c0b5..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/negctrl.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,131 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * The negative control: a corpus of one-off, non-recurring activity must yield
- * ZERO proposals. This is the guard against the whole product's failure mode —
- * a detector that "finds" rituals in noise manufactures busywork instead of
- * surfacing real recurrence. A genuinely quiet corpus must stay silent.
- *
- * The corpus is deliberately not merely unique: it also contains templates that
- * repeat *twice* — just under the recurrence bar — so the test proves the
- * threshold does the work, not that nothing was seeded at all.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { detectRecurrenceCandidates } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import { runPropose, LocalProposer } from '../src/propose.js';
-import { normalizeToolCall } from '../src/normalize.js';
-import type { ActionEvent, StoredState } from '../src/types.js';
-
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-const START = Date.parse('2026-06-01T09:00:00Z');
-const NOW = Date.parse('2026-06-20T09:00:00Z');
-
-/**
- * A corpus that looks busy but recurs at nothing. Every tool template is either
- * a one-off or appears exactly twice (below the min-occurrence bar of 3); every
- * user turn is a distinct, unrelated request that no cluster of three can form.
- */
-function oneOffState(): StoredState {
-  const oneOffCommands = [
-    'lsof -i :8471',
-    'git status',
-    'ls -la src',
-    'cat README.md',
-    'echo hello',
-    'git log --oneline -5',
-    'wc -l package.json',
-    'head -20 CHANGELOG.md',
-  ];
-  // Two templates that recur exactly twice — just under the threshold of 3.
-  const twiceCommands = ['npm test', 'docker ps'];
-
-  const turns = [
-    'Rename the login button to Sign in',
-    'Explain how the parser handles block comments',
-    'Add a dark mode toggle to the settings page',
-    'Draft a changelog entry for the 2.1 release',
-    'Convert this callback into an async function',
-  ];
-
-  const events: ActionEvent[] = [];
-  let seq = 0;
-
-  oneOffCommands.forEach((command, i) => {
-    events.push(toolEvent('Bash', { command }, i, seq++));
-  });
-
-  // Each "twice" command fires on two different days — recurring, but not enough.
-  twiceCommands.forEach((command, i) => {
-    events.push(toolEvent('Bash', { command }, i, seq++));
-    events.push(toolEvent('Bash', { command }, i + 3, seq++));
-  });
-
-  // A single WebFetch and a single MCP call — distinct one-offs, not rituals.
-  events.push(toolEvent('WebFetch', { url: 'https://news.example.com/today' }, 1, seq++));
-  events.push(toolEvent('mcp__tilth__tilth_search', { query: 'parseComment' }, 2, seq++));
-
-  // Each user turn is asked exactly once, on its own day — no repeated intent.
-  turns.forEach((_text, i) => {
-    events.push(userEvent(i, i, seq++));
-  });
-
-  return { version: 1, watermark: {}, turns, events };
-}
-
-function toolEvent(tool: string, input: unknown, day: number, sequence: number): ActionEvent {
-  const epoch = START + day * DAY + sequence * 1000;
-  return {
-    kind: 'tool',
-    ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(),
-    epoch,
-    project: '/Users/me/repo',
-    session: `session-${day}`,
-    tool,
-    template: normalizeToolCall(tool, input),
-  };
-}
-
-function userEvent(turn: number, day: number, sequence: number): ActionEvent {
-  const epoch = START + day * DAY + sequence * 1000;
-  return {
-    kind: 'user',
-    ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(),
-    epoch,
-    project: '/Users/me/repo',
-    session: `session-${day}`,
-    turn,
-  };
-}
-
-describe('negative control', () => {
-  it('a corpus of one-off, non-recurring activity yields no candidates', async () => {
-    const state = oneOffState();
-    const candidates = await detectRecurrenceCandidates(state, { now: NOW });
-    expect(candidates).toHaveLength(0);
-  });
-
-  it('yields zero proposals end to end', async () => {
-    const state = oneOffState();
-    const candidates = await detectRecurrenceCandidates(state, { now: NOW });
-    const result = await runPropose(candidates, state, {
-      proposer: new LocalProposer(),
-      budgetTokens: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY,
-    });
-
-    expect(result.proposals).toHaveLength(0);
-    expect(result.spentTokens).toBe(0);
-  });
-
-  it('the threshold is what silences it — dropping the bar to 2 surfaces the twice-run templates', async () => {
-    // Proves the zero above is the recurrence bar doing its job, not an empty
-    // or malformed corpus: the two templates seeded exactly twice appear the
-    // moment the minimum occurrence count is lowered to two.
-    const state = oneOffState();
-    const loosened = await detectRecurrenceCandidates(state, {
-      now: NOW,
-      minToolOccurrences: 2,
-    });
-    const labels = loosened.map((candidate) => candidate.label);
-    expect(labels).toContain(normalizeToolCall('Bash', { command: 'npm test' }));
-    expect(labels).toContain(normalizeToolCall('Bash', { command: 'docker ps' }));
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/normalize.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/normalize.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 691bc6d..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/normalize.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,61 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Normalization is what makes counting possible: variant calls that differ
- * only in a port, a path, or a sha must collapse to one template, or the
- * counting layer sees many rituals where there is one.
- */
-
-import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
-import { normalizeCommand, normalizeToolCall } from '../src/normalize.js';
-
-describe('normalization collapses variance to one template', () => {
-  it('ports: two lsof calls on different ports are one ritual', () => {
-    const a = normalizeToolCall('Bash', { command: 'lsof -i :8471' });
-    const b = normalizeToolCall('Bash', { command: 'lsof -i :8473' });
-    expect(a).toBe(b);
-    expect(a).toBe('Bash(lsof -i :{port})');
-  });
-
-  it('paths: reads of different files are one ritual', () => {
-    const a = normalizeToolCall('Bash', { command: 'cat /Users/alice/proj/foo.ts' });
-    const b = normalizeToolCall('Bash', { command: 'cat /Users/bob/other/bar.ts' });
-    expect(a).toBe(b);
-    expect(a).toBe('Bash(cat {path})');
-  });
-
-  it('shas: checkouts of different commits are one ritual', () => {
-    const a = normalizeToolCall('Bash', { command: 'git checkout 1a2b3c4' });
-    const b = normalizeToolCall('Bash', { command: 'git checkout deadbeefcafe' });
-    expect(a).toBe(b);
-    expect(a).toBe('Bash(git checkout {sha})');
-  });
-
-  it('a compound command collapses every varying field at once', () => {
-    const a = normalizeCommand('git log --since 2026-06-01 abc1234 -- /Users/x/src/app.ts');
-    const b = normalizeCommand('git log --since 2026-07-02 f00dbabe -- /Users/y/lib/core.ts');
-    expect(a).toBe(b);
-    expect(a).toBe('git log --since {date} {sha} -- {path}');
-  });
-
-  it('structured tools keep their identity but slot their arguments away', () => {
-    expect(normalizeToolCall('Read', { file_path: '/a/b/c.ts' }))
-      .toBe(normalizeToolCall('Read', { file_path: '/d/e/f.ts' }));
-    expect(normalizeToolCall('Grep', { pattern: 'foo', path: '/x' })).toBe('Grep({pattern})');
-  });
-
-  it('a web fetch keeps the host (the page you keep checking), slots the path', () => {
-    const same = normalizeToolCall('WebFetch', { url: 'https://rival.example.com/pricing' });
-    const samePath = normalizeToolCall('WebFetch', { url: 'https://rival.example.com/blog/2026' });
-    const other = normalizeToolCall('WebFetch', { url: 'https://github.com/o/r/pull/9' });
-    expect(same).toBe(samePath);
-    expect(same).toBe('WebFetch(rival.example.com)');
-    expect(other).not.toBe(same);
-  });
-
-  it('unknown and mcp tools fall back to name + arg-shape, never raw values', () => {
-    const a = normalizeToolCall('mcp__tilth__tilth_search', { query: 'handleRequest', kind: 'symbol' });
-    const b = normalizeToolCall('mcp__tilth__tilth_search', { query: 'parseLine', kind: 'symbol' });
-    expect(a).toBe(b);
-    expect(a).toBe('mcp__tilth__tilth_search({kind,query})');
-    expect(a).not.toContain('handleRequest');
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/novelty.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/novelty.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 7e551a8..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/novelty.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,155 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * The analyst tier's already-encoded novelty filter (design:
- * docs/loop-hole-episodes-design.md, v3, "L2 analyst"; check c4 in
- * propose.loop.html). Three things must be true, each proven without any
- * hand-listed language (fixture-leak.spec.ts enforces that mechanically):
- *
- *  1. Novelty is COMPUTED, not asked — the real static embedding tier
- *     (potion-base-8M, the same one cluster.ts proves paraphrase recall on)
- *     decides covered vs. novel, with zero LLM calls.
- *  2. The model only CONFIRMS — its own claim can never move the computed
- *     verdict, whether it agrees or not.
- *  3. A covered finding is never top-ranked — `rankByNovelty` enforces that
- *     as a structural sort property, not a single hardcoded example.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { StaticEmbeddingProvider, defaultModelDir } from '../src/embed/static.js';
-import type { EmbeddingProvider } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import {
-  confirmNovelty,
-  judgeNovelty,
-  judgeNoveltyBatch,
-  rankByNovelty,
-  type RankableByNovelty,
-} from '../src/novelty.js';
-import {
-  NOVELTY_ALREADY_ENCODED,
-  NOVELTY_COVERED_FINDINGS,
-  NOVELTY_NOVEL_FINDING,
-} from './fixtures.js';
-
-const embedder = StaticEmbeddingProvider.load(defaultModelDir());
-
-describe('judgeNovelty — deterministic computation against the already-encoded set', () => {
-  it('marks a finding covered when it restates an already-encoded standing instruction', async () => {
-    const judgment = await judgeNovelty(NOVELTY_COVERED_FINDINGS[0], NOVELTY_ALREADY_ENCODED, embedder);
-    expect(judgment.verdict).toBe('covered');
-    expect(judgment.matchedEntry).toBe(NOVELTY_ALREADY_ENCODED.instructions[0]);
-    expect(judgment.similarity).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0.7);
-  });
-
-  it('marks a finding covered when it restates an already-invocable skill', async () => {
-    const judgment = await judgeNovelty(NOVELTY_COVERED_FINDINGS[1], NOVELTY_ALREADY_ENCODED, embedder);
-    expect(judgment.verdict).toBe('covered');
-    expect(judgment.matchedEntry).toBe(NOVELTY_ALREADY_ENCODED.skills[0]);
-  });
-
-  it('marks a finding covered when it restates an already-standing loop', async () => {
-    const judgment = await judgeNovelty(NOVELTY_COVERED_FINDINGS[2], NOVELTY_ALREADY_ENCODED, embedder);
-    expect(judgment.verdict).toBe('covered');
-    expect(judgment.matchedEntry).toBe(NOVELTY_ALREADY_ENCODED.loops[0]);
-  });
-
-  it('marks a finding novel when it matches nothing in the already-encoded set', async () => {
-    const judgment = await judgeNovelty(NOVELTY_NOVEL_FINDING, NOVELTY_ALREADY_ENCODED, embedder);
-    expect(judgment.verdict).toBe('novel');
-    expect(judgment.matchedEntry).toBeUndefined();
-    expect(judgment.similarity).toBeLessThan(0.7);
-  });
-
-  it('is deterministic: the same finding against the same set yields byte-identical similarity every time', async () => {
-    const first = await judgeNovelty(NOVELTY_COVERED_FINDINGS[0], NOVELTY_ALREADY_ENCODED, embedder);
-    const second = await judgeNovelty(NOVELTY_COVERED_FINDINGS[0], NOVELTY_ALREADY_ENCODED, embedder);
-    expect(second).toEqual(first);
-  });
-
-  it('marks everything novel, and never calls the embedder, when nothing is on record yet', async () => {
-    let calls = 0;
-    const countingEmbedder: EmbeddingProvider = {
-      embed(texts) {
-        calls += 1;
-        return embedder.embed(texts);
-      },
-    };
-    const judgment = await judgeNovelty(
-      NOVELTY_COVERED_FINDINGS[0],
-      { instructions: [], skills: [], loops: [] },
-      countingEmbedder,
-    );
-    expect(judgment).toEqual({ verdict: 'novel', similarity: 0 });
-    expect(calls).toBe(0);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('judgeNoveltyBatch — one pass over many findings', () => {
-  it('judges each finding independently against the same already-encoded set', async () => {
-    const items = [
-      { id: 'a', text: NOVELTY_COVERED_FINDINGS[0] },
-      { id: 'b', text: NOVELTY_NOVEL_FINDING },
-    ];
-    const verdicts = await judgeNoveltyBatch(items, NOVELTY_ALREADY_ENCODED, embedder);
-    expect(verdicts.get('a')?.verdict).toBe('covered');
-    expect(verdicts.get('b')?.verdict).toBe('novel');
-  });
-});
-
-describe('confirmNovelty — the model only confirms, it never decides', () => {
-  it('keeps a computed "covered" verdict even when the model claims the finding is novel', () => {
-    const confirmed = confirmNovelty({ verdict: 'covered', similarity: 0.79, matchedEntry: 'x' }, 'novel');
-    expect(confirmed.verdict).toBe('covered');
-    expect(confirmed.modelAgreed).toBe(false);
-  });
-
-  it('keeps a computed "novel" verdict even when the model wrongly claims the finding is covered', () => {
-    const confirmed = confirmNovelty({ verdict: 'novel', similarity: 0.3 }, 'covered');
-    expect(confirmed.verdict).toBe('novel');
-    expect(confirmed.modelAgreed).toBe(false);
-  });
-
-  it('records agreement without changing the verdict when the model concurs', () => {
-    const confirmed = confirmNovelty({ verdict: 'covered', similarity: 0.79, matchedEntry: 'x' }, 'covered');
-    expect(confirmed.verdict).toBe('covered');
-    expect(confirmed.modelAgreed).toBe(true);
-  });
-
-  it('leaves modelAgreed null, and the verdict untouched, when the model offers no claim', () => {
-    const confirmed = confirmNovelty({ verdict: 'novel', similarity: 0.1 }, undefined);
-    expect(confirmed.verdict).toBe('novel');
-    expect(confirmed.modelAgreed).toBeNull();
-  });
-});
-
-describe('rankByNovelty — a covered finding is never top-ranked', () => {
-  it('ranks a high-scoring covered finding below a low-scoring novel finding', () => {
-    const ranked = rankByNovelty([
-      { id: 'covered-but-loud', novelty: 'covered', score: 0.98 },
-      { id: 'novel-but-quiet', novelty: 'novel', score: 0.02 },
-    ] as const);
-    expect(ranked.map((item) => item.id)).toEqual(['novel-but-quiet', 'covered-but-loud']);
-  });
-
-  it('breaks ties within the same novelty bucket by score, highest first', () => {
-    const ranked = rankByNovelty([
-      { id: 'low', novelty: 'novel', score: 0.2 },
-      { id: 'high', novelty: 'novel', score: 0.9 },
-      { id: 'mid', novelty: 'novel', score: 0.5 },
-    ] as const);
-    expect(ranked.map((item) => item.id)).toEqual(['high', 'mid', 'low']);
-  });
-
-  it('never lets any covered item precede any novel item, across a mixed and shuffled set', () => {
-    const items: RankableByNovelty[] = [
-      { novelty: 'covered', score: 0.99 },
-      { novelty: 'novel', score: 0.55 },
-      { novelty: 'covered', score: 0.7 },
-      { novelty: 'novel', score: 0.1 },
-      { novelty: 'covered', score: 0.6 },
-      { novelty: 'novel', score: 0.95 },
-    ];
-    const ranked = rankByNovelty(items);
-    const firstCoveredIndex = ranked.findIndex((item) => item.novelty === 'covered');
-    const lastNovelIndex = ranked.map((item) => item.novelty).lastIndexOf('novel');
-    expect(firstCoveredIndex).toBeGreaterThan(lastNovelIndex);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/origin-hygiene.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/origin-hygiene.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 8e47cf7..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/origin-hygiene.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,117 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Corpus origin hygiene: a "user" record in a Claude Code transcript is not
- * automatically a genuine human ask. Two structural markers say otherwise —
- * `isSidechain: true` (the line belongs to a sub-agent's own transcript,
- * spawned by the Task tool, not the operator) and `userType` set to anything
- * other than `"external"` (the transcript's own label for a synthetic/
- * injected turn). Both are read off fields the real JSONL already carries;
- * neither is inferred from the turn's text.
- */
-
-import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
-import { ClaudeCodeAdapter } from '../src/claude-adapter.js';
-import { runExtract } from '../src/extract.js';
-import { StateStore } from '../src/store.js';
-import type { ActionEvent } from '../src/types.js';
-import { makeCorpus } from './helpers.js';
-
-const CWD = '/Users/me/repo';
-
-function userEvents(store: StateStore) {
-  return store.events.filter((e): e is Extract<ActionEvent, { kind: 'user' }> => e.kind === 'user');
-}
-
-/** A record shaped exactly like a real `~/.claude/projects/.../*.jsonl` line,
- * with the origin-marking fields real transcripts carry alongside `type`. */
-function rawUserRecord(text: string, timestamp: string, extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}): unknown {
-  return {
-    parentUuid: null,
-    isSidechain: false,
-    userType: 'external',
-    cwd: CWD,
-    sessionId: 'sess-origin',
-    version: '2.1.0',
-    type: 'user',
-    message: { role: 'user', content: text },
-    uuid: 'uuid-' + timestamp,
-    timestamp,
-    ...extra,
-  };
-}
-
-describe('corpus origin hygiene', () => {
-  it('interns a genuine external, non-sidechain turn as intent', async () => {
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-origin',
-        records: [rawUserRecord('please clean up the stale branches', '2026-07-01T09:00:00.000Z')],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-
-    expect(userEvents(store)).toHaveLength(1);
-  });
-
-  it('never interns a sidechain record as an intent turn', async () => {
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
