-        sessionId: 'sess-origin',
-        records: [
-          rawUserRecord('genuine kickoff ask', '2026-07-01T09:00:00.000Z'),
-          // A sub-agent's own transcript line, folded into the parent session
-          // file the way Task-tool sidechains actually appear.
-          rawUserRecord('act as a senior reviewer and check this diff', '2026-07-01T09:00:05.000Z', { isSidechain: true }),
-        ],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-
-    const events = userEvents(store);
-    expect(events).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(events[0]?.turn).not.toBe(store.internTurn('act as a senior reviewer and check this diff'));
-  });
-
-  it('never interns a non-external userType as an intent turn', async () => {
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-origin',
-        records: [
-          rawUserRecord('genuine kickoff ask', '2026-07-01T09:00:00.000Z'),
-          // Some transcripts label injected/synthetic turns with a non-
-          // "external" userType (e.g. "internal") rather than omitting it.
-          rawUserRecord('synthetic continuation prompt', '2026-07-01T09:00:05.000Z', { userType: 'internal' }),
-        ],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-
-    expect(userEvents(store)).toHaveLength(1);
-  });
-
-  it('drops a record that is both sidechain and non-external in one shot', async () => {
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-origin',
-        records: [
-          rawUserRecord('genuine kickoff ask', '2026-07-01T09:00:00.000Z'),
-          rawUserRecord('benchmark harness worker turn', '2026-07-01T09:00:05.000Z', { isSidechain: true, userType: 'internal' }),
-        ],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-
-    expect(userEvents(store)).toHaveLength(1);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/paraphrase.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/paraphrase.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 78f0526..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/paraphrase.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,80 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Paraphrase recall at the semantic tier — differently-worded same-intent
- * instructions must cohere WITHOUT any hand-listed language in src (no
- * synonym tables, no phrase regexes, no concept lists; fixture-leak.spec
- * enforces that ban mechanically). The signal comes from the static distilled
- * embedding model, so the same behavior holds for phrasings and domains this
- * file never names — that is the difference between a product and an answer
- * key (docs/overfit-postmortem.md).
- *
- * Scope honesty: these groups are synonym-anchored (restart/reboot,
- * clear/wipe, deploy/ship/roll-out). Zero-lexical-overlap asks ("status?" vs
- * "how are things looking", measured 0.04–0.45) are beyond ANY cheap
- * embedding and belong to the LLM merge tier — pinning them here would only
- * invite the next lookup table.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { detectRecurrenceCandidates } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import { StaticEmbeddingProvider, defaultModelDir } from '../src/embed/static.js';
-import { PARAPHRASE_GROUPS } from './fixtures.js';
-import type { ActionEvent, StoredState } from '../src/types.js';
-
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-const START = Date.parse('2026-06-01T09:00:00Z');
-const NOW = Date.parse('2026-06-08T09:00:00Z');
-
-const ALL_TURNS = PARAPHRASE_GROUPS.flat();
-
-/** Six events per group across six days, cycling each group's phrasings. */
-function paraphraseState(): StoredState {
-  const events: ActionEvent[] = [];
-  let seq = 0;
-  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i += 1) {
-    let offset = 0;
-    for (const group of PARAPHRASE_GROUPS) {
-      events.push(userEvent(offset + (i % group.length), i, seq++));
-      offset += group.length;
-    }
-  }
-  return { version: 1, watermark: {}, turns: [...ALL_TURNS], events };
-}
-
-function userEvent(turn: number, day: number, sequence: number): ActionEvent {
-  const epoch = START + day * DAY + sequence * 1000;
-  return {
-    kind: 'user',
-    ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(),
-    epoch,
-    project: '/Users/me/repo',
-    session: `session-${day}`,
-    turn,
-  };
-}
-
-describe('paraphrase recall (semantic tier)', () => {
-  it('collapses each synonym-anchored group into exactly one cluster', async () => {
-    const embedder = StaticEmbeddingProvider.load(defaultModelDir());
-    const candidates = await detectRecurrenceCandidates(paraphraseState(), { now: NOW, embedder });
-    const intents = candidates.filter((candidate) => candidate.kind === 'intent');
-
-    // Exactly one cluster per group: none fragmented, none merged with a neighbor.
-    expect(intents).toHaveLength(PARAPHRASE_GROUPS.length);
-
-    for (const group of PARAPHRASE_GROUPS) {
-      const cluster = intents.find((candidate) =>
-        group.some((turn) => candidate.provenance.some((line) => line.includes(turn))),
-      );
-      expect(cluster, `no cluster found for group "${group[0]}"`).toBeDefined();
-      // Every phrasing of the group landed in this same cluster's provenance.
-      for (const turn of group) {
-        expect(
-          cluster!.provenance.some((line) => line.includes(turn)),
-          `"${turn}" missing from its group's cluster`,
-        ).toBe(true);
-      }
-      // All six events for the group are accounted for by this one cluster.
-      expect(cluster!.eventCount).toBe(6);
-    }
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/pipeline.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/pipeline.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 44e12aa..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/pipeline.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,167 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * The whole-pipeline check: one command runs extract → cluster → propose →
- * report over a corpus dir and writes the briefing + draft pages to an out dir.
- *
- * Two lenses, both driving the real modules with no mock of the unit under test:
- *  - the *command form* — spawn `bun src/run.ts <corpus> <out>` exactly as the
- *    loop file advertises, and prove the files it leaves behind are real loop
- *    pages (parse + lint clean, every check open) and a briefing that links them;
- *  - the *pipeline contract* — call `runPipeline` in-process to assert the
- *    honesty invariants that must survive end-to-end: every emitted citation
- *    exists verbatim in the corpus, and a second pass over an unchanged corpus
- *    is a no-op (the incremental watermark the design promises).
- */
-
-import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
-import { existsSync, mkdtempSync, readFileSync, readdirSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { homedir, tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { extractPayload } from '../../../loop/src/core/extract.js';
-import { lint } from '../../../loop/src/core/lint.js';
-import { parse } from '../../../loop/src/core/parse.js';
-import { ClaudeCodeAdapter } from '../src/claude-adapter.js';
-import { corpusStrings } from '../src/propose.js';
-import { StateStore } from '../src/store.js';
-import { runExtract } from '../src/extract.js';
-import { runPipeline } from '../src/run.js';
-import { makeCorpus, toolCall, userTurn } from './helpers.js';
-
-const RUN_TS = fileURLToPath(new URL('../src/run.ts', import.meta.url));
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-const START = Date.parse('2026-06-01T09:00:00Z');
-
-/** A corpus with two clearly recurring rituals plus a repeated user request. */
-function ritualCorpus(): string {
-  const sessions = [];
-  for (let day = 0; day < 5; day += 1) {
-    const at = (offset: number) => new Date(START + day * DAY + offset * 60_000).toISOString();
-    sessions.push({
-      dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-      sessionId: `session-${day}`,
-      records: [
-        userTurn('What changed in competitor pricing this week?', at(0)),
-        toolCall('Bash', { command: `lsof -i :${8471 + day}` }, at(1)),
-        toolCall('WebFetch', { url: `https://rival.example.com/pricing?d=${day}` }, at(2)),
-        toolCall('Bash', { command: `git status` }, at(3)),
-      ],
-    });
-  }
-  return makeCorpus(sessions);
-}
-
-function outDir(): string {
-  return mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-pipeline-out-'));
-}
-
-/** Locate the bun runtime the loop file's command relies on. */
-function resolveBun(): string {
-  const candidates = [
-    process.env.BUN_PATH,
-    'bun',
-    join(homedir(), '.bun', 'bin', 'bun'),
-  ].filter((c): c is string => typeof c === 'string' && c.length > 0);
-  for (const candidate of candidates) {
-    try {
-      execFileSync(candidate, ['--version'], { stdio: 'ignore' });
-      return candidate;
-    } catch {
-      // try the next candidate
-    }
-  }
-  throw new Error('bun runtime not found — required to run `bun src/run.ts`');
-}
-
-/** Every `- evidence: <line>` a report page cites. */
-function citedEvidence(payload: string): string[] {
-  return payload
-    .split('\n')
-    .filter((line) => line.startsWith('- evidence: '))
-    .map((line) => line.slice('- evidence: '.length));
-}
-
-describe('loop-hole pipeline entry (bun src/run.ts <corpus> <out>)', () => {
-  it('runs the whole pipeline as one command and writes a briefing + draft loops', () => {
-    const corpus = ritualCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-    const bun = resolveBun();
-
-    // Judgment tiers off: the e2e must be deterministic — a live model call
-    // inside a test is nondeterminism and spend, not coverage. The judged
-    // path is proven by triage.spec + facet-cache.spec + synthesis.spec
-    // (application layer) plus the real corpus.
-    execFileSync(bun, [RUN_TS, corpus, out], {
-      stdio: 'pipe',
-      env: {
-        ...process.env,
-        LOOP_HOLE_TRIAGE: 'off',
-        LOOP_HOLE_ANALYST: 'off',
-        LOOP_HOLE_FACETS: 'off',
-        LOOP_HOLE_SYNTHESIS: 'off',
-      },
-    });
-
-    // The briefing page is written and is a real loop document.
-    const briefing = join(out, 'loop-hole-briefing.loop.html');
-    expect(existsSync(briefing)).toBe(true);
-
-    const drafts = readdirSync(out).filter((name) => name.endsWith('-draft.loop.html'));
-    expect(drafts.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-
-    const briefingHtml = readFileSync(briefing, 'utf8');
-    const briefingPayload = extractPayload(briefingHtml)!.payload;
-    expect(briefingPayload).not.toBeNull();
-
-    for (const name of drafts) {
-      const html = readFileSync(join(out, name), 'utf8');
-      const extracted = extractPayload(html);
-      expect(extracted).not.toBeNull();
-      const payload = extracted!.payload;
-      const doc = parse(payload);
-
-      expect(doc.kind).toBe('loop');
-      expect(doc.checks.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-      expect(doc.checks.every((check) => check.state === 'open')).toBe(true);
-      expect(lint(payload, { id: name.replace(/\.loop\.html$/, '') })).toEqual([]);
-
-      // The briefing links every draft it emitted — no orphan pages.
-      expect(briefingPayload).toContain(name);
-    }
-  });
-
-  it('emits only citations that exist verbatim in the corpus, end to end', async () => {
-    const corpus = ritualCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out });
-    expect(result.pages.items.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-
-    // Re-derive the corpus's citable atoms independently of the pipeline.
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(corpus), store);
-    const atoms = corpusStrings(store);
-
-    for (const item of result.pages.items) {
-      const opportunity = readFileSync(item.opportunity.path, 'utf8');
-      const payload = extractPayload(opportunity)!.payload;
-      const cited = citedEvidence(payload);
-      expect(cited.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-      for (const evidence of cited) {
-        expect(atoms.has(evidence)).toBe(true);
-      }
-    }
-  });
-
-  it('is incremental — a second pass over an unchanged corpus reads no new sessions', async () => {
-    const corpus = ritualCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-
-    const first = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out });
-    expect(first.extract.newSessions).toBe(5);
-
-    const second = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out });
-    expect(second.extract.newSessions).toBe(0);
-    expect(second.extract.newEvents).toBe(0);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/polling-origin.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/polling-origin.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index a775759..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/polling-origin.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,111 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Polling origin hygiene (SYN-G, episodes.loop.html c8) — a harness-injected
- * `<task-notification>` block is a literal "user" record in the raw
- * transcript, but no human typed it. The round-4 briefing counted 50 of
- * these as one person "checking on it" (report.loop.html Tried 2026-07-21,
- * defect 1). extract.ts already refuses other CLI/harness wrappers the same
- * way it refuses sidechain/non-external records (origin-hygiene.spec.ts) —
- * this proves that refusal reaches all the way through episode segmentation
- * into polling detection end to end, not just at extraction in isolation.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { ClaudeCodeAdapter } from '../src/claude-adapter.js';
-import { runExtract } from '../src/extract.js';
-import { segmentEpisodes, type Episode } from '../src/episodes.js';
-import { detectPollingFamilies } from '../src/polling.js';
-import { StateStore } from '../src/store.js';
-import { makeCorpus, taskNotificationTurn, toolCall, userTurn } from './helpers.js';
-
-const CWD = '/Users/me/repo';
-const MIN = 60_000;
-const BASE = Date.parse('2026-07-01T09:00:00.000Z');
-
-function at(offsetMs: number): string {
-  return new Date(BASE + offsetMs).toISOString();
-}
-
-async function extractEpisodes(root: string): Promise<Episode[]> {
-  const store = StateStore.empty();
-  await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-  return segmentEpisodes(store);
-}
-
-/** A substantive kickoff: two real tool calls, never mistaken for a "quick ask". */
-function kickoffRecords(): unknown[] {
-  return [
-    userTurn('kick off the background benchmark', at(0), CWD),
-    toolCall('Bash', { command: 'npm run bench -- --background' }, at(1_000), [], CWD),
-    toolCall('Bash', { command: 'git tag bench-run' }, at(2_000), [], CWD),
-  ];
-}
-
-/** A substantive closer: real work, well past the "quick ask" duration ceiling. */
-function closerRecords(): unknown[] {
-  return [userTurn('great, ship it', at(40 * MIN), CWD), toolCall('Bash', { command: 'git push --tags' }, at(43 * MIN), [], CWD)];
-}
-
-describe('polling origin hygiene', () => {
-  it('a burst of harness task-notification records forms zero polling families', async () => {
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-notif-only',
-        records: [
-          ...kickoffRecords(),
-          ...Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) =>
-            taskNotificationTurn('bench-1', 'Watch benchmark progress', `[${i + 1}/6] step`, at(10 * MIN + i * 5 * MIN), CWD),
-          ),
-          ...closerRecords(),
-        ],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const episodes = await extractEpisodes(root);
-    expect(await detectPollingFamilies(episodes)).toEqual([]);
-  });
-
-  it('an identical-timing burst of real user check-ins still detects one polling family', async () => {
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-real-checkins',
-        records: [
-          ...kickoffRecords(),
-          ...Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => userTurn('any update on the benchmark?', at(10 * MIN + i * 5 * MIN), CWD)),
-          ...closerRecords(),
-        ],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const episodes = await extractEpisodes(root);
-    const families = await detectPollingFamilies(episodes);
-
-    expect(families).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(families[0]?.askCount).toBe(6);
-  });
-
-  it('a mixed burst counts only the human asks toward askCount, never the notifications', async () => {
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-mixed',
-        records: [
-          ...kickoffRecords(),
-          userTurn('any update?', at(10 * MIN), CWD),
-          taskNotificationTurn('bench-1', 'Watch benchmark progress', '[1/6] step', at(15 * MIN), CWD),
-          userTurn('still going?', at(20 * MIN), CWD),
-          taskNotificationTurn('bench-1', 'Watch benchmark progress', '[2/6] step', at(25 * MIN), CWD),
-          userTurn('nearly there?', at(30 * MIN), CWD),
-          ...closerRecords(),
-        ],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const episodes = await extractEpisodes(root);
-    const families = await detectPollingFamilies(episodes);
-
-    expect(families).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(families[0]?.askCount).toBe(3);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/polling-sameness.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/polling-sameness.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 6c06959..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/polling-sameness.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,149 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Polling same-ask semantics (SYN-H, episodes.loop.html c9) — c3 already
- * claims a polling family is a "same-intent, short-interval ask family";
- * until now the code only enforced the timing half of that sentence. A
- * rapid design conversation ("why did you name it that?" then a run of
- * unrelated follow-up questions) fired inside one short-interval, trivial-
- * trace burst was indistinguishable from a real "checking on it" burst —
- * both cleared `runsWithinSession`'s timing gate identically.
- *
- * The fix reuses cluster.ts's own embedding seam (never a second embedding
- * stance, never a phrase list): within a timing-qualified run, member asks
- * must also cohere as one connected component at the SAME calibrated
- * threshold `detectRecurrenceCandidates` already uses. Timing narrows to
- * candidate runs; sameness decides real membership within them.
- *
- * These fixtures are calibrated against the real bundled model
- * (potion-base-8M, measured in `.loop-scratch/calibrate-sameness.mjs`, not
- * committed) — own paraphrases, not `POLLING_BURST_TURNS` (see the note on
- * that fixture in fixtures.ts: those six phrases are pragmatically terse
- * enough to sit BELOW this same threshold too, 0.12-0.44, so they stay
- * scoped to demonstrating cluster.ts's lexical blind spot, never asserted
- * here as a "should cohere" case).
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { ClaudeCodeAdapter } from '../src/claude-adapter.js';
-import { runExtract } from '../src/extract.js';
-import { segmentEpisodes } from '../src/episodes.js';
-import { detectPollingFamilies } from '../src/polling.js';
-import { StaticEmbeddingProvider, defaultModelDir } from '../src/embed/static.js';
-import { StateStore } from '../src/store.js';
-import { makeCorpus, toolCall, userTurn } from './helpers.js';
-import { POLLING_CHECKIN_TURNS, POLLING_OFFTOPIC_TURNS } from './fixtures.js';
-
-const CWD = '/Users/me/repo';
-const MIN = 60_000;
-const BASE = Date.parse('2026-07-15T09:00:00.000Z');
-
-function at(offsetMs: number): string {
-  return new Date(BASE + offsetMs).toISOString();
-}
-
-const embedder = StaticEmbeddingProvider.load(defaultModelDir());
-
-/** A substantive kickoff: real trace, never mistaken for a "quick ask". */
-function kickoffRecords(): unknown[] {
-  return [
-    userTurn('kick off the nightly ingest job', at(0), CWD),
-    toolCall('Bash', { command: 'npm run ingest -- --background' }, at(1_000), [], CWD),
-    toolCall('Bash', { command: 'git tag ingest-run' }, at(2_000), [], CWD),
-  ];
-}
-
-/** A substantive closer: real work, well past the "quick ask" duration ceiling. */
-function closerRecords(): unknown[] {
-  return [userTurn('good, wrap it up', at(40 * MIN), CWD), toolCall('Bash', { command: 'git push --tags' }, at(43 * MIN), [], CWD)];
-}
-
-async function detectFamiliesFor(root: string) {
-  const store = StateStore.empty();
-  await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-  const episodes = segmentEpisodes(store);
-  return detectPollingFamilies(episodes, { turns: store.turns, embedder });
-}
-
-describe('polling same-ask semantics', () => {
-  it('a rapid burst of genuinely different quick questions never forms a polling family', async () => {
-    // Six distinct technical questions, one per topic — a design/review
-    // conversation, not a check-in. Measured max pairwise cosine 0.292 on
-    // potion-base-8M, far under the 0.70 calibrated threshold.
-    const questions = [...POLLING_OFFTOPIC_TURNS];
-
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-design-chat',
-        records: [
-          ...kickoffRecords(),
-          ...questions.map((text, i) => userTurn(text, at(10 * MIN + i * 5 * MIN), CWD)),
-          ...closerRecords(),
-        ],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const families = await detectFamiliesFor(root);
-    expect(families).toEqual([]);
-  });
-
-  it('a same check-in asked six different ways still collapses into one polling family', async () => {
-    // Own paraphrase set (not POLLING_BURST_TURNS): a real "is the migration
-    // done" check-in, six distinct phrasings. Measured on potion-base-8M:
-    // one connected component of all six at the 0.70 calibrated threshold
-    // (pairwise range 0.50-0.88).
-    const paraphrases = [...POLLING_CHECKIN_TURNS];
-
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-real-checkins',
-        records: [
-          ...kickoffRecords(),
-          ...paraphrases.map((text, i) => userTurn(text, at(10 * MIN + i * 5 * MIN), CWD)),
-          ...closerRecords(),
-        ],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const families = await detectFamiliesFor(root);
-    expect(families).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(families[0]?.askCount).toBe(6);
-  });
-
-  it('mixed in with the design questions, only the same-ask subset collapses into a family', async () => {
-    // Three off-topic questions interleaved with a five-ask "is it done"
-    // check-in family, all inside the same timing burst. Same-ask semantics
-    // must recover the real family, not fragment it and not swallow the
-    // off-topic asks into it.
-    const offTopic = POLLING_OFFTOPIC_TURNS.slice(0, 3);
-    const checkins = POLLING_CHECKIN_TURNS.slice(0, 5);
-
-    const interleaved: { text: string; offsetMs: number }[] = [];
-    let slot = 0;
-    for (const text of checkins) {
-      interleaved.push({ text, offsetMs: 10 * MIN + slot * 5 * MIN });
-      slot += 1;
-    }
-    for (const text of offTopic) {
-      interleaved.push({ text, offsetMs: 10 * MIN + slot * 5 * MIN });
-      slot += 1;
-    }
-    interleaved.sort((a, b) => a.offsetMs - b.offsetMs);
-
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-mixed-burst',
-        records: [
-          ...kickoffRecords(),
-          ...interleaved.map(({ text, offsetMs }) => userTurn(text, at(offsetMs), CWD)),
-          ...closerRecords(),
-        ],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const families = await detectFamiliesFor(root);
-    expect(families).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(families[0]?.askCount).toBe(5);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/polling.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/polling.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index d75ea3d..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/polling.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,226 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Polling collapse — a same-intent, short-interval "checking on it" burst
- * fired while background work is still running must surface as ONE polling
- * shape (a push-loop candidate), never as N separate intent clusters. The
- * contrast case below proves the risk is real: cluster.ts's embedding-based
- * intent grouping is a documented blind spot for zero-lexical-overlap
- * phrasings (embed/static.ts's "Honest limits"), so a real burst phrased a
- * different way each time fragments into near-singletons and never clears
- * the recurrence floor. polling.ts closes that gap by joining on timing and
- * trace shape alone, never on what the ask says.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { detectPollingFamilies } from '../src/polling.js';
-import type { Episode, EpisodeAction } from '../src/episodes.js';
-import { detectRecurrenceCandidates } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import type { ActionEvent, StoredState } from '../src/types.js';
-import { POLLING_BURST_TURNS } from './fixtures.js';
-
-const BASE = Date.parse('2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z');
-const MIN = 60_000;
-
-function action(template: string, day: number, offsetMs: number): EpisodeAction {
-  const epoch = BASE + day * 86_400_000 + offsetMs;
-  return { tool: template.split('(')[0] ?? template, template, outcome: 'ok', ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(), epoch };
-}
-
-/** A quick "checking on it" ask: no substantive trace, resolves instantly. */
-function quickAsk(session: string, project: string, turn: number, offsetMs: number): Episode {
-  const epoch = BASE + offsetMs;
-  return {
-    session,
-    project,
-    turn,
-    ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(),
-    epoch,
-    actions: [],
-    durationMs: 0,
-    tokens: { input: 0, output: 0 },
-    resolution: 'resolved',
-  };
-}
-
-/** A substantive ask: real work, real duration — never mistaken for a poll. */
-function substantiveAsk(session: string, project: string, turn: number, offsetMs: number, actions: EpisodeAction[]): Episode {
-  const epoch = BASE + offsetMs;
-  const last = actions[actions.length - 1];
-  return {
-    session,
-    project,
-    turn,
-    ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(),
-    epoch,
-    actions,
-    durationMs: last === undefined ? 0 : last.epoch - epoch,
-    tokens: { input: 0, output: 0 },
-    resolution: 'resolved',
-  };
-}
-
-describe('polling recall', () => {
-  it('a same-intent, short-interval ask family during long-running background work collapses into one polling shape', async () => {
-    const session = 'sess-deploy';
-    const project = '/Users/me/repoA';
-
-    // Kicks off the background work — substantive, real trace, not a poll.
-    const kickoff = substantiveAsk(session, project, 0, 0, [
-      action('Bash(npm run deploy -- --background)', 0, 1_000),
-      action('Bash(git tag v1.2.3-rc)', 0, 2_000),
-    ]);
-
-    // The burst: six "checking on it" asks, 5 minutes apart, 25 minutes total —
-    // trivial traces, phrased however you like (this module reads none of it).
-    const burst = Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) =>
-      quickAsk(session, project, i + 1, 10 * MIN + i * 5 * MIN),
-    );
-
-    // The state transition that ends the polling: real work again (a
-    // three-minute trace clears the "quick ask" duration ceiling).
-    const closer = substantiveAsk(session, project, 7, 40 * MIN, [
-      action('Bash(git push --tags)', 0, 43 * MIN),
-    ]);
-
-    // Distractors elsewhere: an unrelated session with its own one-off asks,
-    // spaced far apart, that must never be swept into the family.
-    const distractors = [
-      quickAsk('sess-other', '/Users/me/repoB', 8, 3 * 86_400_000),
-      quickAsk('sess-other', '/Users/me/repoB', 9, 3 * 86_400_000 + 3 * 3_600_000),
-    ];
-
-    const episodes = [kickoff, ...burst, closer, ...distractors];
-    const families = await detectPollingFamilies(episodes);
-
-    expect(families).toHaveLength(1);
-    const family = families[0];
-    if (family === undefined) throw new Error('expected one polling family');
-    expect(family.session).toBe(session);
-    expect(family.project).toBe(project);
-    expect(family.askCount).toBe(6);
-    expect(family.cadenceMs).toEqual({ min: 5 * MIN, median: 5 * MIN, max: 5 * MIN });
-    expect(family.spanMs).toBe(25 * MIN);
-    expect(family.firstSeen).toBe(burst[0]?.ts);
-    expect(family.lastSeen).toBe(burst[5]?.ts);
-    expect(family.provenance).toHaveLength(6);
-    expect(new Set(family.provenance).size).toBe(6);
-
-    // The kickoff and the closer — real work — never join the family.
-    const provenanceTurns = family.provenance.map((p) => p.match(/turn (\d+)/)?.[1]);
-    expect(provenanceTurns).not.toContain('0');
-    expect(provenanceTurns).not.toContain('7');
-  });
-
-  it('the same burst, phrased a different way each time, is invisible to intent clustering alone', async () => {
-    // Prove the fixture actually poses the risk the check names: six asks,
-    // one recurring "checking on it" intent, phrased with zero shared
-    // vocabulary between any pair — a real burst, not a contrived one.
-    const turns = [...POLLING_BURST_TURNS];
-    const events: ActionEvent[] = turns.map((_, i) => ({
-      kind: 'user',
-      ts: new Date(BASE + 10 * MIN + i * 5 * MIN).toISOString(),
-      epoch: BASE + 10 * MIN + i * 5 * MIN,
-      project: '/Users/me/repoA',
-      session: 'sess-deploy',
-      turn: i,
-    }));
-    const state: StoredState = { version: 2, watermark: {}, turns, events };
-
-    const candidates = await detectRecurrenceCandidates(state, {
-      now: BASE + 60 * MIN,
-      minIntentOccurrences: 3,
-    });
-
-    // Not one candidate unifies the burst — intent-only counting sees six
-    // one-off asks below the recurrence floor, not the one family that's
-    // really there.
-    const unifiesTheBurst = candidates.some((c) => c.kind === 'intent' && c.eventCount >= 6);
-    expect(unifiesTheBurst).toBe(false);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('polling precision: adversarial near-misses must not form a family', () => {
-  it('a short-interval burst whose overall span never gets long enough is not "long-running background work"', async () => {
-    // Four quick asks, 2 minutes apart — a normal fast back-and-forth, total
-    // span 6 minutes, nowhere near the 20-minute floor.
-    const episodes = Array.from({ length: 4 }, (_, i) =>
-      quickAsk('sess-fast', '/Users/me/repoC', i, i * 2 * MIN),
-    );
-
-    expect(await detectPollingFamilies(episodes)).toEqual([]);
-  });
-
-  it('asks spaced too far apart never form a run, however many there are', async () => {
-    // Six quick asks, 15 minutes apart — enough total span, but each gap
-    // clears the default 10-minute "short interval" ceiling.
-    const episodes = Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) =>
-      quickAsk('sess-slow', '/Users/me/repoD', i, i * 15 * MIN),
-    );
-
-    expect(await detectPollingFamilies(episodes)).toEqual([]);
-  });
-
-  it('substantive asks at short intervals over a long span are real iterative work, not polling', async () => {
-    // Six asks every 5 minutes for 30 minutes, but each does real work — a
-    // fast, substantive iteration loop, not a trivial "is it done" burst.
-    const episodes = Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) =>
-      substantiveAsk('sess-iterate', '/Users/me/repoE', i, i * 5 * MIN, [
-        action('Edit({file})', 0, i * 5 * MIN + 30_000),
-        action('Bash(npm test)', 0, i * 5 * MIN + 3 * MIN),
-      ]),
-    );
-
-    expect(await detectPollingFamilies(episodes)).toEqual([]);
-  });
-
-  it('a run below the minimum ask count never forms a family', async () => {
-    const episodes = [
-      quickAsk('sess-short', '/Users/me/repoF', 0, 0),
-      quickAsk('sess-short', '/Users/me/repoF', 1, 5 * MIN),
-    ];
-
-    expect(await detectPollingFamilies(episodes)).toEqual([]);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('polling precision: distinct bursts stay distinct', () => {
-  it('two separate polling bursts in one session, split by real work, yield two families — not one merged run', async () => {
-    const session = 'sess-two-jobs';
-    const project = '/Users/me/repoG';
-
-    const firstBurst = Array.from({ length: 5 }, (_, i) =>
-      quickAsk(session, project, i, i * 6 * MIN),
-    );
-    // A three-minute trace — clears the "quick ask" ceiling, breaking the run.
-    const middleWork = substantiveAsk(session, project, 5, 40 * MIN, [
-      action('Bash(npm run build)', 0, 43 * MIN),
-    ]);
-    const secondBurst = Array.from({ length: 5 }, (_, i) =>
-      quickAsk(session, project, i + 6, 60 * MIN + i * 6 * MIN),
-    );
-
-    const episodes = [...firstBurst, middleWork, ...secondBurst];
-    const families = await detectPollingFamilies(episodes);
-
-    expect(families).toHaveLength(2);
-    expect(families.every((f) => f.askCount === 5)).toBe(true);
-    expect(new Set(families.map((f) => f.id)).size).toBe(2);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('polling negative control', () => {
-  it('a varied, ordinary session yields zero polling families', async () => {
-    const episodes: Episode[] = [
-      substantiveAsk('sess-normal', '/Users/me/repoH', 0, 0, [
-        action('Read({file})', 0, 10_000),
-        action('Edit({file})', 0, 20_000),
-      ]),
-      quickAsk('sess-normal', '/Users/me/repoH', 1, 3 * 3_600_000),
-      substantiveAsk('sess-normal', '/Users/me/repoH', 2, 3 * 3_600_000 + 10 * MIN, [
-        action('Bash(npm test)', 0, 3 * 3_600_000 + 11 * MIN),
-      ]),
-      quickAsk('sess-normal', '/Users/me/repoH', 3, 6 * 3_600_000),
-    ];
-
-    expect(await detectPollingFamilies(episodes)).toEqual([]);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/price-basis.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/price-basis.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 151d239..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/price-basis.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,101 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * One price basis (c11, episodes.loop.html) — a ritual/chain card's header
- * count and its price count must derive from the SAME counting basis, and
- * its duration must be the ritual's own trace span, never the enclosing
- * episode's. The round-4 receipts verdict: a ritual firing 3x inside one
- * long-running episode rendered "seen 3x" in the header (`dossier.eventCount`,
- * the corpus's real occurrence count) but "seen 1x" in the price line
- * (`toil.frequency` — `candidateEpisodes` deduped all three firings down to
- * the one enclosing episode), and priced it at "median 4861.0min" — the
- * 81-hour SESSION's `durationMs`, not the ritual's own few minutes.
- *
- * This spec reproduces the defect as a behavior class (a tool-kind ritual
- * that fires several times inside one episode that itself runs on long
- * after the ritual is done), never the one titled example from the observed
- * briefing.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { buildDossier } from '../src/propose.js';
-import type { CandidateScore, RecurrenceCandidate } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import type { ActionEvent, EventTable } from '../src/types.js';
-
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-const HOUR = 3_600_000;
-const MINUTE = 60_000;
-const BASE = Date.parse('2026-07-01T09:00:00.000Z');
-
-const NEUTRAL_SCORE: CandidateScore = {
-  score: 0.5,
-  components: { frequency: 0.5, cadenceRegularity: 0.5, mechanizability: 0.5, manualCost: 0.5, stalenessRisk: 0.5 },
-};
-
-function toolEvent(session: string, project: string, template: string, offsetMs: number): ActionEvent {
-  const epoch = BASE + offsetMs;
-  return {
-    kind: 'tool',
-    ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(),
-    epoch,
-    project,
-    session,
-    tool: template.slice(0, template.indexOf('(')),
-    template,
-  };
-}
-
-function userEvent(session: string, project: string, turn: number, offsetMs: number): ActionEvent {
-  const epoch = BASE + offsetMs;
-  return { kind: 'user', ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(), epoch, project, session, turn };
-}
-
-function toolCandidate(label: string, templates: readonly string[], eventCount: number): RecurrenceCandidate {
-  return {
-    id: `tool:${templates.join(',')}`,
-    kind: 'tool',
-    label,
-    eventCount,
-    sessionCount: 1,
-    projectCount: 1,
-    firstSeen: new Date(BASE).toISOString(),
-    lastSeen: new Date(BASE + 81 * HOUR).toISOString(),
-    provenance: templates.map((template) => `tool template ${template}`),
-    score: NEUTRAL_SCORE,
-  };
-}
-
-describe('one price basis: header count, price count, and duration all derive from the same occurrence set', () => {
-  const RITUAL = 'Bash(cargo run --ritual)';
-  const project = '/Users/me/repoA';
-  const session = 'sess-long-running';
-
-  // One ask anchors ONE 81-hour episode. The ritual itself fires three
-  // times, back to back, within the first few minutes — then unrelated
-  // background work keeps the same episode open for 81 hours before the
-  // session ends. The episode's own `durationMs` (ask to its overall last
-  // action) is ~81 hours; the ritual's own occurrences never take that long.
-  const events: ActionEvent[] = [
-    userEvent(session, project, 0, 0),
-    toolEvent(session, project, RITUAL, 1 * MINUTE),
-    toolEvent(session, project, RITUAL, 2 * MINUTE),
-    toolEvent(session, project, RITUAL, 3 * MINUTE),
-    toolEvent(session, project, 'Bash(watch --background-job)', 81 * HOUR),
-  ];
-  const state: EventTable = { events, turns: ['run the cargo ritual'] };
-  const candidate = toolCandidate(RITUAL, [RITUAL], 3);
-  const dossier = buildDossier(candidate, state);
-
-  it('the header count and the price count agree — both read 3, not 1', () => {
-    expect(dossier.eventCount).toBe(3);
-    expect(dossier.toil.frequency).toBe(dossier.eventCount);
-  });
-
-  it('the median duration reflects the ritual\'s own minutes, never the 81-hour episode', () => {
-    // Each occurrence is priced from the ask to ITS OWN firing: 1, 2, and 3
-    // minutes — median 2 minutes. Never the episode's ~81-hour full span
-    // (which the unrelated trailing action would produce if duration were
-    // read off the enclosing episode instead of the ritual's own trace).
-    expect(dossier.toil.medianDurationMs).toBe(2 * MINUTE);
-    expect(dossier.toil.medianDurationMs).toBeLessThan(10 * MINUTE);
-    expect(dossier.toil.priced).not.toBe(false);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/price-join.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/price-join.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index aaf0ab3..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/price-join.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,150 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Price-join honesty (c11, spikes/loop-hole/report.loop.html) — a proposal
- * card's price line must derive from the SAME evidence set as its header
- * seen-count. `buildDossier`'s tool-kind join used to key off
- * `candidate.label`, but triage's and analyst's own `rebuildCandidate`
- * (triage.ts, analyst.ts) freely retitle `label` to a human-quality title
- * once a candidate is merged/retitled — after that, `label` no longer equals
- * any raw `event.template`, so the join silently matched nothing and every
- * chain/ritual-backed proposal priced to a fabricated "seen 0× · median
- * 0.0min · 0 intervention(s)" beneath a header that still (correctly) read
- * "seen 6× across 2 session(s)". This spec reproduces the defect as a
- * behavior class — any tool-kind candidate whose label no longer matches its
- * own real event template — not the one titled example from the observed
- * briefing, and separately locks in the honest-absence branch: a shape with
- * genuinely no duration evidence must render as unpriced, never as zeros.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { buildDossier } from '../src/propose.js';
-import type { CandidateScore, RecurrenceCandidate } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import type { ActionEvent, EventTable } from '../src/types.js';
-
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-const BASE = Date.parse('2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z');
-
-const NEUTRAL_SCORE: CandidateScore = {
-  score: 0.5,
-  components: { frequency: 0.5, cadenceRegularity: 0.5, mechanizability: 0.5, manualCost: 0.5, stalenessRisk: 0.5 },
-};
-
-function toolEvent(session: string, project: string, template: string, day: number, offsetMs: number): ActionEvent {
-  const epoch = BASE + day * DAY + offsetMs;
-  return {
-    kind: 'tool',
-    ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(),
-    epoch,
-    project,
-    session,
-    tool: template.slice(0, template.indexOf('(')),
-    template,
-  };
-}
-
-function userEvent(session: string, project: string, turn: number, day: number, offsetMs: number): ActionEvent {
-  const epoch = BASE + day * DAY + offsetMs;
-  return { kind: 'user', ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(), epoch, project, session, turn };
-}
-
-/** The shape `analyst.ts`'s `rebuildCandidate` produces after triage merges
- * and retitles a group of tool-template hits: `label` is a chosen human
- * title, and the real evidence lives only in `provenance`'s `tool template
- * <template>` lines — exactly what `memberContent` (cluster.ts) reads. */
-function toolCandidate(label: string, templates: readonly string[], eventCount: number): RecurrenceCandidate {
-  return {
-    id: `tool:${templates.join(',')}`,
-    kind: 'tool',
-    label,
-    eventCount,
-    sessionCount: eventCount,
-    projectCount: 1,
-    firstSeen: new Date(BASE).toISOString(),
-    lastSeen: new Date(BASE + (eventCount - 1) * DAY).toISOString(),
-    provenance: templates.map((template) => `tool template ${template}`),
-    score: NEUTRAL_SCORE,
-  };
-}
-
-describe('buildDossier: tool-kind price join survives a triage/analyst retitle', () => {
-  it('a retitled ritual candidate prices with frequency equal to its own header count and a non-zero median', () => {
-    const template = 'Bash(npm run deploy)';
-    const events: ActionEvent[] = [];
-    for (let day = 0; day < 3; day += 1) {
-      events.push(userEvent(`sess-${day}`, '/repo/a', 0, day, 0));
-      events.push(toolEvent(`sess-${day}`, '/repo/a', template, day, 1_000 + day * 5_000));
-    }
-    const state: EventTable = { events, turns: ['deploy the app'] };
-
-    // label deliberately does NOT match the real template — the exact shape
-    // a triage/analyst retitle leaves behind.
-    const candidate = toolCandidate('Ship the app to production', [template], 3);
-
-    const dossier = buildDossier(candidate, state);
-
-    expect(dossier.eventCount).toBe(3);
-    expect(dossier.toil.frequency).toBe(dossier.eventCount);
-    expect(dossier.toil.medianDurationMs).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-    expect(dossier.toil.priced).not.toBe(false);
-  });
-
-  it('a merged ritual (several real templates unioned under one retitled label) still joins on every member template', () => {
-    const templateA = 'Bash(gh pr merge --auto)';
-    const templateB = 'Bash(git push --delete origin {branch})';
-    const events: ActionEvent[] = [];
-    for (let day = 0; day < 4; day += 1) {
-      events.push(userEvent(`sess-${day}`, '/repo/a', 0, day, 0));
-      events.push(toolEvent(`sess-${day}`, '/repo/a', templateA, day, 1_000));
-      events.push(toolEvent(`sess-${day}`, '/repo/a', templateB, day, 2_000 + day * 1_000));
-    }
-    const state: EventTable = { events, turns: ['merge and clean up'] };
-
-    const candidate = toolCandidate('CI-watch → auto-merge → delete remote branch', [templateA, templateB], 4);
-
-    const dossier = buildDossier(candidate, state);
-
-    expect(dossier.toil.frequency).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-    expect(dossier.toil.medianDurationMs).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-    expect(dossier.toil.priced).not.toBe(false);
-  });
-
-  it('a never-retitled tool candidate (label still the real template) keeps pricing correctly — no regression', () => {
-    const template = 'Bash(npm test)';
-    const events: ActionEvent[] = [];
-    for (let day = 0; day < 3; day += 1) {
-      events.push(userEvent(`sess-${day}`, '/repo/a', 0, day, 0));
-      events.push(toolEvent(`sess-${day}`, '/repo/a', template, day, 1_000));
-    }
-    const state: EventTable = { events, turns: ['run the tests'] };
-
-    const candidate = toolCandidate(template, [template], 3);
-    const dossier = buildDossier(candidate, state);
-
-    expect(dossier.toil.frequency).toBe(dossier.eventCount);
-    expect(dossier.toil.medianDurationMs).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('buildDossier: honest absence — no duration evidence renders unpriced, never zeros', () => {
-  it('a tool candidate whose every occurrence has no preceding ask in its session prices to priced: false', () => {
-    const template = 'Bash(cron-triggered-sweep)';
-    const events: ActionEvent[] = [];
-    // Every occurrence is the FIRST event in its session — episodes.ts
-    // anchors an episode at a user turn, so a tool event with nothing
-    // preceding it in the same session belongs to no episode at all.
-    for (let day = 0; day < 3; day += 1) {
-      events.push(toolEvent(`sess-${day}`, '/repo/a', template, day, 0));
-    }
-    const state: EventTable = { events, turns: [] };
-
-    const candidate = toolCandidate('Run the nightly sweep', [template], 3);
-    const dossier = buildDossier(candidate, state);
-
-    // The header's own count is unaffected — it counts real occurrences,
-    // never episode-anchored ones.
-    expect(dossier.eventCount).toBe(3);
-    expect(dossier.toil.priced).toBe(false);
-    expect(dossier.toil.frequency).toBe(0);
-    expect(dossier.toil.medianDurationMs).toBe(0);
-    expect(dossier.toil.interventions).toBe(0);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/privacy.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/privacy.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index e40c1a5..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/privacy.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,87 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Privacy is a property of the store, not a promise in prose. The extractor
- * reads assistant prose, thinking, tool results and attachments — and must
- * persist none of it. Only events and templates land on disk; concrete tool
- * arguments (ports, paths, shas) are slotted away before they can be written.
- */
-
-import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
-import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { ClaudeCodeAdapter } from '../src/claude-adapter.js';
-import { runExtract } from '../src/extract.js';
-import { StateStore } from '../src/store.js';
-import { makeCorpus } from './helpers.js';
-
-describe('privacy: raw log content never reaches the store', () => {
-  it('drops prose/thinking/results and slots concrete tool arguments', async () => {
-    const ts = '2026-06-10T08:00:00.000Z';
-    const cwd = '/Users/me/repo';
-
-    // Markers that live only in raw-body fields the store must never keep.
-    const proseSecret = 'SECRET_ASSISTANT_PROSE_zzz';
-    const thinkingSecret = 'SECRET_THINKING_zzz';
-    const toolResultSecret = 'SECRET_TOOL_RESULT_zzz';
-    const attachmentSecret = 'SECRET_ATTACHMENT_zzz';
-    // Concrete argument values normalization must strip out of templates.
-    const secretPort = '54321';
-    const secretPath = '/home/ops/creds/production.key';
-    const secretSha = 'cafebabedeadbeef01';
-
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-p',
-        records: [
-          { type: 'user', timestamp: ts, cwd, message: { role: 'user', content: 'check the CI status again' } },
-          {
-            type: 'assistant',
-            timestamp: ts,
-            cwd,
-            message: {
-              role: 'assistant',
-              content: [
-                { type: 'thinking', thinking: thinkingSecret },
-                { type: 'text', text: proseSecret },
-                { type: 'tool_use', name: 'Bash', input: { command: `lsof -i :${secretPort}` } },
-                { type: 'tool_use', name: 'Read', input: { file_path: secretPath } },
-                { type: 'tool_use', name: 'Bash', input: { command: `git show ${secretSha}` } },
-              ],
-            },
-          },
-          {
-            type: 'user',
-            timestamp: ts,
-            cwd,
-            message: { role: 'user', content: [{ type: 'tool_result', content: toolResultSecret }] },
-          },
-          { type: 'attachment', timestamp: ts, cwd, attachment: { type: 'file', text: attachmentSecret } },
-        ],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-
-    // The store did capture the action skeleton.
-    expect(store.events.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-    const templates = store.events.filter((e) => e.kind === 'tool').map((e) => e.kind === 'tool' && e.template);
-    expect(templates).toContain('Bash(lsof -i :{port})');
-    expect(templates).toContain('Bash(git show {sha})');
-    expect(templates).toContain('Read({path})');
-
-    // Serialize exactly as it would be written, and inspect the bytes.
-    const storePath = join(mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-store-')), 'state.json');
-    store.save(storePath);
-    const onDisk = readFileSync(storePath, 'utf8');
-
-    for (const secret of [proseSecret, thinkingSecret, toolResultSecret, attachmentSecret, secretPort, secretPath, secretSha]) {
-      expect(onDisk).not.toContain(secret);
-    }
-    // What survives is only events + templates + interned intent.
-    expect(onDisk).toContain('{port}');
-    expect(onDisk).toContain('check the CI status again');
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/proto.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/proto.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index e915a48..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/proto.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,139 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Prototype-pollution safety (SNAG-14). Intent tokens are corpus strings, and
- * corpus strings become keys the moment the tokenizer consults its synonym
- * table. A plain object used as that table carries the whole `Object.prototype`
- * as phantom entries: `SYNONYMS['constructor']` resolves — through the
- * prototype chain — to the `Object` constructor *function*, so the `?? token`
- * fallback never fires and a function flows into the hasher, which crashes on
- * the first real prompt that says the word "constructor".
- *
- * The fixture corpus could never surface this (nobody wrote "constructor" into
- * a seed); the owner's real corpus hit it on run one. The invariant this file
- * pins: no lookup keyed by a corpus string may walk a prototype chain, so every
- * `Object.prototype` name must tokenize and cluster like any other word.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { detectRecurrenceCandidates } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import type { ActionEvent, StoredState } from '../src/types.js';
-
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-const START = Date.parse('2026-06-01T09:00:00Z');
-const NOW = Date.parse('2026-06-08T09:00:00Z');
-
-/** Every own-enumerable-or-not name that lives on `Object.prototype` — the set
- * a naive object-as-map silently inherits. `constructor` is the one that
- * survives lowercasing and actually collided in the field; the rest guard the
- * invariant against a future tokenizer that stops lowercasing. */
-const PROTOTYPE_NAMES = [
-  'constructor',
-  'toString',
-  'hasOwnProperty',
-  'valueOf',
-  'isPrototypeOf',
-  'toLocaleString',
-  'propertyIsEnumerable',
-  '__proto__',
-] as const;
-
-/** Build a corpus whose recurring intent is a single turn text, repeated across
- * enough sessions to clear the intent-occurrence floor and form one cluster. */
-function intentCorpus(turnText: string, occurrences = 4): StoredState {
-  const turns = [turnText];
-  const events: ActionEvent[] = [];
-  for (let i = 0; i < occurrences; i += 1) {
-    const epoch = START + i * DAY;
-    events.push({
-      kind: 'user',
-      ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(),
-      epoch,
-      project: '/Users/me/repo',
-      session: `session-${i}`,
-      turn: 0,
-    });
-  }
-  return { version: 1, watermark: {}, turns, events };
-}
-
-describe('prototype-name intent tokens tokenize and cluster safely', () => {
-  it('a prompt containing the word "constructor" does not crash the detector (SNAG-14)', async () => {
-    const state = intentCorpus('restart the constructor service after every deploy');
-
-    const candidates = await detectRecurrenceCandidates(state, { now: NOW });
-
-    const intent = candidates.find((candidate) => candidate.kind === 'intent');
-    expect(intent).toBeDefined();
-    // The token must have stayed a string end-to-end; a prototype-chain hit
-    // would have turned it into the Object constructor function before hashing.
-    expect(typeof intent!.label).toBe('string');
-    expect(intent!.label).toContain('constructor');
-    for (const line of intent!.provenance) expect(typeof line).toBe('string');
-  });
-
-  it('every Object.prototype name clusters without walking a prototype chain', async () => {
-    for (const name of PROTOTYPE_NAMES) {
-      const state = intentCorpus(`please review the ${name} handling in the module`);
-
-      // No throw, and a real intent candidate — proving the token was hashed as
-      // a string, not resolved to an inherited function/object.
-      const candidates = await detectRecurrenceCandidates(state, { now: NOW });
-      const intent = candidates.find((candidate) => candidate.kind === 'intent');
-      expect(intent, `no intent candidate for token "${name}"`).toBeDefined();
-      expect(typeof intent!.label).toBe('string');
-    }
-  });
-
-  it('a corpus mixing prototype names with real words still forms one cluster', async () => {
-    // Distinct but near-identical turns that must collapse into one intent —
-    // the clustering path exercised over prototype-collision tokens, not just
-    // the single-turn fast path.
-    const turns = [
-      'refactor the constructor and its toString method',
-      'refactor the constructor plus the toString method again',
-      'refactor the constructor with the toString method once more',
-    ];
-    const events: ActionEvent[] = turns.map((_, i) => {
-      const epoch = START + i * DAY;
-      return {
-        kind: 'user' as const,
-        ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(),
-        epoch,
-        project: '/Users/me/repo',
-        session: `session-${i}`,
-        turn: i,
-      };
-    });
-    const state: StoredState = { version: 1, watermark: {}, turns, events };
-
-    const candidates = await detectRecurrenceCandidates(state, { now: NOW });
-    const intents = candidates.filter((candidate) => candidate.kind === 'intent');
-    expect(intents).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(intents[0]!.eventCount).toBe(3);
-  });
-
-  it('shared-stem phrasings still cluster in the lexical fallback (no synonym table)', async () => {
-    // The hand-listed SYNONYMS table is deleted by design (an answer key, not a
-    // product — docs/overfit-postmortem.md); true synonyms are the static
-    // semantic tier's job. The lexical fallback still owes near-duplicate
-    // recall: phrasings differing by one stemmed token must land together.
-    const turns = ['check for pricing updates today', 'check for pricing changes today'];
-    const events: ActionEvent[] = [];
-    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) {
-      const epoch = START + i * DAY;
-      events.push({
-        kind: 'user',
-        ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(),
-        epoch,
-        project: '/Users/me/repo',
-        session: `session-${i}`,
-        turn: i % turns.length,
-      });
-    }
-    const state: StoredState = { version: 1, watermark: {}, turns, events };
-
-    const candidates = await detectRecurrenceCandidates(state, { now: NOW });
-    const intents = candidates.filter((candidate) => candidate.kind === 'intent');
-    expect(intents).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(intents[0]!.eventCount).toBe(4);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/queue-dedup.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/queue-dedup.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index ac7b538..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/queue-dedup.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,183 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Queued-message re-emission dedup (extract.loop.html c11) — round-5c
- * forensics on the real slice: one session file carried a single authored
- * user message 175 times. SYN-L's uuid dedup (resume-dedup.spec.ts)
- * collapsed the 51 same-uuid copies to one; the surviving 22-strong family
- * turned out to be DISTINCT-uuid records carrying byte-identical content —
- * Conductor's queue machinery re-delivering one authored act, not 22
- * separate asks. Mixed into the same 175 were harness bookkeeping record
- * types (`queue-operation`, `last-prompt`, `attachment`) that are not
- * genuine turns at all.
- *
- * Two closed layers:
- *  1. Record-type allowlist — `applyRecord` (extract.ts) only ever produces
- *     an event from `record.type === 'user' | 'assistant'`; the harness
- *     types above carry neither shape (no `message` field in the real
- *     transcripts) and were already excluded structurally. Proven here
- *     against real-shape fixtures, not assumed.
- *  2. Within-session content collapse (the actual defect): byte-identical
- *     user content within ONE session is the same authored act regardless
- *     of uuid — a human doesn't retype the identical prompt verbatim
- *     several times a minute apart; Conductor's queue does. The same text
- *     asked again in a DIFFERENT session, or a different day, is a genuine
- *     habit and still counts.
- */
-
-import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
-import { mkdtempSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { ClaudeCodeAdapter } from '../src/claude-adapter.js';
-import { runExtract } from '../src/extract.js';
-import { segmentEpisodes } from '../src/episodes.js';
-import { detectPollingFamilies } from '../src/polling.js';
-import { StateStore, EXTRACTION_RULES_VERSION } from '../src/store.js';
-import type { ActionEvent, SessionSource, StoredState } from '../src/types.js';
-import { makeCorpus, userTurn, toolCall, queueOperationTurn, lastPromptTurn, attachmentTurn } from './helpers.js';
-
-const CWD = '/Users/me/repo';
-const MIN = 60_000;
-const BASE = Date.parse('2026-07-24T09:00:00.000Z');
-
-function at(offsetMs: number): string {
-  return new Date(BASE + offsetMs).toISOString();
-}
-
-function userEvents(store: StateStore) {
-  return store.events.filter((e): e is Extract<ActionEvent, { kind: 'user' }> => e.kind === 'user');
-}
-
-/** A record shaped like a real transcript line, with an explicit `uuid` —
- * these tests control identity directly (resume-dedup.spec.ts precedent). */
-function rawUserRecord(uuid: string, text: string, timestamp: string): unknown {
-  return {
-    parentUuid: null,
-    isSidechain: false,
-    userType: 'external',
-    cwd: CWD,
-    type: 'user',
-    message: { role: 'user', content: text },
-    uuid,
-    timestamp,
-  };
-}
-
-describe('queued-message re-emission dedup', () => {
-  it('harness bookkeeping records never intern, and N distinct-uuid identical asks collapse to one', async () => {
-    const askText = 'run the migration script one more time';
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-queue-storm',
-        records: [
-          userTurn('kick off the migration', at(0), CWD),
-          toolCall('Bash', { command: 'npm run migrate -- --background' }, at(1_000), [], CWD),
-          // Harness bookkeeping mixed into the same session — none of these
-          // are turns.
-          queueOperationTurn(askText, at(2 * MIN), 'sess-queue-storm'),
-          lastPromptTurn(askText, 'leaf-uuid-1', 'sess-queue-storm'),
-          attachmentTurn(at(3 * MIN), CWD, 'sess-queue-storm'),
-          // The real defect: 22 distinct-uuid re-deliveries of one authored
-          // act (round-5c's proven shape).
-          ...Array.from({ length: 22 }, (_, i) => rawUserRecord(`uuid-redeliver-${i}`, askText, at(10 * MIN + i * MIN))),
-          userTurn('great, ship it', at(40 * MIN), CWD),
-          toolCall('Bash', { command: 'git push --tags' }, at(43 * MIN), [], CWD),
-        ],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-
-    const askTurn = store.internTurn(askText);
-    expect(userEvents(store).filter((e) => e.turn === askTurn)).toHaveLength(1);
-    // The kickoff + closer asks are still there — only the re-delivered
-    // family collapsed.
-    expect(userEvents(store)).toHaveLength(3);
-
-    const episodes = segmentEpisodes(store);
-    const families = await detectPollingFamilies(episodes);
-    expect(families).toEqual([]);
-  });
-
-  it('the same ask repeated in three separate sessions still counts three times', async () => {
-    const text = 'any update on the deploy?';
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      { dir: '-Users-me-repo', sessionId: 'sess-a', records: [rawUserRecord('uuid-a', text, at(0))] },
-      { dir: '-Users-me-repo', sessionId: 'sess-b', records: [rawUserRecord('uuid-b', text, at(1 * MIN))] },
-      { dir: '-Users-me-repo', sessionId: 'sess-c', records: [rawUserRecord('uuid-c', text, at(2 * MIN))] },
-    ]);
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-
-    const turn = store.internTurn(text);
-    expect(userEvents(store).filter((e) => e.turn === turn)).toHaveLength(3);
-  });
-
-  it('two different asks in one session both survive', async () => {
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-two-asks',
-        records: [rawUserRecord('uuid-1', 'run the linter', at(0)), rawUserRecord('uuid-2', 'run the type checker', at(1 * MIN))],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-
-    expect(userEvents(store)).toHaveLength(2);
-  });
-
-  it('a store built under the pre-c11 rules version triggers full re-extraction, and the re-delivery family collapses', async () => {
-    const text = 'run the migration script one more time';
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-poisoned-queue',
-        records: [
-          queueOperationTurn(text, at(0), 'sess-poisoned-queue'),
-          rawUserRecord('uuid-1', text, at(0)),
-          rawUserRecord('uuid-2', text, at(1 * MIN)),
-          rawUserRecord('uuid-3', text, at(2 * MIN)),
-        ],
-      },
-    ]);
-    const adapter = new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root);
-    let signature = '';
-    for await (const session of adapter.sessions() as AsyncIterable<SessionSource> | Iterable<SessionSource>) {
-      if (session.sessionId === 'sess-poisoned-queue') signature = session.signature;
-    }
-    expect(signature).not.toBe('');
-
-    // A store built under the superseded rules that wrongly kept all three
-    // distinct-uuid re-deliveries as separate events — the exact pre-c11
-    // shape.
-    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-queue-dedup-'));
-    const storePath = join(dir, 'state.json');
-    const poisoned: StoredState = {
-      version: 2,
-      rulesVersion: EXTRACTION_RULES_VERSION - 1,
-      watermark: { 'sess-poisoned-queue': { signature, eventCount: 3 } },
-      turns: [text],
-      events: [0, 1, 2].map((i) => ({
-        kind: 'user' as const,
-        ts: at(i * MIN),
-        epoch: Date.parse(at(i * MIN)),
-        project: CWD,
-        session: 'sess-poisoned-queue',
-        turn: 0,
-      })),
-    };
-    writeFileSync(storePath, JSON.stringify(poisoned));
-
-    const store = StateStore.load(storePath);
-    expect(store.rulesReset).toEqual({ from: EXTRACTION_RULES_VERSION - 1, to: EXTRACTION_RULES_VERSION });
-
-    await runExtract(adapter, store);
-
-    const turn = store.internTurn(text);
-    expect(userEvents(store).filter((e) => e.turn === turn)).toHaveLength(1);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/reach.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/reach.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 7d0552e..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/reach.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,123 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Cross-project reach tiering (design: docs/loop-hole-episodes-design.md, v3,
- * "L2 analyst"; check c5 in propose.loop.html). Two things must be true:
- *
- *  1. Reach is COMPUTED from `projectCount` alone — no model, no frequency
- *     input — before anything is ranked.
- *  2. `rankByReach` enforces cross-project-outranks-single-project as a
- *     structural sort property: a theme spanning three or more projects
- *     always beats a single-project theme, even one with far higher raw
- *     frequency. Ties within the same tier break by frequency.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import type { RecurrenceCandidate } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import {
-  CROSS_PROJECT_REACH_THRESHOLD,
-  candidateReach,
-  judgeReach,
-  rankByReach,
-  type RankableByReach,
-} from '../src/reach.js';
-
-function candidate(overrides: Partial<RecurrenceCandidate> & { id: string }): RecurrenceCandidate {
-  return {
-    kind: 'intent',
-    label: overrides.id,
-    eventCount: 1,
-    sessionCount: 1,
-    projectCount: 1,
-    firstSeen: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
-    lastSeen: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
-    provenance: [],
-    score: { score: 0, components: { frequency: 0, cadenceRegularity: 0, mechanizability: 0, manualCost: 0, stalenessRisk: 0 } },
-    ...overrides,
-  };
-}
-
-describe('judgeReach — deterministic computation from project count alone', () => {
-  it('tiers a theme spanning the threshold count of projects as cross-project', () => {
-    expect(judgeReach(CROSS_PROJECT_REACH_THRESHOLD).tier).toBe('cross-project');
-  });
-
-  it('tiers a theme spanning more than the threshold as cross-project', () => {
-    expect(judgeReach(CROSS_PROJECT_REACH_THRESHOLD + 5).tier).toBe('cross-project');
-  });
-
-  it('tiers a single-project theme as single-project', () => {
-    expect(judgeReach(1).tier).toBe('single-project');
-  });
-
-  it('tiers a theme one short of the threshold as single-project', () => {
-    expect(judgeReach(CROSS_PROJECT_REACH_THRESHOLD - 1).tier).toBe('single-project');
-  });
-
-  it('is deterministic: the same project count always yields the same judgment', () => {
-    const first = judgeReach(4);
-    const second = judgeReach(4);
-    expect(second).toEqual(first);
-  });
-
-  it('respects a caller-supplied threshold', () => {
-    expect(judgeReach(2, 2).tier).toBe('cross-project');
-    expect(judgeReach(2, 5).tier).toBe('single-project');
-  });
-});
-
-describe('candidateReach — decorates a candidate from its own evidence fields', () => {
-  it('reads reach from projectCount and frequency from eventCount, nothing else', () => {
-    const wide = candidate({ id: 'wide', projectCount: 3, eventCount: 4 });
-    const decorated = candidateReach(wide);
-    expect(decorated.reach).toBe('cross-project');
-    expect(decorated.frequency).toBe(4);
-    expect(decorated.id).toBe('wide');
-  });
-
-  it('marks a single-project candidate accordingly regardless of its event count', () => {
-    const narrow = candidate({ id: 'narrow', projectCount: 1, eventCount: 500 });
-    expect(candidateReach(narrow).reach).toBe('single-project');
-  });
-});
-
-describe('rankByReach — a cross-project theme is never outranked by a louder single-project one', () => {
-  it('ranks a low-frequency cross-project theme above a high-frequency single-project theme', () => {
-    const ranked = rankByReach([
-      { id: 'loud-but-narrow', reach: 'single-project', frequency: 500 },
-      { id: 'quiet-but-wide', reach: 'cross-project', frequency: 4 },
-    ] as const);
-    expect(ranked.map((item) => item.id)).toEqual(['quiet-but-wide', 'loud-but-narrow']);
-  });
-
-  it('breaks ties within the same tier by frequency, highest first', () => {
-    const ranked = rankByReach([
-      { id: 'low', reach: 'cross-project', frequency: 3 },
-      { id: 'high', reach: 'cross-project', frequency: 9 },
-      { id: 'mid', reach: 'cross-project', frequency: 6 },
-    ] as const);
-    expect(ranked.map((item) => item.id)).toEqual(['high', 'mid', 'low']);
-  });
-
-  it('never lets any single-project item precede any cross-project item, across a mixed and shuffled set', () => {
-    const items: (RankableByReach & { id: string })[] = [
-      { id: 'a', reach: 'single-project', frequency: 999 },
-      { id: 'b', reach: 'cross-project', frequency: 12 },
-      { id: 'c', reach: 'single-project', frequency: 40 },
-      { id: 'd', reach: 'cross-project', frequency: 1 },
-      { id: 'e', reach: 'single-project', frequency: 22 },
-      { id: 'f', reach: 'cross-project', frequency: 60 },
-    ];
-    const ranked = rankByReach(items);
-    const firstSingleIndex = ranked.findIndex((item) => item.reach === 'single-project');
-    const lastCrossIndex = ranked.map((item) => item.reach).lastIndexOf('cross-project');
-    expect(firstSingleIndex).toBeGreaterThan(lastCrossIndex);
-  });
-
-  it('composes directly on candidates decorated by candidateReach, straight from cluster.ts evidence', () => {
-    const candidates = [
-      candidate({ id: 'single-repo-hotstreak', projectCount: 1, eventCount: 200 }),
-      candidate({ id: 'three-repo-ritual', projectCount: 3, eventCount: 6 }),
-    ];
-    const ranked = rankByReach(candidates.map(candidateReach));
-    expect(ranked.map((item) => item.id)).toEqual(['three-repo-ritual', 'single-repo-hotstreak']);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/recall.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/recall.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 303b7ba..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/recall.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,126 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Recall — the product's core promise made falsifiable: the recurring intent
- * that is really in the logs must survive the counting/clustering layer. This
- * corpus seeds the five rituals the check names — port-busy checks, MCP-server
- * restarts after builds, CI/PR status asks, validate/cockpit re-runs, and the
- * re-audit cascade after source edits — each with realistic variance (rotating
- * ports, rotating feature ids, re-phrased asks) so the detector is exercised
- * through normalization and clustering, not handed pre-collapsed keys. All five
- * must appear in the shortlist; a miss on any one is a hole in the product.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { detectRecurrenceCandidates } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import { normalizeToolCall } from '../src/normalize.js';
-import type { ActionEvent, StoredState } from '../src/types.js';
-
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-const START = Date.parse('2026-06-01T09:00:00Z');
-const NOW = Date.parse('2026-06-08T09:00:00Z');
-
-// The four tool rituals, keyed exactly as the normalizer will template them —
-// computed from concrete calls so the expectation cannot drift from the code.
-const RITUALS = {
-  /** Port-busy check before starting a dev server — rotating port. */
-  portCheck: normalizeToolCall('Bash', { command: 'lsof -i :5173' }),
-  /** MCP-server restart after a rebuild — the stale-server dance. */
-  mcpRestart: normalizeToolCall('Bash', {
-    command: 'npm run build && pkill -f tend-server && npx tend serve',
-  }),
-  /** Cockpit/validate re-run to check the verdict after a change. */
-  validateRerun: normalizeToolCall('Bash', { command: 'npx tend validate' }),
-  /** The re-audit cascade after a source edit — rotating feature id. */
-  reauditCascade: normalizeToolCall('Bash', { command: 'npx tend audit AR-6' }),
-} as const;
-
-/** Rotating concrete forms that must all collapse to the same template. */
-const PORT_COMMANDS = ['lsof -i :5173', 'lsof -i :8471', 'lsof -i :3000', 'lsof -i :9229'];
-const REAUDIT_COMMANDS = ['npx tend audit AR-6', 'npx tend audit AR-7', 'npx tend audit AR-9', 'npx tend audit AR-12'];
-
-/** CI/PR status asks — re-phrased, but one recurring intent. */
-const CI_PR_TURNS = [
-  'check the CI status on the PR',
-  'check the CI status on the PR again',
-  'check the CI build status on the PR',
-];
-
-function fiveRitualState(): StoredState {
-  const events: ActionEvent[] = [];
-  let seq = 0;
-
-  // Four days of the four tool rituals — recurring, with variance.
-  for (let day = 0; day < 4; day += 1) {
-    events.push(toolEvent('Bash', { command: PORT_COMMANDS[day]! }, day, seq++));
-    events.push(toolEvent('Bash', {
-      command: 'npm run build && pkill -f tend-server && npx tend serve',
-    }, day, seq++));
-    events.push(toolEvent('Bash', { command: 'npx tend validate' }, day, seq++));
-    events.push(toolEvent('Bash', { command: REAUDIT_COMMANDS[day]! }, day, seq++));
-  }
-
-  // The CI/PR ask, re-phrased, asked twice each across six days.
-  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i += 1) {
-    events.push(userEvent(i % CI_PR_TURNS.length, i, seq++));
-  }
-
-  return { version: 1, watermark: {}, turns: [...CI_PR_TURNS], events };
-}
-
-function toolEvent(tool: string, input: unknown, day: number, sequence: number): ActionEvent {
-  const epoch = START + day * DAY + sequence * 1000;
-  return {
-    kind: 'tool',
-    ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(),
-    epoch,
-    project: '/Users/me/repo',
-    session: `session-${day}`,
-    tool,
-    template: normalizeToolCall(tool, input),
-  };
-}
-
-function userEvent(turn: number, day: number, sequence: number): ActionEvent {
-  const epoch = START + day * DAY + sequence * 1000;
-  return {
-    kind: 'user',
-    ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(),
-    epoch,
-    project: '/Users/me/repo',
-    session: `session-${day}`,
-    turn,
-  };
-}
-
-describe('recurrence candidate recall', () => {
-  it('finds all five known rituals in the corpus', async () => {
-    const candidates = await detectRecurrenceCandidates(fiveRitualState(), {
-      now: NOW,
-      intentSimilarity: 0.45,
-    });
-    const labels = candidates.map((candidate) => candidate.label);
-
-    // Ritual 1: port-busy checks.
-    expect(labels).toContain(RITUALS.portCheck);
-    // Ritual 2: MCP-server restarts after builds.
-    expect(labels).toContain(RITUALS.mcpRestart);
-    // Ritual 4: validate/cockpit re-runs.
-    expect(labels).toContain(RITUALS.validateRerun);
-    // Ritual 5: the re-audit cascade after source edits.
-    expect(labels).toContain(RITUALS.reauditCascade);
-
-    // Ritual 3: CI/PR status asks — an intent cluster, not a tool template.
-    const intent = candidates.find((candidate) => candidate.kind === 'intent');
-    expect(intent).toBeDefined();
-    expect(intent!.eventCount).toBe(6);
-    const provenance = intent!.provenance.join('\n');
-    expect(provenance).toContain('CI');
-    expect(provenance).toContain('PR');
-
-    // All five rituals surfaced — four tool templates plus the one intent.
-    const distinctToolLabels = new Set(
-      candidates.filter((candidate) => candidate.kind === 'tool').map((candidate) => candidate.label),
-    );
-    expect(distinctToolLabels.size).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(4);
-    expect(candidates.filter((candidate) => candidate.kind === 'intent')).toHaveLength(1);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/refusal-hygiene.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/refusal-hygiene.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 9cd13a4..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/refusal-hygiene.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,404 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Refusal hygiene (c10): every tier that turns a thrown judge error into text
- * a person reads — triage.ts, analyst.ts, synthesis.ts, and run.ts's banners
- * / CLI run-summary lines built on top of them — must render that reason as
- * ONE line, truncated to ~200 chars, never a raw command, prompt, or
- * multi-line dump (the "useless 'Command failed'+full-prompt dump baked into
- * the page" a prior slice run actually hit, report.loop.html's Tried log,
- * 2026-07-08). And when the failure is a judge SPAWN (every real judge is
- * `execFileSync('claude', [...])`, see run.ts's `makeClaudeCliJudge`), the
- * reason must carry the child's own stderr (first line), never `.message`'s
- * "Command failed: <cmd + the whole prompt>" — a rate limit reads as a rate
- * limit, not a wall of shell echo.
- *
- * Three layers, cheapest first: the shared primitive (judge-error.ts) in
- * isolation, each tier's own wiring of it (triage.ts/analyst.ts/synthesis.ts),
- * then the full pipeline (run.ts) proving the same hygienic reason survives
- * into the briefing's banner AND the CLI's run-summary line untouched.
- */
-
-import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { extractPayload } from '../../../loop/src/core/extract.js';
-import { judgeErrorReason, sanitizeReason } from '../src/judge-error.js';
-import { triageIntents, type TriageJudge } from '../src/triage.js';
-import { runAnalyst, type AnalystJudge } from '../src/analyst.js';
-import { runSynthesis, type SynthesisJudge } from '../src/synthesis.js';
-import { runPipeline, joinRefusalReasons, tierSummaryLine, synthesisSummaryLine } from '../src/run.js';
-import type { RecurrenceCandidate } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import type { EventTable } from '../src/types.js';
-import { makeCorpus, toolCall, userTurn } from './helpers.js';
-
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-const START = Date.parse('2026-06-01T09:00:00Z');
-
-/** The shape Node's `execFileSync`/`execSync` throw on a nonzero exit or
- * signal kill — a real judge-spawn failure, not a plain `Error`. */
-interface ChildLikeError extends Error {
-  stderr?: string | Buffer;
-  signal?: string | null;
-  status?: number | null;
-  code?: string;
-}
-
-function makeExecError(opts: {
-  stderr?: string | Buffer;
-  signal?: string;
-  status?: number;
-  code?: string;
-  message?: string;
-}): ChildLikeError {
-  const err = new Error(opts.message ?? 'Command failed') as ChildLikeError;
-  if (opts.stderr !== undefined) err.stderr = opts.stderr;
-  if (opts.signal !== undefined) err.signal = opts.signal;
-  if (opts.status !== undefined) err.status = opts.status;
-  if (opts.code !== undefined) err.code = opts.code;
-  return err;
-}
-
-describe('sanitizeReason — one line, capped, no matter what it was built from', () => {
-  it('collapses a multi-line dump to its first non-blank line', () => {
-    expect(sanitizeReason('\n\n  rate limit exceeded  \nstack trace line 1\nstack trace line 2')).toBe('rate limit exceeded');
-  });
-
-  it('returns an empty string when every line is blank', () => {
-    expect(sanitizeReason('\n   \n\t\n')).toBe('');
-  });
-
-  it('leaves a short single line untouched', () => {
-    expect(sanitizeReason('short reason')).toBe('short reason');
-  });
-
-  it('truncates a long single line to the cap with an ellipsis, never exceeding it', () => {
-    const long = 'x'.repeat(500);
-    const result = sanitizeReason(long);
-    expect(result.length).toBe(200);
-    expect(result.endsWith('…')).toBe(true);
-    expect(result.startsWith('x'.repeat(199))).toBe(true);
-  });
-
-  it('honors a custom max', () => {
-    expect(sanitizeReason('abcdefghij', 5)).toBe('abcd…');
-  });
-
-  it('never lets a newline survive, even from a huge multi-line dump', () => {
-    const dump = Array.from({ length: 50 }, (_, i) => `line ${i} of a stack trace or echoed prompt`).join('\n');
-    const result = sanitizeReason(dump);
-    expect(result).not.toContain('\n');
-    expect(result.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(200);
-  });
-});
-
-describe("judgeErrorReason — a judge spawn failure carries the child's stderr", () => {
-  it('a rate limit reads as a rate limit: stderr\'s first line wins over the huge Command-failed message', () => {
-    const hugePrompt = 'PROMPT-LEAK-MARKER-'.repeat(50);
-    const error = makeExecError({
-      stderr: '\n\nAPIError: 429 rate limit exceeded, retry after 30s\nsome trailing diagnostic noise\n',
