-      message: `Command failed: claude -p "${hugePrompt}"\nAPIError: 429 rate limit exceeded, retry after 30s\nsome trailing diagnostic noise\n`,
-    });
-    const reason = judgeErrorReason(error);
-    expect(reason).toBe('APIError: 429 rate limit exceeded, retry after 30s');
-    expect(reason).not.toContain('\n');
-    expect(reason.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(200);
-    expect(reason).not.toContain('Command failed');
-    expect(reason).not.toContain('PROMPT-LEAK-MARKER');
-  });
-
-  it('reads a Buffer stderr (execFileSync\'s real return type) the same way as a string one', () => {
-    const error = makeExecError({});
-    error.stderr = Buffer.from('auth failed: invalid API key\njunk\n', 'utf8');
-    expect(judgeErrorReason(error)).toBe('auth failed: invalid API key');
-  });
-
-  it('truncates an overlong stderr first line to the cap', () => {
-    const error = makeExecError({ stderr: 'x'.repeat(500) });
-    const reason = judgeErrorReason(error);
-    expect(reason.length).toBe(200);
-    expect(reason.endsWith('…')).toBe(true);
-  });
-
-  it('falls back to a synthetic signal description when stderr never printed anything (a timeout kill)', () => {
-    const error = makeExecError({ stderr: '', signal: 'SIGTERM' });
-    expect(judgeErrorReason(error)).toBe('process terminated by signal SIGTERM');
-  });
-
-  it('falls back to a synthetic status description when there is no stderr or signal', () => {
-    const error = makeExecError({ stderr: '', status: 1 });
-    expect(judgeErrorReason(error)).toBe('process exited with status 1');
-  });
-
-  it('falls back to the error code when the binary itself was never found', () => {
-    const error = makeExecError({ stderr: '', code: 'ENOENT' });
-    expect(judgeErrorReason(error)).toBe('process error: ENOENT');
-  });
-
-  it('last resort: a plain non-child-process Error still collapses to one sanitized line, never the raw dump', () => {
-    const hugePrompt = 'PROMPT '.repeat(200);
-    const error = new Error(`Command failed: claude -p "${hugePrompt}"\nstdout junk\nstderr junk`);
-    const reason = judgeErrorReason(error);
-    expect(reason).not.toContain('\n');
-    expect(reason.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(200);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('joinRefusalReasons — combining per-chunk reasons survives the same cap', () => {
-  it('joins short reasons verbatim with "; "', () => {
-    expect(
-      joinRefusalReasons(['chunk 1/2 refused: judge threw: rate limited', 'chunk 2/2 refused: structural validation']),
-    ).toBe('chunk 1/2 refused: judge threw: rate limited; chunk 2/2 refused: structural validation');
-  });
-
-  it('re-caps the combined string when several near-cap reasons together would exceed one line\'s worth', () => {
-    const long = 'x'.repeat(150);
-    const combined = joinRefusalReasons([long, long, long]);
-    expect(combined.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(200);
-    expect(combined).not.toContain('\n');
-  });
-});
-
-// --- Each tier's own wiring of judge-error.ts ------------------------------
-
-const EMPTY_STATE: EventTable = { events: [], turns: [] };
-
-function minimalCandidate(kind: 'intent' | 'tool', id: string): RecurrenceCandidate {
-  return {
-    id,
-    kind,
-    label: id,
-    eventCount: 1,
-    sessionCount: 1,
-    projectCount: 1,
-    firstSeen: new Date(START).toISOString(),
-    lastSeen: new Date(START).toISOString(),
-    provenance: [`turn 0: ${id}`],
-    score: { score: 1, components: { frequency: 1, cadenceRegularity: 1, mechanizability: 1, manualCost: 1, stalenessRisk: 1 } },
-  };
-}
-
-describe('triage.ts / analyst.ts / synthesis.ts wire judge-error.ts into their onRefusal/reason', () => {
-  it('triageIntents: a thrown judge-spawn error surfaces a one-line, capped, stderr-derived reason — never the prompt', () => {
-    const hugePrompt = 'PROMPT-LEAK-'.repeat(50);
-    const throwing: TriageJudge = () => {
-      throw makeExecError({
-        stderr: 'rate limit exceeded for this account\nsome other noise',
-        message: `Command failed: claude -p "${hugePrompt}"`,
-      });
-    };
-    const reasons: string[] = [];
-    const result = triageIntents([minimalCandidate('intent', 'intent:0')], EMPTY_STATE, throwing, 30, (r) => reasons.push(r));
-
-    expect(result.refusedChunks).toBe(1);
-    expect(reasons).toHaveLength(1);
-    const reason = reasons[0]!;
-    expect(reason).not.toContain('\n');
-    expect(reason.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(200);
-    expect(reason).toContain('rate limit exceeded for this account');
-    expect(reason).not.toContain('Command failed');
-    expect(reason).not.toContain('PROMPT-LEAK-');
-  });
-
-  it('runAnalyst: same hygiene for the agent-lane tier', () => {
-    const hugePrompt = 'PROMPT-LEAK-'.repeat(50);
-    const throwing: AnalystJudge = () => {
-      throw makeExecError({
-        stderr: 'auth failed: invalid API key\nsome other noise',
-        message: `Command failed: claude -p "${hugePrompt}"`,
-      });
-    };
-    const reasons: string[] = [];
-    const result = runAnalyst([minimalCandidate('tool', 'tool:0')], EMPTY_STATE, throwing, 30, (r) => reasons.push(r));
-
-    expect(result).not.toBeNull();
-    expect(result!.refusedChunks).toBe(1);
-    const reason = reasons[0]!;
-    expect(reason).not.toContain('\n');
-    expect(reason.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(200);
-    expect(reason).toContain('auth failed: invalid API key');
-    expect(reason).not.toContain('Command failed');
-    expect(reason).not.toContain('PROMPT-LEAK-');
-  });
-
-  it('runSynthesis: a thrown judge-spawn error yields a one-line, capped, stderr-derived `reason`', () => {
-    const hugePrompt = 'PROMPT-LEAK-'.repeat(50);
-    const throwing: SynthesisJudge = () => {
-      throw makeExecError({
-        stderr: 'context length exceeded\nsome other noise',
-        message: `Command failed: claude -p "${hugePrompt}"`,
-      });
-    };
-    const result = runSynthesis({ proposals: [], themes: [], findings: [], cappedFindings: 0, shapeStats: [] }, throwing);
-
-    expect(result.applied).toBe(false);
-    expect(result.reason).toBeDefined();
-    const reason = result.reason!;
-    expect(reason).not.toContain('\n');
-    expect(reason.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(200);
-    expect(reason).toContain('context length exceeded');
-    expect(reason).not.toContain('Command failed');
-    expect(reason).not.toContain('PROMPT-LEAK-');
-  });
-});
-
-// --- run.ts's rendering surfaces: banners + the CLI run summary ------------
-
-describe('tierSummaryLine / synthesisSummaryLine — the CLI run-summary rendering', () => {
-  it('embeds an unavailable tier\'s reason on one line', () => {
-    const line = tierSummaryLine('triage', { applied: false, dropped: 0, merged: 0, reason: 'rate limit exceeded' });
-    expect(line).toBe('loop-hole: triage UNAVAILABLE — rate limit exceeded');
-    expect(line).not.toContain('\n');
-  });
-
-  it('carries no reason suffix at all when the tier applied cleanly', () => {
-    const line = tierSummaryLine('analyst', { applied: true, dropped: 2, merged: 1 });
-    expect(line).toBe('loop-hole: analyst dropped 2, merged 1');
-  });
-
-  it('the synthesis summary line reads the same `reason` field, one line', () => {
-    const line = synthesisSummaryLine({
-      applied: false,
-      text: '',
-      droppedSentences: 0,
-      repairedSentences: 0,
-      reason: 'context length exceeded',
-    });
-    expect(line).toBe('loop-hole: synthesis UNAVAILABLE — context length exceeded');
-    expect(line).not.toContain('\n');
-  });
-});
-
-describe('end-to-end: a judge spawn failure reads as a rate limit everywhere it surfaces (banner AND run summary)', () => {
-  /** One recurring user ask plus one recurring tool ritual across 4 sessions
-   * — enough to clear the detector's floor for both the triage (intent) and
-   * analyst (tool) lanes in one pipeline pass. */
-  function ritualCorpus(): string {
-    const sessions = [];
-    for (let day = 0; day < 4; day += 1) {
-      const at = (offset: number) => new Date(START + day * DAY + offset * 60_000).toISOString();
-      sessions.push({
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: `session-${day}`,
-        records: [
-          userTurn('is the dev server still stuck?', at(0)),
-          toolCall('Bash', { command: `lsof -i :${8471 + day}` }, at(1)),
-          toolCall('Bash', { command: `kill -9 ${9000 + day}` }, at(2)),
-          toolCall('Bash', { command: 'git status' }, at(3)),
-        ],
-      });
-    }
-    return makeCorpus(sessions);
-  }
-
-  function outDir(): string {
-    return mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-refusal-hygiene-'));
-  }
-
-  function briefingPayload(outPath: string): string {
-    const html = readFileSync(join(outPath, 'loop-hole-briefing.loop.html'), 'utf8');
-    return extractPayload(html)!.payload;
-  }
-
-  const HUGE_PROMPT_MARKER = 'PROMPT-LEAK-MARKER-'.repeat(50);
-
-  function rateLimitedJudge(): () => never {
-    return () => {
-      throw makeExecError({
-        stderr: '\n\nAPIError: 429 rate limit exceeded, retry after 30s\nsome trailing diagnostic noise\n',
-        message: `Command failed: claude -p "${HUGE_PROMPT_MARKER}"\nAPIError: 429 rate limit exceeded, retry after 30s\nsome trailing diagnostic noise\n`,
-      });
-    };
-  }
-
-  it('the triage tier: a thrown judge-spawn error is one line, capped, and stderr-derived in the result, the UNTRIAGED banner, AND the run-summary line — never the prompt', async () => {
-    const corpus = ritualCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out, triageJudge: rateLimitedJudge() });
-
-    expect(result.triage.applied).toBe(false);
-    const reason = result.triage.reason!;
-    expect(reason).toBeDefined();
-    expect(reason).not.toContain('\n');
-    expect(reason.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(200);
-    expect(reason).toContain('APIError: 429 rate limit exceeded');
-    expect(reason).not.toContain('Command failed');
-    expect(reason).not.toContain('PROMPT-LEAK-MARKER');
-
-    const payload = briefingPayload(out);
-    expect(payload).toContain('UNTRIAGED');
-    expect(payload).not.toContain('Command failed');
-    expect(payload).not.toContain('PROMPT-LEAK-MARKER');
-
-    const bannerLine = payload.split('\n').find((line) => line.includes('UNTRIAGED'));
-    expect(bannerLine).toBeDefined();
-    expect(bannerLine).toContain(reason);
-    // The banner really is ONE line: nothing past it on the same physical
-    // line boundary carries a second, unrelated dump fragment.
-    expect((bannerLine!.match(/APIError/g) ?? []).length).toBe(1);
-
-    const summary = tierSummaryLine('triage', result.triage);
-    expect(summary).not.toContain('\n');
-    expect(summary).toContain(reason);
-    expect(summary).not.toContain('Command failed');
-    expect(summary).not.toContain('PROMPT-LEAK-MARKER');
-  });
-
-  it('the analyst tier: same hygiene, in the UN-ANALYZED banner and its run-summary line', async () => {
-    const corpus = ritualCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-    const analystJudge: AnalystJudge = () => {
-      throw makeExecError({
-        stderr: '\n\nAuthenticationError: invalid API key\nsome trailing diagnostic noise\n',
-        message: `Command failed: claude -p "${HUGE_PROMPT_MARKER}"\nAuthenticationError: invalid API key\n`,
-      });
-    };
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out, analystJudge });
-
-    expect(result.analyst.applied).toBe(false);
-    const reason = result.analyst.reason!;
-    expect(reason).not.toContain('\n');
-    expect(reason.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(200);
-    expect(reason).toContain('AuthenticationError: invalid API key');
-    expect(reason).not.toContain('Command failed');
-    expect(reason).not.toContain('PROMPT-LEAK-MARKER');
-
-    const payload = briefingPayload(out);
-    expect(payload).toContain('UN-ANALYZED');
-    expect(payload).not.toContain('PROMPT-LEAK-MARKER');
-
-    const summary = tierSummaryLine('analyst', result.analyst);
-    expect(summary).not.toContain('\n');
-    expect(summary).toContain(reason);
-  });
-
-  it('the synthesis tier: same hygiene, in the UNSYNTHESIZED banner and its run-summary line', async () => {
-    const corpus = ritualCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-    const synthesisJudge: SynthesisJudge = () => {
-      throw makeExecError({
-        stderr: '\n\nAPIError: context length exceeded\nsome trailing diagnostic noise\n',
-        message: `Command failed: claude -p "${HUGE_PROMPT_MARKER}"\nAPIError: context length exceeded\n`,
-      });
-    };
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out, synthesisJudge });
-
-    expect(result.synthesis.applied).toBe(false);
-    const reason = result.synthesis.reason!;
-    expect(reason).not.toContain('\n');
-    expect(reason.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(200);
-    expect(reason).toContain('APIError: context length exceeded');
-    expect(reason).not.toContain('Command failed');
-    expect(reason).not.toContain('PROMPT-LEAK-MARKER');
-
-    const payload = briefingPayload(out);
-    expect(payload).toContain('UNSYNTHESIZED');
-    expect(payload).not.toContain('PROMPT-LEAK-MARKER');
-
-    const summary = synthesisSummaryLine(result.synthesis);
-    expect(summary).not.toContain('\n');
-    expect(summary).toContain(reason);
-  });
-});

-/**
- * repair.ts's own contract, proven at the module level before any caller
- * wires it in: each named unit fixes exactly the form-noise it claims to
- * (table-driven), the whole pipeline is a true no-op on input that already
- * parses, and a unit never changes what a repaired reply MEANS — only how
- * it's punctuated. `attemptRepairedParse` and `repairCitationTokens` get
- * their own sections since they're the seams facets.ts/triage.ts/analyst.ts
- * and synthesis.ts actually wire in front of their validators.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import {
-  attemptRepairedParse,
-  repairCitationTokens,
-  repairHintLine,
-  repairModelOutput,
-} from '../src/repair.js';
-
-describe('repairModelOutput — no-op on clean input', () => {
-  it.each([
-    ['a clean array', '[{"id":"a"}]', 'json-array'] as const,
-    ['a clean object', '{"id":"a"}', 'json-object'] as const,
-    ['an empty array', '[]', 'json-array'] as const,
-    ['whitespace-padded valid JSON', '  [1, 2, 3]  \n', 'json-array'] as const,
-    ['a string already containing an apostrophe', '[{"summary":"don\'t stop"}]', 'json-array'] as const,
-  ])('returns %s byte-identical, unrepaired', (_label, raw, kind) => {
-    const result = repairModelOutput(raw, kind);
-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(false);
-    expect(result.output).toBe(raw);
-    expect(result.pattern).toBeUndefined();
-  });
-
-  it('is a true no-op given fully unparseable prose with no bracket at all', () => {
-    const raw = 'The session shows no recurring pattern.';
-    const result = repairModelOutput(raw, 'json-array');
-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(false);
-    expect(result.output).toBe(raw);
-  });
-
-  it('is a true no-op given a truncated array that never closes', () => {
-    const raw = '[{"id": "a"';
-    const result = repairModelOutput(raw, 'json-array');
-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(false);
-    expect(result.output).toBe(raw);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('repairModelOutput — unit: json-embedded-in-prose', () => {
-  it('extracts a single JSON array wrapped in chatter (the naive-slice case, still handled)', () => {
-    const raw = ['Here is my analysis:', '[{"id":"a"}]', 'Let me know if you need anything else.'].join('\n');
-    const result = repairModelOutput(raw, 'json-array');
-    expect(JSON.parse(result.output)).toEqual([{ id: 'a' }]);
-  });
-
-  it('finds the FIRST balanced span, not the span ending at the LAST bracket in the text — the case naive lastIndexOf slicing gets wrong', () => {
-    const raw = 'the array is [{"id":"a"}] — see also [ref] for details';
-    const result = repairModelOutput(raw, 'json-array');
-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(true);
-    expect(result.pattern).toContain('json-embedded-in-prose');
-    expect(JSON.parse(result.output)).toEqual([{ id: 'a' }]);
-  });
-
-  it('does not miscount a bracket character quoted inside a string as structural', () => {
-    const raw = 'reply: [{"summary": "the array looks like [1,2,3] to me"}] thanks';
-    const result = repairModelOutput(raw, 'json-array');
-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(true);
-    expect(JSON.parse(result.output)).toEqual([{ summary: 'the array looks like [1,2,3] to me' }]);
-  });
-
-  it('gives up cleanly (no repair) when no balanced span exists at all', () => {
-    const raw = 'the array is [{"id": "a"} and it never closes';
-    const result = repairModelOutput(raw, 'json-array');
-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(false);
-    expect(result.output).toBe(raw);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('repairModelOutput — unit: single-quoted-strings', () => {
-  it('converts single-quoted keys and values to double-quoted', () => {
-    const raw = "[{'category': 'friction', 'summary': 'lint keeps failing', 'quotes': ['x']}]";
-    const result = repairModelOutput(raw, 'json-array');
-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(true);
-    expect(result.pattern).toContain('single-quoted-strings');
-    expect(JSON.parse(result.output)).toEqual([{ category: 'friction', summary: 'lint keeps failing', quotes: ['x'] }]);
-  });
-
-  it('unescapes an escaped apostrophe inside a single-quoted string and preserves it literally', () => {
-    const raw = "[{'summary': 'don\\'t stop'}]";
-    const result = repairModelOutput(raw, 'json-array');
-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(true);
-    expect(JSON.parse(result.output)).toEqual([{ summary: "don't stop" }]);
-  });
-
-  it('escapes a literal double quote found inside a single-quoted string', () => {
-    const raw = `[{'summary': 'she said "hi"'}]`;
-    const result = repairModelOutput(raw, 'json-array');
-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(true);
-    expect(JSON.parse(result.output)).toEqual([{ summary: 'she said "hi"' }]);
-  });
-
-  it('never touches an already-valid double-quoted string containing an apostrophe', () => {
-    const raw = '[{"summary": "don\'t stop", "id": "a"}]';
-    const result = repairModelOutput(raw, 'json-array');
-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(false);
-    expect(result.output).toBe(raw);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('repairModelOutput — unit: unquoted-keys', () => {
-  it('quotes bareword object keys', () => {

-    const result = repairModelOutput(raw, 'json-array');
-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(true);
-    expect(result.pattern).toContain('unquoted-keys');

-
-  it('never mistakes a bareword-looking substring inside a string value for a key', () => {
-    const raw = '[{"summary": "the key: value pattern shows up often"}]';
-    const result = repairModelOutput(raw, 'json-array');
-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(false);
-    expect(result.output).toBe(raw);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('repairModelOutput — unit: trailing-commas', () => {
-  it('strips a trailing comma before a closing bracket', () => {

-    const result = repairModelOutput(raw, 'json-array');

-    expect(result.pattern).toContain('trailing-commas');

-
-  it('never strips a comma that is real list separation, not trailing', () => {
-    const raw = '[{"id": "a"}, {"id": "b"}]';
-    const result = repairModelOutput(raw, 'json-array');
-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(false);
-    expect(result.output).toBe(raw);

-
-  it('never touches a comma quoted inside a string value', () => {
-    const raw = '[{"summary": "first, second, third"}]';
-    const result = repairModelOutput(raw, 'json-array');
-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(false);
-    expect(result.output).toBe(raw);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('repairModelOutput — unit: string-where-array (known array fields only)', () => {
-  it('wraps a scalar "quotes" value in a one-element array', () => {
-    const raw = '[{"category": "friction", "summary": "s", "quotes": "the only quote"}]';
-    const result = repairModelOutput(raw, 'json-array');
-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(true);
-    expect(result.pattern).toContain('string-where-array');
-    expect(JSON.parse(result.output)).toEqual([{ category: 'friction', summary: 's', quotes: ['the only quote'] }]);
-  });
-
-  it('never fires on a field already carrying an array', () => {
-    const raw = '[{"quotes": ["already an array"]}]';
-    const result = repairModelOutput(raw, 'json-array');
-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(false);
-    expect(result.output).toBe(raw);

-
-  it('never fires on a field outside the known-array-field list, even a scalar one', () => {

-});
-
-describe('repairModelOutput — unit: null-for-missing-optional', () => {
-  it('normalizes an English sentinel word in an optional field to real JSON null', () => {

-    const result = repairModelOutput(raw, 'json-array');
-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(true);

-  });
-
-  it.each(['none', 'N/A', 'undefined', ''])('normalizes sentinel "%s" the same way', (sentinel) => {

-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(true);
-    expect(JSON.parse(result.output)[0].title).toBeNull();

-
-  it('never touches a required field even when its value is literally the word "null"', () => {
-    const raw = '[{"id": "a", "summary": "null", "quotes": ["x"], "category": "friction"}]';
-    const result = repairModelOutput(raw, 'json-array');
-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(false);

-
-  it('never touches a field outside the optional-sentinel list, e.g. "reason"', () => {
-    const raw = '[{"id": "a", "title": "x", "reason": "null"}]';
-    const result = repairModelOutput(raw, 'json-array');

-
-describe('repairModelOutput — multiple units compose in one pass', () => {
-  it('fixes single quotes, an unquoted key, and a trailing comma together, in one call', () => {

-    const result = repairModelOutput(raw, 'json-array');
-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(true);
-    expect(result.pattern).toContain('json-embedded-in-prose');

-    expect(result.pattern).toContain('unquoted-keys');

-
-describe('repairModelOutput — no-op property (the anti-Goodhart guarantee)', () => {
-  const cleanInputs = [
-    '[]',

-    '[{"category":"friction","summary":"s","quotes":["a","b"]}]',

-  it.each(cleanInputs.map((input, i): [string, string] => [`clean input #${i}`, input]))(

-      const result = repairModelOutput(raw, kind);
-      expect(result.repaired).toBe(false);
-      expect(result.output).toBe(raw);

-
-describe('repairModelOutput — semantic preservation (repair changes form, never meaning)', () => {
-  it('a repaired finding parses to the exact values a human would read from the malformed original', () => {

-    expect(parsed).toEqual([{ category: 'instruction', summary: 'always run the release checklist', quotes: ['run it'] }]);

-
-  it('a repaired triage verdict batch preserves every id, boolean, and merge target exactly', () => {
-    const raw = `[
-      {id: 'intent:0', standable: true, title: 'Check CI status', mergeInto: null, reason: 'clear ask',},

-    ]`;
-    const result = repairModelOutput(raw, 'json-array');
-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(true);

-    expect(parsed).toEqual([
-      { id: 'intent:0', standable: true, title: 'Check CI status', mergeInto: null, reason: 'clear ask' },

-    ]);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('attemptRepairedParse — the wiring seam', () => {
-  interface Item {
-    id: string;
-  }
-  function validate(text: string): Item[] | null {
-    let parsed: unknown;
-    try {
-      parsed = JSON.parse(text);
-    } catch {
-      return null;
-    }
-    if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) return null;
-    const items: Item[] = [];
-    for (const entry of parsed) {
-      if (typeof entry !== 'object' || entry === null) return null;
-      const id = (entry as Record<string, unknown>)['id'];
-      if (typeof id !== 'string') return null;
-      items.push({ id });
-    }
-    return items;
-  }
-
-  it('succeeds unrepaired when the raw text already validates', () => {
-    const outcome = attemptRepairedParse('[{"id":"a"}]', 'json-array', validate);
-    expect(outcome.status).toBe('ok');
-    expect(outcome).toMatchObject({ status: 'ok', repaired: false, value: [{ id: 'a' }] });
-  });
-
-  it('succeeds via repair when the raw text is form-noise but repairable, and names the pattern', () => {
-    const outcome = attemptRepairedParse("[{'id': 'a',}]", 'json-array', validate);
-    expect(outcome.status).toBe('ok');
-    if (outcome.status !== 'ok') throw new Error('unreachable');
-    expect(outcome.repaired).toBe(true);
-    expect(outcome.value).toEqual([{ id: 'a' }]);
-    if (outcome.repaired) {
-      expect(outcome.pattern).toContain('single-quoted-strings');
-      expect(outcome.pattern).toContain('trailing-commas');
-    }
-  });
-
-  it('classifies "wrong" when the text is syntactically valid JSON but fails the validator\'s own semantic rules', () => {
-    const outcome = attemptRepairedParse('[{"id": 5}]', 'json-array', validate);
-    expect(outcome.status).toBe('wrong');
-  });
-
-  it('classifies "malformed-unrepairable" when nothing (not even repair) can produce parseable JSON', () => {
-    const outcome = attemptRepairedParse('the model wrote an essay instead', 'json-array', validate);
-    expect(outcome.status).toBe('malformed-unrepairable');
-  });
-
-  it('classifies "malformed-unrepairable" when repair changes the form but the result still fails to parse', () => {
-    // Single-quote conversion fires (the delimiter changes) but the value's
-    // own content is still fundamentally broken JSON underneath.
-    const outcome = attemptRepairedParse("[{'id': 'a', broken", 'json-array', validate);
-    expect(outcome.status).toBe('malformed-unrepairable');
-  });
-});
-
-describe('repairHintLine', () => {
-  it('names the pattern that required repair', () => {
-    expect(repairHintLine('trailing-commas')).toContain('trailing-commas');
-    expect(repairHintLine('trailing-commas')).toContain('emit strict JSON');
-  });
-});
-
-describe('repairCitationTokens — synthesis citation-token form repair', () => {
-  it.each([
-    ['wrong bracket type (parens)', '(p:1)', '[p:1]'],
-    ['uppercase prefix', '[P:1]', '[p:1]'],
-    ['space after colon', '[p: 1]', '[p:1]'],
-    ['double-quoted id', '[p:"1"]', '[p:1]'],
-    ['single-quoted id', "[p:'1']", '[p:1]'],
-    ['uppercase theme prefix with parens', '(T:2)', '[t:2]'],
-  ])('normalizes %s to the canonical form', (_label, malformed, canonical) => {
-    const result = repairCitationTokens(`Your agents did a thing ${malformed}.`);
-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(true);
-    expect(result.pattern).toBe('citation-token-form');
-    expect(result.output).toContain(canonical);
-  });
-
-  it('is a no-op on an already-canonical token', () => {
-    const sentence = 'Your agents did a thing [p:1].';
-    const result = repairCitationTokens(sentence);
-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(false);
-    expect(result.output).toBe(sentence);
-  });
-
-  it('is a no-op on a sentence with no citation-shaped token at all', () => {
-    const sentence = 'Your agents did a thing with no citation.';
-    const result = repairCitationTokens(sentence);
-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(false);
-    expect(result.output).toBe(sentence);
-  });
-
-  it('never fabricates an id: an unresolvable but well-formed token is left exactly as written', () => {
-    const sentence = 'Something happened here [p:does-not-exist].';
-    const result = repairCitationTokens(sentence);
-    expect(result.repaired).toBe(false);
-    expect(result.output).toBe(sentence);
-  });
-});

-import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { basename, join } from 'node:path';
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import type { Proposal } from '../src/propose.js';
-import { emitProposalPages } from '../src/report.js';
-import { extractPayload } from '../../../loop/src/core/extract.js';
-
-function proposal(candidateId: string, intent: string, score: number, toilFrequency: number): Proposal {
-  return {
-    candidateId,
-    clusterKey: `tool:${candidateId}`,
-    score,
-    intent,
-    form: 'standing-loop',
-    mechanizable: true,
-    mechanizabilityRationale: 'Stable enough to mechanize.',
-    fitnessFunction: {
-      claim: `${intent} stays correct`,
-      method: 'synthetic-test',
-      assertion: 'PASS when the generated result matches the hand-run result; FAIL when it diverges.',
-      draft: true,
-    },
-    payoff: `${intent} saves recurring work.`,
-    citations: [`Bash(${candidateId})`],
-    dossier: {
-      candidateId,
-      clusterKey: `tool:${candidateId}`,
-      kind: 'tool',
-      label: `Bash(${candidateId})`,
-      eventCount: 4,
-      sessionCount: 4,
-      projectCount: 1,
-      projects: ['/Users/me/repo'],
-      cadence: [{ band: '1-3d', fromMs: 86_400_000, toMs: 259_200_000, count: 3 }],
-      exemplars: [`Bash(${candidateId})`],
-      toil: { frequency: toilFrequency, medianDurationMs: 1000, interventions: 0 },
-      firstSeen: '2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z',
-      lastSeen: '2026-06-04T09:00:00.000Z',
-      fingerprint: { kind: 'tool', memberHashes: [`Bash(${candidateId})`] },
-    },
-    tokensUsed: 64,
-  };
-}
-
-describe('nightly briefing', () => {
-  it('ranks same-section proposals by toil and links each one to its opportunity and draft', () => {
-    const outDir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-report-'));
-    // All three share one form and reach tier, so within-section order falls
-    // to toil cost (report.ts's rankByReachThenToil) — the v3 axis that
-    // replaced raw detector score as the briefing's display order.
-    const emitted = emitProposalPages([
-      proposal('low', 'Low toil ritual', 0.12, 1),
-      proposal('high', 'High toil ritual', 0.88, 9),
-      proposal('middle', 'Middle toil ritual', 0.42, 5),

-
-    const html = readFileSync(emitted.briefing.path, 'utf8');

-    expect(payload.indexOf('High toil ritual')).toBeLessThan(payload.indexOf('Middle toil ritual'));
-    expect(payload.indexOf('Middle toil ritual')).toBeLessThan(payload.indexOf('Low toil ritual'));

-    for (const item of emitted.items) {

-      const draftHref = basename(item.draft.path);
-      const row = payload.split('\n').find((line) => line.includes(item.proposal.intent));
-
-      // Rows carry the human-legible evidence (raw scores live on the

-      expect(row).toContain(`seen ${item.proposal.dossier.eventCount}×`);
-      expect(row).toContain(`](${opportunityHref})`);
-      expect(row).toContain(`](${draftHref})`);

-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/resume-dedup.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/resume-dedup.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 06445ea..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/resume-dedup.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,156 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Resumed-session dedup (SYN-L, extract.loop.html c10) — round-5b proved on
- * the real slice: one authored user message, extracted 22 times from one
- * session lineage. A resumed/continued Claude Code session file re-emits
- * prior records verbatim (uuid included); each file generation used to
- * extract them as brand-new events because extraction deduped only on the
- * watermark's session identity, never the record's own. That let a single
- * authored ask masquerade as recurrence — inflating polling-family detection
- * and everything downstream that counts by occurrence.
- *
- * Records here carry the real transcript shape (origin-hygiene.spec.ts's
- * `rawUserRecord` precedent extended with an explicit `uuid`, since these
- * tests need to control identity directly, not just origin fields).
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { ClaudeCodeAdapter } from '../src/claude-adapter.js';
-import { runExtract } from '../src/extract.js';
-import { segmentEpisodes } from '../src/episodes.js';
-import { detectPollingFamilies } from '../src/polling.js';
-import { StateStore } from '../src/store.js';
-import type { ActionEvent } from '../src/types.js';
-import { makeCorpus, userTurn, toolCall } from './helpers.js';
-
-const CWD = '/Users/me/repo';
-const MIN = 60_000;
-
-function userEvents(store: StateStore) {
-  return store.events.filter((e): e is Extract<ActionEvent, { kind: 'user' }> => e.kind === 'user');
-}
-
-/** A record shaped like a real transcript line, with an explicit `uuid` —
- * these tests control identity directly rather than deriving it. */
-function rawUserRecord(uuid: string, text: string, timestamp: string): unknown {
-  return {
-    parentUuid: null,
-    isSidechain: false,
-    userType: 'external',
-    cwd: CWD,
-    type: 'user',
-    message: { role: 'user', content: text },
-    uuid,
-    timestamp,
-  };
-}
-
-describe('resumed-session dedup', () => {
-  it('a record carried into a resumed continuation file extracts exactly once; genuinely new records still land', async () => {
-    const ask = rawUserRecord('uuid-ask-1', "ah ok, let's use skein for the extraction layer", '2026-07-10T09:00:00.000Z');
-    // No explicit uuid — exercises the content+timestamp fallback identity,
-    // the path a hand-built or legacy-shaped tool record takes.
-    const askTool = toolCall('Bash', { command: 'echo noted' }, '2026-07-10T09:00:01.000Z', [], CWD);
-    const newAsk = rawUserRecord('uuid-ask-2', 'ship it', '2026-07-10T09:10:00.000Z');
-
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      { dir: '-Users-me-repo', sessionId: 'sess-original', records: [ask, askTool] },
-      // The resumed continuation: a NEW session file (new session id) that
-      // re-carries the exact prior records — same uuids — plus one genuinely
-      // new turn. Real Claude Code resume/compaction re-emits history this
-      // way; only the file identity changes, never the record's own.
-      { dir: '-Users-me-repo', sessionId: 'sess-resumed', records: [ask, askTool, newAsk] },
-    ]);
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-
-    expect(userEvents(store)).toHaveLength(2); // ask + newAsk, each once — never the resumed file's re-carried copies
-    expect(store.events).toHaveLength(3); // + askTool, also once
-  });
-
-  it('duplicate copies of one ask, re-emitted across a resumed session, form no polling family', async () => {
-    const BASE = Date.parse('2026-07-10T09:00:00.000Z');
-    const askUuid = 'uuid-repeat-ask';
-    const askText = 'any update on the migration?';
-    const at = (offsetMs: number) => new Date(BASE + offsetMs).toISOString();
-
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-resumed-poll',
-        records: [
-          // A substantive kickoff: two real tool calls, never mistaken for a
-          // "quick ask" (mirrors polling-origin.spec.ts's proven shape).
-          userTurn('kick off the migration', at(0), CWD),
-          toolCall('Bash', { command: 'npm run migrate -- --background' }, at(1_000), [], CWD),
-          toolCall('Bash', { command: 'git tag migrate-run' }, at(2_000), [], CWD),
-          // The SAME authored ask, re-carried by six resume/compaction
-          // generations of the SAME session — one identity, six lines.
-          ...Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => rawUserRecord(askUuid, askText, at(10 * MIN + i * 5 * MIN))),
-          // A substantive closer, well past the "quick ask" duration ceiling.
-          userTurn('great, ship it', at(40 * MIN), CWD),
-          toolCall('Bash', { command: 'git push --tags' }, at(43 * MIN), [], CWD),
-        ],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-
-    // The six re-carried copies collapsed to the one record they actually are.
-    const turn = store.internTurn(askText);
-    expect(userEvents(store).filter((e) => e.turn === turn)).toHaveLength(1);
-
-    const episodes = segmentEpisodes(store);
-    const families = await detectPollingFamilies(episodes);
-    expect(families).toEqual([]);
-  });
-
-  it('two genuinely separate asks with identical text but distinct uuids and timestamps both survive', async () => {
-    const text = 'run the tests again';
-    const first = rawUserRecord('uuid-real-1', text, '2026-07-10T09:00:00.000Z');
-    const second = rawUserRecord('uuid-real-2', text, '2026-07-10T10:30:00.000Z');
-
-    const root = makeCorpus([{ dir: '-Users-me-repo', sessionId: 'sess-habit', records: [first, second] }]);
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-
-    const events = userEvents(store);
-    expect(events).toHaveLength(2); // dedup kills copies, never real recurrence
-    expect(new Set(events.map((e) => e.turn)).size).toBe(1); // same interned text, two distinct occurrences
-  });
-
-  it('dedup holds across two incremental runs — the continuation arrives in run 2', async () => {
-    const ask = rawUserRecord('uuid-incremental-ask', 'switch us to skein for extraction', '2026-07-11T09:00:00.000Z');
-
-    const root = makeCorpus([{ dir: '-Users-me-repo', sessionId: 'sess-run1', records: [ask] }]);
-    const adapter = new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root);
-    const storeDir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-resume-dedup-'));
-    const storePath = join(storeDir, 'state.json');
-
-    // Run 1: a fresh store, saved to disk the way a real incremental CLI
-    // invocation would — identity must survive the JSON round-trip, not
-    // just live in one process's memory.
-    const run1 = StateStore.empty();
-    await runExtract(adapter, run1);
-    expect(userEvents(run1)).toHaveLength(1);
-    run1.save(storePath);
-
-    // Run 2: a brand-new process loading that store from disk. The resumed
-    // continuation lands as a new session file in the same corpus,
-    // re-carrying the SAME record (same uuid) plus a genuinely new one.
-    const newAsk = rawUserRecord('uuid-incremental-new', 'ship it', '2026-07-11T09:15:00.000Z');
-    const dir = join(root, '-Users-me-repo');
-    mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
-    writeFileSync(join(dir, 'sess-run2.jsonl'), [ask, newAsk].map((r) => JSON.stringify(r)).join('\n') + '\n');
-
-    const run2 = StateStore.load(storePath);
-    await runExtract(adapter, run2);
-
-    expect(userEvents(run2)).toHaveLength(2); // the original ask, still counted once, + the new one
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/rules-version.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/rules-version.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 1216461..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/rules-version.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,175 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * EXTRACTION_RULES_VERSION (store.ts) is orthogonal to the store's SHAPE
- * version (store-migration.spec.ts): a hygiene fix to extract.ts's semantics
- * — what counts as genuine intent, how a record is deduped — can leave a
- * store holding events that current rules would never have written. Loading
- * such a store must discard events + watermark and force a full, zero-token
- * re-extraction, never let the fix apply only to future records (the SYN-G
- * stale-store poisoning class).
- */
-
-import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
-import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { ClaudeCodeAdapter } from '../src/claude-adapter.js';
-import { runExtract } from '../src/extract.js';
-import { StateStore, EXTRACTION_RULES_VERSION } from '../src/store.js';
-import { extractSummaryLine } from '../src/run.js';
-import type { SessionSource, StoredState } from '../src/types.js';
-import { makeCorpus, userTurn, toolCall } from './helpers.js';
-
-describe('extraction-rules versioning', () => {
-  it('a store built under a superseded rules version triggers full re-extraction, and the poisoned event is gone end to end', async () => {
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-poisoned',
-        records: [
-          userTurn('<task-notification>a background task finished</task-notification>', '2026-05-01T10:00:00.000Z'),
-          toolCall('Bash', { command: 'npm test' }, '2026-05-01T10:00:01.000Z'),
-        ],
-      },
-    ]);
-    const adapter = new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root);
-    let signature = '';
-    for await (const session of adapter.sessions() as AsyncIterable<SessionSource> | Iterable<SessionSource>) {
-      if (session.sessionId === 'sess-poisoned') signature = session.signature;
-    }
-    expect(signature).not.toBe('');
-
-    // A store built under superseded rules that wrongly interned the
-    // task-notification wrapper as a genuine user turn — the exact SYN-G
-    // shape. Its watermark matches the corpus exactly, so under matching
-    // rules this session would be skipped outright.
-    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-rules-version-'));
-    const storePath = join(dir, 'state.json');
-    const poisoned: StoredState = {
-      version: 2,
-      rulesVersion: EXTRACTION_RULES_VERSION - 1,
-      watermark: { 'sess-poisoned': { signature, eventCount: 2 } },
-      turns: ['<task-notification>a background task finished</task-notification>'],
-      events: [
-        {
-          kind: 'user',
-          ts: '2026-05-01T10:00:00.000Z',
-          epoch: Date.parse('2026-05-01T10:00:00.000Z'),
-          project: '/Users/me/repo',
-          session: 'sess-poisoned',
-          turn: 0,
-        },
-        {
-          kind: 'tool',
-          ts: '2026-05-01T10:00:01.000Z',
-          epoch: Date.parse('2026-05-01T10:00:01.000Z'),
-          project: '/Users/me/repo',
-          session: 'sess-poisoned',
-          tool: 'Bash',
-          template: 'Bash(npm test)',
-          outcome: 'unknown',
-        },
-      ],
-    };
-    writeFileSync(storePath, JSON.stringify(poisoned));
-
-    const store = StateStore.load(storePath);
-    expect(store.rulesReset).toEqual({ from: EXTRACTION_RULES_VERSION - 1, to: EXTRACTION_RULES_VERSION });
-
-    const result = await runExtract(adapter, store);
-
-    // Re-read in full despite the on-disk watermark matching the corpus.
-    expect(result.newSessions).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.rulesReset).toEqual({ from: EXTRACTION_RULES_VERSION - 1, to: EXTRACTION_RULES_VERSION });
-
-    // The poisoned turn never re-appears: current rules reject the wrapper.
-    expect(store.events.filter((e) => e.kind === 'user')).toHaveLength(0);
-    expect(store.events).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(store.events[0]?.kind).toBe('tool');
-
-    store.save(storePath);
-    const onDisk = JSON.parse(readFileSync(storePath, 'utf8')) as StoredState;
-    expect(onDisk.rulesVersion).toBe(EXTRACTION_RULES_VERSION);
-  });
-
-  it('a store built under the current rules version behaves incrementally — watermark honored, zero re-reads', async () => {
-    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-rules-version-'));
-    const storePath = join(dir, 'state.json');
-
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-fresh',
-        records: [
-          userTurn('ship the release', '2026-05-01T10:00:00.000Z'),
-          toolCall('Bash', { command: 'npm test' }, '2026-05-01T10:00:01.000Z'),
-        ],
-      },
-    ]);
-    const adapter = new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root);
-    let signature = '';
-    for await (const session of adapter.sessions() as AsyncIterable<SessionSource> | Iterable<SessionSource>) {
-      if (session.sessionId === 'sess-fresh') signature = session.signature;
-    }
-    expect(signature).not.toBe('');
-
-    const current: StoredState = {
-      version: 2,
-      rulesVersion: EXTRACTION_RULES_VERSION,
-      watermark: { 'sess-fresh': { signature, eventCount: 2 } },
-      turns: ['ship the release'],
-      events: [
-        {
-          kind: 'user',
-          ts: '2026-05-01T10:00:00.000Z',
-          epoch: Date.parse('2026-05-01T10:00:00.000Z'),
-          project: '/Users/me/repo',
-          session: 'sess-fresh',
-          turn: 0,
-        },
-        {
-          kind: 'tool',
-          ts: '2026-05-01T10:00:01.000Z',
-          epoch: Date.parse('2026-05-01T10:00:01.000Z'),
-          project: '/Users/me/repo',
-          session: 'sess-fresh',
-          tool: 'Bash',
-          template: 'Bash(npm test)',
-          outcome: 'unknown',
-        },
-      ],
-    };
-    writeFileSync(storePath, JSON.stringify(current));
-
-    const store = StateStore.load(storePath);
-    expect(store.rulesReset).toBeNull();
-
-    const result = await runExtract(adapter, store);
-
-    expect(result.newSessions).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.newEvents).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.rulesReset).toBeNull();
-    expect(store.events).toHaveLength(2);
-  });
-
-  it('the run summary line names the reset, and stays silent when nothing reset', () => {
-    const reset = extractSummaryLine({
-      newSessions: 5,
-      newEvents: 12,
-      totalEvents: 12,
-      excludedSessions: 0,
-      rulesReset: { from: 1, to: 2 },
-    });
-    expect(reset).toContain('re-extracted');
-    expect(reset).toContain('v1');
-    expect(reset).toContain('v2');
-
-    const quiet = extractSummaryLine({
-      newSessions: 0,
-      newEvents: 0,
-      totalEvents: 12,
-      excludedSessions: 0,
-      rulesReset: null,
-    });
-    expect(quiet).not.toContain('re-extracted');
-  });
-});
