diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/session-excludes.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/session-excludes.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 07cf06f..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/session-excludes.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,128 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Operator session excludes: a whole session is kept out of the store when
- * its project path matches a glob from a config list the operator maintains
- * — never a name hand-listed in this test's source or in extract.ts. The
- * excluded count is part of `ExtractRunResult`, the same object the run
- * summary line reads.
- */
-
-import { mkdtempSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
-import { ClaudeCodeAdapter } from '../src/claude-adapter.js';
-import { runExtract } from '../src/extract.js';
-import { StateStore } from '../src/store.js';
-import { compileExcludeGlobs, isExcludedProject, loadExcludeGlobs } from '../src/session-excludes.js';
-import { makeCorpus, toolCall, userTurn } from './helpers.js';
-
-function writeConfig(globs: string[]): string {
-  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-exclude-config-'));
-  const path = join(dir, 'excludes.json');
-  writeFileSync(path, JSON.stringify(globs));
-  return path;
-}
-
-describe('operator session excludes', () => {
-  it('drops every session under an excluded project glob, contributing zero events', async () => {
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-keep',
-        records: [userTurn('ship the release notes', '2026-07-01T09:00:00.000Z')],
-      },
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-worktrees-runner-7',
-        sessionId: 'sess-drop',
-        records: [
-          userTurn('supervise this benchmark pass', '2026-07-01T09:05:00.000Z'),
-          toolCall('Bash', { command: 'npm test' }, '2026-07-01T09:05:01.000Z'),
-        ],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    const result = await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store, {
-      excludeGlobs: ['**/worktrees/**'],
-    });
-
-    expect(result.excludedSessions).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.newSessions).toBe(1);
-    expect(store.events).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(store.watermarkFor('sess-drop')).toBeUndefined();
-    expect(store.watermarkFor('sess-keep')).toBeDefined();
-  });
-
-  it('reports zero excluded sessions when no glob is configured', async () => {
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-a',
-        records: [userTurn('an ordinary ask', '2026-07-01T09:00:00.000Z')],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    const result = await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store);
-
-    expect(result.excludedSessions).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.newSessions).toBe(1);
-  });
-
-  it('counts every excluded session on every pass, independent of the watermark', async () => {
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-benchlab',
-        sessionId: 'sess-bench',
-        records: [userTurn('run the benchmark suite', '2026-07-01T09:00:00.000Z')],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const store = StateStore.empty();
-    const options = { excludeGlobs: ['**/benchlab'] };
-
-    const first = await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store, options);
-    const second = await runExtract(new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root), store, options);
-
-    expect(first.excludedSessions).toBe(1);
-    expect(second.excludedSessions).toBe(1);
-    expect(store.events).toHaveLength(0);
-  });
-
-  it('loads the glob list from a config file the operator owns, not from code', () => {
-    const configPath = writeConfig(['**/harness-*/**', '**/*-benchmark/**']);
-    expect(loadExcludeGlobs(configPath)).toEqual(['**/harness-*/**', '**/*-benchmark/**']);
-  });
-
-  it('treats a missing config file as no excludes, not an error', () => {
-    const missing = join(mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-exclude-missing-')), 'does-not-exist.json');
-    expect(loadExcludeGlobs(missing)).toEqual([]);
-  });
-
-  it('treats a malformed config file (not a JSON array of strings) as no excludes', () => {
-    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-exclude-malformed-'));
-    const path = join(dir, 'excludes.json');
-    writeFileSync(path, JSON.stringify({ not: 'an array' }));
-    expect(loadExcludeGlobs(path)).toEqual([]);
-
-    const mixedPath = join(dir, 'mixed.json');
-    writeFileSync(mixedPath, JSON.stringify(['**/ok/**', 42, null, '**/also-ok/**']));
-    expect(loadExcludeGlobs(mixedPath)).toEqual(['**/ok/**', '**/also-ok/**']);
-  });
-
-  it('matches project globs generically — segment wildcard, deep wildcard, and no accidental partial match', () => {
-    const compiled = compileExcludeGlobs(['/Users/me/worktrees/*/repo', '**/vendor/**']);
-
-    expect(isExcludedProject('/Users/me/worktrees/alpha/repo', compiled)).toBe(true);
-    expect(isExcludedProject('/Users/me/worktrees/beta/repo', compiled)).toBe(true);
-    // '*' stays within one path segment — an extra segment must not match.
-    expect(isExcludedProject('/Users/me/worktrees/alpha/nested/repo', compiled)).toBe(false);
-    expect(isExcludedProject('/Users/me/projects/vendor/thing', compiled)).toBe(true);
-    expect(isExcludedProject('/Users/me/projects/thing', compiled)).toBe(false);
-  });
-
-  it('never excludes anything when the glob list is empty', () => {
-    const compiled = compileExcludeGlobs([]);
-    expect(isExcludedProject('/Users/me/anything', compiled)).toBe(false);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/session-floor.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/session-floor.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index c939ee6..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/session-floor.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,212 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Real-session floor: sessions below a configurable substance floor — fewer
- * than N genuine (non-empty) user turns — are never sent to the facet
- * reader. The floor operates on the digest's own shape (how many real user
- * turns it carries), never on session id or any other name-shaped signal, so
- * a corpus of thousands of smoke-test artifacts costs zero reader calls
- * without anyone maintaining a list of their names (SNAG-18: a real backfill
- * tried to read all 7,562 watermarked sessions because this floor existed
- * only in a guide, never in code).
- */
-
-import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
-import { ClaudeCodeAdapter } from '../src/claude-adapter.js';
-import { runExtract } from '../src/extract.js';
-import { StateStore } from '../src/store.js';
-import type { SessionDigest } from '../src/digest.js';
-import { DEFAULT_MIN_USER_TURNS, meetsSubstanceFloor, type FacetReader } from '../src/facets.js';
-import { FacetStore } from '../src/facet-store.js';
-import { runPipeline } from '../src/run.js';
-import { makeCorpus, toolCall, userTurn } from './helpers.js';
-
-function digestOf(episodes: SessionDigest['episodes']): SessionDigest {
-  return { session: 'sess-x', project: '/repo', date: '2026-06-01T10:00:00.000Z', episodes };
-}
-
-function outDir(): string {
-  return mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-session-floor-'));
-}
-
-function stubReader(calls: string[]): FacetReader {
-  return (prompt) => {
-    calls.push(prompt);
-    return '[]';
-  };
-}
-
-describe('substance floor predicate', () => {
-  it('defaults to requiring at least two genuine user turns', () => {
-    expect(DEFAULT_MIN_USER_TURNS).toBe(2);
-    const oneTurn = digestOf([{ intent: 'one ask', actions: [] }]);
-    const twoTurns = digestOf([
-      { intent: 'first ask', actions: [] },
-      { intent: 'second ask', actions: [] },
-    ]);
-    expect(meetsSubstanceFloor(oneTurn)).toBe(false);
-    expect(meetsSubstanceFloor(twoTurns)).toBe(true);
-  });
-
-  it('is configurable — a caller-supplied floor, not a fixed threshold', () => {
-    const oneTurn = digestOf([{ intent: 'one ask', actions: [] }]);
-    expect(meetsSubstanceFloor(oneTurn, 0)).toBe(true);
-    expect(meetsSubstanceFloor(oneTurn, 1)).toBe(true);
-    expect(meetsSubstanceFloor(oneTurn, 2)).toBe(false);
-    expect(meetsSubstanceFloor(oneTurn, 5)).toBe(false);
-  });
-
-  it('does not count an empty or whitespace-only turn as genuine', () => {
-    const blankPlusReal = digestOf([
-      { intent: '   ', actions: [] },
-      { intent: 'a real ask', actions: [] },
-    ]);
-    expect(meetsSubstanceFloor(blankPlusReal, 2)).toBe(false);
-    expect(meetsSubstanceFloor(blankPlusReal, 1)).toBe(true);
-  });
-
-  it('a session with zero user turns never clears any positive floor', () => {
-    const toolOnly = digestOf([]);
-    expect(meetsSubstanceFloor(toolOnly, 1)).toBe(false);
-    expect(meetsSubstanceFloor(toolOnly, 0)).toBe(true);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('real-session floor — pipeline wiring', () => {
-  it('a session below the configured floor is never sent to the reader; one at/above it is, and its cache entry is the only one written', async () => {
-    const corpus = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-thin',
-        records: [userTurn('quick one-off ask', '2026-06-01T10:00:00.000Z')],
-      },
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo2',
-        sessionId: 'sess-real',
-        records: [
-          userTurn('first real ask', '2026-06-01T10:00:00.000Z'),
-          toolCall('Bash', { command: 'npm test' }, '2026-06-01T10:00:01.000Z'),
-          userTurn('follow-up ask', '2026-06-01T10:05:00.000Z'),
-        ],
-      },
-    ]);
-    const out = outDir();
-    const calls: string[] = [];
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({
-      corpusDir: corpus,
-      outDir: out,
-      facetReader: stubReader(calls),
-      minUserTurns: 2,
-    });
-
-    expect(result.facets.readerCalls).toBe(1);
-    expect(calls).toHaveLength(1);
-
-    const facetStore = FacetStore.load(join(out, 'loop-hole.facets.json'));
-    expect(facetStore.get('sess-real')).toBeDefined();
-    expect(facetStore.get('sess-thin')).toBeUndefined();
-    expect(facetStore.size).toBe(1);
-  });
-
-  it('the floor is genuinely configurable — lowering it admits a session a stricter floor excluded', async () => {
-    const corpus = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-thin',
-        records: [userTurn('quick one-off ask', '2026-06-01T10:00:00.000Z')],
-      },
-    ]);
-
-    const strictOut = outDir();
-    const strictCalls: string[] = [];
-    const strict = await runPipeline({
-      corpusDir: corpus,
-      outDir: strictOut,
-      facetReader: stubReader(strictCalls),
-      minUserTurns: 2,
-    });
-    expect(strict.facets.readerCalls).toBe(0);
-
-    const lenientOut = outDir();
-    const lenientCalls: string[] = [];
-    const lenient = await runPipeline({
-      corpusDir: corpus,
-      outDir: lenientOut,
-      facetReader: stubReader(lenientCalls),
-      minUserTurns: 1,
-    });
-    expect(lenient.facets.readerCalls).toBe(1);
-  });
-
-  it('the floor is a genuine-turn count, not a name filter — a smoke-test-shaped id with enough substance is read; a plainly-named session without it is not', async () => {
-    const corpus = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'anon-fake-smoke-test-9f8e7d',
-        records: [
-          userTurn('first real ask', '2026-06-01T10:00:00.000Z'),
-          userTurn('second real ask', '2026-06-01T10:05:00.000Z'),
-        ],
-      },
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo2',
-        sessionId: 'a-perfectly-ordinary-project-session',
-        records: [userTurn('one quick ask', '2026-06-01T10:00:00.000Z')],
-      },
-    ]);
-    const out = outDir();
-    const calls: string[] = [];
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({
-      corpusDir: corpus,
-      outDir: out,
-      facetReader: stubReader(calls),
-      minUserTurns: 2,
-    });
-
-    expect(result.facets.readerCalls).toBe(1);
-    const facetStore = FacetStore.load(join(out, 'loop-hole.facets.json'));
-    expect(facetStore.get('anon-fake-smoke-test-9f8e7d')).toBeDefined();
-    expect(facetStore.get('a-perfectly-ordinary-project-session')).toBeUndefined();
-  });
-
-  it('with no floor configured, every session is still read — the floor is opt-in, matching existing callers', async () => {
-    const corpus = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-thin',
-        records: [userTurn('quick one-off ask', '2026-06-01T10:00:00.000Z')],
-      },
-    ]);
-    const out = outDir();
-    const calls: string[] = [];
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out, facetReader: stubReader(calls) });
-
-    expect(result.facets.readerCalls).toBe(1);
-  });
-
-  it('a corpus of thousands of below-floor sessions costs zero reader calls', async () => {
-    const sessions = Array.from({ length: 500 }, (_, i) => ({
-      dir: `-Users-me-smoke${i}`,
-      sessionId: `smoke-${i}`,
-      records: [userTurn('x', `2026-06-01T10:${String(i % 60).padStart(2, '0')}:00.000Z`)],
-    }));
-    const corpus = makeCorpus(sessions);
-    const out = outDir();
-    const calls: string[] = [];
-
-    const extracted = await runPipeline({
-      corpusDir: corpus,
-      outDir: out,
-      facetReader: stubReader(calls),
-      minUserTurns: 2,
-    });
-
-    expect(extracted.extract.newSessions).toBe(500);
-    expect(extracted.facets.readerCalls).toBe(0);
-    expect(calls).toHaveLength(0);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/stable-keys.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/stable-keys.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index b024304..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/stable-keys.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,242 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Stable suppression keys (design: docs/loop-hole-episodes-design.md; check
- * c7 in propose.loop.html). A cluster's suppression identity must survive
- * two things a naive label-keyed suppression would break under:
- *
- *  1. Retitling — a candidate's display label is a *chosen* representative
- *     (the shortest member turn), free to change on its own even when the
- *     underlying membership hasn't moved at all.
- *  2. Modest membership drift — a run-to-run change of a member or two in an
- *     otherwise-stable cluster, which changes which turns get folded in
- *     without changing "what this recurring ask is."
- *
- * `fingerprintCandidate` builds the identity from member-content hashes, and
- * `ProposalMemory.isSuppressedNear` matches on those hashes with Jaccard
- * tolerance — the display label never enters either comparison.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { detectRecurrenceCandidates, type RecurrenceCandidate } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import { clusterKey, runPropose, LocalProposer } from '../src/propose.js';
-import { ProposalMemory, fingerprintCandidate, jaccardSimilarity, type ContentFingerprint } from '../src/memory.js';
-import type { ActionEvent, EventTable } from '../src/types.js';
-
-function candidate(overrides: Partial<RecurrenceCandidate> & { id: string }): RecurrenceCandidate {
-  return {
-    kind: 'intent',
-    label: overrides.id,
-    eventCount: 1,
-    sessionCount: 1,
-    projectCount: 1,
-    firstSeen: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
-    lastSeen: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
-    provenance: [],
-    score: {
-      score: 0,
-      components: { frequency: 0, cadenceRegularity: 0, mechanizability: 0, manualCost: 0, stalenessRisk: 0 },
-    },
-    ...overrides,
-  };
-}
-
-/**
- * An intent candidate whose members are exactly `turns[indexes]`, wired the
- * way cluster.ts wires provenance (`turn <index>: <text>`), paired with the
- * fake corpus `memberContent`/`fingerprintCandidate` resolve it against.
- */
-function intentCandidate(
-  id: string,
-  label: string,
-  turns: string[],
-  indexes: number[],
-): { candidate: RecurrenceCandidate; state: EventTable } {
-  return {
-    candidate: candidate({
-      id,
-      kind: 'intent',
-      label,
-      eventCount: indexes.length,
-      provenance: indexes.map((i) => `turn ${i}: ${turns[i]}`),
-    }),
-    state: { events: [], turns },
-  };
-}
-
-const RESTART_TURNS = [
-  'restart the flaky mcp server before standup', // 0
-  'restart the flaky mcp server again', // 1
-  'please restart the flaky mcp server', // 2
-  'restart flaky mcp server one more time', // 3 — v1 only
-  'restart the flaky mcp server right now', // 4 — v2 only
-];
-
-describe('fingerprintCandidate — content, not label', () => {
-  it('is identical for two candidates with the same members but a different display label', () => {
-    const before = intentCandidate('c1', RESTART_TURNS[1]!, RESTART_TURNS, [0, 1, 2]);
-    const after = intentCandidate('c1', RESTART_TURNS[2]!, RESTART_TURNS, [0, 1, 2]); // "retitled"
-
-    expect(clusterKey(before.candidate)).not.toBe(clusterKey(after.candidate));
-
-    const fpBefore = fingerprintCandidate(before.candidate, before.state);
-    const fpAfter = fingerprintCandidate(after.candidate, after.state);
-    expect([...fpAfter.memberHashes].sort()).toEqual([...fpBefore.memberHashes].sort());
-    expect(jaccardSimilarity(fpBefore, fpAfter)).toBe(1);
-  });
-
-  it('degrades gracefully under a modest membership swap', () => {
-    const v1 = intentCandidate('c1', RESTART_TURNS[0]!, RESTART_TURNS, [0, 1, 2, 3]);
-    const v2 = intentCandidate('c1', RESTART_TURNS[0]!, RESTART_TURNS, [0, 1, 2, 4]);
-
-    const similarity = jaccardSimilarity(
-      fingerprintCandidate(v1.candidate, v1.state),
-      fingerprintCandidate(v2.candidate, v2.state),
-    );
-    expect(similarity).toBeCloseTo(3 / 5, 5);
-  });
-
-  it('is zero for wholly unrelated content, and zero across kinds even with matching labels', () => {
-    const turns = ['restart the flaky mcp server again', 'check competitor pricing today'];
-    const a = intentCandidate('a', turns[0]!, turns, [0]);
-    const b = intentCandidate('b', turns[1]!, turns, [1]);
-    expect(
-      jaccardSimilarity(fingerprintCandidate(a.candidate, a.state), fingerprintCandidate(b.candidate, b.state)),
-    ).toBe(0);
-
-    const state: EventTable = { events: [], turns: [] };
-    const toolA = candidate({ id: 'tool-a', kind: 'tool', label: 'Bash(lsof -i :8471)' });
-    const intentA = candidate({ id: 'intent-a', kind: 'intent', label: 'Bash(lsof -i :8471)', provenance: [] });
-    expect(
-      jaccardSimilarity(fingerprintCandidate(toolA, state), fingerprintCandidate(intentA, state)),
-    ).toBe(0);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('ProposalMemory.isSuppressedNear — suppression that survives drift', () => {
-  it('stays suppressed after the candidate is retitled, though the exact key changes', () => {
-    const before = intentCandidate('c1', RESTART_TURNS[1]!, RESTART_TURNS, [0, 1, 2]);
-    const after = intentCandidate('c1', RESTART_TURNS[2]!, RESTART_TURNS, [0, 1, 2]);
-
-    const memory = ProposalMemory.empty();
-    memory.reject(
-      clusterKey(before.candidate),
-      before.candidate.id,
-      'automate the mcp restart',
-      'covered elsewhere',
-      '2026-06-05T10:00:00Z',
-      fingerprintCandidate(before.candidate, before.state),
-    );
-
-    const afterKey = clusterKey(after.candidate);
-    const afterFingerprint = fingerprintCandidate(after.candidate, after.state);
-
-    // The naive exact-key check would miss it — proves the test exercises the drift.
-    expect(memory.isSuppressed(afterKey)).toBe(false);
-    expect(memory.isSuppressedNear(afterKey, afterFingerprint)).toBe(true);
-  });
-
-  it('stays suppressed after modest membership drift (one member swapped, most unchanged)', () => {
-    const v1 = intentCandidate('c1', RESTART_TURNS[0]!, RESTART_TURNS, [0, 1, 2, 3]);
-    const v2 = intentCandidate('c1', RESTART_TURNS[0]!, RESTART_TURNS, [0, 1, 2, 4]);
-
-    const memory = ProposalMemory.empty();
-    memory.reject(
-      clusterKey(v1.candidate),
-      v1.candidate.id,
-      'automate the mcp restart',
-      'not worth a loop',
-      '2026-06-05T10:00:00Z',
-      fingerprintCandidate(v1.candidate, v1.state),
-    );
-
-    expect(
-      memory.isSuppressedNear(clusterKey(v2.candidate), fingerprintCandidate(v2.candidate, v2.state)),
-    ).toBe(true);
-  });
-
-  it('does not suppress a cluster that has substantially reformed into something else', () => {
-    const turns = [...RESTART_TURNS, 'check competitor pricing today', 'any competitor pricing changes'];
-    const rejected = intentCandidate('c1', turns[0]!, turns, [0, 1]);
-    const reformed = intentCandidate('c1', turns[5]!, turns, [5, 6]);
-
-    const memory = ProposalMemory.empty();
-    memory.reject(
-      clusterKey(rejected.candidate),
-      rejected.candidate.id,
-      'automate the mcp restart',
-      'not worth a loop',
-      '2026-06-05T10:00:00Z',
-      fingerprintCandidate(rejected.candidate, rejected.state),
-    );
-
-    expect(
-      memory.isSuppressedNear(clusterKey(reformed.candidate), fingerprintCandidate(reformed.candidate, reformed.state)),
-    ).toBe(false);
-  });
-
-  it('falls back to exact-key matching for legacy entries with no stored fingerprint', () => {
-    const memory = ProposalMemory.empty();
-    memory.reject('intent:legacy label', 'legacy-1', 'legacy intent', 'no reason recorded', '2026-06-05T10:00:00Z');
-    const unrelated: ContentFingerprint = { kind: 'intent', memberHashes: ['zzz'] };
-    expect(memory.isSuppressedNear('intent:legacy label', unrelated)).toBe(true);
-    expect(memory.isSuppressedNear('intent:something else', unrelated)).toBe(false);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('runPropose end-to-end — drift-tolerant suppression through the real detector', () => {
-  const START = Date.parse('2026-06-01T09:00:00Z');
-  const DAY = 86_400_000;
-
-  function stateFor(turns: string[]): EventTable {
-    const events: ActionEvent[] = [];
-    for (let i = 0; i < turns.length * 3; i += 1) {
-      events.push({
-        kind: 'user',
-        ts: new Date(START + i * DAY).toISOString(),
-        epoch: START + i * DAY,
-        project: '/Users/me/repo',
-        session: `session-${i}`,
-        turn: i % turns.length,
-      });
-    }
-    return { events, turns };
-  }
-
-  it('a rejection recorded against v1 still suppresses the drifted, retitled v2 candidate', async () => {
-    const v1Turns = RESTART_TURNS.slice(0, 4); // 0,1,2,3 — shortest member is 1
-    const v2Turns = [RESTART_TURNS[0]!, RESTART_TURNS[2]!, RESTART_TURNS[3]!, RESTART_TURNS[4]!]; // 1 dropped, 4 added — shortest member is now 2
-
-    const v1State = stateFor(v1Turns);
-    const v2State = stateFor(v2Turns);
-    const now = START + v1Turns.length * 3 * DAY;
-
-    const v1Candidates = await detectRecurrenceCandidates(v1State, { now, intentSimilarity: 0.4 });
-    const v2Candidates = await detectRecurrenceCandidates(v2State, { now, intentSimilarity: 0.4 });
-    expect(v1Candidates.length).toBe(1);
-    expect(v2Candidates.length).toBe(1);
-    // The detector chose different representatives for v1 and v2 — a real retitle.
-    expect(v1Candidates[0]!.label).not.toBe(v2Candidates[0]!.label);
-
-    const proposer = new LocalProposer();
-    const memory = ProposalMemory.empty();
-
-    const first = await runPropose(v1Candidates, v1State, { proposer, budgetTokens: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY });
-    const rejected = first.proposals[0]!;
-    memory.reject(
-      rejected.clusterKey,
-      rejected.candidateId,
-      rejected.intent,
-      'handled outside loop-hole',
-      '2026-06-05T10:00:00Z',
-      rejected.dossier.fingerprint,
-    );
-
-    const second = await runPropose(v2Candidates, v2State, {
-      proposer,
-      budgetTokens: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY,
-      memory,
-    });
-
-    expect(second.suppressed).toBe(1);
-    expect(second.proposals.length).toBe(0);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/store-migration.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/store-migration.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 6c69a6a..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/store-migration.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,108 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * A store written before `outcome`/`tokens` existed must still load cleanly:
- * missing tool outcomes default to `'unknown'`, the watermark that gates the
- * next extract pass survives untouched, and the store re-saves stamped as the
- * current version — all without forcing a full re-extraction of sessions the
- * watermark already covers.
- */
-
-import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
-import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { ClaudeCodeAdapter } from '../src/claude-adapter.js';
-import { runExtract } from '../src/extract.js';
-import { StateStore, EXTRACTION_RULES_VERSION } from '../src/store.js';
-import type { SessionSource } from '../src/types.js';
-import { makeCorpus, userTurn, toolCall } from './helpers.js';
-
-/** A v1 payload as it would have been written before outcome/tokens existed:
- * tool events carry no `outcome` field at all, user events carry no `tokens`.
- * Pinned to the CURRENT rules version — this fixture predates only the store
- * SHAPE (rules-version.spec.ts owns the orthogonal RULES-version concern; a
- * mismatch there discards events + watermark, which would defeat every
- * assertion below that they survive migration untouched). */
-function v1Payload() {
-  return {
-    version: 1,
-    rulesVersion: EXTRACTION_RULES_VERSION,
-    watermark: {
-      'sess-legacy': { signature: 'sig-v1', eventCount: 2 },
-    },
-    turns: ['ship the release'],
-    events: [
-      { kind: 'user', ts: '2026-05-01T10:00:00.000Z', epoch: Date.parse('2026-05-01T10:00:00.000Z'), project: '/Users/me/repo', session: 'sess-legacy', turn: 0 },
-      { kind: 'tool', ts: '2026-05-01T10:00:01.000Z', epoch: Date.parse('2026-05-01T10:00:01.000Z'), project: '/Users/me/repo', session: 'sess-legacy', tool: 'Bash', template: 'Bash(npm test)' },
-    ],
-  };
-}
-
-describe('store migration: v1 → v2', () => {
-  it('loads a v1 store cleanly, defaulting missing outcome to unknown and preserving the watermark', () => {
-    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-store-migration-'));
-    const storePath = join(dir, 'state.json');
-    writeFileSync(storePath, JSON.stringify(v1Payload()));
-
-    const store = StateStore.load(storePath);
-
-    expect(store.turns).toEqual(['ship the release']);
-    expect(store.events).toHaveLength(2);
-
-    const toolEvent = store.events.find((e) => e.kind === 'tool');
-    if (toolEvent === undefined || toolEvent.kind !== 'tool') throw new Error('expected a tool event');
-    expect(toolEvent.outcome).toBe('unknown');
-    expect(toolEvent.template).toBe('Bash(npm test)');
-
-    const userEvent = store.events.find((e) => e.kind === 'user');
-    if (userEvent === undefined || userEvent.kind !== 'user') throw new Error('expected a user event');
-    expect(userEvent.tokens).toBeUndefined();
-
-    const watermark = store.watermarkFor('sess-legacy');
-    expect(watermark).toEqual({ signature: 'sig-v1', eventCount: 2 });
-  });
-
-  it('re-saves a migrated store as version 2', () => {
-    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-store-migration-'));
-    const storePath = join(dir, 'state.json');
-    writeFileSync(storePath, JSON.stringify(v1Payload()));
-
-    const store = StateStore.load(storePath);
-    store.save(storePath);
-
-    const onDisk = JSON.parse(readFileSync(storePath, 'utf8')) as { version: number };
-    expect(onDisk.version).toBe(2);
-  });
-
-  it('does not force a full re-extraction of sessions the migrated watermark already covers', async () => {
-    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-store-migration-'));
-    const storePath = join(dir, 'state.json');
-
-    // Build a real corpus and take its actual signature, so the migrated
-    // watermark genuinely matches what the adapter reports next pass.
-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-        sessionId: 'sess-legacy',
-        records: [userTurn('ship the release', '2026-05-01T10:00:00.000Z'), toolCall('Bash', { command: 'npm test' }, '2026-05-01T10:00:01.000Z')],
-      },
-    ]);
-    const adapter = new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root);
-    let signature = '';
-    for await (const session of adapter.sessions() as AsyncIterable<SessionSource> | Iterable<SessionSource>) {
-      if (session.sessionId === 'sess-legacy') signature = session.signature;
-    }
-    expect(signature).not.toBe('');
-
-    const payload = v1Payload();
-    payload.watermark['sess-legacy']!.signature = signature;
-    writeFileSync(storePath, JSON.stringify(payload));
-
-    const store = StateStore.load(storePath);
-    const result = await runExtract(adapter, store);
-
-    expect(result.newSessions).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.newEvents).toBe(0);
-    // The pre-migration events are still there — no silent wipe/rebuild.
-    expect(store.events).toHaveLength(2);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/suppress.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/suppress.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index e596497..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/suppress.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,92 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Suppression memory: a rejected proposal is a decision, not a suggestion. Its
- * cluster does not come back on the next run, and the reason the human gave is
- * kept — so "no" is answered, never silently re-asked.
- */
-
-import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { detectRecurrenceCandidates } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import { runPropose, LocalProposer } from '../src/propose.js';
-import { ProposalMemory } from '../src/memory.js';
-import { seededState } from './cluster-fixture.js';
-
-const NOW = Date.parse('2026-06-05T09:00:00Z');
-
-async function seededCandidates() {
-  return detectRecurrenceCandidates(seededState(), { now: NOW, intentSimilarity: 0.45 });
-}
-
-describe('suppression memory', () => {
-  it('does not re-propose a rejected cluster on the next run, and keeps the reason', async () => {
-    const state = seededState();
-    const candidates = await seededCandidates();
-    const proposer = new LocalProposer();
-
-    const first = await runPropose(candidates, state, { proposer, budgetTokens: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY });
-    expect(first.proposals.length).toBeGreaterThan(1);
-
-    // Reject the top proposal's cluster with a stated reason.
-    const rejected = first.proposals[0]!;
-    const reason = 'already handled by CI — not worth a loop';
-    const memory = ProposalMemory.empty();
-    memory.reject(rejected.clusterKey, rejected.candidateId, rejected.intent, reason, '2026-06-05T10:00:00Z');
-
-    const second = await runPropose(candidates, state, { proposer, budgetTokens: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY, memory });
-
-    // The rejected cluster is gone; nothing else is.
-    expect(second.proposals.some((p) => p.clusterKey === rejected.clusterKey)).toBe(false);
-    expect(second.proposals.length).toBe(first.proposals.length - 1);
-    expect(second.suppressed).toBe(1);
-    for (const survivor of first.proposals.slice(1)) {
-      expect(second.proposals.some((p) => p.clusterKey === survivor.clusterKey)).toBe(true);
-    }
-
-    // The reason is retrievable — the human's "no" is on record.
-    const decision = memory.decisionFor(rejected.clusterKey);
-    expect(decision?.decision).toBe('rejected');
-    expect(decision?.reason).toBe(reason);
-  });
-
-  it('a later acceptance lifts an earlier rejection of the same cluster', async () => {
-    const state = seededState();
-    const candidates = await seededCandidates();
-    const proposer = new LocalProposer();
-    const baseline = await runPropose(candidates, state, { proposer, budgetTokens: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY });
-    const target = baseline.proposals[0]!;
-
-    const memory = ProposalMemory.empty();
-    memory.reject(target.clusterKey, target.candidateId, target.intent, 'not now', '2026-06-05T10:00:00Z');
-    expect(memory.isSuppressed(target.clusterKey)).toBe(true);
-
-    memory.accept(target.clusterKey, target.candidateId, target.intent, '2026-06-06T10:00:00Z');
-    expect(memory.isSuppressed(target.clusterKey)).toBe(false);
-
-    const after = await runPropose(candidates, state, { proposer, budgetTokens: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY, memory });
-    expect(after.proposals.some((p) => p.clusterKey === target.clusterKey)).toBe(true);
-    expect(after.suppressed).toBe(0);
-  });
-
-  it('persists suppression across a save/load round-trip', async () => {
-    const state = seededState();
-    const candidates = await seededCandidates();
-    const proposer = new LocalProposer();
-    const baseline = await runPropose(candidates, state, { proposer, budgetTokens: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY });
-    const target = baseline.proposals[0]!;
-
-    const path = join(mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loophole-memory-')), 'memory.json');
-    const memory = ProposalMemory.empty();
-    memory.reject(target.clusterKey, target.candidateId, target.intent, 'redundant', '2026-06-05T10:00:00Z');
-    memory.save(path);
-
-    const reloaded = ProposalMemory.load(path);
-    expect(reloaded.isSuppressed(target.clusterKey)).toBe(true);
-    expect(reloaded.decisionFor(target.clusterKey)?.reason).toBe('redundant');
-
-    const rerun = await runPropose(candidates, state, { proposer, budgetTokens: Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY, memory: reloaded });
-    expect(rerun.proposals.some((p) => p.clusterKey === target.clusterKey)).toBe(false);
-    expect(rerun.suppressed).toBe(1);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/synthesis-fixtures.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/synthesis-fixtures.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 4bf93ac..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/synthesis-fixtures.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,70 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Shared prose fixtures for the synthesis tier's tests (synthesis.spec.ts) —
- * one home for every quote/intent/label string reused across cases, the same
- * discipline as fixtures.ts (turns) and facet-theme-fixtures.ts (theme
- * summaries): rotate the phrasing, keep the behavior, and fixture-leak.spec.ts
- * scans every one of them for a leak into src/.
- *
- * Titles here are display strings only — synthesis.spec.ts never inspects
- * their wording, only that they're present and distinct — so they're kept
- * short and deliberately unrelated to any production proposal-copy template.
- */
-
-/** An agent-lane tool ritual — cited by the mixed-form dossier's `standing-loop`
- * proposal and by citation-validation cases exercising the "agent" lane. */
-export const AGENT_RITUAL_QUOTE = 'Bash(tail -n 50 CHANGELOG.md)' as const;
-export const AGENT_RITUAL_INTENT = 'the release-notes re-check ritual' as const;
-
-/** A user-lane recurring ask — cited by the mixed-form dossier's `push-loop`
- * proposal. */
-export const USER_ASK_QUOTE = 'is the nightly ingest job still green?' as const;
-export const USER_ASK_INTENT = 'the nightly ingest status check' as const;
-
-/** A workflow-pattern proposal — cited by the mixed-form dossier's
- * `distilled-skill` proposal. */
-export const WORKFLOW_QUOTE = 'always rebuild the static site before checking the preview' as const;
-export const WORKFLOW_INTENT = 'the rebuild-before-preview habit' as const;
-
-/** A tool-candidate proposal — cited by the mixed-form dossier's
- * `missing-tool` proposal. */
-export const TOOL_GAP_QUOTE = 'manually grep every service log line for the request id' as const;
-export const TOOL_GAP_INTENT = 'the request-id log search gap' as const;
-
-/** A standing-instruction proposal — cited by the mixed-form dossier's
- * `standing-instruction` proposal. */
-export const INSTRUCTION_QUOTE = 'never merge without running the full lint suite first' as const;
-export const INSTRUCTION_INTENT = 'the lint-before-merge rule' as const;
-
-/** A cross-session facet theme's label/quote — cited via `[t:<id>]`. */
-export const THEME_QUOTE = 'reboot the sandbox database once a schema migration lands' as const;
-export const THEME_LABEL = 'reboot the sandbox once a migration lands' as const;
-
-/** A per-session facet FINDING's verbatim quote — the person's own words the
- * comprehension tier captured, cited via `[f:<id>]`. In the e2e it must appear
- * verbatim in a corpus user turn so quote-honesty resolves it, and the
- * synthesis judge may quote it back verbatim (double-quoted) inside a sentence
- * that cites the finding. */
-export const FINDING_QUOTE = 'always knead the brioche dough until it turns silky' as const;
-export const FINDING_SUMMARY = 'the silky-brioche kneading standard' as const;
-
-/** The fabricated phrase the misquote counter-check invents — never present in
- * any finding's own quotes, so a sentence quoting it must drop fail-closed. */
-export const FINDING_MISQUOTE = 'scorch every muffin in the communal oven' as const;
-
-export const ALL_SYNTHESIS_FIXTURE_STRINGS: readonly string[] = [
-  AGENT_RITUAL_QUOTE,
-  AGENT_RITUAL_INTENT,
-  USER_ASK_QUOTE,
-  USER_ASK_INTENT,
-  WORKFLOW_QUOTE,
-  WORKFLOW_INTENT,
-  TOOL_GAP_QUOTE,
-  TOOL_GAP_INTENT,
-  INSTRUCTION_QUOTE,
-  INSTRUCTION_INTENT,
-  THEME_QUOTE,
-  THEME_LABEL,
-  FINDING_QUOTE,
-  FINDING_SUMMARY,
-  FINDING_MISQUOTE,
-];
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/synthesis-person.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/synthesis-person.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 627eb6b..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/synthesis-person.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,232 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Person-level synthesis (src/synthesis.ts, check c13 on report.loop.html) —
- * the paid comprehension tier's per-session facet FINDINGS must reach the
- * synthesis dossier as first-class citable items, alongside the classified +
- * priced proposals and the cross-session themes, so the briefing prose can be
- * written about the PERSON (their standing instructions, quality bars,
- * working patterns, frictions) in their own verbatim voice — not just narrate
- * the proposal ranking. Themes are aggregates and lose the verbatim voice;
- * findings carry it. This is the same evaporation class as the pre-#44
- * typology collapse, one layer up: intelligence spent on fragments, then the
- * specific material dropped one layer before the page.
- *
- * Four behavior classes, all at the application layer (a deterministic stub
- * stands in for the model; the model itself is proven on real data + human
- * verdicts, never mocked as the unit under test):
- *  - (a) a dossier built from findings carries them as `[f:<id>]`-citable
- *        items in their own namespace, verbatim quotes intact;
- *  - (b) prose citing a finding token with a verbatim quote survives;
- *  - (c) prose citing an invented finding id, or MISQUOTING a real finding, is
- *        dropped fail-closed with the drop counted;
- *  - (d) END TO END through the one-command pipeline: on a corpus carrying
- *        facet findings, the emitted briefing prose cites at least one finding
- *        item — prose citing only proposal items leaves the person's own voice
- *        on the table, the failing condition this check exists to catch.
- */
-
-import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { extractPayload } from '../../../loop/src/core/extract.js';
-import { runPipeline } from '../src/run.js';
-import type { FacetCategory, FacetReader } from '../src/facets.js';
-import {
-  buildSynthesisDossier,
-  validateSynthesisProse,
-  type DossierFinding,
-} from '../src/synthesis.js';
-import { makeCorpus, toolCall, userTurn } from './helpers.js';
-import {
-  FINDING_MISQUOTE,
-  FINDING_QUOTE,
-  FINDING_SUMMARY,
-} from './synthesis-fixtures.js';
-
-function finding(spec: {
-  session: string;
-  category: FacetCategory;
-  summary: string;
-  quotes: string[];
-}): DossierFinding {
-  return { session: spec.session, category: spec.category, summary: spec.summary, quotes: spec.quotes };
-}
-
-describe('buildSynthesisDossier — findings as first-class citable items', () => {
-  it('carries every finding in its own [f:<id>] namespace with verbatim quotes intact', () => {
-    const dossier = buildSynthesisDossier([], [], [
-      finding({ session: 'sess-a', category: 'quality-bar', summary: FINDING_SUMMARY, quotes: [FINDING_QUOTE] }),
-    ]);
-
-    expect(dossier.findings).toHaveLength(1);
-    const [item] = dossier.findings;
-    expect(item!.kind).toBe('finding');
-    expect(item!.category).toBe('quality-bar');
-    expect(item!.quotes).toEqual([FINDING_QUOTE]);
-    // The id is a dossier-local sequence number in its own namespace — a
-    // proposal and a finding may reuse the same number, disambiguated by p:/f:.
-    expect(item!.id).toBe('1');
-  });
-
-  it('caps findings by a deterministic priority (category weight × frequency) and COUNTS the capped-out ones', () => {
-    // instruction outweighs tool-candidate; a two-quote finding outweighs a
-    // one-quote one within the same category. With a cap of 1, the single
-    // richest finding survives and the rest are counted, never silently cut.
-    const findings = [
-      finding({ session: 's1', category: 'tool-candidate', summary: FINDING_SUMMARY, quotes: [FINDING_QUOTE] }),
-      finding({ session: 's2', category: 'instruction', summary: FINDING_SUMMARY, quotes: [FINDING_QUOTE, FINDING_MISQUOTE] }),
-      finding({ session: 's3', category: 'instruction', summary: FINDING_SUMMARY, quotes: [FINDING_QUOTE] }),
-    ];
-
-    const dossier = buildSynthesisDossier([], [], findings, { maxFindings: 1 });
-
-    expect(dossier.findings).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(dossier.findings[0]!.category).toBe('instruction');
-    expect(dossier.findings[0]!.frequency).toBe(2);
-    expect(dossier.cappedFindings).toBe(2);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('validateSynthesisProse — finding citations survive, misquotes drop', () => {
-  const dossier = buildSynthesisDossier([], [], [
-    finding({ session: 'sess-a', category: 'quality-bar', summary: FINDING_SUMMARY, quotes: [FINDING_QUOTE] }),
-  ]);
-  const fId = dossier.findings[0]!.id;
-
-  it('keeps a sentence citing a finding and quoting it verbatim', () => {
-    const result = validateSynthesisProse(`You keep insisting your agents "${FINDING_QUOTE}" [f:${fId}].`, dossier);
-    expect(result.droppedSentences).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.validatedText).toContain(`[f:${fId}]`);
-  });
-
-  it('drops a sentence citing a finding id that does not resolve in the dossier', () => {
-    const result = validateSynthesisProse('You always said this once [f:does-not-exist].', dossier);
-    expect(result.validatedText).toBe('');
-    expect(result.droppedSentences).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.dropped[0]!.reason).toBe('unresolvable-citation');
-  });
-
-  it('drops a sentence that MISQUOTES a real finding — the quoted text is nowhere in its quotes', () => {
-    const result = validateSynthesisProse(`You told your agents to "${FINDING_MISQUOTE}" [f:${fId}].`, dossier);
-    expect(result.validatedText).toBe('');
-    expect(result.droppedSentences).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.dropped[0]!.reason).toBe('misquote');
-  });
-});
-
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-const START = Date.parse('2026-06-01T09:00:00Z');
-
-/** A corpus whose sessions carry the person's own verbatim standing bar as a
- * user turn, so a facet reader can surface it as a finding whose quote
- * resolves against the session's own digest (facets.ts's quote-honesty gate). */
-function findingCorpus(): string {
-  const sessions = [];
-  for (let day = 0; day < 2; day += 1) {
-    const at = (offset: number) => new Date(START + day * DAY + offset * 60_000).toISOString();
-    sessions.push({
-      dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-      sessionId: `finding-session-${day}`,
-      records: [
-        userTurn(FINDING_QUOTE, at(0)),
-        userTurn('and keep the changelog current on every merge', at(1)),
-        toolCall('Bash', { command: 'git status' }, at(2)),
-      ],
-    });
-  }
-  return makeCorpus(sessions);
-}
-
-/** A reader that reports the person's standing bar as one quality-bar finding
- * per session, quoting the exact user turn so quote-honesty resolves it. */
-function findingReader(): FacetReader {
-  return () =>
-    JSON.stringify([{ category: 'quality-bar', summary: FINDING_SUMMARY, quotes: [FINDING_QUOTE] }]);
-}
-
-/** Pull the first finding straight out of the REAL prompt a synthesis call
- * receives — never a guessed id — so a stub judge cites an item that actually
- * exists. */
-function firstFindingFromPrompt(prompt: string): { id: string; quotes: string[] } {
-  const marker = 'FACET FINDINGS — cite as [f:<id>]:\n';
-  const start = prompt.indexOf(marker);
-  if (start === -1) throw new Error('synthesis prompt carried no FACET FINDINGS block');
-  const bodyStart = start + marker.length;
-  const bodyEnd = prompt.indexOf('\n\n', bodyStart);
-  const findings = JSON.parse(prompt.slice(bodyStart, bodyEnd === -1 ? undefined : bodyEnd)) as Array<{
-    id: string;
-    quotes: string[];
-  }>;
-  const [first] = findings;
-  if (first === undefined) throw new Error('the dossier carried no findings to cite');
-  return first;
-}
-
-const outDirs: string[] = [];
-afterEach(() => {
-  for (const dir of outDirs.splice(0)) rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
-});
-
-function outDir(): string {
-  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loop-hole-synthesis-person-'));
-  outDirs.push(dir);
-  return dir;
-}
-
-function briefingPayload(outPath: string): string {
-  const html = readFileSync(join(outPath, 'loop-hole-briefing.loop.html'), 'utf8');
-  return extractPayload(html)!.payload;
-}
-
-describe('pipeline wiring — the person is in the briefing, not just the ranking (c13, end to end)', () => {
-  it('a person-first judge citing a finding survives end to end; the emitted briefing quotes the person in the finding namespace', async () => {
-    const corpus = findingCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({
-      corpusDir: corpus,
-      outDir: out,
-      facetReader: findingReader(),
-      synthesisJudge: (prompt) => {
-        const first = firstFindingFromPrompt(prompt);
-        return `## Standing bars\nYou keep telling your agents "${first.quotes[0]}" [f:${first.id}].`;
-      },
-    });
-
-    expect(result.synthesis.applied).toBe(true);
-    expect(result.synthesis.droppedSentences).toBe(0);
-
-    const payload = briefingPayload(out);
-    expect(payload).not.toContain('UNSYNTHESIZED');
-    // The person's own voice, in the finding namespace, made it to the page.
-    expect(payload).toMatch(/\[f:\d+\]/);
-    expect(payload).toContain(FINDING_QUOTE);
-  });
-
-  it('prose citing ONLY proposal items leaves the finding namespace off the page — the person-blind failing condition', async () => {
-    const corpus = findingCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-
-    // A judge that cites only proposals: on a corpus that carries findings,
-    // its briefing never quotes the person in the [f:<id>] namespace.
-    const proposalOnly = (prompt: string): string => {
-      const marker = 'PROPOSALS — cite as [p:<id>]:\n';
-      const start = prompt.indexOf(marker) + marker.length;
-      const end = prompt.indexOf('\n\nFACET THEMES', start);
-      const proposals = JSON.parse(prompt.slice(start, end)) as Array<{ id: string; eventCount: number }>;
-      const first = proposals[0];
-      if (first === undefined) return '## Recurs\nNothing recurs here at all in this run.';
-      return `## Recurs\nThis recurred ${first.eventCount} time(s) [p:${first.id}].`;
-    };
-
-    await runPipeline({
-      corpusDir: corpus,
-      outDir: out,
-      facetReader: findingReader(),
-      synthesisJudge: proposalOnly,
-    });
-
-    const payload = briefingPayload(out);
-    expect(payload).not.toMatch(/\[f:\d+\]/);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/synthesis.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/synthesis.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index c398168..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/synthesis.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,513 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * The synthesis tier (src/synthesis.ts, design: docs/loop-hole-episodes-
- * design.md "v4: the synthesis inversion") — the one call that WRITES the
- * briefing's lead instead of leaving it to report.ts's template glue. Four
- * properties are proven here, all at the application layer (a deterministic
- * stub stands in for the model; the model itself is proven on real data +
- * human verdicts, never mocked as the unit under test):
- *  - citation discipline: a sentence survives only when it ends in exactly
- *    one citation token that resolves against the dossier;
- *  - number recomputation: every number a surviving sentence states must
- *    match its cited item's own numbers, exactly;
- *  - fail-closed refusal: a throwing judge, or one whose every sentence fails
- *    validation, degrades to the template briefing with a visible
- *    UNSYNTHESIZED banner — never a partial, unmarked degradation;
- *  - form-flow correctness: the dossier's forms come from each proposal's own
- *    `form` field (typology.ts + themes.ts's category mapping), never from
- *    the coarser `dossier.kind` — a mixed-shape input must never collapse
- *    into one form.
- */
-
-import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { extractPayload } from '../../../loop/src/core/extract.js';
-import { runPipeline } from '../src/run.js';
-import type { Proposal } from '../src/propose.js';
-import type { ProposalForm } from '../src/typology.js';
-import type { FacetCategory } from '../src/facets.js';
-import type { FacetTheme } from '../src/themes.js';
-import {
-  buildSynthesisDossier,
-  runSynthesis,
-  validateSynthesisProse,
-  type SynthesisJudge,
-} from '../src/synthesis.js';
-import { makeCorpus, toolCall, userTurn } from './helpers.js';
-import {
-  AGENT_RITUAL_INTENT,
-  AGENT_RITUAL_QUOTE,
-  INSTRUCTION_INTENT,
-  INSTRUCTION_QUOTE,
-  THEME_LABEL,
-  THEME_QUOTE,
-  TOOL_GAP_INTENT,
-  TOOL_GAP_QUOTE,
-  USER_ASK_INTENT,
-  USER_ASK_QUOTE,
-  WORKFLOW_INTENT,
-  WORKFLOW_QUOTE,
-} from './synthesis-fixtures.js';
-
-interface ProposalSpec {
-  id: string;
-  form: ProposalForm;
-  kind: 'tool' | 'intent' | 'facet-theme';
-  intent: string;
-  quotes: string[];
-  eventCount?: number;
-  sessionCount?: number;
-  projectCount?: number;
-  toil?: { frequency: number; medianDurationMs: number; interventions: number };
-  score?: number;
-}
-
-function makeProposal(spec: ProposalSpec): Proposal {
-  const eventCount = spec.eventCount ?? 4;
-  const sessionCount = spec.sessionCount ?? 4;
-  const projectCount = spec.projectCount ?? 1;
-  const toil = spec.toil ?? { frequency: eventCount, medianDurationMs: 120_000, interventions: 0 };
-  return {
-    candidateId: spec.id,
-    clusterKey: `${spec.kind}:${spec.id}`,
-    score: spec.score ?? 0.5,
-    intent: spec.intent,
-    form: spec.form,
-    mechanizable: true,
-    mechanizabilityRationale: 'stable shape',
-    fitnessFunction: {
-      claim: `${spec.intent} stays correct unattended`,
-      method: 'synthetic-test',
-      assertion: 'PASS when the scheduled run matches the manual result; FAIL otherwise.',
-      draft: true,
-    },
-    payoff: 'reclaims recurring hand-work',
-    citations: spec.quotes,
-    dossier: {
-      candidateId: spec.id,
-      clusterKey: `${spec.kind}:${spec.id}`,
-      kind: spec.kind,
-      label: spec.intent,
-      eventCount,
-      sessionCount,
-      projectCount,
-      projects: Array.from({ length: Math.max(projectCount, 1) }, (_, i) => `/repo/${spec.id}-${i}`),
-      cadence: [],
-      exemplars: spec.quotes,
-      toil,
-      firstSeen: '2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z',
-      lastSeen: '2026-06-07T09:00:00.000Z',
-      fingerprint: { kind: spec.kind, memberHashes: [spec.id] },
-    },
-    tokensUsed: 40,
-  };
-}
-
-interface ThemeSpec {
-  id: string;
-  category: FacetCategory;
-  form: ProposalForm;
-  label: string;
-  quotes: string[];
-  occurrences?: number;
-  sessions?: string[];
-  projects?: string[];
-}
-
-function makeTheme(spec: ThemeSpec): FacetTheme {
-  const sessions = spec.sessions ?? ['session-a', 'session-b'];
-  const projects = spec.projects ?? ['/repo/a', '/repo/b'];
-  return {
-    id: spec.id,
-    category: spec.category,
-    form: spec.form,
-    label: spec.label,
-    sessions,
-    projects,
-    quotes: spec.quotes,
-    occurrences: spec.occurrences ?? spec.quotes.length,
-    firstSeen: '2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z',
-    lastSeen: '2026-06-05T09:00:00.000Z',
-  };
-}
-
-describe('buildSynthesisDossier — mixed-form honesty (form-flow correctness)', () => {
-  it('a mixed-shape set of proposals keeps every form distinct — never collapsed to one', () => {
-    const proposals = [
-      makeProposal({ id: 'p-instruction', form: 'standing-instruction', kind: 'facet-theme', intent: INSTRUCTION_INTENT, quotes: [INSTRUCTION_QUOTE] }),

-    const dossier = buildSynthesisDossier(proposals, []);

-    expect(dossier.shapeStats).toHaveLength(5);

-    expect(new Set(dossier.proposals.map((item) => item.form)).size).toBe(5);

-

-    // instead of `proposal.form` would read this as ONE shape, exactly the

-    // Tried, 2026-07-03: "25 standing loops, 0 everything else").
-    const proposals = [
-      makeProposal({ id: 'p-instruction', form: 'standing-instruction', kind: 'facet-theme', intent: INSTRUCTION_INTENT, quotes: [INSTRUCTION_QUOTE] }),
-      makeProposal({ id: 'p-skill', form: 'distilled-skill', kind: 'facet-theme', intent: WORKFLOW_INTENT, quotes: [WORKFLOW_QUOTE] }),
-      makeProposal({ id: 'p-tool', form: 'missing-tool', kind: 'facet-theme', intent: TOOL_GAP_INTENT, quotes: [TOOL_GAP_QUOTE] }),
-    ];
-
-    const dossier = buildSynthesisDossier(proposals, []);
-
-    expect(dossier.shapeStats).toHaveLength(3);
-    expect(new Set(dossier.shapeStats.map((stat) => stat.form))).toEqual(
-      new Set<ProposalForm>(['standing-instruction', 'distilled-skill', 'missing-tool']),
