```diff
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-const START = Date.parse('2026-06-01T09:00:00Z');
-
-/** A small, clearly-recurring corpus — just enough for the propose stage to
- * survive at least one proposal, deterministically. */
-function ritualCorpus(): string {
-  const sessions = [];
-  for (let day = 0; day < 4; day += 1) {
-    const at = (offset: number) => new Date(START + day * DAY + offset * 60_000).toISOString();
-    sessions.push({
-      dir: '-Users-me-repo',
-      sessionId: `session-${day}`,
-      records: [
-        userTurn('is the nightly ingest job still green?', at(0)),
-        toolCall('Bash', { command: `lsof -i :${8471 + day}` }, at(1)),
-        toolCall('Bash', { command: 'git status' }, at(2)),
-      ],
-    });
-  }
-  return makeCorpus(sessions);
-}
-
-const outDirs: string[] = [];
-afterEach(() => {
-  for (const dir of outDirs.splice(0)) rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
-});
-
-function outDir(): string {
-  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loop-hole-synthesis-'));
-  outDirs.push(dir);
-  return dir;
-}
-
-function briefingPayload(outPath: string): string {
-  const html = readFileSync(join(outPath, 'loop-hole-briefing.loop.html'), 'utf8');
-  return extractPayload(html)!.payload;
-}
-
-/** Pull the first proposal straight out of the REAL prompt text a synthesis
- * call would receive — never a guessed id — so a stub judge can cite a
- * dossier item that actually exists without depending on this file's own
- * id-assignment assumptions. */
-function firstProposalFromPrompt(prompt: string): { id: string; eventCount: number; sessionCount: number } {
-  const marker = 'PROPOSALS — cite as [p:<id>]:\n';
-  const start = prompt.indexOf(marker) + marker.length;
-  const end = prompt.indexOf('\n\nFACET THEMES', start);
-  const proposals = JSON.parse(prompt.slice(start, end)) as Array<{ id: string; eventCount: number; sessionCount: number }>;
-  const [first] = proposals;
-  if (first === undefined) throw new Error('test corpus produced no proposals to cite');
-  return first;
-}
-
-describe('pipeline wiring — template-fallback e2e stays green with all judges off', () => {
-  it('with no synthesisJudge configured, the briefing renders the plain template plus an UNSYNTHESIZED banner, no Receipts heading', async () => {
-    const corpus = ritualCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out });
-
-    expect(result.synthesis).toEqual({ applied: false, text: '', droppedSentences: 0, repairedSentences: 0 });
-    const payload = briefingPayload(out);
-    expect(payload).toContain('UNSYNTHESIZED');
-    expect(payload).toContain('## At a glance');
-    expect(payload).not.toContain('## Receipts');
-  });
-
-  it('a throwing synthesisJudge degrades visibly too — the banner carries the reason, the template still renders', async () => {
-    const corpus = ritualCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-    const throwing: SynthesisJudge = () => {
-      throw new Error('synthesis model unavailable');
-    };
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out, synthesisJudge: throwing });
-
-    expect(result.synthesis.applied).toBe(false);
-    expect(result.synthesis.reason).toContain('synthesis model unavailable');
-    const payload = briefingPayload(out);
-    expect(payload).toContain('UNSYNTHESIZED');
-    expect(payload).toContain('synthesis model unavailable');
-    expect(payload).not.toContain('## Receipts');
-  });
-});
-
-describe('pipeline wiring — a validated synthesis lead becomes the briefing\'s lead, receipts follow', () => {
-  it('a judge that cites the real dossier survives validation end to end; the deterministic form sections still follow as receipts', async () => {
-    const corpus = ritualCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-
-    const citing: SynthesisJudge = (prompt) => {
-      const first = firstProposalFromPrompt(prompt);
-      return `## What recurs\nYou did this ${first.eventCount} time(s) across ${first.sessionCount} session(s) [p:${first.id}].`;
-    };
-
-    const result = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out, synthesisJudge: citing });
-
-    expect(result.synthesis.applied).toBe(true);
-    expect(result.synthesis.droppedSentences).toBe(0);
-
-    const payload = briefingPayload(out);
-    expect(payload).not.toContain('UNSYNTHESIZED');
-    expect(payload).toContain('## What recurs');
-    expect(payload).toContain('## Receipts');
-
-    const receiptsIndex = payload.indexOf('## Receipts');
-    const standingLoopsIndex = payload.indexOf('## Standing loops', receiptsIndex);
-    expect(standingLoopsIndex).toBeGreaterThan(receiptsIndex);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/tier-floors.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/tier-floors.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index c4649f1..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/tier-floors.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,183 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Tier floors (propose.loop.html c12) — the SYN-F diagnosis pinned: the
- * remainder-budget sequence ran abundant tiers first, so on any real corpus
- * the mixed intent pass filled every user slot (polling got
- * `maxUser - maxUser = 0`) and the tool pass filled the agent remainder
- * (chains got `max - user - tool = 0`). Detection fired — 39 chains, 14
- * polling families on the real slice — and emission was ARITHMETICALLY zero.
- * c10's mixed-shape fixture couldn't catch it: its sparse corpus left
- * remainders everywhere.
- *
- * The pinned behavior: an evidence tier with detected candidates holds a
- * small reserved quota AHEAD of the abundant passes — rare, high-signal
- * material (a missing tool, a push loop) is never starved to zero by sheer
- * intent volume. Totals never exceed the same caps as before.
- */
-
-import { mkdtempSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
-import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
-import { join } from 'node:path';
-import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { runPipeline } from '../src/run.js';
-import { makeCorpus, toolCallRecord, toolResult, userTurn } from './helpers.js';
-import { POLLING_CHECKIN_TURNS } from './fixtures.js';
-
-// Early enough that day 80 still lands in the past — extract.ts's sane-window
-// guard drops future timestamps (that lesson cost this spec its first RED).
-const BASE = Date.parse('2026-03-01T09:00:00.000Z');
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-const MIN = 60_000;
-
-function ts(day: number, offsetMin = 0): string {
-  return new Date(BASE + day * DAY + offsetMin * MIN).toISOString();
-}
-
-/** Twelve distinct recurring asks — more than every user slot the pipeline
- * has (DEFAULT_MAX_USER_PROPOSALS is 10, and this spec passes maxProposals
- * low enough that intents alone can fill the whole run). Same text per
- * intent across its sessions so cluster.ts groups each one. */
-const INTENT_ASKS = [
-  'is the nightly ingest job still green?',
-  'how did the morning sync land',
-  'give me the open review queue',
-  'anything waiting on my sign off',
-  'summarize the overnight alerts',
-  'is staging still healthy',
-  'what merged since yesterday',
-  'list the flaky suites from last night',
-  'did the backup window complete',
-  'show the slow-query digest',
-  'what did the crawler pick up',
-  'is the billing export current',
-] as const;
-
-/** A corpus whose intent volume alone fills every slot, which ALSO carries a
- * workaround chain (failing status probe -> hand-read log), a clean
- * procedure chain, recurring tool templates (agent lane), and a polling
- * burst — the material the old remainder arithmetic starved to zero. */
-function abundantCorpus(): string {
-  const dir = '-Users-me-repo';
-  const sessions = [];
-
-  for (const [i, ask] of INTENT_ASKS.entries()) {
-    for (let d = 0; d < 4; d += 1) {
-      sessions.push({
-        dir,
-        sessionId: `intent-${i}-${d}`,
-        records: [userTurn(ask, ts(i * 4 + d, 0))],
-      });
-    }
-  }
-
-  // Agent-lane tool recurrence — competes for the agent remainder the way
-  // the real corpus's rituals did.
-  for (const tool of ['make lint-all', 'make typegen', 'make docs-sync']) {
-    for (let d = 50; d < 53; d += 1) {
-      sessions.push({
-        dir,
-        sessionId: `tool-${tool.replaceAll(' ', '_')}-${d}`,
-        records: [
-          userTurn(`run ${tool} please`, ts(d, 0)),
-          toolCallRecord(`m-${tool}-${d}`, `t-${tool}-${d}`, 'Bash', { command: tool }, ts(d, 1), {}),
-          toolResult(`t-${tool}-${d}`, false, ts(d, 1), 'ok'),
-        ],
-      });
-    }
-  }
-
-  // Workaround chain: the status endpoint fails, the agent hand-reads the
-  // deploy log every time — missing-tool evidence.
-  for (let d = 60; d < 63; d += 1) {
-    sessions.push({
-      dir,
-      sessionId: `workaround-${d}`,
-      records: [
-        userTurn(`did deploy ${d} finish`, ts(d, 0)),
-        toolCallRecord(`m-wa-${d}-1`, `t-wa-${d}-1`, 'Bash', { command: 'curl https://deploy-status.example.com/health' }, ts(d, 1), {}),
-        toolResult(`t-wa-${d}-1`, true, ts(d, 1), 'connection refused'),
-        toolCallRecord(`m-wa-${d}-2`, `t-wa-${d}-2`, 'Bash', { command: 'cat /var/log/deploy.log' }, ts(d, 2), {}),
-        toolResult(`t-wa-${d}-2`, false, ts(d, 2), 'deploy finished'),
-      ],
-    });
-  }
-
-  // Clean procedure chain — distilled-skill evidence.
-  for (let d = 70; d < 73; d += 1) {
-    sessions.push({
-      dir,
-      sessionId: `skill-${d}`,
-      records: [
-        userTurn(`please run the release pipeline for build ${d}`, ts(d, 0)),
-        toolCallRecord(`m-skill-${d}-1`, `t-skill-${d}-1`, 'Bash', { command: 'npm run build' }, ts(d, 1), {}),
-        toolResult(`t-skill-${d}-1`, false, ts(d, 1), 'build ok'),
-        toolCallRecord(`m-skill-${d}-2`, `t-skill-${d}-2`, 'Bash', { command: 'npm test' }, ts(d, 2), {}),
-        toolResult(`t-skill-${d}-2`, false, ts(d, 2), 'tests ok'),
-      ],
-    });
-  }
-
-  // Polling burst — push-loop evidence. POLLING_CHECKIN_TURNS (not
-  // POLLING_BURST_TURNS): same-ask under the real calibrated embedder
-  // (episodes.loop.html c9), so the real pipeline's semantic gate still
-  // recognizes this as one family.
-  sessions.push({
-    dir,
-    sessionId: 'polling-burst',
-    records: POLLING_CHECKIN_TURNS.map((text, i) => userTurn(text, ts(80, 10 + i * 5))),
-  });
-
-  return makeCorpus(sessions);
-}
-
-describe('tier floors — abundant tiers can never starve detected trace evidence to zero', () => {
-  const outDirs: string[] = [];
-  afterEach(() => {
-    for (const dir of outDirs.splice(0)) rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
-  });
-
-  function outDir(): string {
-    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'loop-hole-tier-floors-'));
-    outDirs.push(dir);
-    return dir;
-  }
-
-  it('a corpus whose intents alone fill every slot still emits its detected chain and polling proposals', async () => {
-    const corpus = abundantCorpus();
-    const out = outDir();
-
-    // maxProposals 12: the 12 recurring intents can fill the whole run on
-    // their own — the exact shape the real slice hit at 25.
-    const result = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: corpus, outDir: out, maxProposals: 12 });
-
-    expect(result.chains, 'both chains must be detected').toBe(2);
-    expect(result.pollingFamilies, 'the polling burst must be detected').toBe(1);
-
-    const forms = result.propose.proposals.map((p) => p.form);
-    const counts = new Map<string, number>();
-    for (const form of forms) counts.set(form, (counts.get(form) ?? 0) + 1);
-
-    expect(counts.get('push-loop') ?? 0, `push-loop starved to zero (got: ${forms.sort().join(', ')})`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
-    expect(
-      (counts.get('missing-tool') ?? 0) + (counts.get('distilled-skill') ?? 0),
-      `chain-backed forms starved to zero (got: ${forms.sort().join(', ')})`,
-    ).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
-    expect(counts.get('standing-loop') ?? 0, 'abundant intents still hold most slots').toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(6);
-    expect(result.propose.proposals.length, 'floors reserve within the cap, never above it').toBeLessThanOrEqual(12);
-  });
-
-  it('with no detected trace evidence, floors reserve nothing and intents keep every slot', async () => {
-    const dir = '-Users-me-repo';
-    const sessions = [];
-    for (const [i, ask] of INTENT_ASKS.entries()) {
-      for (let d = 0; d < 4; d += 1) {
-        sessions.push({ dir, sessionId: `intent-${i}-${d}`, records: [userTurn(ask, ts(i * 4 + d, 0))] });
-      }
-    }
-    const result = await runPipeline({ corpusDir: makeCorpus(sessions), outDir: outDir(), maxProposals: 12 });
-
-    expect(result.chains).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.pollingFamilies).toBe(0);
-    expect(result.propose.proposals.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(10);
-    expect(new Set(result.propose.proposals.map((p) => p.form))).toEqual(new Set(['standing-loop']));
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/toil-rank.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/toil-rank.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 3ed5eef..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/toil-rank.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,146 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Toil-value ranking (c11, propose.loop.html) — the briefing's within-tier
- * order is cost-of-toil, never raw recurrence. The 2026-07-18 owner verdict
- * on the real slice output: a 30-second markdown-export convenience (seen
- * often, zero friction) must never outrank a 13-minute audit-discipline
- * failure (seen rarely, three human interventions) — the earlier formula
- * (frequency × median duration only, report.ts's now-removed `toilCost`)
- * already priced that case correctly in isolation, but buried `interventions`
- * as a same-value tie-break that a real value tie almost never reaches. This
- * spec locks the friction-weighted formula (toil.ts's `toilValue`) into the
- * actual ranking seam (report.ts's `rankByReachThenToil`), not just the pure
- * function in isolation.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { toilValue, type ToilPricing } from '../src/toil.js';
-import { rankByReachThenToil, type EmittedProposalPages } from '../src/report.js';
-import type { Proposal } from '../src/propose.js';
-
-function pricing(overrides: Partial<ToilPricing>): ToilPricing {
-  return { frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 1_000, interventions: 0, ...overrides };
-}
-
-function item(id: string, projectCount: number, toil: ToilPricing): EmittedProposalPages {
-  const proposal: Proposal = {
-    candidateId: id,
-    clusterKey: `tool:${id}`,
-    score: 0.5,
-    intent: id,
-    form: 'standing-loop',
-    mechanizable: true,
-    mechanizabilityRationale: 'Stable enough to mechanize.',
-    fitnessFunction: {
-      claim: `${id} stays correct`,
-      method: 'synthetic-test',
-      assertion: 'PASS when the generated result matches the hand-run result; FAIL when it diverges.',
-      draft: true,
-    },
-    payoff: `${id} saves recurring work.`,
-    citations: [`Bash(${id})`],
-    dossier: {
-      candidateId: id,
-      clusterKey: `tool:${id}`,
-      kind: 'tool',
-      label: `Bash(${id})`,
-      eventCount: toil.frequency,
-      sessionCount: toil.frequency,
-      projectCount,
-      projects: Array.from({ length: Math.max(projectCount, 1) }, (_, i) => `/Users/me/repo${i}`),
-      cadence: [],
-      exemplars: [`Bash(${id})`],
-      toil,
-      firstSeen: '2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z',
-      lastSeen: '2026-06-04T09:00:00.000Z',
-      fingerprint: { kind: 'tool', memberHashes: [`Bash(${id})`] },
-    },
-    tokensUsed: 64,
-  };
-  return {
-    proposal,
-    opportunity: { id: `${id}-opportunity`, path: `/tmp/${id}-opportunity.loop.html`, href: `${id}-opportunity.loop.html` },
-    draft: { id: `${id}-draft`, path: `/tmp/${id}-draft.loop.html`, href: `${id}-draft.loop.html` },
-  };
-}
-
-describe('toilValue — pure cost-of-toil scalar (toil.ts)', () => {
-  it('a low-frequency high-cost, high-friction shape prices above a high-frequency trivial one', () => {
-    const auditSweep = toilValue(pricing({ frequency: 3, medianDurationMs: 12 * 60_000, interventions: 3 }));
-    const mdExport = toilValue(pricing({ frequency: 13, medianDurationMs: 30_000, interventions: 0 }));
-    expect(auditSweep).toBeGreaterThan(mdExport);
-  });
-
-  it('is pure and deterministic: the same pricing always yields the same value', () => {
-    const toil = pricing({ frequency: 5, medianDurationMs: 45_000, interventions: 2 });
-    expect(toilValue(toil)).toBe(toilValue(toil));
-    expect(toilValue({ ...toil })).toBe(toilValue({ ...toil }));
-  });
-
-  it('a shape with no measured duration still prices above zero (the floor), never erasing its own frequency', () => {
-    const value = toilValue(pricing({ frequency: 4, medianDurationMs: 0, interventions: 0 }));
-    expect(value).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-  });
-
-  it('an unpriced shape (no duration evidence at all) values at zero, never a fabricated positive number', () => {
-    expect(toilValue({ frequency: 0, medianDurationMs: 0, interventions: 0, priced: false })).toBe(0);
-  });
-});
-
-describe('rankByReachThenToil — briefing order is cost-of-toil, never raw frequency (c11)', () => {
-  it('a rare, costly, friction-heavy item outranks a frequent, trivial item in the same reach tier', () => {
-    const auditSweep = item('audit-sweep', 1, pricing({ frequency: 3, medianDurationMs: 12 * 60_000, interventions: 3 }));
-    const mdExport = item('md-export', 1, pricing({ frequency: 13, medianDurationMs: 30_000, interventions: 0 }));
-
-    const ranked = rankByReachThenToil([mdExport, auditSweep]);
-
-    expect(ranked.map((r) => r.proposal.candidateId)).toEqual(['audit-sweep', 'md-export']);
-  });
-
-  it('friction alone can flip the order: fewer, shorter-but-costlier-in-interventions occurrences outrank more frequent, cleaner ones', () => {
-    // Old formula (frequency × median duration, no friction weight):
-    // heavyFriction = 2 * 100_000 = 200_000; clean = 5 * 50_000 = 250_000 — clean would win.
-    // New formula (recurrence × median duration × (1 + interventions)):
-    // heavyFriction = 2 * 100_000 * 6 = 1_200_000; clean = 5 * 50_000 * 1 = 250_000 — heavyFriction wins.
-    const heavyFriction = item('heavy-friction', 1, pricing({ frequency: 2, medianDurationMs: 100_000, interventions: 5 }));
-    const clean = item('clean', 1, pricing({ frequency: 5, medianDurationMs: 50_000, interventions: 0 }));
-
-    const ranked = rankByReachThenToil([clean, heavyFriction]);
-
-    expect(ranked.map((r) => r.proposal.candidateId)).toEqual(['heavy-friction', 'clean']);
-  });
-
-  it('reach-tier precedence is preserved: a cross-project item with trivial toil still outranks a single-project item with enormous toil', () => {
-    const crossProject = item('cross', 3, pricing({ frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 100, interventions: 0 }));
-    const singleProjectHeavy = item(
-      'single-heavy',
-      1,
-      pricing({ frequency: 1_000, medianDurationMs: 60 * 60_000, interventions: 50 }),
-    );
-
-    const ranked = rankByReachThenToil([singleProjectHeavy, crossProject]);
-
-    expect(ranked.map((r) => r.proposal.candidateId)).toEqual(['cross', 'single-heavy']);
-  });
-
-  it('an unpriced item never outranks a priced peer in the same reach tier', () => {
-    const unpriced = item('unpriced', 1, { frequency: 0, medianDurationMs: 0, interventions: 0, priced: false });
-    const priced = item('priced', 1, pricing({ frequency: 1, medianDurationMs: 100, interventions: 0 }));
-
-    const ranked = rankByReachThenToil([unpriced, priced]);
-
-    expect(ranked.map((r) => r.proposal.candidateId)).toEqual(['priced', 'unpriced']);
-  });
-
-  it('is pure and deterministic: re-ranking the same items, in a different starting order, yields the same order', () => {
-    const a = item('a', 1, pricing({ frequency: 4, medianDurationMs: 20_000, interventions: 1 }));
-    const b = item('b', 1, pricing({ frequency: 2, medianDurationMs: 90_000, interventions: 2 }));
-    const c = item('c', 1, pricing({ frequency: 9, medianDurationMs: 5_000, interventions: 0 }));
-
-    const rankedForward = rankByReachThenToil([a, b, c]).map((r) => r.proposal.candidateId);
-    const rankedShuffled = rankByReachThenToil([c, a, b]).map((r) => r.proposal.candidateId);
-    const rankedReverse = rankByReachThenToil([c, b, a]).map((r) => r.proposal.candidateId);
-
-    expect(rankedShuffled).toEqual(rankedForward);
-    expect(rankedReverse).toEqual(rankedForward);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/toil.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/toil.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index cdc4498..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/toil.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,186 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Toil pricing (c5, docs/loop-hole-episodes-design.md line 15) — every
- * episode-backed candidate must carry frequency, median duration, and
- * intervention count that a completely independent pass over the SAME raw
- * episodes reproduces exactly. "Independent" means this spec never reads
- * `candidate.toil` to build its own expectation: it walks the episode
- * fixtures itself (occurrence count, its own median implementation, its own
- * error tally) and only then compares against what the candidate carried.
- * A hardcoded or eyeballed number would have to coincidentally match every
- * one of these recomputations across every case below, including the
- * adversarial one that edits a single episode and expects the price to move.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { detectChainCandidates } from '../src/chains.js';
-import { priceToil } from '../src/toil.js';
-import type { Episode, EpisodeAction } from '../src/episodes.js';
-
-const BASE = Date.parse('2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z');
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-
-function action(template: string, outcome: EpisodeAction['outcome'], day: number, offsetMs: number): EpisodeAction {
-  const epoch = BASE + day * DAY + offsetMs;
-  return { tool: template.split('(')[0] ?? template, template, outcome, ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(), epoch };
-}
-
-function episode(session: string, project: string, day: number, actions: EpisodeAction[]): Episode {
-  const askEpoch = BASE + day * DAY;
-  const last = actions[actions.length - 1];
-  return {
-    session,
-    project,
-    turn: 0,
-    ts: new Date(askEpoch).toISOString(),
-    epoch: askEpoch,
-    actions,
-    durationMs: last === undefined ? 0 : last.epoch - askEpoch,
-    tokens: { input: 0, output: 0 },
-    resolution: 'resolved',
-  };
-}
-
-/** A five-step shape (as in chains.spec.ts) whose LAST step's outcome and
- * whose finish offset (hence durationMs) the caller controls, so occurrence
- * count, error count, and the duration distribution can all be varied
- * independently across the fixture below. */
-function shipFixTrace(day: number, finishOffsetMs: number, lastOutcome: EpisodeAction['outcome']): EpisodeAction[] {
-  return [
-    action('Bash(npm test)', 'error', day, 0),
-    action('Read({log})', 'ok', day, 1_000),
-    action('Bash(grep {pattern} {log})', 'ok', day, 2_000),
-    action('Edit({file})', 'ok', day, 3_000),
-    action('Bash(git push)', lastOutcome, day, finishOffsetMs),
-  ];
-}
-
-/** Naive, independently-written recomputation — deliberately not sharing a
- * line with src/toil.ts's implementation — used to cross-check every case
- * below without trusting the module under test to grade its own homework. */
-function recomputeToil(episodes: readonly Episode[]) {
-  const durations = episodes.map((e) => e.durationMs).slice().sort((a, b) => a - b);
-  let medianDurationMs: number;
-  if (durations.length === 0) {
-    medianDurationMs = 0;
-  } else if (durations.length % 2 === 1) {
-    medianDurationMs = durations[(durations.length - 1) / 2]!;
-  } else {
-    const upper = durations.length / 2;
-    medianDurationMs = (durations[upper - 1]! + durations[upper]!) / 2;
-  }
-  let interventions = 0;
-  for (const ep of episodes) {
-    for (const a of ep.actions) if (a.outcome === 'error') interventions += 1;
-  }
-  return { frequency: episodes.length, medianDurationMs, interventions };
-}
-
-describe('toil pricing: recomputed from the corpus, not fabricated', () => {
-  it('a recurring five-step shape carries frequency, median duration, and interventions matching an independent recomputation', () => {
-    // Three episodes running the identical shape (same template::outcome
-    // sequence throughout, including the push outcome), diverging only in
-    // how long each one took to finish.
-    const matching: Episode[] = [
-      episode('sess-1', '/Users/me/repoA', 1, shipFixTrace(1, 4_000, 'ok')),
-      episode('sess-2', '/Users/me/repoA', 2, shipFixTrace(2, 10_000, 'ok')),
-      episode('sess-3', '/Users/me/repoB', 3, shipFixTrace(3, 40_000, 'ok')),
-    ];
-    const candidates = detectChainCandidates(matching);
-    expect(candidates).toHaveLength(1);
-    const candidate = candidates[0];
-    if (candidate === undefined) throw new Error('expected one chain candidate');
-
-    const expected = recomputeToil(matching);
-    expect(candidate.toil.frequency).toBe(expected.frequency);
-    expect(candidate.toil.medianDurationMs).toBe(expected.medianDurationMs);
-    expect(candidate.toil.interventions).toBe(expected.interventions);
-
-    // Sanity: the numbers are not degenerate placeholders.
-    expect(candidate.toil.frequency).toBe(3);
-    expect(candidate.toil.medianDurationMs).toBe(10_000); // median of 4000, 10000, 40000
-    expect(candidate.toil.interventions).toBe(3); // one npm-test error per episode
-  });
-
-  it('an even-sized group medians across the two middle durations, and interventions count every error action, not just resolution', () => {
-    const group: Episode[] = [
-      episode('sess-a', '/Users/me/repoA', 1, shipFixTrace(1, 2_000, 'ok')),
-      episode('sess-b', '/Users/me/repoA', 2, shipFixTrace(2, 6_000, 'ok')),
-      episode('sess-c', '/Users/me/repoA', 3, shipFixTrace(3, 8_000, 'ok')),
-      episode('sess-d', '/Users/me/repoA', 4, shipFixTrace(4, 20_000, 'ok')),
-    ];
-
-    const priced = priceToil(group);
-    const expected = recomputeToil(group);
-
-    expect(priced).toEqual(expected);
-    expect(priced.medianDurationMs).toBe((6_000 + 8_000) / 2);
-    expect(priced.interventions).toBe(4); // one npm-test error per episode, four episodes
-  });
-
-  it('priceToil is pure and total: an empty episode list prices to zero, never a throw — and marks itself unpriced', () => {
-    expect(priceToil([])).toEqual({ frequency: 0, medianDurationMs: 0, interventions: 0, priced: false });
-  });
-
-  it('adversarial: editing one episode in the group moves the priced numbers by exactly the recomputed delta', () => {
-    const before: Episode[] = [
-      episode('sess-1', '/Users/me/repoA', 1, shipFixTrace(1, 4_000, 'ok')),
-      episode('sess-2', '/Users/me/repoA', 2, shipFixTrace(2, 4_000, 'ok')),
-      episode('sess-3', '/Users/me/repoA', 3, shipFixTrace(3, 4_000, 'ok')),
-    ];
-    const beforePriced = priceToil(before);
-    expect(beforePriced).toEqual(recomputeToil(before));
-    expect(beforePriced.interventions).toBe(3);
-
-    // Introduce one extra failure (grep fails this time) in a single episode
-    // and stretch its duration — both the intervention count and the median
-    // must track the edit, proving the numbers are read off the trace, not
-    // memoized from the first computation.
-    const after: Episode[] = [
-      before[0]!,
-      before[1]!,
-      episode('sess-3', '/Users/me/repoA', 3, [
-        action('Bash(npm test)', 'error', 3, 0),
-        action('Read({log})', 'ok', 3, 1_000),
-        action('Bash(grep {pattern} {log})', 'error', 3, 2_000),
-        action('Edit({file})', 'ok', 3, 3_000),
-        action('Bash(git push)', 'ok', 3, 60_000),
-      ]),
-    ];
-    const afterPriced = priceToil(after);
-    const afterExpected = recomputeToil(after);
-
-    expect(afterPriced).toEqual(afterExpected);
-    expect(afterPriced.interventions).toBe(4); // 1 + 1 + 2
-    expect(afterPriced.medianDurationMs).toBe(4_000); // durations now 4000, 4000, 60000
-    expect(afterPriced).not.toEqual(beforePriced);
-  });
-
-  it('chain candidates from a full corpus expose toil that matches recomputation over exactly the episodes they cite', () => {
-    const recurring: Episode[] = [
-      episode('sess-1', '/Users/me/repoA', 1, shipFixTrace(1, 3_000, 'ok')),
-      episode('sess-2', '/Users/me/repoA', 2, shipFixTrace(2, 9_000, 'ok')),
-      episode('sess-3', '/Users/me/repoB', 3, shipFixTrace(3, 27_000, 'ok')),
-      episode('sess-4', '/Users/me/repoB', 4, shipFixTrace(4, 15_000, 'ok')),
-    ];
-    const distractors: Episode[] = [
-      episode('sess-5', '/Users/me/repoC', 5, [action('Bash(npm test)', 'ok', 5, 0)]),
-      episode('sess-6', '/Users/me/repoC', 6, [action('Bash(git push)', 'ok', 6, 0)]),
-    ];
-
-    const candidates = detectChainCandidates([...recurring, ...distractors]);
-    expect(candidates).toHaveLength(1);
-    const candidate = candidates[0];
-    if (candidate === undefined) throw new Error('expected one chain candidate');
-    expect(candidate.occurrences).toBe(4);
-
-    // Independently recompute over the SAME episodes the candidate cites
-    // (identified by provenance, not by re-using the fixture array order),
-    // and require an exact match.
-    const citedSessions = new Set(candidate.provenance.map((p) => /^session (\S+)/.exec(p)?.[1]));
-    const citedEpisodes = recurring.filter((ep) => citedSessions.has(ep.session));
-    expect(citedEpisodes).toHaveLength(4);
-
-    const expected = recomputeToil(citedEpisodes);
-    expect(candidate.toil).toEqual(expected);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/triage-chunking.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/triage-chunking.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 00f8d7d..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/triage-chunking.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,301 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * Judgment batch resilience (c11): the triage tier's head is judged in
- * CHUNKS, one judge call per chunk, and each chunk is validated
- * independently. A malformed or throwing chunk refuses ONLY that chunk —
- * counted (`refusedChunks`) and surfaced (`onRefusal`, once per refusal) —
- * never the whole pass; every other chunk's verdicts still apply, and its
- * own candidates fall through untouched (the same pass-through path
- * `applyTriage` already gives anything beyond the judged head).
- *
- * Cross-chunk merges are out of scope by construction, not a separate check:
- * each chunk is validated against only ITS OWN candidate ids, so a verdict
- * naming a `mergeInto` target in a different chunk is indistinguishable from
- * an invented id and refuses the chunk that named it.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { DEFAULT_TRIAGE_CHUNK_SIZE, triageIntents } from '../src/triage.js';
-import type { RecurrenceCandidate } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import type { ActionEvent, StoredState } from '../src/types.js';
-
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-const START = Date.parse('2026-06-01T09:00:00Z');
-
-/** `count` distinct recurring turns, each seen across 4 sessions/days. */
-function state(count: number): StoredState {
-  const turns = Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) => `turn-${i} label`);
-  const events: ActionEvent[] = [];
-  for (let day = 0; day < 4; day += 1) {
-    for (let turn = 0; turn < turns.length; turn += 1) {
-      const epoch = START + day * DAY + turn * 1000;
-      events.push({
-        kind: 'user',
-        ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(),
-        epoch,
-        project: '/repo/a',
-        session: `session-${day}-${turn}`,
-        turn,
-      });
-    }
-  }
-  return { version: 1, watermark: {}, turns, events };
-}
-
-function candidate(turn: number): RecurrenceCandidate {
-  return {
-    id: `intent:${turn}`,
-    kind: 'intent',
-    label: `turn-${turn} label`,
-    eventCount: 4,
-    sessionCount: 4,
-    projectCount: 1,
-    firstSeen: new Date(START).toISOString(),
-    lastSeen: new Date(START + 3 * DAY).toISOString(),
-    provenance: [`turn ${turn}: turn-${turn} label`],
-    score: { score: 1 - turn * 0.01, components: { frequency: 1, cadenceRegularity: 1, mechanizability: 1, manualCost: 1, stalenessRisk: 1 } },
-  };
-}
-
-/** A judge that always stands up every candidate it's shown, titled after its own label. */
-function standUpAll(prompts: string[]): (prompt: string) => string {
-  return (prompt) => {
-    prompts.push(prompt);
-    const ids = [...prompt.matchAll(/"id":\s*"(intent:\d+)"/g)].map((m) => m[1]!);
-    return JSON.stringify(ids.map((id) => ({ id, standable: true, title: `Do the ${id} thing`, mergeInto: null, reason: 'ok' })));
-  };
-}
-
-describe('triage batch resilience — chunking', () => {
-  it('splits a head larger than chunkSize into more than one judge call', () => {
-    const candidates = Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => candidate(i));

-    expect(result.refusedChunks).toBe(0);

-  it('never sends one flat oversized prompt: each chunk prompt names only its own candidates', () => {

-

-      for (const turn of expectedChunks[i]!) {
-        expect(prompt).toContain(`"intent:${turn}"`);
-      }

-        expect(prompt).not.toContain(`"intent:${turn}"`);

-
-  it('a malformed middle chunk refuses only that chunk — other chunks still apply, the refused one passes through untouched', () => {

-    let call = 0;

-  it('a throwing judge on one chunk does not stop the remaining chunks from being attempted and applied', () => {

-  it('a head that fits in exactly one chunk stays at one judge call (boundary: count === chunkSize)', () => {

-    expect(result.refusedChunks).toBe(0);

-
-  it('an unrepairable chunk still refuses fail-closed, classified malformed-unrepairable', () => {
-    const candidates = Array.from({ length: 3 }, (_, i) => candidate(i));

-
-    const result = triageIntents(candidates, state(3), judge, 3);

-    expect(result.refusedChunks).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.repairedChunks).toBe(0);

-
-  it('syntactically valid JSON that fails the verdict schema is classified "wrong" — the genuine judgment failure', () => {

-    const judge = (): string => JSON.stringify([{ id: 'intent:0', standable: true, reason: 'r' }]); // standable with no title — untitled survivor
-
-    const result = triageIntents(candidates, state(3), judge, 3);

-    expect(result.refusedChunks).toBe(1);

-
-    const result = triageIntents(candidates, state(3), judge, 3);
-
-    expect(result.malformedUnrepairableChunks).toBe(1);

-  it('a repair-hint line carries into the NEXT chunk only, after the previous chunk needed repair', () => {

-    let call = 0;

-      call += 1;
-      const ids = [...prompt.matchAll(/"id":\s*"(intent:\d+)"/g)].map((m) => m[1]!);

diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/triage.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/triage.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index 43d7d5f..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/triage.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,149 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * The judgment tier's application layer — the code that decides whether a
- * model's triage verdicts are USABLE. The model itself is a seam (TriageJudge)
- * and is proven on the real corpus + the owner's precision check; what this
- * file pins is the fail-closed contract around it: invented ids, duplicate
- * verdicts, merge chains, merges into dropped candidates, untitled survivors,
- * or unparseable output refuse the WHOLE triage. And when verdicts are valid,
- * merges are recomputed from the store's events — counts and provenance can
- * never be fabricated by prose.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { applyTriage, parseTriageVerdicts, triageIntents } from '../src/triage.js';
-import type { RecurrenceCandidate } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import type { ActionEvent, StoredState } from '../src/types.js';
-
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-const START = Date.parse('2026-06-01T09:00:00Z');
-
-/** Two same-ask clusters (turns 0 and 1) + one fragment cluster (turn 2). */
-function state(): StoredState {
-  const turns = ['any progress?', 'is it finished?', 'add them'];
-  const events: ActionEvent[] = [];
-  for (let day = 0; day < 4; day += 1) {
-    for (let turn = 0; turn < turns.length; turn += 1) {
-      const epoch = START + day * DAY + turn * 1000;
-      events.push({
-        kind: 'user',
-        ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(),
-        epoch,
-        project: turn === 0 ? '/repo/a' : '/repo/b',
-        session: `session-${day}-${turn}`,
-        turn,
-      });
-    }
-  }
-  return { version: 1, watermark: {}, turns, events };
-}
-
-function candidate(id: string, turn: number, label: string): RecurrenceCandidate {
-  return {
-    id,
-    kind: 'intent',
-    label,
-    eventCount: 4,
-    sessionCount: 4,
-    projectCount: 1,
-    firstSeen: new Date(START).toISOString(),
-    lastSeen: new Date(START + 3 * DAY).toISOString(),
-    provenance: [`turn ${turn}: ${label}`],
-    score: { score: 0.4 - turn * 0.05, components: { frequency: 1, cadenceRegularity: 1, mechanizability: 1, manualCost: 1, stalenessRisk: 1 } },
-  };
-}
-
-const A = candidate('intent:0', 0, 'any progress?');
-const B = candidate('intent:1', 1, 'is it finished?');
-const C = candidate('intent:2', 2, 'add them');
-const IDS = new Set([A.id, B.id, C.id]);
-
-const VALID = JSON.stringify([
-  { id: A.id, standable: true, title: 'Check whether background work finished', mergeInto: null, reason: 'progress ritual' },
-  { id: B.id, standable: true, mergeInto: A.id, reason: 'same ask, different words' },
-  { id: C.id, standable: false, reason: 'anaphoric fragment' },
-]);
-
-describe('triage application layer', () => {
-  it('merges same-asks (recomputed from events), drops fragments, retitles', () => {
-    const verdicts = parseTriageVerdicts(VALID, IDS)!;
-    expect(verdicts).not.toBeNull();
-    const result = applyTriage([A, B, C], verdicts, state());
-
-    expect(result.candidates).toHaveLength(1);
-    expect(result.dropped).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.merged).toBe(1);
-    const survivor = result.candidates[0]!;
-    expect(survivor.label).toBe('Check whether background work finished');
-    // Counts recomputed from the UNION of both clusters' events, not prose.
-    expect(survivor.eventCount).toBe(8);
-    expect(survivor.projectCount).toBe(2);
-    // Verbatim provenance from both members survives the merge.
-    expect(survivor.provenance.join('\n')).toContain('any progress?');
-    expect(survivor.provenance.join('\n')).toContain('is it finished?');
-  });
-
-  it('candidates beyond the triaged head pass through untouched', () => {
-    const judge = (): string => JSON.stringify([
-      { id: A.id, standable: true, title: 'Check progress', mergeInto: null, reason: 'ok' },
-    ]);
-    const result = triageIntents([A, B, C], state(), judge, 1)!;
-    expect(result.candidates.map((c) => c.id).sort()).toEqual([A.id, B.id, C.id].sort());
-  });
-
-  it.each([
-    ['invented id', JSON.stringify([{ id: 'intent:999', standable: true, title: 'x', reason: 'r' }])],
-    ['duplicate verdicts', JSON.stringify([
-      { id: 'intent:0', standable: false, reason: 'r' },
-      { id: 'intent:0', standable: true, title: 'x', reason: 'r' },
-    ])],
-    ['merge chain', JSON.stringify([
-      { id: 'intent:0', standable: true, mergeInto: 'intent:1', reason: 'r' },
-      { id: 'intent:1', standable: true, mergeInto: 'intent:2', reason: 'r' },
-      { id: 'intent:2', standable: true, title: 'x', reason: 'r' },
-    ])],
-    ['merge into dropped', JSON.stringify([
-      { id: 'intent:0', standable: true, mergeInto: 'intent:2', reason: 'r' },
-      { id: 'intent:2', standable: false, reason: 'r' },
-    ])],
-    ['untitled survivor', JSON.stringify([{ id: 'intent:0', standable: true, reason: 'r' }])],
-    ['self-merge', JSON.stringify([{ id: 'intent:0', standable: true, mergeInto: 'intent:0', reason: 'r' }])],
-    ['not json', 'the model wrote an essay instead'],
-  ])('refuses the whole triage on %s', (_name, raw) => {
-    expect(parseTriageVerdicts(raw, IDS)).toBeNull();
-  });
-
-  it('a throwing judge refuses only its own chunk, not the whole pass — a single-chunk head degrades to fully untriaged', () => {
-    const judge = (): string => {
-      throw new Error('model unavailable');
-    };
-    const result = triageIntents([A, B, C], state(), judge);
-    expect(result.refusedChunks).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.totalChunks).toBe(1);
-    expect(result.candidates.map((c) => c.id).sort()).toEqual([A.id, B.id, C.id].sort());
-  });
-
-  it('onRefusal reports why: a thrown judge names the thrown error', () => {
-    const judge = (): string => {
-      throw new Error('model unavailable');
-    };
-    let reason: string | undefined;
-    const result = triageIntents([A, B, C], state(), judge, 30, (r) => { reason = r; });
-    expect(result.refusedChunks).toBe(1);
-    expect(reason).toContain('model unavailable');
-  });
-
-  it('onRefusal reports why: malformed output names the structural failure, not a thrown error', () => {
-    let reason: string | undefined;
-    const judge = (): string => 'the model wrote an essay instead';
-    const result = triageIntents([A, B, C], state(), judge, 30, (r) => { reason = r; });
-    expect(result.refusedChunks).toBe(1);
-    expect(reason).toContain('structural validation');
-  });
-
-  it('onRefusal never fires on a successful triage', () => {
-    const judge = (): string => VALID;
-    let fired = false;
-    triageIntents([A, B, C], state(), judge, 30, () => { fired = true; });
-    expect(fired).toBe(false);
-  });
-});
diff --git a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/typology.spec.ts b/spikes/loop-hole/tests/typology.spec.ts
deleted file mode 100644
index b8d5f03..0000000
--- a/spikes/loop-hole/tests/typology.spec.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,307 +0,0 @@
-/**
- * The typology contract (design: docs/loop-hole-episodes-design.md, "L2
- * analyst"; check c6 in propose.loop.html). Two things must be true:
- *
- *  1. Every emitted proposal carries one of the five forms — an unrecognized
- *     form (or an unrecognized shape) is a structural violation, not a weird
- *     but tolerable entry.
- *  2. The legal shape-to-form matrix lives in code and is enforced fail-
- *     closed across the WHOLE analyst-pass batch: one illegal pairing
- *     anywhere in the batch — a polling family proposed as a standing loop,
- *     a workaround chain proposed as a standing instruction — refuses every
- *     proposal in that pass, mirroring triage.ts's `parseTriageVerdicts`.
- *
- * The shape-derivation helpers are exercised against REAL detector output
- * (chains.ts, polling.ts, cluster.ts, facets.ts), not hand-typed shape
- * strings, so the contract is proven against the actual class of thing it
- * gates, not a stand-in for it.
- */
-
-import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
-import { detectChainCandidates, type ChainCandidate } from '../src/chains.js';
-import { detectPollingFamilies, type PollingFamily } from '../src/polling.js';
-import type { CandidateKind, RecurrenceCandidate } from '../src/cluster.js';
-import type { Episode, EpisodeAction } from '../src/episodes.js';
-import type { FacetFinding } from '../src/facets.js';
-import {
-  EVIDENCE_SHAPES,
-  PROPOSAL_FORMS,
-  isLegalPairing,
-  legalFormsFor,
-  shapeOfCandidate,
-  shapeOfCandidateKind,
-  shapeOfChain,
-  shapeOfFacetFinding,
-  shapeOfPollingFamily,
-  validateTypology,
-  type EvidenceShape,
-  type ProposalForm,
-  type TypedProposal,
-} from '../src/typology.js';
-
-const BASE = Date.parse('2026-06-01T09:00:00.000Z');
-const DAY = 86_400_000;
-
-function action(template: string, outcome: EpisodeAction['outcome'], day: number, offsetMs: number): EpisodeAction {
-  const epoch = BASE + day * DAY + offsetMs;
-  return { tool: template.split('(')[0] ?? template, template, outcome, ts: new Date(epoch).toISOString(), epoch };
-}
-
-function episode(session: string, project: string, day: number, actions: EpisodeAction[]): Episode {
-  const askEpoch = BASE + day * DAY;
-  const last = actions[actions.length - 1];
-  return {
-    session,
-    project,
-    turn: 0,
-    ts: new Date(askEpoch).toISOString(),
-    epoch: askEpoch,
-    actions,
-    durationMs: last === undefined ? 0 : last.epoch - askEpoch,
-    tokens: { input: 0, output: 0 },
-    resolution: 'resolved',
-  };
-}
-
-/** A clean, error-free five-step trace: read → grep → edit, repeatable as-is. */
-function cleanTrace(day: number): EpisodeAction[] {
-  return [
-    action('Read({config})', 'ok', day, 0),
-    action('Bash(grep {pattern} {config})', 'ok', day, 1_000),
-    action('Edit({config})', 'ok', day, 2_000),
-  ];
-}
-
-/** A trace that hits a failure and routes around it: the workaround shape. */
-function workaroundTrace(day: number): EpisodeAction[] {
-  return [
-    action('Bash(deploy)', 'error', day, 0),
-    action('Bash(cat {deploy_log})', 'ok', day, 1_000),
-    action('Bash(deploy --retry)', 'ok', day, 2_000),
-  ];
-}
-
-function realChainCandidate(traceBuilder: (day: number) => EpisodeAction[]): ChainCandidate {
-  const episodes: Episode[] = [

- * unchanged corpus must open no session and add no event; only a session whose
- * bytes changed is re-read.

-
-import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';

-describe('watermark: incremental over an unchanged corpus', () => {

-    const root = makeCorpus([
-      {
-        dir: '-Users-me-repoA',

-        records: [
-          userTurn('run the tests', '2026-06-01T10:00:00.000Z'),

-      },
-    ]);

-    const adapter = new ClaudeCodeAdapter(root);
-
-    const first = await runExtract(adapter, store);

-    const eventsAfterFirst = store.events.length;
-    expect(eventsAfterFirst).toBeGreaterThan(0);
-
-    // Unchanged corpus → nothing new, table byte-identical in size.
-    const second = await runExtract(adapter, store);
-    expect(second.newSessions).toBe(0);
-    expect(second.newEvents).toBe(0);
-    expect(store.events.length).toBe(eventsAfterFirst);
-
-    // Change one session's bytes → exactly that session is re-read.

-    expect(third.newSessions).toBe(1);
-    expect(store.events.length).toBe(eventsAfterFirst + 1);

-        dir: '-Users-me-repoC',

-

-
-    appendRecord(root, '-Users-me-repoC', 'sess-c', toolCall('Bash', { command: 'git diff' }, '2026-06-03T12:01:00.000Z'));
```

```diff
diff --git a/src/core/providers/opencode.ts b/src/core/providers/opencode.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..1435ea4
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/core/providers/opencode.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,384 @@
+import type { ActionManifest, AgentProvider, ProviderLaunchSpec, Turn } from './types.ts';
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// PLUGIN_SOURCE — bundled opencode JS plugin written verbatim to disk.
+//
+// Runs inside opencode's JS runtime. Cannot import rctrl. All logic is inline.
+// No-ops unless RCTRL_STATE and RCTRL_SESSION_ID are set (inert in normal use).
+// On session.status {type:"idle"}: writes events/session-id and touches events/stop.
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+export const PLUGIN_SOURCE = `import { mkdir, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
+import { join } from "node:path";
+
+async function fireStop(sessionID) {
+  const state = process.env.RCTRL_STATE;
+  const rctrlSession = process.env.RCTRL_SESSION_ID;
+  if (!state || !rctrlSession) return;
+  const eventsDir = join(state, "sessions", rctrlSession, "events");
+  await mkdir(eventsDir, { recursive: true });
+  await writeFile(join(eventsDir, "session-id"), String(sessionID), "utf8");
+  await writeFile(join(eventsDir, "stop"), "", "utf8");
+  const ts = String(Date.now() * 1_000_000);
+  const logPath = join(eventsDir, "log");
+  const { appendFile } = await import("node:fs/promises");
+  await appendFile(logPath, ts + "\\n", "utf8");
+}
+
+export const Plugin = async () => ({
+  event: async ({ event }) => {
+    if (
+      event?.type === "session.status" &&
+      event?.properties?.status?.type === "idle" &&
+      !!process.env.RCTRL_STATE &&
+      !!process.env.RCTRL_SESSION_ID
+    ) {
+      await fireStop(event?.properties?.sessionID ?? "");
+    }
+  },
+});
+
+export default Plugin;
+`;
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// parseTranscript — parse opencode export JSON, return last assistant text.
+// Pure / total: never throws; returns '' on empty/whitespace/malformed input.
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+type JsonObj = Record<string, unknown>;
+
+function parseTranscript(content: string): string {
+  if (content.trim().length === 0) return '';
+  let obj: unknown;
+  try {
+    obj = JSON.parse(content);
+  } catch {
+    return '';
+  }
+  if (obj === null || typeof obj !== 'object') return '';
+  const root = obj as JsonObj;
+  const messages = root.messages;
+  if (!Array.isArray(messages) || messages.length === 0) return '';
+
+  // Find the last message whose info.role === 'assistant'.
+  let lastAssistantMsg: JsonObj | null = null;
+  for (let i = messages.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
+    const m = messages[i];
+    if (m === null || typeof m !== 'object') continue;
+    const msg = m as JsonObj;
+    const info = msg.info;
+    if (info !== null && typeof info === 'object') {
+      const inf = info as JsonObj;
+      if (inf.role === 'assistant') {
+        lastAssistantMsg = msg;
+        break;
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  if (lastAssistantMsg === null) return '';
+
+  const parts = lastAssistantMsg.parts;
+  if (!Array.isArray(parts)) return '';
+
+  const textParts: string[] = [];
+  for (const part of parts) {
+    if (part === null || typeof part !== 'object') continue;
+    const p = part as JsonObj;
+    if (p.type === 'text' && typeof p.text === 'string') {
+      textParts.push(p.text);
+    }
+  }
+
+  return textParts.join('');
+}
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// extractAssistantMessages — internal helper: return messages whose
+// info.role === 'assistant', in order. Never throws; returns [] on malformed.
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+function extractAssistantMessages(root: JsonObj): JsonObj[] {
+  const messages = root.messages;
+  if (!Array.isArray(messages)) return [];
+  const result: JsonObj[] = [];
+  for (const m of messages) {
+    if (m === null || typeof m !== 'object') continue;
+    const msg = m as JsonObj;
+    const info = msg.info;
+    if (info === null || typeof info !== 'object') continue;
+    const inf = info as JsonObj;
+    if (inf.role === 'assistant') result.push(msg);
+  }
+  return result;
+}
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// extractTextFromParts — internal helper: concatenate type:'text' parts.
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+function extractTextFromParts(msg: JsonObj): string {
+  const parts = msg.parts;
+  if (!Array.isArray(parts)) return '';
+  const textParts: string[] = [];
+  for (const part of parts) {
+    if (part === null || typeof part !== 'object') continue;
+    const p = part as JsonObj;
+    if (p.type === 'text' && typeof p.text === 'string') {
+      textParts.push(p.text);
+    }
+  }
+  return textParts.join('');
+}
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// parseRoot — shared JSON parse + root validation. Returns null on malformed.
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+function parseRoot(content: string): JsonObj | null {
+  if (content.trim().length === 0) return null;
+  let obj: unknown;
+  try {
+    obj = JSON.parse(content);
+  } catch {
+    return null;
+  }
+  if (obj === null || typeof obj !== 'object') return null;
+  return obj as JsonObj;
+}
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// extractOpencodeTurnsFromContent — pure, total: one Turn per assistant
+// message, in order. Index is 0-based. Text = concatenation of type:'text'
+// parts. Never throws; returns [] for empty/malformed input.
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+export function extractOpencodeTurnsFromContent(content: string): Turn[] {
+  const root = parseRoot(content);
+  if (root === null) return [];
+  const assistants = extractAssistantMessages(root);
+  return assistants.map((msg, idx) => ({ index: idx, text: extractTextFromParts(msg) }));
+}
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// extractOpencodeActionsFromContent — pure, defensive:
+//
+// CONFIDENCE LEVEL: medium. RELIABLE fields: finalMessage (reuses
+// parseTranscript logic), turnCount (= number of assistant messages).
+// DEFENSIVE/INFERRED fields: tool extraction scans assistant parts for
+// tool-call-like entries. Tool-call part shape is NOT documented in the
+// opencode export spec — it is inferred by analogy with Claude's tool_use
+// format and Codex/Gemini extractors:
+//
+//   { type: 'tool-call', toolName: string, args?: {...} | input?: {...} }
+//
+// Tool-result errors inferred as:
+//   { type: 'tool-result', isError: true, content?: string | output?: string }
+//
+// TODO(opencode): verify tool-call part shape against a real tool-using
+// transcript and refine field extraction.
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+function pushUnique(arr: string[], val: string): void {
+  if (!arr.includes(val)) arr.push(val);
+}
+
+export function extractOpencodeActionsFromContent(content: string): ActionManifest {
+  const root = parseRoot(content);
+  if (root === null) {
+    return {
+      toolsUsed: {},
+      filesRead: [],
+      filesEdited: [],
+      filesWritten: [],
+      bashCommands: [],
+      errors: [],
+      finalMessage: '',
+      turnCount: 0,
+    };
+  }
+
+  const toolsUsed: Record<string, number> = {};
+  const filesRead: string[] = [];
+  const filesEdited: string[] = [];
+  const filesWritten: string[] = [];
+  const bashCommands: string[] = [];
+  const errors: string[] = [];
+
+  const assistants = extractAssistantMessages(root);
+
+  for (const msg of assistants) {
+    const parts = msg.parts;
+    if (!Array.isArray(parts)) continue;
+
+    for (const part of parts) {
+      if (part === null || typeof part !== 'object') continue;
+      const p = part as JsonObj;
+
+      // Defensive tool-call extraction. Accept { toolName } (camelCase) with
+      // { args } or { input } containing optional file_path / command fields.
+      if (typeof p.type === 'string' && p.type.includes('tool-call')) {
+        const toolName = typeof p.toolName === 'string' ? p.toolName : undefined;
+        if (toolName !== undefined) {
+          toolsUsed[toolName] = (toolsUsed[toolName] ?? 0) + 1;
+
+          const argsField =
+            p.args !== undefined && typeof p.args === 'object'
+              ? (p.args as JsonObj)
+              : p.input !== undefined && typeof p.input === 'object'
+                ? (p.input as JsonObj)
+                : null;
+
+          if (argsField !== null) {
+            const filePath =
+              typeof argsField.file_path === 'string' ? argsField.file_path : undefined;
+            const command = typeof argsField.command === 'string' ? argsField.command : undefined;
+
+            if (toolName === 'Read' && filePath !== undefined) pushUnique(filesRead, filePath);
+            else if ((toolName === 'Edit' || toolName === 'MultiEdit') && filePath !== undefined)
+              pushUnique(filesEdited, filePath);
+            else if (toolName === 'Write' && filePath !== undefined)
+              pushUnique(filesWritten, filePath);
+            else if (toolName === 'Bash' && command !== undefined) bashCommands.push(command);
+          }
+        }
+      }
+
+      // Defensive tool-result error extraction.
+      if (typeof p.type === 'string' && p.type.includes('tool-result') && p.isError === true) {
+        const msg =
+          typeof p.content === 'string'
+            ? p.content
+            : typeof p.output === 'string'
+              ? p.output
+              : typeof p.message === 'string'
+                ? p.message
+                : '';
+        if (msg.length > 0) errors.push(msg);
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  const lastAssistant: JsonObj | null =
+    assistants.length > 0 ? (assistants[assistants.length - 1] ?? null) : null;
+
+  return {
+    toolsUsed,
+    filesRead,
+    filesEdited,
+    filesWritten,
+    bashCommands,
+    errors,
+    finalMessage: lastAssistant !== null ? extractTextFromParts(lastAssistant) : '',
+    turnCount: assistants.length,
+  };
+}
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// opencodePluginShouldFire — pure gating predicate mirrored in the plugin.
+// True iff event is session.status idle AND both rctrl env vars are set.
+// Never throws.
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+export function opencodePluginShouldFire(
+  event: unknown,
+  env: Record<string, string | undefined>,
+): boolean {
+  if (event === null || typeof event !== 'object') return false;
+  const e = event as JsonObj;
+  if (e.type !== 'session.status') return false;
+  const props = e.properties;
+  if (props === null || typeof props !== 'object') return false;
+  const p = props as JsonObj;
+  const status = p.status;
+  if (status === null || typeof status !== 'object') return false;
+  const s = status as JsonObj;
+  if (s.type !== 'idle') return false;
+  return !!env?.RCTRL_STATE && !!env?.RCTRL_SESSION_ID;
+}
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// mergeOpencodePluginConfig — pure: idempotently add pluginAbsPath to the
+// opencode config's "plugin" array. Preserves all other keys.
+// Returns valid JSON string. Never throws.
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+export function mergeOpencodePluginConfig(existing: string | null, pluginAbsPath: string): string {
+  let config: JsonObj = {};
+  if (existing !== null && existing.trim().length > 0) {
+    try {
+      const parsed = JSON.parse(existing);
+      if (parsed !== null && typeof parsed === 'object' && !Array.isArray(parsed)) {
+        config = parsed as JsonObj;
+      }
+    } catch {
+      // malformed → treat as empty
+    }
+  }
+
+  const existingPlugins = Array.isArray(config.plugin) ? (config.plugin as unknown[]) : [];
+  const alreadyPresent = existingPlugins.some((p) => p === pluginAbsPath);
+  if (!alreadyPresent) {
+    config = { ...config, plugin: [...existingPlugins, pluginAbsPath] };
+  }
+
+  return JSON.stringify(config, null, 2);
+}
+
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// OpenCodeProvider
+// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+const opencodeProvider: AgentProvider = {
+  name: 'opencode',
+
+  stopEventName: 'session.status',
+
+  // opencode's TUI shows no startup dialogs in a fresh cwd.
+  startupDialogs: [],
+
+  readyMatch: /Ask anything\.\.\.|Build · /,
+
+  buildLaunch(opts): ProviderLaunchSpec {
+    const args: string[] = [];
+    if (opts.model !== undefined) {
+      args.push('-m', opts.model);
+    }
+    return {
+      bin: 'opencode',
+      args,
+      env: {},
+      // opencode uses a global plugin (installed by installGlobalPlugin), not
+      // per-cwd config files. Nothing to write into the session cwd.
+      files: [],
+    };
+  },
+
+  parseTranscript(content: string): string {
+    return parseTranscript(content);
+  },
+
+  exportTranscript(sessionId: string): readonly string[] {
+    return ['opencode', 'export', sessionId];
+  },
+
+  extractActions(content: string): ActionManifest {
+    return extractOpencodeActionsFromContent(content);
+  },
+
+  extractTurns(content: string): Turn[] {
+    return extractOpencodeTurnsFromContent(content);
+  },
+
+  // The bundled plugin: installed once as infrastructure alongside stop.sh.
+  // installGlobalPlugin (hooks adapter) writes PLUGIN_SOURCE to
+  // hooksDir/opencode-stop.ts and merges that path into the user's global
+  // opencode config so opencode loads it on every launch.
+  globalPlugin: {
+    fileName: 'opencode-stop.ts',
+    content: PLUGIN_SOURCE,
+  },
+} as const;
+
+export const OpenCodeProvider: AgentProvider = opencodeProvider;
```

| X1 | error | `test/unit/hygiene.test.ts` | 64 | a private key block was added. a credential in a commit is a credential published, and deleting the line later leaves it in the history. take the value out, rotate it, and read the credential from the environment at run time. |


```diff
diff --git a/src/core/hygiene.ts b/src/core/hygiene.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..8f0a5b0
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/core/hygiene.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,102 @@
+// The hygiene gate (§E) — pure scans over the staged diff, run after scoped
+// staging and before smoke. Three detectors, three real incident classes:
+// "verified but did nothing" (empty-diff on agent work), "verified but
+// leaking" (credentials in added lines), "verified but destructive" (a file
+// mostly deleted without the worker saying so). High precision over recall —
+// a noisy gate gets disabled by its users, which is worse than a narrow one.
+// All failures are retryable with evidence; run-node owns routing.
+
+export interface HygieneInput {
+  workKind: 'prompt' | 'phases' | 'command';
+  stagedFiles: readonly string[];
+  diff: string; // the staged diff text
+  numstat: readonly { file: string; added: number; deleted: number }[];
+  finalMessage: string; // the worker's final message (deletion re-statement lives here)
+}
+
+export interface HygieneFailure {
+  kind: 'empty-diff' | 'secret' | 'deletion';
+  evidence: string;
+}
+
+// ~12 high-signal patterns. Precision over recall: each of these is a
+// near-certain credential, not a maybe. Scanned over ADDED lines only —
+// removing a secret must never be punished.
+const SECRET_PATTERNS: readonly [RegExp, string][] = [
+  [/AKIA[0-9A-Z]{16}/, 'AWS access key id'],
+  [/-----BEGIN (?:RSA |EC |OPENSSH |PGP )?PRIVATE KEY(?: BLOCK)?-----/, 'private key block'],
+  [/ghp_[A-Za-z0-9]{36}/, 'GitHub personal access token'],
+  [/github_pat_[A-Za-z0-9_]{22,}/, 'GitHub fine-grained token'],
+  [/sk-ant-[A-Za-z0-9-]{20,}/, 'Anthropic API key'],
+  [/sk-[A-Za-z0-9]{20}T3BlbkFJ[A-Za-z0-9]{20}/, 'OpenAI API key'],
+  [/xox[bpoas]-[0-9A-Za-z-]{10,}/, 'Slack token'],
+  [/AIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{35}/, 'Google API key'],
+  [/glpat-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20}/, 'GitLab token'],
+  [/npm_[A-Za-z0-9]{36}/, 'npm token'],
+  [/eyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.eyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}/, 'signed JWT'],
+  [
+    /(?:api[_-]?key|secret|password)["']?\s*[:=]\s*["'][A-Za-z0-9+/]{32,}["']/i,
+    'inline credential assignment',
+  ],
+];
+
+const DELETION_PCT = 0.5;
+const DELETION_FLOOR = 100; // lines — both thresholds must trip
+
+export function checkDiffHygiene(input: HygieneInput): HygieneFailure | null {
+  // 1 — empty-diff attribution: agent work that claims done on nothing.
+  // Command work may legitimately be effect-free (probes, verifications).
+  if (input.workKind !== 'command' && input.stagedFiles.length === 0) {
+    return {
+      kind: 'empty-diff',
+      evidence:
+        'your attempt produced no changes — an agent node cannot claim done on nothing. ' +
+        'If no change is needed, run the gate so its stamp lands in your diff.',
+    };
+  }
+
+  // 2 — secrets in ADDED lines, attributed to their file via the diff headers.
+  let currentFile = '?';
+  for (const line of input.diff.split('\n')) {
+    if (line.startsWith('+++ b/')) {
+      currentFile = line.slice('+++ b/'.length);
+      continue;
+    }
+    if (!line.startsWith('+')) continue;
+    for (const [pattern, label] of SECRET_PATTERNS) {
+      if (pattern.test(line)) {
+        return {
+          kind: 'secret',
+          evidence:
+            `${label} detected in ${currentFile} — remove the credential and reference it ` +
+            `via an environment variable or secret store instead; committed secrets are ` +
+            `compromised the moment they land.`,
+        };
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  // 3 — the deletion tripwire: one file losing most of itself, in bulk,
+  // without the worker saying so. The escape is deterministic: re-state the
+  // deletion in the final message (any mention of the file alongside a
+  // deletion word) and the wire passes.
+  for (const n of input.numstat) {
+    const total = n.added + n.deleted;
+    if (n.deleted > DELETION_FLOOR && total > 0 && n.deleted / total > DELETION_PCT) {
+      const restated =
+        input.finalMessage.includes(n.file) &&
+        /delet|remov|rewrit|replac/i.test(input.finalMessage);
+      if (!restated) {
+        return {
+          kind: 'deletion',
+          evidence:
+            `${n.file} loses ${n.deleted} lines (${Math.round((n.deleted / total) * 100)}% of ` +
+            `its diff) — if intentional, re-state it in your final message naming the file ` +
+            `and the deletion; unexplained bulk deletion does not publish.`,
+        };
+      }
+    }
+  }
+
+  return null;
+}
diff --git a/test/unit/hygiene.test.ts b/test/unit/hygiene.test.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..57c91f1
--- /dev/null
+++ b/test/unit/hygiene.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,121 @@
+// Adoption ladder §E — the hygiene gate's pure detectors. Two real incident
+// classes from Bernstein's archive ("verified but did nothing", "verified but
+// destructive") plus the credential class every public repo fears. High
+// precision over recall: a noisy gate gets disabled by its users, which is
+// worse than a narrow one.
+import { describe, expect, test } from 'bun:test';
+import { checkDiffHygiene } from '../../src/core/hygiene.ts';
+
+const NUM = (file: string, added: number, deleted: number) => ({ file, added, deleted });
+
+describe('checkDiffHygiene (§E)', () => {
+  test('clean diff passes', () => {
+    const r = checkDiffHygiene({
+      workKind: 'prompt',
+      stagedFiles: ['src/a.ts'],
+      diff: '+export const a = 1;\n',
+      numstat: [NUM('src/a.ts', 1, 0)],
+      finalMessage: '',
+    });
+    expect(r).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  test('empty staged set on agent work is the empty-diff failure', () => {
+    const r = checkDiffHygiene({
+      workKind: 'prompt',
+      stagedFiles: [],
+      diff: '',
+      numstat: [],
+      finalMessage: 'all done!',
+    });
+    expect(r?.kind).toBe('empty-diff');
+    expect(r?.evidence).toContain('no changes');
+  });
+
+  test('command work may legitimately be effect-free', () => {
+    const r = checkDiffHygiene({
+      workKind: 'command',
+      stagedFiles: [],
+      diff: '',
+      numstat: [],
+      finalMessage: '',
+    });
+    expect(r).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  test('an AWS key in the added lines is a secrets failure naming the file', () => {
+    const diff = [
+      'diff --git a/src/config.ts b/src/config.ts',
+      '+++ b/src/config.ts',
+      '+const key = "AKIAIOSFODNN7EXAMPLE";',
+    ].join('\n');
+    const r = checkDiffHygiene({
+      workKind: 'prompt',
+      stagedFiles: ['src/config.ts'],
+      diff,
+      numstat: [NUM('src/config.ts', 1, 0)],
+      finalMessage: '',
+    });
+    expect(r?.kind).toBe('secret');
+    expect(r?.evidence).toContain('src/config.ts');
+  });
+
+  test('a private key block is a secrets failure', () => {
+    const diff = '+++ b/deploy/key.pem\n+-----BEGIN RSA PRIVATE KEY-----\n+abc\n';
+    const r = checkDiffHygiene({
+      workKind: 'prompt',
+      stagedFiles: ['deploy/key.pem'],
+      diff,
+      numstat: [NUM('deploy/key.pem', 2, 0)],
+      finalMessage: '',
+    });
+    expect(r?.kind).toBe('secret');
+  });
+
+  test('REMOVING a secret is not a violation (deleted lines are not scanned)', () => {
+    const diff =
+      '+++ b/src/config.ts\n-const key = "AKIAIOSFODNN7EXAMPLE";\n+const key = env.KEY;\n';
+    const r = checkDiffHygiene({
+      workKind: 'prompt',
+      stagedFiles: ['src/config.ts'],
+      diff,
+      numstat: [NUM('src/config.ts', 1, 1)],
+      finalMessage: '',
+    });
+    expect(r).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  test('>50% and >100 lines deleted in one file trips the deletion wire', () => {
+    const r = checkDiffHygiene({
+      workKind: 'prompt',
+      stagedFiles: ['src/big.ts'],
+      diff: '+++ b/src/big.ts\n',
+      numstat: [NUM('src/big.ts', 10, 150)],
+      finalMessage: '',
+    });
+    expect(r?.kind).toBe('deletion');
+    expect(r?.evidence).toContain('src/big.ts');
+  });
+
+  test('a large deletion the worker explicitly re-states passes the wire', () => {
+    const r = checkDiffHygiene({
+      workKind: 'prompt',
+      stagedFiles: ['src/big.ts'],
+      diff: '+++ b/src/big.ts\n',
+      numstat: [NUM('src/big.ts', 10, 150)],
+      finalMessage: 'Rewrote the module. DELETION INTENDED: src/big.ts loses its legacy half.',
+    });
+    expect(r).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  test('small deletions never trip (both thresholds required)', () => {
+    const r = checkDiffHygiene({
+      workKind: 'prompt',
+      stagedFiles: ['src/small.ts'],
+      diff: '+++ b/src/small.ts\n',
+      numstat: [NUM('src/small.ts', 1, 60)], // >50% but not >100 lines
+      finalMessage: '',
+    });
+    expect(r).toBeNull();
+  });
+});
```

```diff
diff --git a/src/copeca/results/signing.py b/src/copeca/results/signing.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..555d7c2
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/copeca/results/signing.py
@@ -0,0 +1,137 @@
+"""Ed25519 detached signatures over an artifact's content_hash.
+
+Architecture: pure crypto boundary. These helpers compute on values — a key
+object in, bytes/bool out. No filesystem or network I/O lives here; PEM bytes
+are read/written by callers (the CLI and artifact/verification adapters). This
+keeps signing and verification deterministic, total, and trivially testable.
+
+Why this exists (F-C2): the integrity manifest (a SHA-256 over per-file hashes,
+stored inside the zip) detects accidental corruption but NOT deliberate
+tampering — an attacker edits a file, recomputes the manifest, and it passes. A
+detached signature over the content_hash that only a private-key holder can
+produce closes that hole: a tampered-and-recomputed artifact fails signature
+verification because the attacker cannot re-sign the new content_hash.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from cryptography.exceptions import InvalidSignature
+from cryptography.hazmat.primitives import serialization
+from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.asymmetric.ed25519 import (
+    Ed25519PrivateKey,
+    Ed25519PublicKey,
+)
+
+
+def generate_keypair() -> tuple[Ed25519PrivateKey, Ed25519PublicKey]:
+    """Generate a fresh Ed25519 keypair.
+
+    Returns:
+        (private_key, public_key). The private key signs; the public key (safe to
+        publish) verifies.
+    """
+    private_key = Ed25519PrivateKey.generate()
+    return private_key, private_key.public_key()
+
+
+def sign(content_hash: str, private_key: Ed25519PrivateKey) -> bytes:
+    """Produce a detached signature over ``content_hash``.
+
+    Pure function — no I/O. The message signed is the UTF-8 encoding of the
+    hex content_hash string, so the signature is bound to that exact value.
+
+    Args:
+        content_hash: The artifact's content_hash (hex SHA-256 string).
+        private_key: The Ed25519 private key to sign with.
+
+    Returns:
+        The 64-byte raw Ed25519 signature.
+    """
+    return private_key.sign(content_hash.encode("utf-8"))
+
+
+def verify_signature(content_hash: str, signature: bytes, public_key: Ed25519PublicKey) -> bool:
+    """Verify a detached signature over ``content_hash``.
+
+    Pure & total — returns False for any invalid or malformed signature rather
+    than raising, so callers get a clean boolean contract.
+
+    Args:
+        content_hash: The content_hash the signature is claimed to cover.
+        signature: The detached signature bytes.
+        public_key: The trusted Ed25519 public key.
+
+    Returns:
+        True iff ``signature`` is a valid Ed25519 signature by ``public_key``
+        over ``content_hash``; False otherwise.
+    """
+    try:
+        public_key.verify(signature, content_hash.encode("utf-8"))
+        return True
+    except InvalidSignature:
+        return False
+
+
+def serialize_private_key_pem(private_key: Ed25519PrivateKey) -> bytes:
+    """Serialize a private key to unencrypted PKCS#8 PEM bytes.
+
+    Pure function — no I/O. Callers write the bytes to a file. The key is
+    unencrypted: protect the file with filesystem permissions and keep it out of
+    version control (operator responsibility — copeca never stores keys).
+    """
+    return private_key.private_bytes(
+        encoding=serialization.Encoding.PEM,
+        format=serialization.PrivateFormat.PKCS8,
+        encryption_algorithm=serialization.NoEncryption(),
+    )
+
+
+def serialize_public_key_pem(public_key: Ed25519PublicKey) -> bytes:
+    """Serialize a public key to SubjectPublicKeyInfo PEM bytes (pure, no I/O)."""
+    return public_key.public_bytes(
+        encoding=serialization.Encoding.PEM,
+        format=serialization.PublicFormat.SubjectPublicKeyInfo,
+    )
+
+
+def load_private_key_pem(pem: bytes) -> Ed25519PrivateKey:
+    """Load an Ed25519 private key from unencrypted PEM bytes.
+
+    Pure function — the caller reads the file. Raises if the PEM is not an
+    unencrypted Ed25519 private key.
+
+    Raises:
+        ValueError: If the PEM does not decode to an Ed25519 private key.
+    """
+    key = serialization.load_pem_private_key(pem, password=None)
+    if not isinstance(key, Ed25519PrivateKey):
+        raise ValueError(f"Expected an Ed25519 private key, got {type(key).__name__}")
+    return key
+
+
+def load_public_key_pem(pem: bytes) -> Ed25519PublicKey:
+    """Load an Ed25519 public key from PEM bytes.
+
+    Pure function — the caller reads the file.
+
+    Raises:
+        ValueError: If the PEM does not decode to an Ed25519 public key.
+    """
+    key = serialization.load_pem_public_key(pem)
+    if not isinstance(key, Ed25519PublicKey):
+        raise ValueError(f"Expected an Ed25519 public key, got {type(key).__name__}")
+    return key
+
+
+def load_private_key_file(path: str) -> Ed25519PrivateKey:
+    """Read a private key PEM file and load it (thin I/O wrapper for the CLI)."""
+    from pathlib import Path
+
+    return load_private_key_pem(Path(path).read_bytes())
+
+
+def load_public_key_file(path: str) -> Ed25519PublicKey:
+    """Read a public key PEM file and load it (thin I/O wrapper for the CLI)."""
+    from pathlib import Path
+
+    return load_public_key_pem(Path(path).read_bytes())
```

```diff
-pub(crate) struct Services {
```

```diff
-}

-            cache: Arc::new(OutlineCache::new()),
-            session: Arc::new(Session::new()),
-            bloom: Arc::new(BloomFilterCache::new()),
-            tracker: Arc::new(ThreadTracker::new()),
```
