+      return '<svg viewBox="0 0 120 80" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">' +
+        '<rect class="tc-bg tc-frame" x="8" y="8" width="104" height="64" rx="4" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<line class="tc-ln" x1="20" y1="40" x2="100" y2="40" stroke-width="2"/>' +
+        '<circle class="tc-wh tc-frame" cx="22" cy="40" r="6" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<circle class="tc-wh tc-frame" cx="50" cy="40" r="6" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<circle class="tc-ac" cx="78" cy="40" r="7"/>' +
+        '<circle class="tc-wh tc-frame" cx="100" cy="40" r="6" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<line class="tc-ln" x1="50" y1="40" x2="50" y2="24" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<rect class="tc-wh tc-frame" x="38" y="14" width="24" height="12" rx="2" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<line class="tc-la" x1="50" y1="40" x2="50" y2="58" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<rect class="tc-wh tc-frame" x="38" y="56" width="24" height="12" rx="2" stroke-width="1"/>' +
+      '</svg>';
+    }
+    if (phase === 'build') {
+      // stacked blocks: three ascending rects (foundation, mid, accent top) + workpiece panel with connector
+      return '<svg viewBox="0 0 120 80" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">' +
+        '<rect class="tc-wh tc-frame" x="18" y="46" width="36" height="22" rx="2" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<rect class="tc-wh tc-frame" x="24" y="28" width="24" height="20" rx="2" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<rect class="tc-ac" x="30" y="14" width="14" height="16" rx="2"/>' +
+        '<rect class="tc-wh tc-frame" x="58" y="36" width="42" height="32" rx="3" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<line class="tc-la" x1="64" y1="46" x2="94" y2="46" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<line class="tc-la" x1="64" y1="54" x2="94" y2="54" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<circle class="tc-fg" cx="88" cy="43" r="3"/>' +
+        '<line class="tc-ln" x1="54" y1="52" x2="58" y2="52" stroke-width="2"/>' +
+        '<line class="tc-ln" x1="54" y1="48" x2="58" y2="52" stroke-width="2"/>' +
+      '</svg>';
+    }
+    if (phase === 'verify') {
+      // test panel: framed card + row dividers + two accent check circles + pending empty circle + label lines
+      return '<svg viewBox="0 0 120 80" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">' +
+        '<rect class="tc-wh tc-frame" x="18" y="10" width="84" height="60" rx="3" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<line class="tc-la" x1="18" y1="24" x2="102" y2="24" stroke-width="1"/>' +
+        '<line class="tc-la" x1="18" y1="38" x2="102" y2="38" stroke-width="1"/>' +
+        '<line class="tc-la" x1="18" y1="52" x2="102" y2="52" stroke-width="1"/>' +
+        '<circle class="tc-ac" cx="32" cy="31" r="5"/>' +
+        '<path d="M29 31 l2 3 l5-5" class="tc-wh" stroke-width="1.5" fill="none"/>' +
+        '<circle class="tc-ac" cx="32" cy="45" r="5"/>' +
+        '<path d="M29 45 l2 3 l5-5" class="tc-wh" stroke-width="1.5" fill="none"/>' +
+        '<circle class="tc-ln" cx="32" cy="59" r="5" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<line class="tc-la" x1="44" y1="31" x2="86" y2="31" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<line class="tc-la" x1="44" y1="45" x2="78" y2="45" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<line class="tc-la" x1="44" y1="59" x2="72" y2="59" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+      '</svg>';
+    }
+    if (phase === 'review') {
+      // reading glasses over document: bg card + text lines + two lens circles + accent irises + temple arms
+      return '<svg viewBox="0 0 120 80" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">' +
+        '<rect class="tc-bg tc-frame" x="22" y="12" width="76" height="56" rx="3" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<line class="tc-la" x1="32" y1="30" x2="88" y2="30" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<line class="tc-la" x1="32" y1="38" x2="88" y2="38" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<line class="tc-la" x1="32" y1="46" x2="72" y2="46" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<line class="tc-la" x1="32" y1="54" x2="80" y2="54" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<circle class="tc-wh tc-frame" cx="47" cy="22" r="10" stroke-width="2"/>' +
+        '<circle class="tc-wh tc-frame" cx="73" cy="22" r="10" stroke-width="2"/>' +
+        '<line class="tc-ln" x1="57" y1="22" x2="63" y2="22" stroke-width="2"/>' +
+        '<line class="tc-ln" x1="20" y1="18" x2="37" y2="22" stroke-width="2"/>' +
+        '<line class="tc-ln" x1="100" y1="18" x2="83" y2="22" stroke-width="2"/>' +
+        '<circle class="tc-ac" cx="47" cy="22" r="4"/>' +
+        '<circle class="tc-ac" cx="73" cy="22" r="4"/>' +
+      '</svg>';
+    }
+    if (kind === 'persona') {
+      // contact card: framed card + portrait circle + avatar silhouette + name/role lines
+      return '<svg viewBox="0 0 120 80" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">' +
+        '<rect class="tc-wh tc-frame" x="14" y="10" width="92" height="60" rx="4" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<circle class="tc-bg tc-frame" cx="42" cy="36" r="18" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<circle class="tc-ac" cx="42" cy="30" r="8"/>' +
+        '<path class="tc-ac" d="M26 52 q0-14 16-14 q16 0 16 14" fill-rule="evenodd"/>' +
+        '<line class="tc-la" x1="68" y1="26" x2="98" y2="26" stroke-width="2"/>' +
+        '<line class="tc-la" x1="68" y1="36" x2="98" y2="36" stroke-width="2"/>' +
+        '<line class="tc-la" x1="68" y1="46" x2="88" y2="46" stroke-width="2"/>' +
+      '</svg>';
+    }
+    if (kind === 'opportunity') {
+      // spotlight + bullseye: bg frame + cone polygon + two target rings + accent bullseye + beam connector
+      return '<svg viewBox="0 0 120 80" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">' +
+        '<rect class="tc-bg tc-frame" x="8" y="8" width="104" height="64" rx="4" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<polygon class="tc-la" points="20,14 20,66 70,52 70,28" fill="none" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<polygon class="tc-wh" points="20,14 20,66 70,52 70,28" opacity="0.6"/>' +
+        '<circle class="tc-wh tc-frame" cx="84" cy="40" r="20" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+        '<circle class="tc-wh tc-frame" cx="84" cy="40" r="12" stroke-width="1"/>' +
+        '<circle class="tc-ac" cx="84" cy="40" r="6"/>' +
+        '<line class="tc-ln" x1="70" y1="40" x2="64" y2="40" stroke-width="2"/>' +
+      '</svg>';
+    }
+    // fallback: document with folded tab + accent label pill + content lines
+    return '<svg viewBox="0 0 120 80" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">' +
+      '<path class="tc-wh tc-frame" d="M28 18 L28 72 L92 72 L92 18 L70 18 L64 10 L28 10 Z" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+      '<line class="tc-la" x1="28" y1="18" x2="92" y2="18" stroke-width="1"/>' +
+      '<line class="tc-la" x1="38" y1="28" x2="82" y2="28" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+      '<line class="tc-la" x1="38" y1="36" x2="82" y2="36" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+      '<line class="tc-la" x1="38" y1="44" x2="82" y2="44" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+      '<line class="tc-la" x1="38" y1="52" x2="70" y2="52" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+      '<line class="tc-la" x1="38" y1="60" x2="76" y2="60" stroke-width="1.5"/>' +
+      '<rect class="tc-ac" x="38" y="24" width="16" height="6" rx="1"/>' +
+    '</svg>';
+  }
+
+  // Small mono-uppercase label above the card title.
+  // E.g. "build · feature" or "verify · opportunity".
+  function eyebrowFor(item) {
+    var parts = [];
+    if (item && item.journey_phase) parts.push(item.journey_phase);
+    if (item && item.kind)          parts.push(item.kind);
+    return parts.join(' · ');
+  }
+
+  // Render one card as an <a> linking to the entity's polyglot.
+  // item: { id, title?, what?, status?, journey_phase?, kind?, thumbnail? }
+  // hrefPrefix: path prefix for the link (e.g. 'features/' from garden, '' from feature)
+  function renderEntityCard(item, hrefPrefix) {
+    if (!item || !item.id) return '';
+    hrefPrefix = hrefPrefix || '';
+    var href = esc(hrefPrefix + item.id + '.tend.html');
+    var eyebrow = eyebrowFor(item);
+    var title   = item.title || item.id;
+    var text    = item.what || '';
+
+    var headerContent;
+    if (item.thumbnail && item.thumbnail.kind === 'svg' && item.thumbnail.content) {
+      headerContent = item.thumbnail.content;
+    } else {
+      headerContent = autoIcon(item);
+    }
+
+    var eyebrowHtml = eyebrow
+      ? '<p class="tend-card__eyebrow">' + esc(eyebrow) + '</p>'
+      : '';
+    var textHtml = text
+      ? '<p class="tend-card__text">' + esc(text) + '</p>'
+      : '';
+
+    return '<a class="tend-card" href="' + href + '">' +
+      '<div class="tend-card__header">' + headerContent + '</div>' +
+      '<div class="tend-card__body">' +
+        eyebrowHtml +
+        '<p class="tend-card__title">' + esc(title) + '</p>' +
+        textHtml +
+        '<div class="tend-card__footer">' +
+          '<span class="tend-card__id">' + esc(item.id) + '</span>' +
+          '<span class="tend-card__arrow">→</span>' +
+        '</div>' +
+      '</div>' +
+    '</a>';
+  }
+
+  // Render a section containing a card grid.
+  // items: array of card-item objects. Returns '' when items is empty (presence-driven).
+  // label: visible <h2> heading.
+  // sectionId: id= attribute for TOC anchor.
+  // hrefPrefix: forwarded to renderEntityCard.
+  function renderEntityCardGrid(items, label, sectionId, hrefPrefix) {
+    if (!isNonEmptyArray(items)) return '';
+    var cards = items.map(function (item) {
+      return renderEntityCard(item, hrefPrefix);
+    }).join('');
+    return '<section id="' + esc(sectionId) + '">' +
+      '<h2>' + esc(label) + '</h2>' +
+      '<div class="tend-card-grid">' + cards + '</div>' +
+    '</section>';
+  }
+
+  // Checks list — compact rows that expand a per-check <details>.
+  //
+  // Each row mirrors the detail-step pattern: a <details id="rail-check-<id>">
+  // whose <summary> is the compact at-a-glance line (verdict glyph + id + a
+  // ONE-line clamped description) and whose body holds the full description,
+  // method/level chips, the validates_job binding, the degradation sublabel,
+  // and the evidence path. The summary ALSO carries id="rail-<id>" so existing
+  // bet-probe (href="#rail-<id>") + step-trace links land on the row, and the
+  // cross-highlight handler can open + flash it.
+  function renderChecks(data) {
+    var checks = data && data.checks;
+    if (!isNonEmptyArray(checks)) return '';
+    var auditByCheck = {};
+    var hasVerdicts = false;
+    if (data.audit && isNonEmptyArray(data.audit.verdicts)) {
+      data.audit.verdicts.forEach(function (v) { auditByCheck[v.check_id] = v; });
+      hasVerdicts = true;
+    }
+    var passCount = 0;
+
+    var items = checks.map(function (ch) {
+      var verdict = auditByCheck[ch.id];
+      var status = (verdict && verdict.verdict) || ch.status || 'missing';
+      if (verdict && verdict.verdict === 'pass') passCount++;
+      var statusClass = AC_STATUS_TO_CLASS[status] === undefined ? glyphClassFor(status) : AC_STATUS_TO_CLASS[status];
+      var glyph = (status === 'pass') ? '✓' : (status === 'partial' ? '◑' : (status === 'fail' ? '✕' : acGlyphFor(status)));
+      var methodPill = ch.method ? pill(ch.method, 'method-' + slug(ch.method)) : '';
+      var levelPill = ch.integration_level ? '<span class="detail-criterion__level detail-criterion__level--' + esc(ch.integration_level) + '">' + esc(ch.integration_level) + '</span>' : '';
+      var pillsRow = (methodPill || levelPill)
+        ? '<div class="detail-criterion__pills">' + methodPill + levelPill + '</div>'
+        : '';
+      // The persona-job this check anchors to (when bound) — surfaced in the
+      // expanded body so the reader can trace the check to its validation clause.
+      var jobRow = isNonEmptyString(ch.validates_job)
+        ? '<div class="detail-criterion__job">validates job <code>' + esc(ch.validates_job) + '</code></div>'
+        : '';
+      // Truth sub-label — a muted note on WHY a recorded verdict is degraded.
+      // The ONLY degradation signal persisted in oo-data at render time is
+      // `validates_job_satisfied === false`: the test passes mechanically but does
+      // NOT assert the bound persona-job's measurable outcome (written by
+      // tend-audit; see AuditVerdict in src/core/types.ts). Mock-only + staleness
+      // are computed server-side at validate time (mock-detection.ts re-parses the
+      // test file; staleness re-hashes evidence_path) and are NEVER written back
+      // onto the verdict — so the browser renderer genuinely cannot know them and
+      // must not fabricate a label. Only the honest, on-disk flag surfaces here.
+      var sublabel = (verdict && verdict.validates_job_satisfied === false)
+        ? '<div class="detail-criterion__sublabel">job not asserted</div>'
+        : '';
+      var evidence = (verdict && verdict.evidence_path)
+        ? '<div class="detail-criterion__evidence"><span class="detail-criterion__evidence-connector">└</span> <code class="evidence-path" data-copy="' + esc(verdict.evidence_path) + '">' + esc(verdict.evidence_path) + '</code></div>'
+        : '';
+      var desc = ch.description || '';
+      var titleAttr = desc ? ' title="' + esc(desc) + '"' : '';
+      return '<details class="detail-criterion" id="rail-check-' + esc(ch.id) + '">' +
+        '<summary class="detail-criterion__row" id="rail-' + esc(ch.id) + '"' + titleAttr + '>' +
+          '<span class="detail-criterion__status detail-criterion__status--' + esc(statusClass) + '">' + esc(glyph) + '</span>' +
+          '<span class="detail-criterion__id">' + esc(ch.id) + '</span>' +
+          '<span class="detail-criterion__desc detail-criterion__desc--clamp">' + esc(desc) + '</span>' +
+        '</summary>' +
+        '<div class="detail-criterion__body">' +
+          (desc ? '<p class="detail-criterion__full">' + esc(desc) + '</p>' : '') +
+          sublabel +
+          jobRow +
+          pillsRow +
+          evidence +
+        '</div>' +
+      '</details>';
+    }).join('');
+
+    var label = checks.some(function (c) { return c.method; }) ? 'checks' : 'acceptance';
+    // Section summary: "N ✓/M" from real audit verdicts (M = check count). With
+    // zero verdicts we show the plain count only — never a fabricated "0 ✓/M".
+    var countLabel = hasVerdicts
+      ? (passCount + ' ✓/' + checks.length)
+      : String(checks.length);
+    return railSection(label, '<ul class="detail-criteria">' + items + '</ul>', { collapsible: true, count: countLabel });
+  }
+
+  function renderSteps(data) {
+    var steps = data && data.steps;
+    if (!isNonEmptyArray(steps)) return '';
+    var items = steps.map(function (s) {
+      var status = s.status || 'pending';
+      var glyph = s.status_glyph || glyphFor(status);
+      var test = s.test || {};
+      var testBlock = (test.path || test.asserts || test.run_cmd)
+        ? '<div class="detail-step__block detail-step__block--test"><span class="detail-step__label">Test</span>' +
+          (test.path    ? '<code class="detail-step__path">' + esc(test.path) + '</code>' : '') +
+          (test.asserts ? '<p>' + esc(test.asserts) + '</p>' : '') +
+          (test.run_cmd ? '<span class="action-cmd" data-copy="' + esc(test.run_cmd) + '" title="click to copy">$ ' + esc(test.run_cmd) + '</span>' : '') +
+        '</div>'
+        : '';
+      var implBlock = isNonEmptyString(s.implement)
+        ? '<div class="detail-step__block detail-step__block--impl"><span class="detail-step__label">Implement</span><p>' + esc(s.implement) + '</p></div>'
+        : '';
+      var descBlock = (!testBlock && !implBlock && isNonEmptyString(s.description))
+        ? '<div class="detail-step__block"><p>' + esc(s.description) + '</p></div>'
+        : '';
+      var traces = isNonEmptyArray(s.traces_to)
+        ? '<div class="detail-step__traces">traces → ' + s.traces_to.map(function (c) {
+            return '<a class="trace-step" href="#rail-' + esc(c) + '">' + esc(c) + '</a>';
+          }).join(' ') + '</div>'
+        : '';
+      return '<details class="detail-step" id="rail-step-' + esc(s.id) + '">' +
+        '<summary class="detail-step__header">' +
+          glyphSpan(glyph, glyphClassFor(status)) +
+          '<span class="detail-step__id">' + esc(s.id) + '</span>' +
+          '<span class="detail-step__title">' + esc(s.title || s.description ? (s.title || s.description.slice(0, 60)) : '') + '</span>' +
+        '</summary>' +
+        '<div class="detail-step__body">' + testBlock + implBlock + descBlock + traces + '</div>' +
+      '</details>';
+    }).join('');
+    return railSection('steps', '<div class="detail-steps">' + items + '</div>', { collapsible: true, count: steps.length });
+  }
+
+  function renderSmoke(data) {
+    var smoke = data && data.smoke;
+    if (!smoke || !isNonEmptyString(smoke.cmd)) return '';
+    var resultClass = 'detail-smoke__result--' + esc(smoke.last_result || 'unknown');
+    var body = '<div class="detail-smoke__cmd" data-copy="' + esc(smoke.cmd) + '" title="click to copy"><span class="muted">$ </span>' + esc(smoke.cmd) + '</div>' +
+      (smoke.verify ? '<div class="detail-smoke__verify">verify: "' + esc(smoke.verify) + '"</div>' : '') +
+      '<div class="detail-smoke__result ' + resultClass + '">last run: ' + esc(smoke.last_run || 'never') + '</div>';
+    return railSection('smoke', body, { collapsible: true });
+  }
+
+  // Catalog — garden-only field. data.catalog.{phases,personas,opportunities}.
+  // Catalog renders as compact .detail-rel-item rows in the rail — same
+  // visual pattern as the feature rail's depends-on / enables / subpages
+  // lists. Description goes to the title= tooltip (the narrative is where
+  // descriptions belong at page scale; the rail is at-a-glance index).
+  //
+  // Each row is an `<a>` linking to that catalog entry's own polyglot
+  // (e.g. clicking the `solo-dev` persona goes to `features/solo-dev.tend.html`
+  // from the garden, or `solo-dev.tend.html` sibling from a feature page).
+  // The path is relative because the renderer doesn't know its own URL —
+  // garden lives one level above features/ so its rows need `features/`
+  // prefix; feature + subpage rows are siblings.
+  function renderCatalog(data, meta) {
+    var catalog = data && data.catalog;
+    if (!catalog) return '';
+    var aggregate = data.features_aggregate || {};
+    var isGarden = meta && meta.kind === 'garden';
+    var hrefPrefix = isGarden ? 'features/' : '';
+
+    // Compute per-persona / per-opportunity / per-phase counts from the
+    // flat features_aggregate array. Each entry mirrors the source
+    // feature's personas[] / solves[] / journey_phase fields (see
+    // toCatalogEntry in src/core/side-effects.ts). Subpages are skipped —
+    // their parent owns the count. If the aggregate is an object with
+    // pre-computed `by_persona` / `by_opportunity` indices (older shape),
+    // fall back to those instead.
+    var entries = Array.isArray(aggregate) ? aggregate : (aggregate.entries || []);
+    var byPersona = (!Array.isArray(aggregate) && aggregate.by_persona) || {};
+    var byOpportunity = (!Array.isArray(aggregate) && aggregate.by_opportunity) || {};
+    var byPhase = (!Array.isArray(aggregate) && aggregate.by_phase) || {};
+    if (Array.isArray(aggregate)) {
+      for (var i = 0; i < entries.length; i++) {
+        var e = entries[i];
+        if (e && e.is_subpage) continue;
+        (e.personas || []).forEach(function (pid) {
+          (byPersona[pid] = byPersona[pid] || []).push(e);
+        });
+        (e.solves || []).forEach(function (oid) {
+          (byOpportunity[oid] = byOpportunity[oid] || []).push(e);
+        });
+        if (e.journey_phase) {
+          (byPhase[e.journey_phase] = byPhase[e.journey_phase] || []).push(e);
+        }
+      }
+    }
+    var out = '';
+
+    // Which catalog ids actually have a polyglot on disk. A catalog entry
+    // (persona / opportunity / phase) only earns an `<a>` when a matching
+    // `<id>.tend.html` exists — otherwise the link 404s (e.g. an opportunity
+    // that no feature ever turned into a page, or a journey-phase lookup verb
+    // that has no polyglot at all). The signal is already in browser scope:
+    // features_aggregate is built from readAllFeatures(), which reads EVERY
+    // .tend.html in docs/tend/features/ — including catalog-description polyglots
+    // (persona / opportunity pages live there too). So an id with a polyglot is
+    // exactly an id present in the aggregate. No extra snapshot field needed.
+    var polyglotIds = {};
+    for (var pi = 0; pi < entries.length; pi++) {
+      if (entries[pi] && entries[pi].id) polyglotIds[entries[pi].id] = true;
+    }
+
+    function catalogRow(entry, anchorPrefix, count, countLabel) {
+      var id = entry.id;
+      var label = entry.label || entry.name || id;
+      var descTitle = entry.description ? ' title="' + esc(entry.description) + '"' : '';
+      var countChip = (count != null)
+        ? ' <span class="detail-rel-item__count">' + count + (countLabel ? ' ' + esc(countLabel) : '') + '</span>'
+        : '';
+      // Link only when the polyglot exists; otherwise render the id as plain
+      // text (a dead <a> is worse than no link).
+      var idHtml = polyglotIds[id]
+        ? '<a class="dep-link" href="' + hrefPrefix + esc(id) + '.tend.html">' + esc(id) + '</a>'
+        : '<span class="dep-link dep-link--unlinked">' + esc(id) + '</span>';
+      return '<li class="detail-rel-item" id="' + anchorPrefix + esc(id) + '"' + descTitle + '>' +
+        idHtml +
+        '<span class="detail-rel-item__title">' + esc(label) + '</span>' +
+        countChip +
+      '</li>';
+    }
+
+    if (isNonEmptyArray(catalog.phases)) {
+      var phases = catalog.phases.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
+        return (a.order != null ? a.order : 9999) - (b.order != null ? b.order : 9999);
+      });
+      var phasesHtml = '<ul class="detail-rel-list">' + phases.map(function (p) {
+        var n = (byPhase[p.id] || []).length;
+        return catalogRow(p, 'phase-', n, n === 1 ? 'feature' : 'features');
+      }).join('') + '</ul>';
+      out += railSection('phases', phasesHtml, { collapsible: true, count: catalog.phases.length });
+    }
+
+    if (isNonEmptyArray(catalog.personas)) {
+      var personasHtml = '<ul class="detail-rel-list">' + catalog.personas.map(function (p) {
+        var n = (byPersona[p.id] || []).length;
+        return catalogRow(p, 'persona-', n, n === 1 ? 'feature' : 'features');
+      }).join('') + '</ul>';
+      out += railSection('personas', personasHtml, { collapsible: true, count: catalog.personas.length });
+    }
+
+    if (isNonEmptyArray(catalog.opportunities)) {
+      var oppHtml = '<ul class="detail-rel-list">' + catalog.opportunities.map(function (o) {
+        var n = (byOpportunity[o.id] || []).length;
+        return catalogRow(o, 'opp-', n, n === 1 ? 'solved' : 'solved');
+      }).join('') + '</ul>';
+      out += railSection('opportunities', oppHtml, { collapsible: true, count: catalog.opportunities.length });
+    }
+
+    return out;
+  }
+
+  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+  // PERSONA / OPPORTUNITY rail blocks — emit ONLY when the polyglot carries
+  // data.persona or data.opportunity at root. Garden polyglots don't (they
+  // hold catalog.personas[] / catalog.opportunities[] which are a different
+  // shape) so the generic feature + garden rails are untouched.
+  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+
+  // Truncate a string to `n` chars, preserving the full text in a `title`
+  // tooltip on the visible span. Returns { visible, full } so the caller can
+  // decide how to render.
+  function truncated(text, n) {
+    var s = String(text == null ? '' : text);
+    if (s.length <= n) return { visible: s, full: s, truncated: false };
+    return { visible: s.slice(0, n).replace(/\s+\S*$/, '') + '…', full: s, truncated: true };
+  }
+
+  // Compact id+title row used for bound_features / solving_features. Above 8
+  // entries we show the first 8 then a "+ N more →" link. The link is plain
+  // text today; a query-param view is planned.
+  function renderFeatureSummaryList(features, label, moreHref) {
+    if (!isNonEmptyArray(features)) return '';
+    function renderRow(f) {
+      var status = f.status || 'draft';
+      var title = f.title ? '<span class="detail-rel-item__title">' + esc(f.title) + '</span>' : '';
+      return '<li class="detail-rel-item">' +
+        glyphSpan(f.status_glyph || glyphFor(status), glyphClassFor(status)) +
+        '<a class="dep-link" href="' + esc(f.id) + '.tend.html">' + esc(f.id) + '</a>' +
+        title +
+      '</li>';
+    }
+    // Same GRAPH-band overflow rule as renderRelList: all when ≤6, else top-5 +
+    // a "+N more" expander whose overflow stays in the DOM (self-contained).
+    var body = (features.length > 6)
+      ? overflowList(features, renderRow, 5)
+      : '<ul class="detail-rel-list">' + features.map(renderRow).join('') + '</ul>';
+    return railSection(label, body, { collapsible: true, count: features.length });
+  }
+
+  // Simple id-list — used for persona.felt_opportunities + opportunity.personas.
+  function renderIdList(label, ids) {
+    if (!isNonEmptyArray(ids)) return '';
+    var rows = ids.map(function (id) {
+      return '<li class="detail-rel-item">' +
+        '<a class="dep-link" href="' + esc(id) + '.tend.html">' + esc(id) + '</a>' +
+      '</li>';
+    }).join('');
+    return railSection(label, '<ul class="detail-rel-list">' + rows + '</ul>', { collapsible: true, count: ids.length });
+  }
+
+  // The persona's journey THROUGH the product: the top-level features that serve
+  // this persona, grouped by journey_phase and laid out in the project's phase
+  // order — entry phase first, exit phase last. Answers "where do they enter,
+  // which features do they touch, where do they exit", derived from the
+  // bound_features snapshot (each carries journey_phase). Phase order comes from
+  // the snapshotted journey_phases; falls back to first-seen so it still renders
+  // before a re-aggregate. Subpages are excluded — the journey is through
+  // capabilities, not their drill-downs.
+  function renderPersonaJourney(persona) {
+    if (!persona) return '';
+    var feats = isNonEmptyArray(persona.bound_features) ? persona.bound_features : [];
+    var topLevel = feats.filter(function (f) { return f && f.is_subpage !== true; });
+    if (!topLevel.length) return '';
+    function featItem(f) {
+      var status = f.status || 'draft';
+      var title = f.title ? '<span class="detail-rel-item__title">' + esc(f.title) + '</span>' : '';
+      return '<li class="detail-rel-item">' +
+        glyphSpan(f.status_glyph || glyphFor(status), glyphClassFor(status)) +
+        '<a class="dep-link" href="' + esc(f.id) + '.tend.html">' + esc(f.id) + '</a>' + title +
+      '</li>';
+    }
+    var byPhase = {};
+    var unphased = [];
+    topLevel.forEach(function (f) {
+      if (isNonEmptyString(f.journey_phase)) (byPhase[f.journey_phase] = byPhase[f.journey_phase] || []).push(f);
+      else unphased.push(f);
+    });
+    var ordered = (isNonEmptyArray(persona.journey_phases) ? persona.journey_phases : [])
+      .filter(function (p) { return byPhase[p]; });
+    Object.keys(byPhase).forEach(function (p) { if (ordered.indexOf(p) === -1) ordered.push(p); });
+    var steps = ordered.map(function (phase, i) {
+      var edge = i === 0 ? 'enter' : (i === ordered.length - 1 && ordered.length > 1 ? 'exit' : '');
+      var edgeTag = edge ? '<span class="persona-journey__edge">' + edge + '</span>' : '';
+      return '<div class="persona-journey__step' + (edge ? ' persona-journey__step--' + edge : '') + '">' +
+        '<div class="persona-journey__phase">' + esc(phase) + edgeTag + '</div>' +
+        '<ul class="detail-rel-list">' + byPhase[phase].map(featItem).join('') + '</ul>' +
+      '</div>';
+    }).join('');
+    // Top-level features with no journey_phase yet are still the persona's — show
+    // them in a trailing group so nothing is dropped before the map is fully phased.
+    if (unphased.length) {
+      steps += '<div class="persona-journey__step persona-journey__step--unphased">' +
+        '<div class="persona-journey__phase">unphased</div>' +
+        '<ul class="detail-rel-list">' + unphased.map(featItem).join('') + '</ul>' +
+      '</div>';
+    }
+    if (!steps) return '';
+    return railSection('journey', '<div class="persona-journey">' + steps + '</div>', { collapsible: true, count: topLevel.length });
+  }
+
+  // Returns { identity, graph }: the persona's identity meta (jobs count,
+  // archetype, jobs list) goes in the IDENTITY band; its journey through the
+  // product (bound features by phase) + felt opportunities go in the GRAPH band.
+  function renderPersonaRail(persona) {
+    if (!persona) return { identity: '', graph: '' };
+    var identity = '';
+    var jobs = isNonEmptyArray(persona.jobs) ? persona.jobs : [];
+    var boundChecks = isNonEmptyArray(persona.bound_checks) ? persona.bound_checks : [];
+
+    // 1. Meta line — "jobs: N" when jobs[] non-empty.
+    if (jobs.length) {
+      identity += railSection('persona', '<div class="detail-rel-item__aside"><span class="detail-rel-item__aside-key">jobs:</span> ' + jobs.length + '</div>');
+    }
+
+    // 2. Archetype tease — truncated to ~120 chars, full version in `title=`.
+    if (isNonEmptyString(persona.archetype)) {
+      var t = truncated(persona.archetype, 120);
+      var titleAttr = t.truncated ? ' title="' + esc(t.full) + '"' : '';
+      identity += railSection('archetype', '<div class="detail-rel-item"' + titleAttr + '>' + esc(t.visible) + '</div>');
+    }
+
+    // 3. Jobs list — each job gets [<index>], truncated text, check-count chip.
+    //    Zero check-coverage shows a ⚠ marker.
+    if (jobs.length) {
+      var jobRows = jobs.map(function (job, idx) {
+        var count = boundChecks.filter(function (b) { return b.job_index === idx; }).length;
+        var jt = truncated(job, 80);
+        var jobTitle = jt.truncated ? ' title="' + esc(jt.full) + '"' : '';
+        var chipLabel = count + ' check' + (count === 1 ? '' : 's');
+        var chipVariant = count > 0 ? 'mid' : 'muted';
+        var warn = count === 0 ? '<span class="detail-rel-item__warn" aria-label="no bound checks">⚠</span>' : '';
+        return '<li class="detail-rel-item detail-rel-item--stack"' + jobTitle + '>' +
+          '<div class="detail-rel-item__row">' +
+            '<span class="detail-rel-item__title">[' + idx + '] ' + esc(jt.visible) + '</span>' +
+            chip(chipLabel, chipVariant) + warn +
+          '</div>' +
+        '</li>';
+      }).join('');
+      identity += railSection('jobs', '<ul class="detail-rel-list">' + jobRows + '</ul>', { collapsible: true, count: jobs.length });
+    }
+
+    // 4 + 5. Outward links — bound features + felt opportunities (GRAPH band).
+    var graph = renderPersonaJourney(persona) +
+      renderIdList('opportunities felt', persona.felt_opportunities);
+
+    return { identity: identity, graph: graph };
+  }
+
+  // Returns { identity, graph }: value/confidence chips + outcome/risk signals
+  // are the opportunity's identity (IDENTITY band); personas feeling the pain +
+  // solving features are its outward links (GRAPH band).
+  function renderOpportunityRail(opportunity) {
+    if (!opportunity) return { identity: '', graph: '' };
+    var identity = '';
+
+    // 1. Chips line — value + confidence severity chips.
+    var chips = [];
+    if (opportunity.value)      chips.push(chip('value: ' + opportunity.value, chipSeverity(opportunity.value), 'value rank'));
+    if (opportunity.confidence) chips.push(chip('confidence: ' + opportunity.confidence, chipSeverity(opportunity.confidence), 'confidence rank'));
+    var chipsHtml = chipGroup(chips);
+    if (chipsHtml) {
+      identity += railSection('opportunity', '<div class="detail-rel-item">' + chipsHtml + '</div>');
+    }
+
+    // 2. Outcome metric — subtle row.
+    // 3. Riskiest assumption — subtle row.
+    // 4. Last updated — subtle row.
+    var metricRow = subtleRow('metric', opportunity.outcome_metric);
+    var riskRow   = subtleRow('risk',   opportunity.riskiest_assumption);
+    var updRow    = subtleRow('updated', opportunity.last_updated);
+    if (metricRow || riskRow || updRow) {
+      identity += railSection('signals', '<div class="detail-rel-item detail-rel-item--stack">' + metricRow + riskRow + updRow + '</div>');
+    }
+
+    // 5 + 6. Outward links — personas feeling the pain + solving features.
+    var graph = renderIdList('personas feeling this', opportunity.personas) +
+      renderFeatureSummaryList(opportunity.solving_features, 'solving features', '#solving-features');
+
+    return { identity: identity, graph: graph };
+  }
+
+  // Garden's scoreboard + ready-now built from features_aggregate.
+  function renderAggregate(data) {
+    var agg = data && data.features_aggregate;
+    if (!agg) return '';
+    var out = '';
+
+    // Scoreboard counts derived from by_phase entries
+    var allEntries = [];
+    Object.keys(agg.by_phase || {}).forEach(function (k) {
+      (agg.by_phase[k] || []).forEach(function (e) { allEntries.push(e); });
+    });
+    if (allEntries.length) {
+      var byStatus = {};
+      allEntries.forEach(function (e) { byStatus[e.status] = (byStatus[e.status] || 0) + 1; });
+      var totals = '<div class="scoreboard-strip">' +
+        '<span class="scoreboard-strip__stat"><strong>' + esc(String(agg.feature_count || allEntries.length)) + '</strong> features</span>';
+      ['verified', 'in-progress', 'planned', 'draft', 'approved', 'implemented'].forEach(function (s) {
+        if (byStatus[s]) totals += '<span class="scoreboard-strip__stat"><strong>' + esc(String(byStatus[s])) + '</strong> ' + esc(s) + '</span>';
+      });
+      totals += '</div>';
+      out += railSection('scoreboard', totals);
+    }
+
+    if (isNonEmptyArray(agg.ready_now)) {
+      var readyList = '<ul class="detail-rel-list">' + agg.ready_now.map(function (e) {
+        return '<li class="detail-rel-item">' +
+          glyphSpan(e.status_glyph || glyphFor(e.status || 'draft'), glyphClassFor(e.status || 'draft')) +
+          '<a class="dep-link" href="' + esc(e.href || ('features/' + e.id + '.tend.html')) + '">' + esc(e.id) + '</a>' +
+          (e.title ? '<span class="detail-rel-item__title">' + esc(e.title) + '</span>' : '') +
+          (e.priority ? ' ' + pill(e.priority, 'id') : '') +
+        '</li>';
+      }).join('') + '</ul>';
+      out += railSection('ready now', readyList, { collapsible: true, count: agg.ready_now.length });
+    }
+
+    // Full features index — every feature reachable from this garden, sorted
+    // alphabetically. Gives the rail a navigable jump-table to any page
+    // without needing the graph UI.
+    if (allEntries.length) {
+      var seen = {};
+      var indexEntries = allEntries.filter(function (e) {
+        if (!e || !e.id || seen[e.id]) return false;
+        seen[e.id] = true;
+        return true;
+      }).sort(function (a, b) { return a.id.localeCompare(b.id); });
+      var indexList = '<ul class="detail-rel-list">' + indexEntries.map(function (e) {
+        var status = e.status || 'draft';
+        return '<li class="detail-rel-item">' +
+          glyphSpan(e.status_glyph || glyphFor(status), glyphClassFor(status)) +
+          '<a class="dep-link" href="' + esc(e.href || ('features/' + e.id + '.tend.html')) + '">' + esc(e.id) + '</a>' +
+          (e.title ? '<span class="detail-rel-item__title">' + esc(e.title) + '</span>' : '') +
+        '</li>';
+      }).join('') + '</ul>';
+      out += railSection('all features', indexList, { collapsible: true, count: indexEntries.length });
+    }
+
+    return out;
+  }
+
+  function renderAudit(data) {
+    var audit = data && data.audit;
+    if (!audit || !isNonEmptyArray(audit.verdicts)) return '';
+    var counts = { pass: 0, partial: 0, fail: 0, deferred: 0 };
+    audit.verdicts.forEach(function (v) { if (counts[v.verdict] != null) counts[v.verdict]++; });
+    // Build result label: when deferred tail exists, show "verified · N deferred (reasons)"
+    var resultLabel = esc(audit.result || 'pending');
+    var deferredTail = '';
+    if (counts.deferred > 0) {
+      var deferredReasons = audit.verdicts
+        .filter(function (v) { return v.verdict === 'deferred'; })
+        .map(function (v) { return esc(v.deferred_reason || v.deferred_category || 'deferred'); })
+        .join(', ');
+      deferredTail = '<div class="journey-row"><span class="journey-row__key">deferred</span>' +
+        '<span class="journey-row__val">' + counts.deferred + ' deferred — ' + deferredReasons + '</span></div>';
+    }
+    var verdictsLine = counts.pass + ' pass';
+    if (counts.partial > 0) verdictsLine += ' · ' + counts.partial + ' partial';
+    if (counts.fail > 0) verdictsLine += ' · ' + counts.fail + ' fail';
+    if (counts.deferred > 0) verdictsLine += ' · ' + counts.deferred + ' deferred';
+    var body = '<div class="detail-audit">' +
+      '<div class="journey-row"><span class="journey-row__key">result</span><span class="journey-row__val">' + resultLabel + '</span></div>' +
+      '<div class="journey-row"><span class="journey-row__key">verdicts</span><span class="journey-row__val">' +
+        verdictsLine +
+      '</span></div>' +
+      deferredTail +
+    '</div>';
+    return railSection('audit', body, { collapsible: true });
+  }
+
+  function renderProgress(data) {
+    var p = data && data.progress;
+    if (!p) return '';
+    function bar(label, pct, cls) {
+      pct = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Number(pct) || 0));
+      return '<div class="detail-progress__row">' +
+        '<span class="detail-progress__label">' + esc(label) + '</span>' +
+        '<span class="detail-progress__bar"><span class="detail-progress__fill ' + esc(cls) + '" style="width:' + pct + '%"></span></span>' +
+        '<span class="detail-progress__pct">' + pct + '%</span>' +
+      '</div>';
+    }
+    var body = '<div class="detail-progress">' +
+      (p.implementation != null ? bar('impl', p.implementation, 'detail-progress__fill--imp') : '') +
+      (p.verification != null   ? bar('eval', p.verification,   'detail-progress__fill--acc') : '') +
+    '</div>';
+    // No outer collapsible — progress is at-a-glance.
+    return '<div class="detail-section">' + body + '</div>';
+  }
+
+  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+  // HEALTH COCKPIT — top-of-main strip + rail block (garden only)
+  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+
+  // Plain-English verdict word. The verdict color tokens stay
+  // (.health-verdict--{green,yellow,red}); only the WORD becomes legible.
+  var VERDICT_WORD = { green: 'Sound', yellow: 'Needs attention', red: 'Broken' };
+
+  // Plain-English labels for the reason codes that actually GATE the verdict
+  // (the structural + truth set in deriveVerdict, src/core/project-health.ts).
+  // The map IS the filter: only codes present here surface in the why-line, so
+  // advisory/flow/value codes (blocked / unblocked / orphan_opportunity /
+  // unbound_persona / weak_persona_binding / clean) are excluded — they don't
+  // drive the verdict, so they're not "why". Labels are legible phrases, not the
+  // raw snake_case codes (no jargon chips in the cockpit).
+  var GATING_REASON_LABEL = {
+    broken_refs: 'broken references',
+    cycle_detected: 'dependency cycle',
+    orphan_check: 'orphan check',
+    coverage_unclaimed: 'unclaimed code',
+    stale_evidence: 'stale evidence',
+    mock_only_evidence: 'mocked evidence',
+    false_done: 'false-done',
+    narrative_cite_stale: 'stale narrative cite',
+    narrative_media_orphan: 'orphan media',
+  };
+
+  /**
+   * renderLifecycleBar(lc) — the cockpit's one segmented build bar. Every active
+   * bet sits in exactly one of four stages (verified → built → building →
+   * planned, done-to-not left-to-right). The blocked sub-count of a non-verified
+   * stage and the at_risk (stale) sub-count of verified render as a hatched
+   * sub-portion of that stage's segment. Present-only: a stage with count 0
+   * renders neither a segment nor a legend entry. Returns '' when lc is absent
+   * or has no active bets. This replaces the old verified-N/M bar + ready/blocked
+   * rows — one bar, one denominator (lc.total === truth.auditable).
+   */
+  function renderLifecycleBar(lc) {
+    if (!lc) return '';
+    var STAGES = [
+      { key: 'verified', label: 'verified', flagKey: 'at_risk', flagWord: 'stale' },
+      { key: 'built',    label: 'built',    flagKey: 'blocked', flagWord: 'blocked' },
+      { key: 'building', label: 'building', flagKey: 'blocked', flagWord: 'blocked' },
+      { key: 'planned',  label: 'planned',  flagKey: 'blocked', flagWord: 'blocked' }
+    ];
+    var total = lc.total || 0;
+    var verifiedCount = (lc.verified && lc.verified.count) || 0;
+    var segs = '';
+    var legend = '';
+    for (var i = 0; i < STAGES.length; i++) {
+      var s = STAGES[i];
+      var stage = lc[s.key] || {};
+      var count = stage.count || 0;
+      if (count <= 0) continue; // present-only — empty stages never render
+      var flag = stage[s.flagKey] || 0;
+      var solid = Math.max(0, count - flag);
+      // The segment is a flex row sized by count; the flagged portion is hatched.
+      var fills = '<i class="lifecycle-fill lifecycle-fill--' + s.key + '" style="flex:' + solid + '"></i>';
+      if (flag > 0) {
+        fills += '<i class="lifecycle-fill lifecycle-fill--' + s.key + ' lifecycle-fill--hatch" style="flex:' + flag + '"></i>';
+      }
+      segs += '<span class="lifecycle-seg lifecycle-seg--' + s.key + '" style="flex:' + count + '">' + fills + '</span>';
+      legend += '<li class="lifecycle-legend__item lifecycle-legend__item--' + s.key + '">' +
+        '<span class="lifecycle-swatch lifecycle-swatch--' + s.key + '"></span>' +
+        '<span class="lifecycle-legend__label">' + esc(s.label) + '</span> ' +
+        '<b class="lifecycle-legend__n">' + esc(String(count)) + '</b>' +
+        (flag > 0
+          ? '<span class="lifecycle-legend__sub">' + esc(String(flag) + ' ' + s.flagWord) + '</span>'
+          : '') +
+      '</li>';
+    }
+    if (!segs) return ''; // no active bets → no bar
+    return '<div class="lifecycle">' +
+      '<div class="lifecycle__head">' +
+        '<span class="lifecycle__label">build</span>' +
+        '<span class="lifecycle__ratio"><b>' + esc(String(verifiedCount)) + '</b> / ' + esc(String(total)) + '</span>' +
+      '</div>' +
+      '<div class="lifecycle-bar" role="img" aria-label="' + esc(verifiedCount + ' of ' + total + ' bets verified') + '">' + segs + '</div>' +
+      '<ul class="lifecycle-legend">' + legend + '</ul>' +
+    '</div>';
+  }
+
+  /**
+   * renderHealthCockpitInner(data) — the garden health cockpit body (verdict →
+   * why → lifecycle bar → flow pointers → refinement backlog), WITHOUT the
+   * rail's .detail-section wrapper. Returns '' when health_snapshot is absent.
+   *
+   * Both surfaces consume this: the rail (renderHealthCockpitRail, degraded
+   * gardens) and the masthead band (renderMastheadGarden). Single source of
+   * truth — the cockpit reads identically wherever it lands.
+   *
+   * Top→bottom, legible + hierarchical (no jargon, no raw reason-code chips):
+   *   1. Verdict — a plain word + color, the biggest element.
+   *   2. Progress — verified N/M + a bar (M = auditable, the honest denominator).
+   *   3. Flow + value — relabeled pointer rows. Only rows with data render.
+   *   4. Refinement backlog — collapsed, sub-grouped spine / content, calm tone.
+   */
+  function renderHealthCockpitInner(data) {
+    var snap = data && data.health_snapshot;
+    if (!snap) return '';
+    var verdict = snap.verdict || 'yellow';
+    var flow    = snap.flow   || {};
+    var value   = snap.value  || {};
+
+    // 1. Verdict — the biggest element. Plain word, colored via the existing
+    //    severity token. Falls back to the raw key if an unknown verdict slips in.
+    var verdictWord = VERDICT_WORD[verdict] || verdict;
+    var verdictHtml =
+      '<div class="health-verdict health-verdict--' + esc(verdict) + '">' + esc(verdictWord) + '</div>';
+
+    // 1b. Why-line — what a non-green verdict is reacting to, in plain language.
+    //     A verdict word alone ("Needs attention") says nothing actionable; this
+    //     lists the GATING reason codes (mapped to legible phrases, advisory codes
+    //     filtered out) joined with " · ". Green is Sound, so it shows no why-line.
+    var whyHtml = '';
+    if (verdict !== 'green' && isNonEmptyArray(snap.reason_codes)) {
+      var whyReasons = snap.reason_codes
+        .map(function (code) { return GATING_REASON_LABEL[code]; })
+        .filter(function (label) { return label != null; });
+      if (whyReasons.length) {
+        whyHtml = '<div class="health-why">' + esc(whyReasons.join(' · ')) + '</div>';
+      }
+    }
+
+    // 2. Progress — the lifecycle bar. ONE segmented bar over the active bets
+    //    (verified / built / building / planned, with blocked + stale hatched),
+    //    carrying the verified N / M ratio in its head. Replaces the old
+    //    verified-N/M bar AND the ready/blocked rows — one bar, one denominator
+    //    (lifecycle.total === truth.auditable). Presence-driven on snap.lifecycle.
+    var barHtml = renderLifecycleBar(snap.lifecycle);
+
+    // 3. Flow + value — relabeled, plain-language rows. Counts (ready / blocked)
+    //    render even at zero so the reader sees "blocked 0" rather than guessing;
+    //    pointer rows (bottleneck / next / weakest) render only when present.
+    function flowRow(key, val) {
+      return '<div class="health-rail-row">' +
+        '<span class="health-rail-row__key">' + esc(key) + '</span>' +
+        '<span class="health-rail-row__val">' + val + '</span>' +
+      '</div>';
+    }
+    function featureLink(id) {
+      return '<a class="dep-link" href="features/' + esc(id) + '.tend.html">' + esc(id) + '</a>';
+    }
+    // ready / blocked counts now live IN the lifecycle bar (its segments + the
+    // hatched sub-portions). The flow rows keep only the graph pointers — the
+    // single things worth clicking to: the bottleneck, the next bet, the gap.
+    var flowRows = '';
+    if (isNonEmptyString(flow.bottleneck_feature_id)) flowRows += flowRow('bottleneck', featureLink(flow.bottleneck_feature_id));
+    if (isNonEmptyString(value.highest_value_unblocked)) flowRows += flowRow('highest-value next', featureLink(value.highest_value_unblocked));
+    if (isNonEmptyString(value.weakest_opportunity_coverage)) flowRows += flowRow('weakest opportunity', featureLink(value.weakest_opportunity_coverage));
+
+    // 4. Refinement backlog — the connectedness + completeness nudges, grouped
+    //    + routed, sub-grouped by section (spine first, then content). CALM:
+    //    muted tone, no severity color. A backlog, not an alarm. Presence-driven
+    //    on coherence.total > 0. The field keeps its name (health_snapshot.coherence)
+    //    for schema stability; the user-facing label here is "to refine".
+    var backlogHtml = '';
+    var coherence = snap.coherence;
+    if (coherence && coherence.total > 0 && isNonEmptyArray(coherence.groups)) {
+      function groupRow(g) {
+        g = g || {};
+        return '<div class="health-coherence-row">' +
+          '<span class="health-coherence-row__label">' + esc(String(g.label || g.kind || '')) + '</span>' +
+          '<span class="health-coherence-row__count">' + esc(String(g.count != null ? g.count : '')) + '</span>' +
+          (isNonEmptyString(g.route)
+            ? '<span class="health-coherence-row__route">' + esc(g.route) + '</span>'
+            : '') +
+        '</div>';
+      }
+      // Sub-group by section. Groups without a section fall under 'spine'
+      // (the connectedness-invariant default — completeness groups carry 'content').
+      var SECTIONS = [
+        { key: 'spine',   label: 'spine' },
+        { key: 'content', label: 'content' }
+      ];
+      var sectionsHtml = SECTIONS.map(function (s) {
+        var members = coherence.groups.filter(function (g) {
+          var sec = (g && g.section) || 'spine';
+          return sec === s.key;
+        });
+        if (!members.length) return '';
+        return '<div class="health-coherence__subhead">' + esc(s.label) + '</div>' +
+          members.map(groupRow).join('');
+      }).join('');
+      // Collapsed-summary hint — name the top group labels (by count, the order
+      // buildCoherence already sorts) so a closed cockpit reads as a move ("what
+      // to refine") not a bare number. Show up to 2 labels; the rest roll into a
+      // "+N more" tail. Labels (not raw rule kinds) — same vocabulary as the rows.
+      var HINT_LABELS = 2;
+      var topLabels = coherence.groups.slice(0, HINT_LABELS).map(function (g) {
+        return (g && g.label) || (g && g.kind) || '';
+      }).filter(function (l) { return l.length > 0; });
+      var moreCount = coherence.groups.length - topLabels.length;
+      var hintText = topLabels.join(', ');
+      if (moreCount > 0) hintText += ' +' + moreCount + ' more';
+      var hintHtml = hintText
+        ? '<span class="health-coherence__hint">' + esc(hintText) + '</span>'
+        : '';
+      backlogHtml = '<details class="health-coherence collapsible-section">' +
+        '<summary class="health-coherence__summary">' +
+          '<span class="collapsible-toggle">▾</span>' +
+          '<span class="health-coherence__label">to refine</span>' +
+          hintHtml +
+          '<span class="health-coherence__total">' + esc(String(coherence.total)) + '</span>' +
+        '</summary>' +
+        sectionsHtml +
+      '</details>';
+    }
+
+    return '<div class="health-rail-block">' +
+      verdictHtml +
+      whyHtml +
+      barHtml +
+      flowRows +
+      backlogHtml +
+    '</div>';
+  }
+
+  // renderHealthCockpitRail(data) — the rail-wrapped cockpit. When the masthead
+  // carries the cockpit (the normal garden case), renderPage skips this; the
+  // wrapper survives so a garden WITHOUT a masthead still shows health in the rail.
+  function renderHealthCockpitRail(data) {
+    var inner = renderHealthCockpitInner(data);
+    if (!inner) return '';
+    return railSection('health', inner, { collapsible: true });
+  }
+
+  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+  // MASTHEAD — the full-width editorial band above the three columns (M1).
+  //
+  // ONE component, presence-driven, class-aware by data (no per-kind scaffold).
+  // Four variants, decided entirely from data:
+  //   GARDEN              — meta.kind === 'garden'. Title + dek-as-tagline +
+  //                         the health cockpit (moved out of the rail) + catalog
+  //                         chips with view-time counts.
+  //   PERSONA/OPPORTUNITY — data.persona / data.opportunity set. Archetype quote
+  //                         (persona) or description lede (opportunity) as the
+  //                         standfirst, portrait/status card, count chips.
+  //   RICH                — a feature/subpage WITH a dek OR media/thumbnail.
+  //                         Kicker + h1 + dek standfirst + persona/solves chips,
+  //                         visual card + status card.
+  //   DEGRADED            — none of the above (no dek AND no media). Returns ''
+  //                         so the scaffold's compact header is kept.
+  //
+  // When the masthead renders it ABSORBS (renderPage suppresses) the rail's top
+  // meta strip and (garden) the rail health cockpit; oo-boot suppresses the
+  // top-chrome status chip. Nothing is copied — each fact has one home.
+  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+
+  // True when this polyglot earns a masthead band. Garden + persona/opportunity
+  // anchors with content always do; a plain feature does only when it carries a
+  // dek OR renderable media (a kind:diagram entry or an svg thumbnail).
+  function mastheadVisual(data) {
+    // Priority: first kind:diagram media > svg thumbnail > none.
+    var media = data && data.media;
+    if (media) {
+      var keys = Object.keys(media);
+      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
+        var m = media[keys[i]];
+        if (m && m.kind === 'diagram' && isNonEmptyString(m.content)) {
+          return { svg: m.content, alt: m.alt || '' };
+        }
+      }
+    }
+    var t = data && data.thumbnail;
+    if (t && t.kind === 'svg' && isNonEmptyString(t.content)) {
+      return { svg: t.content, alt: t.alt || '' };
+    }
+    return null;
+  }
+
+  function hasMasthead(data, meta) {
+    if (!data) return false;
+    var kind = meta && meta.kind;
+    if (kind === 'garden') return true;
+    if (data.persona) return isNonEmptyString(data.persona.archetype) || Boolean(mastheadVisual(data));
+    if (data.opportunity) return isNonEmptyString(data.opportunity.description) || Boolean(mastheadVisual(data));
+    return isNonEmptyString(data.dek) || Boolean(mastheadVisual(data));
+  }
+
+  function mastheadTitle(data) {
+    return (data && (data.title || data.id)) || '';
+  }
+
+  // The kicker line — kind · journey_phase · status word, mono caps.
+  function mastheadKicker(parts) {
+    var clean = parts.filter(function (p) { return isNonEmptyString(p); });
+    if (!clean.length) return '';
+    return '<p class="masthead-tend__kicker">' +
+      clean.map(function (p) { return esc(p); }).join(' <span class="masthead-tend__kicker-sep">·</span> ') +
+    '</p>';
+  }
+
+  // The visual card (white, bordered) — first kind:diagram media > thumbnail.
+  function mastheadVisualCard(visual, title) {
+    if (!visual) return '';
+    var alt = visual.alt || (title ? title + ' illustration' : 'illustration');
+    return '<figure class="masthead-tend__visual" role="img" aria-label="' + esc(alt) + '">' +
+      visual.svg +
+    '</figure>';
+  }
+
+  // Persona / solves chips, linked to their sibling polyglots.
+  function mastheadRefChips(data) {
+    var chips = [];
+    (isNonEmptyArray(data.personas) ? data.personas : []).forEach(function (p) {
+      chips.push('<a class="masthead-tend__chip masthead-tend__chip--persona" href="' + esc(p) + '.tend.html">' + esc(p) + '</a>');
+    });
+    (isNonEmptyArray(data.solves) ? data.solves : []).forEach(function (s) {
+      chips.push('<a class="masthead-tend__chip masthead-tend__chip--solves" href="' + esc(s) + '.tend.html">' + esc(s) + '</a>');
+    });
+    if (!chips.length) return '';
+    return '<div class="masthead-tend__chips">' + chips.join('') + '</div>';
+  }
+
+  // The status card — status chip + honest check tally, deep-linking to the
+  // rail's checks section. A verified/pass tally reads "n/n checks"; an
+  // unproven bet reads "N checks open" — never a false green.
+  function mastheadStatusCard(data) {
+    var status = data && data.status;
+    if (!isNonEmptyString(status)) return '';
+    var checks = isNonEmptyArray(data.checks) ? data.checks : [];
+    var verdicts = (data.audit && isNonEmptyArray(data.audit.verdicts)) ? data.audit.verdicts : [];
+    var passed = verdicts.filter(function (v) { return v && v.verdict === 'pass'; }).length;
+
+    var tally = '';
+    if (checks.length) {
+      // A deep-link target: the first check's rail anchor (the checks section).
+      var anchorId = checks[0].id;
+      var anchor = isNonEmptyString(anchorId) ? '#rail-' + esc(anchorId) : '#checks';
+      var label = (passed > 0)
+        ? esc(String(passed)) + '/' + esc(String(checks.length)) + ' checks'
+        : esc(String(checks.length)) + ' checks open';
+      tally = '<a class="masthead-tend__status-checks" href="' + anchor + '">' + label + '</a>';
+    }
+    var statusChip = '<span class="masthead-tend__status-chip">' +
+      glyphSpan(glyphFor(status), glyphClassFor(status)) + ' ' + esc(status) +
+    '</span>';
+    return '<div class="masthead-tend__status-card">' + statusChip + tally + '</div>';
+  }
+
+  // Count chip helper for anchor / garden bands — "<n> <label>".
+  function mastheadCountChip(n, singular, plural, href) {
+    var label = esc(String(n)) + ' ' + esc(n === 1 ? singular : plural);
+    if (isNonEmptyString(href)) {
+      return '<a class="masthead-tend__chip" href="' + esc(href) + '">' + label + '</a>';
+    }
+    return '<span class="masthead-tend__chip">' + label + '</span>';
+  }
+
+  // The garden tagline — the dek, split deterministically at the first sentence
+  // boundary. The lead sentence reads as the project's claim (accent-italic via
+  // .masthead-tend__tagline-lead); the rest follows in the muted serif tone. A
+  // single-sentence dek yields lead = whole dek, no trailing fragment. The split
+  // matches renderDek's terminator regex so the boundary is consistent.
+  function renderGardenTagline(dek) {
+    if (!isNonEmptyString(dek)) return '';
+    var text = String(dek).trim();
+    // First sentence = up to and including the first .!? terminator (with any
+    // trailing quote/bracket), then optional whitespace before the rest.
+    var m = text.match(/^([\s\S]*?[.!?]+(?:["')\]]+)?)(\s+)([\s\S]+)$/);
+    var lead, rest;
+    if (m) { lead = m[1].trim(); rest = m[3].trim(); }
+    else { lead = text; rest = ''; }
+    var html = '<span class="masthead-tend__tagline-lead">' + inlineCode(lead) + '</span>';
+    if (rest) html += ' <span class="masthead-tend__tagline-rest">' + inlineCode(rest) + '</span>';
+    return '<p class="masthead-tend__tagline">' + html + '</p>';
+  }
+
+  // ── GARDEN band ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+  function renderMastheadGarden(data, meta) {
+    var title = mastheadTitle(data) || (meta && meta.project_name) || 'garden';
+    var kicker = mastheadKicker(['garden', data && data.status]);
+    var titleHtml = '<h1 class="masthead-tend__title">' + esc(title) + '</h1>';
+    var dekHtml = renderGardenTagline(data.dek);
+
+    // Catalog chips — counts computed at view time from live data.
+    var catalog = data.catalog || {};
+    var nPersonas = isNonEmptyArray(catalog.personas) ? catalog.personas.length : 0;
+    var nOpps = isNonEmptyArray(catalog.opportunities) ? catalog.opportunities.length : 0;
+    var agg = data.features_aggregate;
+    var aggEntries = Array.isArray(agg)
+      ? agg
+      : (agg && Array.isArray(agg.entries) ? agg.entries : []);
+    if ((!aggEntries || !aggEntries.length) && agg && agg.by_phase) {
+      var seen = {};
+      aggEntries = [];
+      Object.keys(agg.by_phase).forEach(function (k) {
+        (agg.by_phase[k] || []).forEach(function (e) {
+          if (e && e.id && !seen[e.id]) { seen[e.id] = true; aggEntries.push(e); }
+        });
+      });
+    }
+    // "N bets" must match the cockpit's denominator — the count of ACTIVE BETS
+    // (isActiveBet: not a persona/opportunity anchor, not superseded). That set
+    // INCLUDES child features with a parent (still real bets), so the raw
+    // non-subpage aggregate filter is wrong on both ends. The honest source is
+    // the lifecycle bar's own total (= truth.auditable), computed server-side
+    // over isActiveBet. Read it; fall back to truth.auditable; only when there
+    // is no health snapshot at all fall back to the non-subpage aggregate count.
+    var snap = data.health_snapshot;
+    var nBets;
+    if (snap && snap.lifecycle && snap.lifecycle.total != null) {
+      nBets = snap.lifecycle.total;
+    } else if (snap && snap.truth && snap.truth.auditable != null) {
+      nBets = snap.truth.auditable;
+    } else {
+      nBets = (aggEntries || []).filter(function (e) { return e && !e.is_subpage; }).length;
+    }
+    var chipBits = [
+      mastheadCountChip(nPersonas, 'persona', 'personas'),
+      mastheadCountChip(nOpps, 'opportunity', 'opportunities'),
+      mastheadCountChip(nBets, 'bet', 'bets'),
+      '<a class="masthead-tend__chip masthead-tend__chip--board" href="board.html">board →</a>',
+    ];
+    var chipsHtml = '<div class="masthead-tend__chips masthead-tend__chips--catalog">' + chipBits.join('') + '</div>';
+
+    // The cockpit verdict card — the rail's renderHealthCockpitRail content,
+    // unwrapped (no detail-section), moved into the band.
+    var cockpitInner = renderHealthCockpitInner(data);
+    var cockpitHtml = cockpitInner
+      ? '<aside class="masthead-tend__cockpit">' + cockpitInner + '</aside>'
+      : '';
+
+    return '<div class="masthead-tend masthead-tend--garden">' +
+      '<div class="masthead-tend__inner">' +
+        '<div class="masthead-tend__lead">' +
+          kicker + titleHtml + dekHtml + chipsHtml +
+        '</div>' +
+        cockpitHtml +
+      '</div>' +
+    '</div>';
+  }
+
+  // ── PERSONA / OPPORTUNITY band ──────────────────────────────────────────────
+  function renderMastheadAnchor(data, meta) {
+    var title = mastheadTitle(data);
+    var visual = mastheadVisual(data);
+    var isPersona = Boolean(data.persona);
+    var variant = isPersona ? 'persona' : 'opportunity';
+    var kind = (meta && meta.kind) || (isPersona ? 'persona' : 'opportunity');
+
+    var standfirst = '';
+    var chips = [];
+    if (isPersona) {
+      var p = data.persona;
+      if (isNonEmptyString(p.archetype)) {
+        standfirst = '<blockquote class="masthead-tend__dek masthead-tend__dek--quote">' + inlineCode(p.archetype) + '</blockquote>';
+      }
+      var nJobs = isNonEmptyArray(p.jobs) ? p.jobs.length : 0;
+      var nBound = isNonEmptyArray(p.bound_features) ? p.bound_features.length : 0;
+      if (nJobs) chips.push(mastheadCountChip(nJobs, 'job', 'jobs'));
+      if (nBound) chips.push(mastheadCountChip(nBound, 'bound feature', 'bound features'));
+    } else {
+      var o = data.opportunity;
+      if (isNonEmptyString(o.description)) {
+        standfirst = '<p class="masthead-tend__dek">' + inlineCode(o.description) + '</p>';
+      }
+      if (o.value)      chips.push('<span class="masthead-tend__chip">' + esc('value: ' + o.value) + '</span>');
+      if (o.confidence) chips.push('<span class="masthead-tend__chip">' + esc('confidence: ' + o.confidence) + '</span>');
+      var nSolving = isNonEmptyArray(o.solving_features) ? o.solving_features.length : 0;
+      if (nSolving) chips.push(mastheadCountChip(nSolving, 'solved-by', 'solved-by'));
+    }
+
+    var kicker = mastheadKicker([kind, data && data.status]);
+    var chipsHtml = chips.length ? '<div class="masthead-tend__chips">' + chips.join('') + '</div>' : '';
+    var visualHtml = mastheadVisualCard(visual, title);
+    var rightHtml = visualHtml
+      ? '<div class="masthead-tend__aside">' + visualHtml + '</div>'
+      : '';
+
+    return '<div class="masthead-tend masthead-tend--' + variant + '">' +
+      '<div class="masthead-tend__inner">' +
+        '<div class="masthead-tend__lead">' +
+          kicker +
+          '<h1 class="masthead-tend__title">' + esc(title) + '</h1>' +
+          standfirst + chipsHtml +
+        '</div>' +
+        rightHtml +
+      '</div>' +
+    '</div>';
+  }
+
+  // ── RICH band (feature / subpage with a dek or media) ───────────────────────
+  function renderMastheadRich(data, meta) {
+    var title = mastheadTitle(data);
+    var visual = mastheadVisual(data);
+    var kicker = mastheadKicker([(meta && meta.kind) || 'feature', data && data.journey_phase, data && data.status]);
+    var dekHtml = isNonEmptyString(data.dek)
+      ? '<p class="masthead-tend__dek">' + inlineCode(data.dek) + '</p>'
+      : '';
+    var chipsHtml = mastheadRefChips(data);
+    var visualHtml = mastheadVisualCard(visual, title);
+    var statusHtml = mastheadStatusCard(data);
+    var asideHtml = (visualHtml || statusHtml)
+      ? '<div class="masthead-tend__aside">' + visualHtml + statusHtml + '</div>'
+      : '';
+
+    return '<div class="masthead-tend masthead-tend--rich">' +
+      '<div class="masthead-tend__inner">' +
+        '<div class="masthead-tend__lead">' +
+          kicker +
+          '<h1 class="masthead-tend__title">' + esc(title) + '</h1>' +
+          dekHtml + chipsHtml +
+        '</div>' +
+        asideHtml +
+      '</div>' +
+    '</div>';
+  }
+
+  // The single entry. Returns '' for the degraded case (no band).
+  function renderMasthead(data, meta) {
+    if (!hasMasthead(data, meta)) return '';
+    if (meta && meta.kind === 'garden') return renderMastheadGarden(data, meta);
+    if (data.persona || data.opportunity) return renderMastheadAnchor(data, meta);
+    return renderMastheadRich(data, meta);
+  }
+
+  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+  // ENTRY POINT
+  // ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+
+  // Header illustration — the page's own thumbnail, shown beside the title.
+  // Presence-driven: returns '' when no authored svg thumbnail exists, so the
+  // title spans the full header width (no empty reserved gap).
+  function renderHeaderThumb(data) {
+    var t = data && data.thumbnail;
+    if (!t || t.kind !== 'svg' || !t.content) return '';
+    var alt = t.alt || ((((data && (data.title || data.id)) || 'page')) + ' illustration');
+    return '<div class="article-head__thumb-inner" role="img" aria-label="' + esc(alt) + '">' + t.content + '</div>';
+  }
+
+  // Garden header health headline — a compact, labeled health indicator for the
+  // top header. GARDEN-ONLY (gated on meta.kind + health_snapshot presence;
+  // feature pages carry no health_snapshot). It is deliberately distinct from
+  // the bet-status glyph wired in oo-boot (which shows the garden's OWN
+  // bet-status, e.g. "in-progress"): this one is labeled `health`, colored via
+  // the .health-verdict--{green,yellow,red} tokens, and reports the
+  // verified/auditable progress count — project soundness, not bet lifecycle.
+  // Returns '' for non-gardens and when no health_snapshot exists.
+  function renderHeaderHealth(data, meta) {
+    if (!meta || meta.kind !== 'garden') return '';
+    var snap = data && data.health_snapshot;
+    if (!snap) return '';
+    var verdict = snap.verdict || 'yellow';
+    // Verified N / M over the ACTIVE BETS — the same honest denominator the
+    // lifecycle bar uses, so the header and the bar can never disagree. Prefer
+    // the lifecycle block (the bar's own source); fall back to truth.auditable
+    // (identical by construction) for pre-lifecycle snapshots. We deliberately
+    // do NOT fall back to truth.total: counting all polyglots (catalog
+    // descriptions + the garden) as a verification denominator is the
+    // misleading "/42" this redesign removes — better to show no count than a
+    // wrong one.
+    var lc = snap.lifecycle;
+    var truth = snap.truth || {};
+    var verified = (lc && lc.verified) ? lc.verified.count : truth.verified;
+    var denom = (lc && lc.total != null) ? lc.total : truth.auditable;
+    var count = '';
+    if (verified != null && denom != null) {
+      count = '<span class="header-health__count">' +
+        esc(String(verified)) + '/' + esc(String(denom)) +
+      '</span>';
+    }
+    return '<span class="header-health">' +
+      '<span class="header-health__label">health</span>' +
+      '<span class="health-verdict health-verdict--' + esc(verdict) + '">' + esc(VERDICT_WORD[verdict] || verdict) + '</span>' +
+      count +
+    '</span>';
+  }
+
+  // renderWhatMissingPanel(data, meta) — VR-6 "What's missing" decision panel.
+  //
+  // Garden main column ONLY. Reads health_snapshot.what_missing (a pure
+  // computed block from computeProjectHealth). Three signals:
+  //   1. Uncovered persona jobs — grouped by persona
+  //   2. Incomplete bets — N features missing ≥1 slot, linked by id
+  //   3. Unclaimed code — N source files owned by no feature
+  //
+  // Presence-driven: returns '' when no signal is present (no uncovered_intent,
+  // no incomplete_bets, no unclaimed_code), or when called on a feature page.
+  // NEVER shows red/error styling — calm, neutral, human-facing.
+  function renderWhatMissingPanel(data, meta) {
+    if (!meta || meta.kind !== 'garden') return '';
+    var snap = data && data.health_snapshot;
+    if (!snap) return '';
+    var wm = snap.what_missing;
+    if (!wm) return '';
+
+    // Determine if there is anything to show.
+    var hasIntent = isNonEmptyArray(wm.uncovered_intent);
+    var hasBets = wm.incomplete_bets > 0;
+    var hasCode = wm.unclaimed_code > 0;
+    if (!hasIntent && !hasBets && !hasCode) return '';
+
+    var html = '';
+
+    // 1. Uncovered persona jobs
+    if (hasIntent) {
+      var personaGroupsHtml = wm.uncovered_intent.map(function (pg) {
+        pg = pg || {};
+        var jobs = isNonEmptyArray(pg.uncovered_jobs) ? pg.uncovered_jobs : [];
+        var jobsHtml = jobs.map(function (job) {
+          return '<li class="whats-missing-tend__job">' + esc(String(job)) + '</li>';
+        }).join('');
+        return '<div class="whats-missing-tend__persona">' +
+          '<div class="whats-missing-tend__persona-name">' + esc(String(pg.persona_name || pg.persona_id || '')) + '</div>' +
+          '<ul class="whats-missing-tend__job-list">' + jobsHtml + '</ul>' +
+        '</div>';
+      }).join('');
+      html +=
+        '<div class="whats-missing-tend__section">' +
+        '<div class="whats-missing-tend__section-label">uncovered jobs</div>' +
+        personaGroupsHtml +
+        '</div>';
+    }
+
+    // 2. Incomplete bets
+    if (hasBets) {
+      var ids = isNonEmptyArray(wm.incomplete_bet_ids) ? wm.incomplete_bet_ids : [];
+      var linksHtml = ids.map(function (id) {
+        return '<a href="features/' + esc(String(id)) + '.tend.html" class="tend-link">' + esc(String(id)) + '</a>';
+      }).join('');
+      var moreCount = wm.incomplete_bets - ids.length;
+      var moreSuffix = moreCount > 0
+        ? '<span class="whats-missing-tend__bet-links"> +' + esc(String(moreCount)) + ' more</span>'
+        : '';
+      html +=
+        '<div class="whats-missing-tend__section">' +
+        '<div class="whats-missing-tend__section-label">incomplete bets</div>' +
+        '<div class="whats-missing-tend__bets-count">' +
+          esc(String(wm.incomplete_bets)) + ' feature' + (wm.incomplete_bets !== 1 ? 's' : '') + ' with empty slots' +
+        '</div>' +
+        '<div class="whats-missing-tend__bet-links">' + linksHtml + moreSuffix + '</div>' +
+        '</div>';
+    }
+
+    // 3. Unclaimed code
+    if (hasCode) {
+      var sample = isNonEmptyArray(wm.unclaimed_code_sample) ? wm.unclaimed_code_sample : [];
+      var sampleHtml = sample.map(function (p) {
+        return '<li class="whats-missing-tend__code-path">' + esc(String(p)) + '</li>';
+      }).join('');
+      var morePaths = wm.unclaimed_code - sample.length;
+      var morePathSuffix = morePaths > 0
+        ? '<li class="whats-missing-tend__code-path">&hellip;and ' + esc(String(morePaths)) + ' more</li>'
+        : '';
+      html +=
+        '<div class="whats-missing-tend__section">' +
+        '<div class="whats-missing-tend__section-label">unclaimed code</div>' +
+        '<div class="whats-missing-tend__code-count">' +
+          esc(String(wm.unclaimed_code)) + ' source file' + (wm.unclaimed_code !== 1 ? 's' : '') + ' owned by no feature' +
+        '</div>' +
+        '<ul class="whats-missing-tend__code-sample">' + sampleHtml + morePathSuffix + '</ul>' +
+        '</div>';
+    }
+
+    return '<section class="whats-missing-tend">' +
+      '<h2 class="whats-missing-tend__heading">What\'s missing</h2>' +
+      html +
+    '</section>';
+  }
+
+  function renderPage(data, meta) {
+    data = data || {};
+    meta = meta || {};
+
+    // The masthead band (M1) — full-width above the three columns. Presence-
+    // driven; '' for the degraded case. When it renders it ABSORBS the rail's
+    // top meta strip, the rail health cockpit (garden), and the top-chrome
+    // status chip (the last suppressed by oo-boot via mastheadActive).
+    var masthead = renderMasthead(data, meta);
+    var mastheadActive = masthead !== '';
+
+    // Main column shape discriminator. Persona / opportunity polyglots
+    // describe a catalog entity, not a feature bet — so they skip the
+    // WWIFWWh bet article and render their own shape. Decisions, references,
+    // narrative, superseded banner still apply (presence-driven).
+    var mainBody;
+    // Card-grid sections rendered after narrative and before decisions.
+    // Each context has one source; '' when source is empty (presence-driven).
+    var cardsHtml = '';
+    // The sectionId used to build cardsHtml (e.g. 'features', 'subpages').
+    // Used to suppress duplicates when the narrative resolved the same token inline.
+    var cardsSectionId = '';
+    // Accumulator: tracks which reserved tokens ([features]/[personas]/[opportunities])
+    // were resolved inline by the narrative. Used to suppress the automatic
+    // duplicate section that would otherwise appear after the narrative.
+    var acc = { resolvedReserved: {} };
+
+    if (data.persona) {
+      mainBody = renderPersonaMain(data);
+      // c) Persona polyglot: source from data.persona.bound_features[].
+      var boundFeatures = data.persona && data.persona.bound_features;
+      if (isNonEmptyArray(boundFeatures)) {
+        cardsSectionId = 'bound-features';
+        cardsHtml = renderEntityCardGrid(boundFeatures, 'Bound features', cardsSectionId, '');
+      }
+    } else if (data.opportunity) {
+      mainBody = renderOpportunityMain(data);
+      // d) Opportunity polyglot: source from data.opportunity.solving_features[].
+      var solvingFeatures = data.opportunity && data.opportunity.solving_features;
+      if (isNonEmptyArray(solvingFeatures)) {
+        cardsSectionId = 'solving-features';
+        cardsHtml = renderEntityCardGrid(solvingFeatures, 'Solving features', cardsSectionId, '');
+      }
+    } else {
+      mainBody = renderBetArticle(data);
+      var isGarden = meta && meta.kind === 'garden';
+      if (isGarden) {
+        // a) Garden: source from data.features_aggregate (flat array or legacy object shape).
+        // Thumbnail is NOT carried in catalog entries; autoIcon() fills the gap.
+        var agg = data.features_aggregate;
+        var aggEntries = Array.isArray(agg)
+          ? agg
+          : (agg && Array.isArray(agg.entries) ? agg.entries : null);
+        if (!aggEntries && agg && agg.by_phase) {
+          // Legacy by_phase object — flatten to a unique entries list.
+          var seen = {};
+          aggEntries = [];
+          Object.keys(agg.by_phase).forEach(function (k) {
+            (agg.by_phase[k] || []).forEach(function (e) {
+              if (e && e.id && !seen[e.id]) { seen[e.id] = true; aggEntries.push(e); }
+            });
+          });
+        }
+        if (isNonEmptyArray(aggEntries)) {
+          // Filter out subpages (is_subpage: true) — parent owns the card.
+          var topLevel = aggEntries.filter(function (e) { return !e.is_subpage; });
+          cardsSectionId = 'features';
+          cardsHtml = renderEntityCardGrid(topLevel, 'Features', cardsSectionId, 'features/');
+        }
+      } else if (isNonEmptyArray(data.subpages_resolved) || isNonEmptyArray(data.subpages)) {
+        // b) Feature with subpages: prefer the resolved snapshot (carries
+        // title + what + thumbnail + journey_phase from each child); fall back
+        // to id-only items from data.subpages[] when the snapshot is absent
+        // (e.g., a freshly hand-edited polyglot before tend aggregate runs).
+        var subpageItems = isNonEmptyArray(data.subpages_resolved)
+          ? data.subpages_resolved
+          : data.subpages.map(function (id) {
+              return typeof id === 'string' ? { id: id } : id;
+            });
+        cardsSectionId = 'subpages';
+        cardsHtml = renderEntityCardGrid(subpageItems, 'Subpages', cardsSectionId, '');
+      }
+    }
+
+    // Garden kind: decisions + references belong in feature polyglots, not on
+    // the overview page. The garden's narrative is its landing-page article;
+    // decisions there would be project-level noise the reader didn't ask for.
+    // Resolve narrative now — this fills acc.resolvedReserved with any reserved
+    // card-grid tokens ([features]/[personas]/[opportunities]) embedded inline.
+    var narrativeHtml = renderNarrative(data, acc);
+    // Suppress the automatic card-grid when the narrative already inlined the same
+    // section via a reserved token — compare sectionIds, not HTML string content.
+    if (cardsHtml && acc.resolvedReserved[cardsSectionId]) {
+      cardsHtml = '';
+    }
+    var main =
+      // The dek MOVES into the masthead when the band renders — never duplicated
+      // in main. Degraded pages (no band) keep the dek standfirst here.
+      (mastheadActive ? '' : renderDek(data)) +
+      renderSupersededBanner(data) +
+      mainBody +
+      narrativeHtml +
+      cardsHtml +
+      // Garden ONLY: "What's missing" decision panel (VR-6). Reads from
+      // health_snapshot.what_missing — never recomputes. Absent on feature pages.
+      (isGarden ? renderWhatMissingPanel(data, meta) : '') +
+      (isGarden ? '' : renderDecisions(data)) +
+      (isGarden ? '' : renderReferences(data));
+
+    // The rail's outer .detail-panel is fixed-height with overflow:hidden.
+    // .detail-meta is flex-shrink:0 (sticky top); everything after goes
+    // inside .detail-body which is flex:1 + overflow-y:auto — that's the
+    // wrapper that actually scrolls when content exceeds viewport height.
+    // The rail groups every section into THREE fixed-order bands — IDENTITY →
+    // GRAPH → PROOF — so a reader builds muscle-memory wayfinding by position.
+    // IDENTITY = who/what this is (journey, parent, persona/opportunity identity);
+    // GRAPH = how it links out (edges, bound/solving features); PROOF = whether
+    // it holds (checks, steps, smoke, audit, progress + the garden's catalog +
+    // aggregate). Each band is presence-driven: empty → no wrapper (see railBand).
+    var anchorRail = (data && data.persona)
+      ? renderPersonaRail(data.persona)
+      : (data && data.opportunity ? renderOpportunityRail(data.opportunity) : { identity: '', graph: '' });
+
+    var railBody =
+      // The garden cockpit lives in the masthead when the band renders; the rail
+      // wrapper only fires for a garden with no band (degraded). Absorb = remove.
+      // It sits ABOVE the bands — it is the garden's verdict, not a band section.
+      (mastheadActive ? '' : renderHealthCockpitRail(data)) +
+      railBand('identity', 'identity', [
+        renderJourney(data),
+        renderParent(data),
+        anchorRail.identity,
+      ]) +
+      railBand('graph', 'graph', [
+        renderRelList('depends on', data.dependencies),
+        renderRelList('enables',    data.enables),
+        renderRelList('subpages',   data.subpages),
+        anchorRail.graph,
+      ]) +
+      railBand('proof', 'proof', [
+        renderSteps(data),
+        renderChecks(data),
+        renderSmoke(data),
+        renderCatalog(data, meta),
+        renderAggregate(data),
+        renderAudit(data),
+        renderProgress(data),
+      ]);
+
+    // Topic extension hook: anything pushed into data._customSections (string
+    // of HTML) is appended to rail. Topics in OO:CUSTOM can build this up
+    // before render is called, OR use window.OO_AFTER_RENDER to append DOM
